### [1] [Gradient-Based Latent Decomposition Reveals Mechanisms of Feature Degradation in Weakly Supervised Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24835)
*Vinceline Bertrand,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了弱监督层次模型中粗粒度特征与细粒度特征之间存在的表征不对称性，并提出了基于梯度的正交潜变量分解方法（H-VAE）来解释这一现象。实验表明，仅约4.4%的潜变量与监督梯度对齐，而95.6%的细粒度病理信息依赖于正交残差子空间，导致恶性度预测性能显著下降（AUC差距0.314）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弱监督层次模型存在一种持续性的不对称性：粗粒度的病灶类型特征在重建过程中得以保留，而细粒度的恶性度线索则发生退化，这直接影响乳腺X光筛查的临床可靠性。现有方法未能从机制上解释这一现象。

Method: 提出了基于梯度的正交潜变量分解方法（Gradient-based Orthogonal Latent Decomposition），用于层次变分自编码器（H-VAE）。将潜空间划分为任务对齐分量（z₁，受粗粒度监督梯度引导）和正交残差（z_res，捕获剩余表征能力），从而从机制上解释粗细粒度特征的不对称性。

Result: 在CBIS-DDSM的3,550个乳腺ROI上：仅~4.4%的潜变量幅度与监督梯度对齐，~95.6%位于正交残差；Stage-1 AUC 0.866，Stage-2 AUC 0.552；重建稳定性差距Δ_diag=5%（p=0.005），分类差距Δ_AUC=0.314（p<0.001）。潜变量消融实验证实两类任务的特征都主要位于z_res，结构上解释了重建为何不成比例地损害病理稳定性。与MIL和MTL的对比验证了跨架构和跨模态的泛化性。

Conclusion: 在高维空间中，单一的粗粒度监督信号仅能隔离出一个稀疏的一维潜方向，迫使关键的细粒度特征落入脆弱的残差子空间中，从而结构性地解释了弱监督层次模型中的粗细粒度不对称性。

Original Abstract: Weakly supervised hierarchical models exhibit a persistent asymmetry: coarse lesion-type features are preserved under reconstruction while fine-grained malignancy cues degrade---a pattern with direct consequences for the clinical reliability of breast cancer screening pipelines. We introduce gradient-based orthogonal latent decomposition for hierarchical Variational Autoencoders~(H-VAEs) to mechanistically explain this asymmetry. The latent space is partitioned into a task-aligned component~($z_1$), shaped by coarse supervisory gradients, and an orthogonal residual~($z_{\text{res}}$) capturing remaining representational capacity. On~3,550 mammographic Regions of Interest~(ROIs) from CBIS-DDSM, only~$\sim$4.4\% of latent magnitude aligns with supervisory gradients, leaving~$\sim$95.6\% in the orthogonal residual upon which fine-grained pathology prediction primarily depends. The model achieves Stage-1~AUC~0.866 and Stage 2~AUC~0.552, with a reconstruction stability gap of $Δ_{\text{diag}}=5\%$ ($p=0.005$) and a classification gap of $Δ_{\text{AUC}}=0.314$ ($p{<}0.001$). Latent ablation confirms that features for both tasks reside heavily in~$z_{\text{res}}$, structurally explaining why reconstruction degrades pathology stability disproportionately. Comparisons with Multi-Instance Learning~(MIL) and Multi-Task Learning~(MTL) confirm generalization across architectures and modalities. These findings reveal that in high-dimensional spaces, a single coarse supervisory signal isolates only a sparse 1D latent direction, forcing critical fine-grained features into the vulnerable residual subspace.

中文摘要翻译: 弱监督层次模型表现出一种持续的不对称性：粗粒度的病灶类型特征在重建过程中得以保留，而细粒度的恶性度线索则发生退化——这一模式对乳腺X光筛查管线的临床可靠性有直接影响。我们引入了基于梯度的正交潜变量分解方法用于层次变分自编码器（H-VAE），以从机制上解释这种不对称性。潜空间被划分为任务对齐分量（z₁，由粗粒度监督梯度塑造）和正交残差（z_res，捕获剩余的表征能力）。在来自CBIS-DDSM的3,550个乳腺感兴趣区域（ROI）上，仅约4.4%的潜变量幅度与监督梯度对齐，约95.6%位于正交残差中，而细粒度病理预测主要依赖于后者。该模型在Stage-1上达到AUC 0.866，在Stage-2上达到AUC 0.552，重建稳定性差距Δ_diag=5%（p=0.005），分类差距Δ_AUC=0.314（p<0.001）。潜变量消融实验证实，两类任务的特征都大量分布在z_res中，从结构上解释了为何重建不成比例地损害病理稳定性。与多实例学习（MIL）和多任务学习（MTL）的对比验证了该方法跨架构和跨模态的泛化性。这些发现揭示：在高维空间中，单一的粗粒度监督信号仅能隔离出一个稀疏的一维潜方向，迫使关键的细粒度特征落入脆弱的残差子空间中。

</details>


### [2] [Track-Leakage-Free Hold-Out Self-Validation for Photogrammetric Reconstruction: Protocol, Sensitivity, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24852)
*Behnam Asadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无轨迹泄漏的留出协议（Track-Leakage-Free Hold-Out Protocol），用于评估摄影测量3D重建的内部几何一致性，而非绝对精度。实验表明该协议无法替代控制点评估，也不能作为通用粗差检测器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 摄影测量中的3D重建缺乏外部真值验证时，其自身可靠性未知。现有方法无法区分内部一致性与绝对精度，需要一种无需地面真值即可评估重建可靠性的协议。

Method: 提出无轨迹泄漏的留出协议：保留确定性子集图像，每张图像仅针对至少两张保留图像可见的3D点进行重定位（轨迹级屏障），聚合为mAA置信度。留出观测仍参与光束法平差构建可信结构，确保无轨迹泄漏。

Result: 四个主要发现：(i)好的重建自一致性达毫度级；(ii)阈值化自一致性饱和，与绝对精度不相关（相关系数-0.57至+0.98，95%CI包含零）；(iii)仅当模型碎片化时置信度下降，单一自一致但全局畸变的模型可逃逸检测（4次捕获中3次误差55-106m但置信度1.00）；(iv)该二分法在ETH3D和IMC 2025基准上成立。

Conclusion: 无轨迹泄漏留出协议衡量的是内部几何一致性而非绝对精度：既不能替代控制点评估，也不是通用的粗差检测器。

Original Abstract: Automated photogrammetric inspection emits metric measurements from a 3D reconstruction whose own correctness is normally unknown without an external survey. Can a reconstruction estimate its own reliability with no ground truth? We formalise a track-leakage-free hold-out protocol: a deterministic subset of images is withheld and each re-localised against only 3D points seen by at least two retained images -- a track-level barrier so a view is never tested against structure it helped create -- aggregated into an mAA confidence. Held-out observations still entered the bundle adjustment that built the trusted structure, so we call it track-leakage-free (a stricter re-mapping variant confirms this at 3/5 deg). Across operational GNSS-referenced captures plus ETH3D, EuRoC and the IMC 2025 benchmark we report four findings. (i) The protocol is computationally well-posed: good reconstructions score millidegree self-consistency. (ii) Thresholded self-consistency saturates and does not track absolute accuracy: confidence stays near 1.00 while true RTK error swings up to 14x within a capture, and the per-capture correlation is sign-unstable across five captures (-0.57 to +0.98; pooled 95% CI spanning zero), so it cannot gate accuracy. (iii) It flags gross failure only when the failure destroys internal consistency: a fragmenting model drops confidence, but a single self-consistent, globally-distorted model evades it -- three of four captures gave one model wrong by 55-106 m at confidence 1.00. (iv) The same dichotomy holds on independent ETH3D and IMC 2025 ground truth. Track-leakage-free hold-out measures internal geometric consistency, not absolute accuracy: neither a substitute for control-point assessment nor a general gross-failure gate. We release the protocol and degradation harness.

中文摘要翻译: 自动化摄影测量检测从3D重建中输出度量测量值，而该重建本身的正确性通常在没有外部测量时是未知的。重构能否在没有地面真值的情况下估计自身的可靠性？我们形式化了一种无轨迹泄漏的留出协议：保留一个确定性的图像子集，每张图像仅针对至少两张保留图像可见的3D点进行重定位——这是一种轨迹级屏障，使得视图永远不会针对它帮助构建的结构进行测试——最终聚合成mAA置信度。留出的观测仍然参与了构建可信结构的光束法平差，因此我们称之为无轨迹泄漏（一种更严格的重新映射变体在3/5度分辨率下确认了这一点）。在运营级GNSS参考捕获数据以及ETH3D、EuRoC和IMC 2025基准上，我们报告了四个发现。(i) 该协议在计算上是良态的：好的重构得分达到毫度量级自一致性。(ii) 阈值化的自一致性趋于饱和，且不跟踪绝对精度：置信度保持在接近1.00，而真实RTK误差在单次捕获内波动高达14倍，且五次捕获的每次捕获相关性符号不稳定（-0.57至+0.98；合并95%置信区间跨越零），因此它无法门控精度。(iii) 它仅在粗差破坏了内部一致性时才标记粗差：碎片化的模型会降低置信度，但单一自一致、全局畸变的模型能规避检测——四次捕获中有三次给出了一个误差为55-106米的模型，置信度为1.00。(iv) 同样的二分法在独立的ETH3D和IMC 2025地面真值上成立。无轨迹泄漏的留出协议衡量的是内部几何一致性，而非绝对精度：既不能替代控制点评估，也不是通用的粗差检测器。我们发布了该协议及退化测试工具。

</details>


### [3] [OPERA: Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation for Universal Biomedical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25108)
*Zihan Li,Feiyang Liu,Dandan Shan,Ruibo Wang,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPERA框架，通过离线策略学习的专家路由与测试时自适应，解决生物医学图像分析中跨中心、跨模态的分布偏移问题，无需重训练专家模型即可提升多数据集上的性能与校准质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物医学图像分析面临严重的分布偏移（不同扫描仪、协议、患者群体），高性能模型需要反复进行领域特定的微调，这在标签稀缺或隐私限制数据共享时变得不切实际，亟需一种无需重训练即可部署的解决方案。

Method: OPERA是一个多智能体集成框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 专家画像模块，离线学习选择策略；(2) 置信度校准，通过温度调整优化概率输出；(3) 分布感知自适应，利用无标签测试数据的统计量在批次级别动态调整类权重。实例级路由通过互模型一致性和预测熵为每个样本分配最合适的专家。

Result: 在9个数据集（涵盖眼底照相、胸部X光、CT、MRI和多模态诊断基准）上，与30多种基线方法在分类、分割和多模态任务中对比，OPERA在性能和校准质量上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 离线策略引导的专家智能体协调是无需重训练即可实现可部署生物医学AI的实用路径。

Original Abstract: Biomedical image analysis spans diverse modalities and tasks, yet real-world deployment is hindered by severe distribution shifts across scanners, protocols, and patient populations. High-performing models consequently require repeated domain-specific fine-tuning, which is a costly cycle that becomes impractical when labels are scarce or privacy constraints limit data sharing. We propose OPERA (Offline Policy-guided Expert Routing and Adaptation), a multi-agent ensemble framework that addresses this deployment bottleneck by treating expert weight assignment as an offline policy learning problem: a routing policy is learned from a small validation set without gradient updates to any expert agent, then deployed with test-time adaptation to handle distribution shift. OPERA coordinates heterogeneous specialist agents through complementary mechanisms. The expert profiling module learns selection policies offline, enabling informed allocation of expertise. Each agent undergoes confidence calibration through temperature adjustment, ensuring more reliable probabilistic outputs. OPERA also incorporates distribution aware adaptation, where class weights are dynamically adjusted at the batch level using statistics derived from unlabeled test data. Instance level routing assigns each sample to the most suitable expert by leveraging inter model agreement and predictive entropy. We evaluate OPERA on 9 datasets covering fundus photography, chest X-ray, CT, MRI, and multimodal diagnostic benchmarks, comparing against 30+ baselines across classification, segmentation, and multimodal settings. OPERA consistently improves performance and calibration quality, demonstrating that offline policy-guided expert agents coordination is a practical path to deployable biomedical AI without retraining. Code is on \href{https://github.com/HUANGLIZI/OPERA}{GitHub}.

中文摘要翻译: 生物医学图像分析涵盖了多种模态和任务，但其在实际部署中受到跨扫描仪、协议和患者群体的严重分布偏移的阻碍。高性能模型因此需要反复进行领域特定的微调，这一成本高昂的循环在标签稀缺或隐私限制数据共享时变得不切实际。我们提出OPERA（离线策略引导的专家路由与自适应），这是一个多智能体集成框架，通过将专家权重分配视为离线策略学习问题来解决这一部署瓶颈：路由策略从一个小型验证集上学习，无需对任何专家智能体进行梯度更新，随后结合测试时自适应来应对分布偏移。OPERA通过互补机制协调异构专家智能体：专家画像模块离线学习选择策略，实现基于信息的专长分配；每个智能体通过温度调整进行置信度校准，确保更可靠的概率输出；OPERA还引入了分布感知自适应，利用无标签测试数据的统计量在批次级别动态调整类权重。实例级路由通过利用模型间一致性和预测熵为每个样本分配最合适的专家。我们在涵盖眼底照相、胸部X光、CT、MRI和多模态诊断基准的9个数据集上评估OPERA，与30多种基线方法在分类、分割和多模态设置中进行比较。OPERA在性能和校准质量上均取得一致提升，证明离线策略引导的专家智能体协调是一条无需重训练即可实现可部署生物医学AI的实用路径。代码已在GitHub上开源。

</details>


### [4] [DisasterTD: Disaster Toponym Disambiguation Using Multimodal LLMs and Cross-View Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24856)
*Wenping Yin,Ziqi Liu,Naixia Mou,Weijia Li,Danfeng Hong,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DisasterTD 框架，融合多模态大语言模型的语义推理与跨视角地理定位技术，解决社交媒体影像中地理指代模糊导致的精准定位难题，在飓风哈维数据集上显著提升了灾害地理定位精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 社交媒体影像（SMI）能为灾害感知和应急响应提供及时、细粒度的地面视角信息，但其地理引用常模糊或歧义，导致精准地理定位困难，亟需一种有效的消歧方法。

Method: 提出 DisasterTD 框架，首先利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）从噪声文本中提取地名并生成候选地理位置；然后通过 SMI、遥感影像（RSI）及可选的街景影像（SVI）之间的跨视角匹配，对候选结果进行验证和精炼。

Result: 在飓风哈维数据集上，DisasterTD 在 1000m、500m、250m、100m、50m 范围内分别达到 71.62%、62.36%、57.99%、52.09%、47.01% 的定位准确率，平均误差降至 11.33 km，中位误差降至 0.68 km，显著优于仅用 MLLM 或仅用跨视角匹配的基线方法，尤其在模糊地名场景下提升最大。

Conclusion: 将 MLLM 候选生成与跨视角验证相结合能够有效实现细粒度的灾害地理定位，验证了语义推理与跨视角证据融合在灾害地理定位中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Social media imagery (SMI) provides timely and fine-grained ground perspectives that are valuable for situational awareness and emergency response. Unlike satellite or aerial imagery, SMI can capture disaster impacts and ground-level conditions in a timely manner. However, geographic references in SMI are often vague or ambiguous, making accurate geolocalization challenging. To address this issue, we propose DisasterTD, a disaster toponym disambiguation framework that integrates multimodal large language model (MLLMs)-based semantic reasoning with cross-view geolocalization. First, MLLMs extract toponyms and generate candidate geolocations from noisy textual inputs. Then, cross-view matching between SMI, remote sensing imagery (RSI), and optionally street-view imagery (SVI) is used to verify and refine these candidate results. We evaluate DisasterTD on the Hurricane Harvey dataset, where SMI is augmented with collected RSI and SVI to construct a cross-view benchmark for disaster geolocalization. The dataset is divided into four categories based on toponym clarity and ambiguity, allowing a fine-grained performance analysis across scenarios. Results show that DisasterTD consistently outperforms MLLM-only and cross-view-only baselines without disambiguation, achieving geolocalization accuracies of 71.62% within 1000 m, 62.36% within 500 m, 57.99% within 250 m, 52.09% within 100 m, and 47.01% within 50 m, while reducing the mean and median errors to 11.33 km and 0.68 km, respectively. The largest improvements appear in ambiguous toponyms, where semantic reasoning with cross-view evidence reduces candidate dispersion and errors. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of integrating MLLM-based candidate generation with cross-view verification for fine-grained disaster geolocalization.

中文摘要翻译: 社交媒体影像（SMI）能够提供及时且细粒度的地面视角信息，对于态势感知和应急响应非常有价值。与卫星或航空影像不同，SMI 可以及时捕捉灾害影响和地面状况。然而，SMI 中的地理引用往往模糊或存在歧义，使得精准地理定位面临挑战。针对这一问题，我们提出了 DisasterTD——一种灾害地名消歧框架，该框架将基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的语义推理与跨视角地理定位相结合。首先，MLLMs 从含噪声的文本输入中提取地名并生成候选地理位置；然后，利用 SMI、遥感影像（RSI）以及可选的街景影像（SVI）之间的跨视角匹配，对这些候选结果进行验证和精炼。我们在飓风哈维数据集上评估了 DisasterTD，该数据集的 SMI 辅以收集的 RSI 和 SVI，构建了一个用于灾害地理定位的跨视角基准。根据地名清晰度和歧义性将数据集分为四类，从而能够跨场景进行细粒度的性能分析。结果表明，DisasterTD 始终优于仅使用 MLLM 和仅使用跨视角匹配的无消歧基线方法，在 1000 米、500 米、250 米、100 米和 50 米范围内的地理定位准确率分别达到 71.62%、62.36%、57.99%、52.09% 和 47.01%，同时将平均误差和中位误差分别降低至 11.33 公里和 0.68 公里。最大的改进出现在模糊地名场景中，结合跨视角证据的语义推理减少了候选分散性和误差。这些发现证明了将基于 MLLM 的候选生成与跨视角验证相结合对于细粒度灾害地理定位的有效性。

</details>


### [5] [WHTMix: Efficient Stereo Depth Estimation via Walsh-Hadamard Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25234)
*Prathyush Sajith,Emadeldeen Hamdan,Ahmet Enis Cetin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用Walsh-Hadamard数据无关标记混合器替代立体视觉Transformer中的全局自注意力，在对数-线性复杂度下实现全局上下文混合，在合成驾驶数据上以2.46倍计算减少和2.65倍延迟降低达到同等精度，并引入混合对数视差损失函数以提升远距离物体深度估计性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 立体深度估计需要在紧延迟预算下高分辨率运行，但Transformer中的全局自注意力随像素数二次增长，成为计算瓶颈。

Method: 1) 用数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard变换替代立体Transformer中的联合自注意力模块，在对数-线性复杂度下实现全局标记混合；2) 保留数据依赖的交叉注意力进行左右视角对应匹配；3) 引入混合对数视差损失函数，对远距离小视差像素赋予更高权重。

Result: 在合成驾驶数据上，该方法在端点误差上与注意力基线持平，同时将模型计算量减少2.46倍，单图像推理延迟降低2.65倍。复杂度分析表明优势取决于序列长度与通道宽度的比值，高分辨率立体匹配尤其受益。混合对数视差损失在不增加计算开销的情况下降低了远距离物体的误差。

Conclusion: 数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard标记混合器可有效替代立体Transformer中的全局自注意力，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算和延迟，且混合对数视差损失能进一步改善远距离物体估计。该方法在高分辨率立体匹配场景尤其有效，但对分类Transformer不适用。

Original Abstract: Stereo depth estimation for driving, robotics and augmented reality must run at high resolution under tight latency budgets, yet in transformer-based matchers the global self-attention that aggregates scene context grows quadratically with the number of pixels and comes to dominate runtime. We show that the joint self-attention stage of a stereo transformer, whose role is to spread context across both views, can be replaced by a data-independent Walsh-Hadamard token mixer that mixes tokens globally in the transform domain at log-linear cost, while the data-dependent cross-attention that performs left-right correspondence is retained. On synthetic driving data the mixer matches the attention baseline in end-point error while reducing model compute by a factor of 2.46 and single-image inference latency by a factor of 2.65. A complexity analysis shows the benefit is governed by the ratio of sequence length to channel width, which explains why high-resolution stereo matching is a particularly favorable setting and why classification transformers are not; we confirm this token-to-channel scaling on non-stereo long-sequence benchmarks. Furthermore, we introduce a hybrid log-disparity loss function designed to up-weight small-disparity pixels corresponding to long-range objects. This approach reduces the error on distant objects without incurring any additional computational overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 用于驾驶、机器人和增强现实的立体深度估计必须在紧延迟预算下以高分辨率运行，然而在基于Transformer的匹配器中，聚合场景上下文的全局自注意力随像素数量二次增长，成为运行时的主要开销。我们发现，立体Transformer中联合自注意力阶段——其作用是在两个视图间传播上下文——可以被一个数据无关的Walsh-Hadamard标记混合器替代，该混合器在对数-线性复杂度下在变换域中全局混合标记，而执行左右对应匹配的数据依赖交叉注意力则被保留。在合成驾驶数据上，该混合器在端点误差上与注意力基线持平，同时将模型计算量减少2.46倍，单图像推理延迟降低2.65倍。复杂度分析表明，该收益取决于序列长度与通道宽度的比值，这解释了为何高分辨率立体匹配是特别有利的场景，而分类Transformer则不是；我们在非立体长序列基准上确认了这种标记到通道的缩放特性。此外，我们引入了一种混合对数视差损失函数，旨在对远距离物体对应的小视差像素赋予更高权重。该方法在不产生额外计算开销的情况下降低了远距离物体的误差。

</details>


### [6] [Harm is not Universal: Community-Specific Toxicity Detection is Urgently Needed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24898)
*Xinnuo Xu,Anja Thieme,Daniela Massiceti,Ioana Tanase,Rita Marques,Melanie Fernandez Pradier,Martin Grayson,Camilla Longden,Cecily Morrison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文论证了文本到图像生成模型中的毒性检测应采用社区特异性方法，而非通用检测器。通过与残障社区合作，发现现有检测器对矮小症和盲人/低视力社区存在严重检测失败，而提示适配和微调方法虽有改进但仍远低于通用检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有毒性检测器采用"一刀切"的统一安全准则，无法有效保护边缘化社区。实证发现约35%被标记为安全的图像实际上对残障社区有害，因此需要社区特异性的毒性检测方法。

Method: 与残障专家合作制定矮小症和盲人/低视力两个社区的安全准则；构建包含2,400张标注T2I图像的基准数据集；评估大型视觉语言模型和通用毒性检测器的零样本性能；比较提示适配方法（ICL、VQA）和参数高效微调方法的检测效果。

Result: 通用检测器在零样本设置下F1得分低于随机猜测（0.32和0.37）；GPT-4o通过提示适配达到F1 0.50和0.78；参数高效微调使小模型（0.5b-7b）最优达到F1 0.48和0.59，但对准则变化敏感；所有CTD方法均远低于通用检测的F1≈0.9。

Conclusion: 社区特异性毒性检测是必要且可行的，但当前性能与通用检测存在巨大差距，需要持续的研究投入来解决这一挑战。

Original Abstract: State-of-the-art toxicity detectors for text-to-image generation adopt a one-size-fits-all approach: a single universal model applying fixed safety guidelines to all users. Our empirical evidence shows that these detectors fail to shield marginalized communities: approximately 35% of generated images labeled safe are considered harmful by disability communities. In this position paper, we argue for community-specific toxicity detection (CTD). To demonstrate its feasibility, we collaborate with disability experts to develop safety guidelines for two communities: dwarfism and blind/low vision. Using a dataset of 2,400 annotated T2I-generated images we demonstrate that both large vision-language models and existing general-purpose toxicity detectors catastrophically fail to recognize harmful content under these guidelines in zero-shot settings with F1 score lower than random guessing (F1 0.32 and 0.37). Promisingly, prompt-based adaptation methods (ICL, VQA) substantially improve harm detection performance (GPT-4o: F1 0.50 and 0.78), while parameter-efficient fine-tuning improves smaller models (0.5b-7b with best F1 0.48 and 0.59) with less than 100 demonstrations, but remains sensitive to evolving guidelines. Despite these gains, CTD performance remains far below F1 $\approx 0.9$ achieved for general-purpose toxicity detection, highlighting the challenge and the need for sustained research effort.

中文摘要翻译: 最先进的文本到图像生成毒性检测器采用"一刀切"的方法：一个单一的通用模型对所有用户应用固定的安全准则。我们的实证证据表明，这些检测器未能保护边缘化社区：大约35%被标记为安全的生成图像被残障社区认为是有害的。在这篇立场论文中，我们主张采用社区特异性毒性检测（Community-Specific Toxicity Detection, CTD）。为证明其可行性，我们与残障专家合作，为两个社区制定了安全准则：矮小症和盲人/低视力群体。利用包含2,400张标注的T2I生成图像的数据集，我们证明大型视觉语言模型和现有的通用毒性检测器在这些准则下的零样本设置中灾难性地未能识别有害内容，其F1得分低于随机猜测（F1分别为0.32和0.37）。令人鼓舞的是，基于提示的适配方法（上下文学习ICL、视觉问答VQA）显著提升了有害内容检测性能（GPT-4o：F1分别为0.50和0.78），而参数高效微调在少于100个演示样本的情况下改进较小模型（0.5b-7b，最佳F1分别为0.48和0.59），但该方法对准则的变化仍然敏感。尽管取得了这些进展，社区特异性毒性检测的性能仍远低于通用毒性检测所达到的F1≈0.9，这凸显了当前面临的挑战以及持续研究投入的必要性。

</details>


### [7] [Human-in-the-Loop Signature Bootstrapping for UAV Hyperspectral PFM-1 Mine Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25310)
*Sagar Lekhak,Prasanna Reddy Pulakurthi,Emmett J. Ientilucci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机可见光-近红外高光谱成像中的PFM-1地雷检测问题，比较了SAM、MF、ACE和CEM四种检测器，以及三种光谱签名获取方式。实验发现ACE仅需两轮九次候选检查即可确认所有目标区域，而SAM变体需要数千次候选审查，揭示了检测器实际操作性差异的显著差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高光谱成像对材料判别有用，但实际地雷筛查中，除检测率外，需检查的虚警数量也直接影响操作效率。现有研究多关注ROC-AUC等指标，缺乏对实际审查工作量的评估。

Method: 使用四种检测器（SAM、MF、ACE、CEM）处理无人机VNIR高光谱图像中的PFM-1地雷检测；比较三种签名获取方式（地面实测SVC签名、完全信息场景内核心像素签名、模拟人机交互引导的签名自举法）；除ROC-AUC和平均精度外，引入目标发现曲线和空间候选审查计数作为评估指标。

Result: 完全审查自举法在验证全部七个目标区域后可达到完全信息签名的性能；但不同检测器所需审查工作量差异巨大：ACE只需两轮共九次候选检查即可确认所有区域，而SAM变体需要数千次候选审查才能定位最终目标。

Conclusion: 在PFM-1地雷检测中，检测器的选择对实际操作效率（即需审查的候选数量）有决定性影响，ACE在候选审查效率上显著优于SAM；仅关注AUC等传统指标不足以全面评估检测器在实战中的适用性。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) is useful for material discrimination, but operational mine screening also depends on how many false alarms must be inspected before targets are found. This paper studies PFM-1 landmine detection in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) HSI using spectral angle mapper (SAM), matched filter (MF), adaptive coherence estimator (ACE), and constrained energy minimization (CEM). We compare a ground-measured SVC signature, a fully informed in-scene core-pixel signature, and a simulated human-in-the-loop signature bootstrap. Besides receiver operating characteristic area under the curve and average precision, we report target-discovery curves and spatial candidate-review counts. Full-review bootstrapping reaches the fully informed in-scene signature case after all seven target regions are verified, but the required inspection effort varies strongly: ACE confirms all regions in two rounds and nine candidate inspections, whereas the SAM variants need thousands of candidate reviews for their final target locations. Code is available at https://github.com/SagarLekhak/IEEE_WHISPERS_2026_UAV_HSI_PFM1.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱成像（HSI）对材料判别具有重要作用，但实际地雷筛查的效果还取决于在找到目标之前需要检查多少虚警。本文研究了在无人机（UAV）可见光-近红外（VNIR）高光谱图像中，使用光谱角填图（SAM）、匹配滤波（MF）、自适应相干估计（ACE）和约束能量最小化（CEM）进行PFM-1地雷检测。我们比较了地面实测SVC光谱签名、完全信息场景内核心像素签名，以及模拟人机交互的签名自举法。除接收者操作特征曲线下面积（ROC-AUC）和平均精度外，我们还报告了目标发现曲线和空间候选审查计数。完全审查自举法在验证全部七个目标区域后可达到完全信息场景内签名的性能，但所需的检查工作量差异很大：ACE仅需两轮共九次候选检查即可确认所有区域，而SAM变体则需要数千次候选审查才能确定最终目标位置。代码开源在https://github.com/SagarLekhak/IEEE_WHISPERS_2026_UAV_HSI_PFM1。

</details>


### [8] [Mage-VL: An Efficient Codec-Native Streaming Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24904)
*Senqiao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Jinghao Guo,Yifei Shen,Xinjie Zhang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Haoqing Wang,Xiao Li,Peng Zhang,Xiang An,Yin Xie,Zhening Liu,Xun Guo,Jiahao Li,Shicheng Zheng,Jinglu Wang,Zongyu Guo,Wenxuan Xie,Zihan Zheng,Yuxuan Luo,Bin Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mage-VL提出了一种编解码器原生的流式基础模型，通过自定义的Mage-ViT分词器（利用运动向量和残差能量选择性编码动态区域）将视觉令牌消耗降低75%以上，并结合双系统架构（轻量级System 1事件门控+因果System 2解码器）实现实时多模态理解，在视频理解和空间推理上显著超越基线模型，同时推理速度提升3.5倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型受Moravec悖论困扰：在复杂离线视觉推理上表现优异，但在简单流式感知任务上效率低下且处理缓慢。目标是构建一个高效、实时的流式多模态基础模型。

Method: 1) 提出Mage-ViT自定义分词器：利用运动向量和残差能量在稀疏锚点帧(I帧)和预测帧(P帧)上选择性编码动态、高熵区域，在16×16补丁级别将视觉令牌消耗降低75%以上；2) 构建AI4AI数据流水线：包含提示-代码联合优化的多模态字幕生成和AI驱动的性能诊断；3) 设计双系统架构：轻量级System 1事件门控用于主动流式感知，因果System 2解码器用于深度推理。

Result: Mage-VL-4B在静态任务上与Qwen3-VL-4B持平，在视频理解和2D/3D空间推理上取得显著提升，推理速度提升高达3.5倍，全面超越15B Phi-4-reasoning-vision基线。此外提供了七项关键经验发现，涵盖预训练数据效率、可变分辨率缩放、编解码加速、VideoQA SFT冗余、运动-空间协同、AI4AI数据流水线和Zero-Vision SFT多模态强化学习。

Conclusion: Mage-VL通过编解码器原生设计、高效视觉分词和双系统架构，成功解决了VLM在流式感知任务上的效率瓶颈，实现了实时多模态理解与交互，并在多个基准上达到了领先性能。

Original Abstract: Standard vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from Moravec's paradox: they excel at complex offline visual reasoning but struggle with simple streaming perception tasks and process them inefficiently. We present Mage-VL, an efficient codec-native streaming foundation model for real-time multimodal understanding and interaction. At its core, our custom tokenizer, Mage-ViT, replaces uniform frame sampling by selectively encoding dynamic, entropy-rich regions using motion vectors and residual energy across sparse anchor (I) and predicted (P) frames. Operating at a 16 x 16 patch level, this reduces visual token consumption by over 75% while preserving spatiotemporal context. Trained from scratch on approximately 560M unlabeled images and 100M unlabeled video frames, Mage-ViT matches or outperforms flagship encoders trained on billions of image-text pairs. We establish AI4AI data pipelines encompassing prompt-code joint optimization for multimodal captioning and AI-driven performance diagnosis to guide training recipes. Furthermore, through a bio-inspired dual-system architecture - a lightweight System 1 event gate and a causal System 2 decoder - Mage-VL enables proactive streaming perception. Extensive evaluations show that Mage-VL-4B matches Qwen3-VL-4B on static tasks while achieving strong gains in video understanding and 2D/3D spatial reasoning, with up to a 3.5x wall-clock inference speedup, and comprehensively surpasses the 15B Phi-4-reasoning-vision baseline. Beyond model artifacts, we deliver seven key empirical findings covering pre-training data efficiency, variable-resolution scaling, codec system acceleration, VideoQA SFT redundancy, motion-spatial synergy, AI4AI data pipelines, and Zero-Vision SFT for multimodal RL.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）深受Moravec悖论之困：它们在复杂的离线视觉推理任务中表现出色，却在简单的流式感知任务上举步维艰且处理效率低下。本文提出Mage-VL，一种高效的编解码器原生流式基础模型，专为实时多模态理解与交互而设计。其核心在于我们自研的分词器Mage-ViT，该分词器摒弃了均匀帧采样，转而利用运动向量和残差能量，在稀疏锚点帧（I帧）和预测帧（P帧）上选择性编码动态且富含熵的区域。在16×16的补丁级别上运行，该策略将视觉令牌消耗降低了75%以上，同时保留了时空上下文信息。Mage-ViT在约5.6亿张无标签图像和1亿帧无标签视频上从头训练，其性能可与在数十亿图文对上训练的旗舰级编码器相媲美甚至更优。我们构建了AI4AI数据流水线，涵盖面向多模态字幕生成的提示-代码联合优化以及AI驱动的性能诊断，用以指导训练方案。此外，通过一种受生物启发的双系统架构——轻量级的System 1事件门控和因果式的System 2解码器——Mage-VL实现了主动的流式感知。大量评估表明，Mage-VL-4B在静态任务上与Qwen3-VL-4B不相上下，在视频理解和2D/3D空间推理上取得了显著提升，推理速度最高提升3.5倍，并全面超越了150亿参数的Phi-4-reasoning-vision基线。除模型成果外，我们还交付了七项关键的经验发现，涵盖预训练数据效率、可变分辨率缩放、编解码系统加速、VideoQA SFT冗余、运动-空间协同、AI4AI数据流水线，以及面向多模态强化学习的Zero-Vision SFT。

</details>


### [9] [PerceptionBench: Evaluating Atomic Visual Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24957)
*Zichao Lin,Yifeng Xie,Bowen Qu,Haiming Wang,Jia Li,Haoning Wu,Yuhao Dong,Zuhao Yang,Jinguo Zhu,Haoyu Lu,Zijia Zhao,Tongtian Yue,Zhangyang Qi,Junwei Yang,Mengfan Dong,Peizhou Cao,Chenzhuang Du,Zaida Zhou,Haotian Yao,Hao Yang,Hongcheng Gao,Lin Sui,Weihong Li,Xinxing Zu,Jia Chen,Yao Wang,Xiaoxue Wu,Yalin Wang,Y. Charles,Yiping Bao,Yangyang Liu,Zhiqi Huang,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PerceptionBench是一个专门评估多模态大语言模型原子视觉感知能力的基准，通过自下而上的错误分类法定义了十种原子感知能力，并构建了3000个验证问题。实验发现，前沿模型在原子感知任务上准确率均低于60%，感知相关幻觉是最薄弱的环节。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准无法有效隔离评估感知能力：整体评估将感知错误与推理或知识错误混淆，而应用驱动基准覆盖的领域狭窄且碎片化。需要一种能单独测量原子视觉感知能力的标准。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法：首先诊断前沿MLLMs在42个现有基准上的最早失败点，构建错误分类法，其中感知分支定义了十种原子感知能力。基于该分类法，构建3000个经验证的问题，每个问题隔离单一感知能力，难度源于感知而非推理或知识。

Result: 在16个前沿MLLM上测试，所有模型准确率均未超过60%；感知相关幻觉是平均表现最弱的能力；相似的整体分数可能掩盖截然不同的能力轮廓。

Conclusion: 原子视觉感知能力仍未得到充分解决，PerceptionBench为衡量和诊断MLLMs的视觉感知边界提供了能力级标准。

Original Abstract: We introduce PerceptionBench, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the atomic visual perception capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing benchmarks often fail to isolate perception: holistic evaluations conflate perceptual errors with failures in reasoning or domain knowledge, while application-driven benchmarks only cover narrow, fragmented domains shaped by heuristic designs. To address these limitations, PerceptionBench adopts a bottom-up approach: by diagnosing the earliest failure points in the responses of frontier MLLMs across 42 existing benchmarks, we construct an error taxonomy whose perception branch defines ten atomic perceptual capabilities. Guided by this taxonomy, we construct 3,000 verified questions with short, unambiguous answers, each isolating a single capability, with difficulty stemming from perception rather than reasoning or knowledge. Benchmark results across sixteen frontier MLLMs reveal that atomic perception remains largely unsolved---no model reaches 60\% accuracy, perception-related hallucination is the weakest capability on average, and similar overall scores conceal sharply divergent capability profiles. PerceptionBench thus provides a capability-level standard for measuring and diagnosing the visual perception boundaries of MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了PerceptionBench，这是一个专门设计用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）原子视觉感知能力的基准。现有基准往往无法有效隔离感知能力：整体评估将感知错误与推理或领域知识的失败混为一谈，而应用驱动基准仅覆盖由启发式设计决定的狭窄、碎片化领域。为解决这些局限，PerceptionBench采用自下而上的方法：通过诊断前沿MLLMs在42个现有基准上响应中的最早失败点，我们构建了一个错误分类法，其感知分支定义了十种原子感知能力。在该分类法指导下，我们构建了3000个经验证的问题，每个问题具有简短、明确的答案，各自隔离单一能力，且难度源于感知而非推理或知识。在十六个前沿MLLM上的基准测试结果表明，原子感知能力在很大程度上仍未得到解决——没有模型达到60%的准确率，感知相关幻觉是平均最弱的能力，且相似的整体分数掩盖了截然不同的能力轮廓。因此，PerceptionBench为衡量和诊断MLLMs的视觉感知边界提供了能力级标准。

</details>


### [10] [Enabling Fully Integer-Only Inference for Lightweight Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24981)
*Thanh Cong Le,Michal Szczepanski,Martyna Poreba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出I-LW-DETR，首个全整数轻量级DETR检测器，通过尺度保留分裂卷积、SD-ShiftGELU和约束Shiftmax三个关键组件，实现了前向传播中所有操作（包括Transformer非线性函数）的纯整数运算，在保持适度精度损失的同时将模型大小压缩约3.6倍、计算成本降低一个数量级以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Vision Transformer检测器在NPU和微控制器上部署困难，因为其关键组件（可变形注意力、特征融合、非线性激活函数）与整数算术不兼容。已有的量化检测器要么保留Softmax、GELU、LayerNorm等算子，要么专注于重型骨干网络，导致轻量级检测Transformer缺乏端到端的纯整数实现。

Method: 提出三个关键组件：(1) 尺度保留分裂卷积（scale-preserving split convolution），为多尺度投影器的每个分支分配独立激活尺度；(2) SD-ShiftGELU，一种符号依赖的GELU近似，保留逐元素行为同时避免精度退化；(3) 约束Shiftmax，维持稳定的Softmax归一化。这些组件共同构建了全整数前向传播的轻量级DETR检测器I-LW-DETR。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的量化流水线能在不同模型尺度上一致地产生高效的纯整数模型，仅带来适度的精度退化，同时将模型大小压缩约3.6倍，计算成本降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: I-LW-DETR成功实现了轻量级DETR检测器的全整数化部署，填补了轻量级检测Transformer缺乏端到端整数实现的空白，为在NPU和微控制器等资源受限设备上部署Vision Transformer检测器提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformer detectors now approach the accuracy of CNNs but remain difficult to deploy on NPUs and microcontrollers because key components, including deformable attention, feature fusion, and nonlinear activation functions, are not natively compatible with integer arithmetic. Existing quantized detectors either retain operators such as Softmax, GELU, and LayerNorm or focus on heavyweight backbones, leaving lightweight detection transformers without an end-to-end integer implementation. We address this gap with I-LW-DETR, the first fully integer-only lightweight DETR, in which every operation in the forward pass, including transformer nonlinearities, is executed in integer arithmetic. I-LW-DETR is built upon three key components: a scale-preserving split convolution that assigns independent activation scale to each branch of the multi-scale projector; SD-ShiftGELU, a sign-dependent GELU approximation that preserves element-wise behavior while avoiding the accuracy degradation; and a constrained Shiftmax that maintains stable Softmax normalization. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed quantization pipeline consistently produces efficient fully integer-only models across different model scales. Across all model scales, the proposed pipeline incurs only a moderate accuracy degradation while reducing the model size by approximately $3.6\times$ and the computational cost by more than one order of magnitude.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉Transformer检测器如今已接近CNN的精度，但仍难以部署在NPU和微控制器上，因为其关键组件（包括可变形注意力、特征融合和非线性激活函数）与整数算术不兼容。现有的量化检测器要么保留Softmax、GELU和LayerNorm等算子，要么专注于重型骨干网络，导致轻量级检测Transformer缺乏端到端的纯整数实现。我们通过I-LW-DETR填补了这一空白，这是首个全整数轻量级DETR，其前向传播中的每一个操作（包括Transformer非线性函数）均以整数算术执行。I-LW-DETR基于三个关键组件构建：尺度保留分裂卷积，为多尺度投影器的每个分支分配独立的激活尺度；SD-ShiftGELU，一种符号依赖的GELU近似，在保持逐元素行为的同时避免精度退化；以及约束Shiftmax，维持稳定的Softmax归一化。实验结果表明，所提出的量化流水线能在不同模型尺度上一致地产生高效的纯整数模型。在所有模型尺度上，该流水线仅带来适度的精度退化，同时将模型大小压缩约3.6倍，计算成本降低一个数量级以上。

</details>


### [11] [Unifying Active Learning and Semi-Supervised Learning for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25014)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Cheng Wan,Heejong Kim,Johannes C. Paetzold,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RegAL，一个统一主动学习与半监督学习的医学图像分割框架，通过共享的拓扑感知Pareto优化在极低标注场景下联合选择标注样本和利用未标注数据，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主动学习（AL）和半监督学习（SSL）在医学图像分割中独立设计和优化，导致目标不匹配和冷启动阶段训练不稳定，亟需一个统一的框架来协同处理样本选择和未标注数据利用。

Method: 提出RegAL框架，通过共享的拓扑感知Pareto优化联合耦合样本获取与未标注数据利用。沿三个互补轴（体素级不确定性、特征多样性、拓扑一致性指标）选择解剖学信息丰富的边缘案例进行标注，同时使用相同标准识别几何稳定的图集候选，用于微分同胚配准引导的数据增强，训练自监督Mean Teacher分割网络。

Result: 在BraTS 2021、dHCP和ProstateX三个数据集上，RegAL在极低标注场景下保持稳定，在Dice和边界距离（ASD、HD95）指标上持续优于最先进的AL、SSL及主动半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: RegAL通过统一主动学习与半监督学习的共享优化目标，有效解决了冷启动问题，在标注样本极度稀缺的医学图像分割任务中实现了稳定且优越的性能。

Original Abstract: In practical settings, medical image segmentation models are often developed with limited annotated data rather than fully labeled datasets. Training frequently begins in ultra-low labeled regimes where only a small number of volumes are annotated. In such scenarios, practitioners must simultaneously decide which cases to annotate and how to best use the remaining unlabeled data. Although active learning (AL) and semi-supervised learning (SSL) both target annotation scarcity, they are typically designed and optimized independently, resulting in objective mismatch and unstable training during early-stage "cold start" conditions. We propose RegAL, a unified active semi-supervised framework governed by a shared topology-aware Pareto optimization that couples sample acquisition with unlabeled data utilization. RegAL evaluates images along three complementary axes, voxel-wise uncertainty, feature diversity, and a novel topological consistency metric, to select anatomically informative edge cases for annotation. On the other hand, the same criteria are used to identify geometrically stable atlas candidates for diffeomorphic registration-guided augmentation to train a self-supervised Mean Teacher segmentation network. Across BraTS 2021, dHCP, and ProstateX, RegAL remains stable with few labeled volumes and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art AL, SSL, and active semi-supervised baselines across Dice and boundary-distance (ASD, HD95) metrics under extreme annotation scarcity.

中文摘要翻译: 在实际应用中，医学图像分割模型通常是在有限标注数据而非全标注数据集上开发的。训练往往起始于极低标注场景，此时仅有少量样本被标注。在此类场景中，实践者必须同时决定哪些病例需要标注以及如何最优地利用剩余的未标注数据。尽管主动学习（AL）和半监督学习（SSL）都针对标注稀缺问题，但它们通常被独立设计和优化，导致目标不匹配以及在早期"冷启动"条件下训练不稳定。我们提出RegAL，一个由共享的拓扑感知Pareto优化驱动的统一主动半监督框架，该优化将样本获取与未标注数据利用相结合。RegAL沿三个互补轴——体素级不确定性、特征多样性以及一种新颖的拓扑一致性指标——评估图像，以选择解剖学信息丰富的边缘案例进行标注。另一方面，使用相同的标准识别几何稳定的图集候选，用于微分同胚配准引导的数据增强，以训练自监督Mean Teacher分割网络。在BraTS 2021、dHCP和ProstateX数据集上，RegAL在少量标注样本下保持稳定，并在极端标注稀缺条件下，在Dice和边界距离（ASD、HD95）指标上持续优于最先进的AL、SSL及主动半监督基线方法。

</details>


### [12] [Diff-ID: Identity Consistent Facial Image Generation and Morphing via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25078)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais,Zhenhua Feng,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Diff-ID 是一个基于扩散模型的框架，通过双交叉注意力适配器（ArcFace+CLIP）和伪判别器损失，在保持身份一致性的同时生成高保真人脸图像，并在身份-真实感权衡上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成扩散模型在人脸图像合成中难以在高分辨率下保持身份完整性，这对于安全系统、生物认证等隐私敏感应用至关重要，身份漂移会破坏系统的可信度与功能性。

Method: 1) 构建21万张由CelebA-HQ、FFHQ、LAION-Face合成的自定义数据集，并通过微调BLIP模型生成描述文本以增强身份感知。2) 在微调的Stable Diffusion UNet中集成ArcFace和CLIP嵌入的双交叉注意力适配器。3) 提出基于ArcFace余弦相似度的伪判别器损失，并采用指数时间步加权。

Result: 在未见面孔上，Diff-ID在原始ArcFace人脸相似度上未超过InstantID，但取得了显著更低的FID，并在基于FIQ的身份-真实感权衡指标上表现最强。同时提出了统一的DDIM变形管线，无需逐身份微调即可实现面部插值。

Conclusion: 身份保持与照片真实感应联合评估而非孤立看待，高身份相似度并不保证输出真实感。论文提出Face Image Quality (FIQ)作为补充性比率得分，将身份相似度与感知真实感相结合，同时以FS和FID作为主要指标。

Original Abstract: Generative diffusion models have revolutionized facial image synthesis, yet robust identity preservation in high resolution outputs remains a critical challenge. This issue is especially vital for security systems, biometric authentication, and privacy sensitive applications, where any drift in identity integrity can undermine trust and functionality. We introduce Diff-ID, a diffusion based framework that enforces identity consistency while delivering photorealistic quality. Central to our approach is a custom 210K image dataset synthesized from CelebA-HQ, FFHQ, and LAION-Face and captioned via a fine tuned BLIP model to bolster identity awareness during training. Diff-ID integrates ArcFace and CLIP embeddings through a dual cross attention adapter within a fine tuned Stable Diffusion UNet. To further reinforce identity fidelity, we propose a pseudo discriminator loss based on ArcFace cosine similarity with exponential timestep weighting. Experiments on held out and unseen faces show that Diff-ID does not exceed InstantID in raw ArcFace Face Similarity, but achieves substantially lower FID and the strongest FIQ based identity--realism trade off among the evaluated methods. We also present a unified DDIM based morphing pipeline that enables qualitative facial interpolation without per identity fine tuning. We further argue that identity preservation and photorealism should be evaluated jointly rather than in isolation, as high identity similarity alone does not guarantee realistic outputs. To make this trade off explicit, we report Face Image Quality (FIQ) as a complementary ratio based score that combines identity similarity and perceptual realism while keeping FS and FID as the primary metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 生成扩散模型彻底改变了人脸图像合成技术，但在高分辨率输出中保持鲁棒的身份信息仍然是一个关键挑战。这一问题对于安全系统、生物认证和隐私敏感应用尤为重要，因为身份完整性的任何偏差都可能削弱信任和功能性。我们提出Diff-ID，一个基于扩散的框架，在强制身份一致性的同时生成逼真的图像。我们方法的核心是一个由CelebA-HQ、FFHQ和LAION-Face合成的21万张图像自定义数据集，并通过微调BLIP模型生成描述文本以增强训练过程中的身份感知能力。Diff-ID通过微调后的Stable Diffusion UNet中的双交叉注意力适配器集成ArcFace和CLIP嵌入。为了进一步增强身份保真度，我们提出了一种基于ArcFace余弦相似度且带有指数时间步加权的伪判别器损失。在保留面孔和未见面孔上的实验表明，Diff-ID在原始ArcFace人脸相似度上未超过InstantID，但获得了显著更低的FID以及评估方法中最强的基于FIQ的身份-真实感权衡。我们还提出了一个基于DDIM的统一变形管线，无需逐身份微调即可实现定性面部插值。我们进一步主张，身份保持和照片真实感应联合评估而非孤立看待，因为高身份相似度本身并不能保证输出结果的真实性。为了明确这种权衡，我们报告了Face Image Quality (FIQ)作为补充性的比率得分，它结合了身份相似度和感知真实感，同时保留FS和FID作为主要指标。

</details>


### [13] [MorphUNet: Alpha-Controlled Biometric Transport for Diffusion-Based Face Morphing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25092)
*Taimoor Rizwan,Sara Atito,Zhenhua Feng,Muhammad Awais,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphUNet，首个基于扩散模型、使用可训练的分离双交叉注意力机制的人脸融合攻击框架，通过α控制的生物特征传输实现双身份融合，在多项指标上超越现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于GAN或简单扩散模型的人脸融合方法在身份平衡性、图像质量和跨数据集检测鲁棒性方面存在不足，需要一种更可控、更高质量的双身份融合攻击方法。

Method: MorphUNet将双亲生成建模为α控制的生物特征传输：用CLIP提取外观特征、ArcFace提取身份特征，对齐到CLIP兼容的token空间；在去噪U-Net中引入可训练的双交叉注意力机制（Biometric Transport Layer），分别关注每个双亲身份再通过α融合残差；使用DDIM反演潜在插值获得一致的去噪起点，并通过弱亲引导选择避免向单一方坍塌。

Result: 在FEI和FRLL数据集上，MorphUNet在欺骗至少3/6个识别系统时MAP分别达0.919和0.886，FID为35.19和44.86；同数据集下APCER@5%BPCER最高；跨数据集迁移下APCER达0.996（FEI）和0.946（FRLL），难以被检测。

Conclusion: MorphUNet作为一种新的扩散融合框架，在攻击潜力、图像质量和检测规避方面均优于现有方法，对身份验证系统构成显著威胁。

Original Abstract: Face morphing attacks create synthetic images verifiable against multiple identities, threatening border control and identity verification systems. We introduce MorphUNet, a diffusion morphing framework formulating two-parent generation as alpha-controlled biometric transport: each parent is decomposed into CLIP appearance and ArcFace identity evidence, aligned into a CLIP-compatible token space, with the two contributors preserved as separate identity-aware token banks. To our knowledge, MorphUNet is the first diffusion-based morphing framework using trainable parent-separated dual cross-attention inside the denoising U-Net: a Biometric Transport Layer carrying parent-specific identity evidence through denoising, attending to each parent separately before combining residuals via the morphing parameter alpha. DDIM-inverted latent interpolation gives a coherent denoising start, while weaker-parent-guided selection favours morphs maximising the lower parent-similarity score, reducing collapse toward one contributor. We evaluate MorphUNet against three state-of-the-art baselines (StableMorph, MIPGAN-II, and MorDIFF) on FEI and FRLL using six recognition systems, and propose CFD-based unseen-identity stress testing across gender and ethnicity pairing, demographic shifts, and parent-similarity extremes. MorphUNet achieves the best Morphing Attack Potential (MAP) when at least three of six systems are fooled by one morph, reaching 0.919 on FEI and 0.886 on FRLL, and obtains the best FID on both datasets (35.19 FEI, 44.86 FRLL). It also gives the highest APCER at 5% BPCER in the same-dataset setting, and remains highly difficult to detect under cross-dataset transfer, with APCER 0.996 on FEI and 0.946 on FRLL. The full evaluation analyses MAP, MAD, per-system vulnerability, identity balance, image quality, top/bottom-similarity stress tests, and CFD unseen-identity robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 人脸融合攻击能够创建可被多个身份验证通过的合成图像，对边境控制和身份验证系统构成威胁。本文提出MorphUNet，一种扩散融合框架，将双亲生成建模为α控制的生物特征传输：每个双亲被分解为CLIP外观特征和ArcFace身份特征，对齐到CLIP兼容的token空间，两个贡献者作为独立的身份感知token库保存。据我们所知，MorphUNet是首个在去噪U-Net内部使用可训练的分离双交叉注意力机制的扩散融合框架：生物特征传输层在去噪过程中携带双亲特定的身份证据，分别关注每个双亲后再通过融合参数α组合残差。DDIM反演潜在插值提供一致的去噪起点，弱亲引导选择则倾向于最大化较低双亲相似度分数的融合图像，减少向某一方坍塌。我们在FEI和FRLL数据集上使用六个识别系统将MorphUNet与三个最先进的基线方法（StableMorph、MIPGAN-II和MorDIFF）进行比较，并提出了基于CFD的未见身份压力测试，涵盖性别与种族配对、人口统计偏移和双亲相似度极端情况。MorphUNet在至少三个系统被同一融合图像欺骗时达到了最佳融合攻击潜力（MAP），在FEI上为0.919，在FRLL上为0.886，并在两个数据集上均取得了最佳FID（FEI: 35.19, FRLL: 44.86）。在同数据集设置下，它在5% BPCER时取得了最高的APCER，并且在跨数据集迁移下仍然极难被检测，APCER在FEI上为0.996，在FRLL上为0.946。完整的评估分析了MAP、MAD、各系统脆弱性、身份平衡性、图像质量、高/低相似度压力测试以及CFD未见身份鲁棒性。

</details>


### [14] [IMPRINT: Image-Conditioned Query Enrichment for Long-Tail Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25106)
*Jelin Raphael Akkara,Filippo Ziliotto,Luciano Serafini,Lamberto Ballan,Tommaso Campari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPRINT框架，通过web图片丰富文本查询，提升具身AI在细粒度目标导航中的零样本定位能力，无需训练或修改导航策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本ObjectNav方法依赖纯文本查询，当目标类别语义特异性增强时（如细粒度子类别），文本查询的可靠性显著下降。

Method: IMPRINT是一个即插即用的零样本框架：用web图片丰富文本查询，经视觉语言模型编码后与语义地图匹配生成相似度图，聚合后实现上下文感知定位，整个过程无需训练或修改导航策略。

Result: 在OVON和HSSD-rare（新提出的长尾基准）上，图像条件查询持续改善目标定位并提升端到端导航性能；进一步分析表明定位增益转化为导航收益的关键瓶颈在于下游检测质量。

Conclusion: 图像增强查询能有效改进零样本细粒度目标导航，但下游检测质量是当前长尾具身导航的关键系统瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Embodied AI increasingly relies on queryable semantic maps built from pre-trained vision-language models to enable zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav). However, existing approaches typically depend on text-only queries, which become less reliable as semantic specificity increases toward fine-grained object categories. We introduce IMPRINT, a zero-shot plug-and-play framework that enriches textual object queries with web-sourced images to improve grounding in queryable maps. Retrieved images are encoded using a vision-language model, matched against the semantic map to produce similarity maps, and aggregated to yield context-aware localization. Notably, this requires no training or modification of the underlying navigation policy. To explicitly evaluate long-tail behavior, we present HSSD-rare, a new ObjectNav benchmark built on Habitat Synthetic Scenes and featuring semantically specific subcategories. Across both OVON and HSSD-rare, image-conditioned queries consistently improve object grounding and yield end-to-end navigation gains. Further analysis reveals that translating localization gains to navigation performance depends critically on downstream detection quality, highlighting a key systems bottleneck in long-tail embodied navigation.

中文摘要翻译: 具身AI日益依赖基于预训练视觉语言模型构建的可查询语义地图，以实现零样本目标导航（ObjectNav）。然而，现有方法通常依赖纯文本查询，随着语义特异性向细粒度物体类别增加，其可靠性逐渐下降。我们提出IMPRINT，一个零样本即插即用框架，通过引入网络来源图片来丰富文本对象查询，从而提升在可查询地图中的定位能力。检索到的图片经由视觉语言模型编码，与语义地图匹配生成相似度图，再经聚合产生上下文感知的定位结果。值得注意的是，该方法无需训练或修改底层导航策略。为显式评估长尾行为，我们提出HSSD-rare——基于Habitat Synthetic Scenes构建的新ObjectNav基准，包含语义上具体化的子类别。在OVON和HSSD-rare两个基准上，图像条件查询一致性地改善了目标定位，并带来了端到端导航性能的提升。进一步分析揭示，将定位增益转化为导航性能的关键取决于下游检测质量，这凸显了长尾具身导航中的一个关键系统瓶颈。

</details>


### [15] [LENS: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Zooming for Keyframe Sampling in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25125)
*Ce Zhang,Jinxi He,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LENS是一种无需训练的关键帧采样框架，通过动态分配空间放大（zoom-in）和时间缩小（zoom-out）的帧预算，在有限上下文窗口中实现对长视频的空间细节与时间覆盖的平衡，显著提升了多模态大模型在长视频理解任务上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大模型在处理长视频时受限于上下文窗口，现有的关键帧采样方法在空间细节和时间覆盖之间存在冲突，难以有效导航广阔的空时搜索空间。

Method: LENS是一种无需训练的关键帧采样框架，根据文本查询动态决定何时放大以获取细粒度细节（空间zoom-in）和何时缩小以获取更广上下文（时间zoom-out），在有限的帧预算下自适应分配：空间zoom-in突出单帧内查询相关区域，时间zoom-out通过多帧聚合扩展时间范围，使模型能在多种粒度上进行推理。

Result: 在多个长视频基准上，LENS一致优于先前的关键帧采样方法，并显著超过均匀采样，例如在Video-MME上将Qwen2.5-VL的准确率从53.3%提升至60.7%。

Conclusion: LENS通过动态平衡空间细节与时间覆盖，有效提升了多模态大模型的长视频理解能力，且无需额外训练。

Original Abstract: Despite rapid progress in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), understanding long-form videos is still bottlenecked by limited context windows. While recent keyframe sampling methods attempt to mitigate this by distilling video inputs into a compact set of query-relevant frames, navigating the vast spatio-temporal search space remains challenging, as spatial detail and temporal coverage often conflict. To address this, we introduce LENS, a training-free keyframe sampling framework that dynamically decides when to zoom in for fine-grained details and when to zoom out for broader context based on the text query. Concretely, LENS adaptively allocates a limited frame budget between spatial zoom-ins, which highlight query-relevant regions within individual frames, and temporal zoom-outs, which expand the temporal scope through multi-frame aggregation, enabling the model to reason across multiple granularities while capturing both high-fidelity details and long-range context. Across diverse long-form video benchmarks, LENS consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art keyframe sampling methods and delivers substantial gains over uniform sampling, improving Video-MME accuracy from 53.3% to 60.7% with Qwen2.5-VL.Code is available at https://github.com/zhangce01/LENS.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）取得了快速进展，理解长视频仍然受限于有限的上下文窗口。尽管最近的关键帧采样方法试图通过将视频输入提炼为紧凑的查询相关帧集合来缓解这一问题，但导航广阔的空时搜索空间仍然具有挑战性，因为空间细节和时间覆盖常常相互冲突。为解决这一问题，我们提出了LENS，一种无需训练的关键帧采样框架，它能够根据文本查询动态决定何时放大以获取细粒度细节，何时缩小以获取更广的上下文。具体而言，LENS在有限的帧预算中自适应分配空间放大（zoom-in）和时间缩小（zoom-out）：空间放大突出单帧内与查询相关的区域，时间缩小则通过多帧聚合扩展时间范围，使模型能够在多种粒度上进行推理，同时捕获高保真细节和长程上下文。在多个长视频基准测试中，LENS一致优于先前最先进的关键帧采样方法，并在均匀采样基础上取得了显著提升，将Qwen2.5-VL在Video-MME上的准确率从53.3%提升至60.7%。代码已开源：https://github.com/zhangce01/LENS。

</details>


### [16] [FIDAC: An Easy-to-use Pipeline to Extract and Interpret Interpersonal Distance From Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25146)
*Keshav Rastogi,Eugy Han,Jeremy N. Bailenson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIDAC是一个开源库，通过融合多个面部检测模型的结果，从视频中提取和分析人际距离信息，并包含人工编码流程和深度畸变校正工具。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视频中人与人之间的距离包含重要的社会感知信息，但这类信息难以从视频输入中直接提取和解释，现有单一面部检测模型存在局限性。

Method: 开发了FIDAC开源库，融合多个开源面部检测模型的数据以弥补单一模型不足，并引入人脸选择的人工编码流程和减少深度畸变的基准校正工具来实现更精确的追踪。

Result: 目前是工具开发与框架设计阶段，计划下一步评估其在多种深度和方向下测量人际距离的有效性，并整合同步性等空间关系分析特征。

Conclusion: FIDAC提供了一种将面部检测结果转化为可操作的人际距离数据的有效方案，未来将通过更全面的评估和功能扩展来完善该工具。

Original Abstract: The distance between persons reveals significant information about their perception of each other. However, such information is not easily extractable and interpretable from video input. We developed an open-sourced library, Facial Interpersonal Distance Analysis and Coding (FIDAC) that transforms facial detection results into actionable data about location and interpersonal distance. This tool merges data from multiple open-source facial detection models, strategically compensating for gaps in any individual model. In addition, we include methods for more accurate tracking, such as a pipeline for human coding of the selection of faces and a benchmarking tool to reduce depth distortion. For next steps, we plan on building upon FIDAC by evaluating its effectiveness at measuring interpersonal distance at various depths and orientations while further integrating features of proxemic analysis such as synchrony into its software.

中文摘要翻译: 人与人之间的距离揭示了他们对彼此感知的重要信息。然而，这类信息难以从视频输入中直接提取和解释。我们开发了一个开源库——面部人际距离分析与编码（FIDAC），该工具可将面部检测结果转化为关于位置和人际距离的可操作数据。该工具融合了多个开源面部检测模型的数据，策略性地弥补了单一模型的不足。此外，我们还纳入了更精确追踪的方法，例如用于人脸选择的人工编码流程和减少深度畸变的基准校正工具。下一步，我们计划在FIDAC的基础上，评估其在多种深度和方向下测量人际距离的有效性，同时进一步整合如同步性等空间关系分析特征到其软件中。

</details>


### [17] [OpenPVMapper: A Multi-source, Nationwide Database of Rooftop Photovoltaic Systems in France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25153)
*Gabiel Kasmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenPVMapper 是一个覆盖法国全境的屋顶光伏安装多源数据库，通过聚合深度学习航拍检测、OpenStreetMap 和概率建筑级检测数据集，收录了 113.5 万套光伏装置，总装机容量约 15.01 GWp，整体精度约 74-75%，并以 CC-BY 开源协议发布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目前缺乏开放的、全面的、安装级别的屋顶光伏系统数据集：公共登记处仅聚合超过容量阈值的数据，而基于遥感检测的方法通常局限于单一方法、有限地理范围或单一时间点。构建一个多渠道融合的高质量数据集具有迫切需求。

Method: 通过聚合和协调多个互补数据源构建数据库：基于深度学习的航拍影像检测流程、OpenStreetMap 数据以及一个概率性建筑级检测数据集，并对分层抽样样本进行人工验证。

Result: 数据库包含 1,135,850 个光伏装置，总装机容量约 15.01 GWp；对 1,862 个装置的分层抽样人工审查显示整体精度约为 74-75%；跨独立来源的相互印证带来了显著的精度提升。

Conclusion: 通过聚合独立数据源而非依赖单一检测方法，OpenPVMapper 达到了任何单一来源都无法单独提供的置信水平，同时具有可扩展性，未来可纳入更多数据源。

Original Abstract: Rooftop photovoltaic (PV) systems account for the vast majority of PV grid connections, yet no open, comprehensive, installation-level dataset of these systems exists: public registries aggregate data only above a capacity threshold, and remote sensing-based detection efforts, while extensive, are typically confined to a single method, a limited geographic scope, or a single point in time. We introduce OpenPVMapper, a nationwide, multi-source database of rooftop PV installations in mainland France, built by aggregating and reconciling complementary sources: a deep learning-based detection pipeline deployed on nationwide aerial imagery, OpenStreetMap and a probabilistic building-level detection dataset. The resulting database contains 1,135,850 installations, totaling approximately 15.01~GWp of installed capacity, each documented with its provenance, detection method, and, where available, a manual validation flag. Manual review of a stratified sample of 1,862 installations places the database's overall precision at approximately 74--75\%, with corroboration across independent sources bringing a substantial, quantified precision gain. By aggregating independent sources rather than relying on any single detection method, OpenPVMapper reaches a level of confidence beyond what any one source could provide on its own, while remaining extensible to further sources as they become available. It is released under an open (CC-BY) license alongside the full source code used to build it.

中文摘要翻译: 屋顶光伏系统占据光伏并网的绝大多数，但目前尚不存在开放的、全面的、安装级别的此类系统数据集：公共登记处仅聚合超过容量阈值的数据，而基于遥感检测的研究虽然广泛，但通常局限于单一方法、有限的地理范围或单一时间点。我们提出了 OpenPVMapper——一个覆盖法国本土全境的全国性多源屋顶光伏安装数据库，通过聚合与协调多个互补数据源构建而成：基于深度学习的目标检测流程部署在全国航拍影像上、OpenStreetMap 以及一个概率性建筑级检测数据集。最终数据库包含 1,135,850 个光伏装置，总装机容量约 15.01 GWp，每个装置均记录了其来源、检测方法以及（如可用）人工验证标记。对 1,862 个装置的分层抽样人工审查显示，数据库的整体精度约为 74-75%，跨独立来源的相互印证带来了显著的量化精度提升。通过聚合独立数据源而非依赖任何单一检测方法，OpenPVMapper 达到了任何单一来源都无法单独提供的置信水平，同时保持可扩展性，能纳入未来可用的更多数据源。该数据库以开放许可（CC-BY）协议发布，并附有用于构建它的完整源代码。

</details>


### [18] [OrganLens: Organ-Specific Representation Learning for CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25164)
*Zhixuan Ge,Anqi Li,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrganLens是一种通过自监督学习实现CT影像中器官级别表征学习的方法，通过对共享编码器进行器官身份条件约束，结合器官特异性蒸馏和解剖掩膜监督，在不依赖外部分割掩膜的情况下生成11个器官的特异性表征，并在多个数据集上显著提升了器官相关任务的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CT基础模型通常生成单一的整体级表征，而解剖感知方法要么编码预分割的器官体积（去除临床相关的周围上下文信息），要么将图像显式解耦为器官标记组（未在特征形成前对共享编码器进行选定器官的条件约束），缺乏一种既能保留上下文信息又能实现器官特异性表征学习的有效方法。

Method: OrganLens采用共享CT编码器，通过器官身份条件约束编码过程，结合器官特异性蒸馏（organ-specific distillation）和解剖掩膜监督（anatomy-mask supervision）来塑造特征，最终通过解剖加权池化（anatomy-weighted pooling）生成器官特异性表征。推理时不依赖外部分割掩膜。

Result: 在CT-RATE上，心脏表征将心脏肥大AUROC从0.910提升至0.953；在NLST上，肺部表征使肺癌死亡率的Harrell C-index提升14.2%；在INSPECT上，全局表征在文本到图像和图像到文本检索中分别达到Recall@10为33.09%和32.04%。

Conclusion: OrganLens提供了一种可扩展的、基于共享编码器的器官特异性CT表征学习方法，器官匹配的表征在器官相关任务中提供更强的任务相关信号，而全局表征保持广泛的实用性，为医学研究社区提供了一个可复用的框架用于跨队列和临床终点的器官特异性疾病研究。

Original Abstract: A CT examination captures multiple organs, but many biomedical questions concern abnormalities, prognosis, or longitudinal change in a specific organ. These questions require a separate representation for each organ within the same CT volume. Existing CT foundation models commonly produce a single volume-level representation, while recent anatomy-aware methods either encode pre-separated organ volumes or explicitly disentangle images into organ token groups. The former may remove clinically relevant surrounding context, while the latter does not condition a shared encoder on a selected organ before its features are formed. We introduce OrganLens for organ-specific representation learning through self-supervision. An organ identity conditions a shared CT encoder, while organ-specific distillation and anatomy-mask supervision shape features for anatomy-weighted pooling into organ-specific representations. At inference, the shared model produces 11 organ-specific representations without external segmentation masks. We evaluate OrganLens on CT-RATE, RAD-ChestCT, INSPECT, and NLST across diverse acquisitions and downstream evaluations. Relative to CT-pretrained DINOv2, heart representations raise CT-RATE cardiomegaly AUROC from 0.910 to 0.953, while lung representations improve the Harrell C-index for NLST lung-cancer mortality by 14.2\%. The global representation reaches INSPECT Recall@10 of 33.09\% and 32.04\% for text-to-image and image-to-text retrieval, respectively. Across organ-related tasks, anatomically matched representations provide stronger task-relevant signal, while the global representation retains broad utility. OrganLens offers a scalable approach to organ-specific CT representation learning with a shared encoder. More broadly, it provides the medical research community with a reusable framework for studying organ-specific disease across cohorts and clinical endpoints.

中文摘要翻译: CT检查会捕获多个器官，但许多生物医学问题关注的是特定器官的异常、预后或纵向变化。这些问题需要对同一CT体素中的每个器官进行单独的表征。现有的CT基础模型通常生成单一的体素级表征，而近期解剖感知方法要么编码预分离的器官体积，要么将图像显式解耦为器官标记组。前者可能去除临床相关的周围上下文信息，而后者未能在特征形成前对共享编码器进行选定器官的条件约束。我们提出OrganLens，通过自监督学习实现器官特异性表征学习。器官身份对共享CT编码器进行条件约束，而器官特异性蒸馏和解剖掩膜监督塑造特征，通过解剖加权池化形成器官特异性表征。在推理时，共享模型无需外部分割掩膜即可生成11个器官特异性表征。我们在CT-RATE、RAD-ChestCT、INSPECT和NLST上评估了OrganLens，涵盖了不同的采集方式和下游评估任务。与CT预训练的DINOv2相比，心脏表征将CT-RATE上心脏肥大的AUROC从0.910提升至0.953，而肺部表征将NLST上肺癌死亡率的Harrell C-index提升了14.2%。全局表征在INSPECT上文本到图像和图像到文本检索的Recall@10分别达到33.09%和32.04%。在器官相关任务中，解剖匹配的表征提供更强的任务相关信号，而全局表征保持广泛的实用性。OrganLens提供了一种可扩展的、基于共享编码器的器官特异性CT表征学习方法。更广泛地，它为医学研究社区提供了一个可复用的框架，用于跨队列和临床终点的器官特异性疾病研究。

</details>


### [19] [LGFNet: A CTC-Guided Local-Global Fusion Framework for Single-Channel Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25197)
*Chongjian Wang,Zhenghang Hou,Junjie Gao,Xiaofang Zhong,Shiyuan Han,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LGFNet是一个基于CTC引导的序列到序列睡眠分期框架，通过局部-全局融合编码器、CTC-注意力联合训练和三阶段解码策略，在单通道低延迟场景下显著提升了睡眠分期精度和跨数据集泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有睡眠分期方法面临长程时间依赖、N1期等模糊阶段转换困难、跨受试者/采样率/电极分布漂移等挑战，尤其在可穿戴设备所需的单通道低延迟场景下问题更为突出。

Method: 1) 局部-全局融合编码器(Local-Global Fusion encoder)联合建模细粒度时间动态与长程睡眠结构；2) CTC-注意力联合训练范式统一时间对齐与上下文依赖建模；3) 三阶段解码策略，包括CTC引导解码和基于Viterbi的平滑，减少误差累积并保证生理一致性。

Result: 在五个公开基准数据集上的跨数据集评估中，LGFNet一致优于现有最先进单通道方法。在Sleep-EDF-78上，相比DMIN，准确率提升+1.27%，宏F1提升+1.74%，Kappa系数提升+1.93%，在N1期和转换段上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: LGFNet通过局部-全局融合编码器和CTC-注意力联合训练范式，有效解决了睡眠分期中的长程依赖和阶段转换模糊问题，在不同采样率、电极分布和记录环境下表现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Sleep staging remains challenging due to long-range temporal dependencies, ambiguous stage transitions-particularly in N1-and substantial distribution shifts across subjects, sampling rates, and EEG montages. These difficulties are further amplified in single-channel, low-latency scenarios required by wearable and real-world applications. To address these issues, we propose LGFNet, a CTC-guided sequence-to-sequence framework for robust sleep staging. LGFNet introduces a Local-Global Fusion encoder that jointly models fine-grained temporal dynamics and long-range sleep structure, overcoming the limitations of conventional serial hybrid architectures. A CTC-Attention joint training paradigm is adopted to unify temporal alignment with context-dependent modeling, enabling more accurate recognition of stage boundaries and transitions. Furthermore, a three-stage decoding strategy is devised, leveraging CTC-guided decoding and Viterbi-based smoothing to reduce error accumulation and enforce physiological consistency. Extensive cross-dataset evaluations on five public benchmarks demonstrate that LGFNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-channel methods. In particular, on Sleep-EDF-78, LGFNet surpasses DMIN by +1.27% accuracy, +1.74% macro-F1, and +1.93% kappa, with pronounced gains on N1 and transition segments, highlighting its robustness and strong generalization across diverse sampling rates, montages, and recording environments.

中文摘要翻译: 睡眠分期仍然面临长程时间依赖、模糊的阶段转换（尤其是N1期）以及跨受试者、采样率和脑电导联分布的大幅分布漂移等挑战。这些困难在可穿戴和现实应用所需的单通道、低延迟场景下进一步加剧。为解决这些问题，我们提出了LGFNet，一个基于CTC引导的序列到序列框架用于鲁棒睡眠分期。LGFNet引入了局部-全局融合编码器，联合建模细粒度时间动态和长程睡眠结构，克服了传统串行混合架构的局限。采用CTC-注意力联合训练范式，将时间对齐与上下文依赖建模相统一，实现对阶段边界和转换的更准确识别。此外，设计了三阶段解码策略，利用CTC引导解码和基于Viterbi的平滑来减少误差累积并保证生理一致性。在五个公开基准数据集上的广泛跨数据集评估表明，LGFNet一致优于现有的最先进单通道方法。特别是在Sleep-EDF-78数据集上，LGFNet相比DMIN在准确率上提升+1.27%，宏平均F1提升+1.74%，Kappa系数提升+1.93%，在N1期和转换段上增益尤为显著，突显了其在不同采样率、导联分布和记录环境下的鲁棒性和强泛化能力。

</details>


### [20] [ObliCity: A Benchmark and Baseline for Roof-to-Ground Projection Displacement Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25210)
*Kai Li,Yupeng Deng,Ligao Deng,Zhihao Xi,Chenhao Wang,Jierui Zhang,Yingrui Ji,Yu Meng,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将屋顶到足迹偏移向量（RFOV）提取作为独立学习任务，并构建了首个大规模倾斜城市遥感基准数据集ObliCity，同时设计了基于ODE的DragRoof框架，通过模拟连续拖动过程实现几何一致的偏移场预测，在RFOV提取上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有倾斜遥感影像中建筑屋顶与足迹间的几何投影位移问题未被充分解决，已有方法要么忽略形变，要么在分割框架中隐式处理，导致几何校正进展缓慢。本文旨在显式地将几何对齐与语义分割解耦，专门研究RFOV提取任务。

Method: 将RFOV提取定义为独立学习任务；构建ObliCity数据集（含高分辨率无人机影像和全球卫星数据）；提出DragRoof框架——受人类标注行为启发的ODE（常微分方程）方法，通过模拟将屋顶连续拖向足迹的过程，学习确定性且几何一致的偏移场，并利用结束令牌自适应判断收敛。

Result: 在ObliCity数据集上的大量实验表明，DragRoof在RFOV提取任务上达到最先进水平，需要的推理步数更少，同时在方向精度和长度精度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据集和模型为倾斜遥感影像中的投影位移校正建立了原则性基础，源代码和数据集将公开发布。

Original Abstract: Oblique-view urban remote sensing imagery inevitably exhibits geometric projection displacements between building roofs and footprints, leading to significant distortions in spatial structure. Existing approaches either ignore these deformations or handle them implicitly within segmentation-based frameworks, where progress is dominated by general segmentation advances rather than improvements in geometric correction. In this work, we explicitly define roof-to-footprint offset vector (RFOV) extraction as an independent learning task that decouples geometric alignment from semantic segmentation. To support this task, we introduce the Oblique City dataset (ObliCity), the first large-scale benchmark that integrates high-resolution UAV imagery and globally distributed satellite data, covering diverse city morphologies and camera perspectives. Methodologically, we reformulate DragOSM into DragRoof, an ODE-based framework inspired by human annotation behavior. By simulating the continuous process of dragging roofs toward their footprints, DragRoof learns deterministic, geometry-consistent offset fields and adaptively determines convergence through an end token. Extensive experiments on ObliCity demonstrate that DragRoof achieves state-of-the-art RFOV extraction performance, requiring fewer inference steps while delivering superior directional and length accuracy. Our dataset and model establish a principled foundation for studying projection displacement correction in oblique remote sensing imagery. The source code and dataset will be avaliable at https://github.com/likaiucas/DragRoof.

中文摘要翻译: 倾斜视角的城市遥感影像不可避免地会在建筑屋顶与足迹之间产生几何投影位移，导致空间结构出现显著畸变。现有方法要么忽略这些形变，要么在基于分割的框架中隐式处理它们——其进展主要依赖于通用分割方法的进步，而非几何校正本身的改进。在本工作中，我们将屋顶到足迹偏移向量（RFOV）提取明确定义为一个独立的学习任务，从而将几何对齐与语义分割解耦。为支撑该任务，我们引入了Oblique City数据集（ObliCity），这是首个整合高分辨率无人机影像与全球分布卫星数据的大规模基准数据集，覆盖多样的城市形态和相机视角。在方法论上，我们将DragOSM重新构思为DragRoof——一个受人类标注行为启发、基于常微分方程（ODE）的框架。通过模拟将屋顶连续拖向足迹的过程，DragRoof学习确定性且几何一致的偏移场，并利用结束令牌自适应地判断收敛。在ObliCity上的大量实验表明，DragRoof在RFOV提取上达到了最先进水平，所需推理步数更少，同时在方向精度和长度精度上均表现更优。我们的数据集和模型为研究倾斜遥感影像中的投影位移校正建立了原则性基础。源代码和数据集将在https://github.com/likaiucas/DragRoof上公开。

</details>


### [21] [CD-RMOT-Bench: Benchmarking the Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25239)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域指代多目标跟踪（CD-RMOT）问题，构建统一基准CD-RMOT-Bench，并提出查询中心自适应（QCA）框架，揭示了域偏移下语言引导跟踪的脆弱性，为鲁棒跨域跟踪开辟新方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RMOT研究多在同域设置下进行，忽略了实际应用中不可避免的视觉域偏移（如天气、视角变化）对语言引导跟踪鲁棒性的影响，因此需要系统研究跨域场景下RMOT的退化机制与应对方法。

Method: 提出查询中心自适应（QCA）框架，通过稳定连接视觉轨迹与指代表达的查询空间来提升跨域鲁棒性；同时构建CD-RMOT-Bench统一基准，包含真实晴朗域数据、对齐数字孪生变体以及真实恶劣域视频，支持受控天气/视角偏移分析和合成-真实迁移评估。

Result: 实验表明域偏移严重降低RMOT性能，失败不仅源于目标检测错误，更关键的是不稳定的表达式条件时序关联与目标选择。QCA建立了强基线，CD-RMOT-Bench为跨域语言引导跟踪提供了系统评估平台。

Conclusion: 跨域RMOT是一个重要且尚未被充分探索的问题，域偏移对语言引导跟踪的破坏主要在于表达式关联与目标选择而非检测本身，QCA能有效缓解这一问题，CD-RMOT-Bench为后续研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) extends tracking from category-driven perception to language-guided understanding by grounding object trajectories in natural-language expressions. Despite recent progress, existing RMOT studies are largely conducted under in-domain settings, leaving the robustness of language-conditioned tracking under inevitable visual domain shifts unexplored. In this paper, we study Cross-Domain Referring Multi-Object Tracking (CD-RMOT), a new and challenging problem that evaluates whether an RMOT model trained on a labeled source domain can reliably follow natural-language expressions in an unlabeled target domain with different visual conditions. To support systematic study, we construct CD-RMOT-Bench, a unified benchmark that combines real clear-domain referring tracking data, aligned digital-twin variants, and real adverse-domain videos. CD-RMOT-Bench enables both controlled weather/viewpoint shift analysis and realistic synthetic-real transfer evaluation under a shared RMOT protocol. Further, we provide a Query-Centric Adaptation (QCA) framework, designed to stabilize the query space that bridges visual trajectories and referring expressions. Extensive experiments reveal that domain shifts severely degrade RMOT performance, where the failure is not merely caused by object detection errors but more critically by unstable expression-conditioned temporal association and target selection. QCA establishes a strong baseline, while CD-RMOT-Bench opens a new direction for robust language-guided tracking across visual domains.

中文摘要翻译: 指代多目标跟踪（RMOT）通过将目标轨迹与自然语言表达进行关联，将跟踪从类别驱动的感知扩展为语言引导的理解。尽管近期取得了进展，但现有的RMOT研究主要在同域设置下进行，尚未探索在不可避免的视觉域偏移下语言引导跟踪的鲁棒性。本文研究跨域指代多目标跟踪（CD-RMOT），这是一个新颖且具有挑战性的问题，旨在评估在源有标签域上训练的RMOT模型能否在视觉条件不同的无标签目标域中可靠地遵循自然语言表达。为支持系统性研究，我们构建了CD-RMOT-Bench，这是一个统一基准，结合了真实晴朗域指代跟踪数据、对齐的数字孪生变体以及真实恶劣域视频。CD-RMOT-Bench在统一的RMOT协议下，同时支持受控的天气/视角偏移分析以及逼真的合成-真实迁移评估。此外，我们提出了查询中心自适应（QCA）框架，旨在稳定连接视觉轨迹与指代表达的查询空间。大量实验表明，域偏移严重降低了RMOT的性能，其失败不仅由目标检测错误引起，更关键的是不稳定的表达式条件时序关联与目标选择。QCA建立了强基线，而CD-RMOT-Bench为跨视觉域的鲁棒语言引导跟踪开辟了新方向。

</details>


### [22] [Medical world models in healthcare: foundations, applications, and challenges for trustworthy clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25242)
*Zhaoyan Chen,Zhongxiu Cong,Zhuanfeng Jin,Wanshu Fan,Dongsheng Zhou,Qi Ai,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过结构化叙事合成和可重复的证据映射，系统综述了医学世界模型的概念边界、技术基础、应用领域和证据要求，筛选了1,455条记录并纳入98篇文献，其中14项研究符合严格的医学世界模型经验定义。研究发现该领域围绕四个核心能力（患者状态表征、时间动态建模、干预条件模拟、临床监督规划）组织，现有研究主要提供回顾性、任务特异性或临床前阶段的技术可行性证据，临床转化面临干预表征、因果识别、不确定性估计等多方面挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学世界模型旨在将医学人工智能从静态预测扩展到动态建模，通过表征患者状态的演变以及其对时间推移和临床干预的响应。现有研究分散且定义模糊，缺乏对该领域概念、技术和证据的系统性梳理与边界界定。

Method: 采用结构化叙事合成与可重复证据映射的方法，先筛选1,455条不重复记录，最终纳入98篇文献，其中14项研究符合严格的医学世界模型经验定义。据此将领域组织为四个核心能力框架，并梳理了应用领域与证据要求。

Result: 现有研究在轨迹预测和候选干预方案对比方面提供了早期技术可行性证据，但大多数仍为回顾性、任务特异性或临床前研究。证据基础受到纵向干预数据不完整、动作语义不一致、因果可识别性有限、长程误差累积、不确定性估计不足以及外部验证缺乏等多方面限制。

Conclusion: 临床转化依赖于精确的干预表征、稳健的因果与力学基础、校准的轨迹级不确定性、安全约束的规划以及针对有临床意义的终点的前瞻性多中心验证。

Original Abstract: Medical world models offer a framework for extending medical artificial intelligence beyond static prediction by representing evolving patient states and modelling how they change over time and in response to clinical interventions. This Review defines the conceptual boundaries, technical foundations, application domains, and evidence requirements of the field through a structured narrative synthesis with reproducible evidence mapping.We screened 1,455 unique records and assembled a corpus of 98 sources, including 14 studies that met a strict empirical definition of a medical world model. The field is organised around four capabilities: patient state representation, temporal dynamics modelling, intervention-conditioned simulation, and clinician-supervised planning. Evidence spans medical imaging, longitudinal electronic health records, treatment response modelling, physiological and multimodal state modelling, ultrasound and surgical interaction, and population and health-system simulation; clinical digital twins are treated as a cross-cutting integration framework.Current studies provide early evidence of technical feasibility for trajectory forecasting and comparison of candidate interventions, but most remain retrospective, task-specific, or preclinical. The evidence base is further limited by incomplete longitudinal intervention data, inconsistent action semantics, limited causal identifiability, long-horizon error accumulation, inadequate uncertainty estimation, and limited external validation. Clinical translation will therefore depend on precise intervention representations, robust causal and mechanistic grounding, calibrated trajectory-level uncertainty, safety-constrained planning, and prospective multicentre validation against clinically meaningful endpoints.

中文摘要翻译: 医学世界模型为将医学人工智能从静态预测扩展到动态建模提供了一个框架，能够表征患者状态的演变及其随时间推移和临床干预的响应。本综述通过结构化叙事合成与可重复的证据映射，界定了该领域的概念边界、技术基础、应用领域和证据要求。我们筛选了1,455条不重复记录，构建了一个包含98篇文献的语料库，其中14项研究符合严格的医学世界模型经验定义。该领域围绕四个核心能力组织：患者状态表征、时间动态建模、干预条件模拟和临床监督规划。证据涵盖医学影像、纵向电子健康记录、治疗反应建模、生理与多模态状态建模、超声与手术交互，以及人群与卫生系统模拟；临床数字孪生被视为跨领域的整合框架。现有研究在轨迹预测和候选干预方案对比方面提供了早期技术可行性证据，但大多数仍为回顾性、任务特异性或临床前研究。该证据基础进一步受到纵向干预数据不完整、动作语义不一致、因果可识别性有限、长程误差累积、不确定性估计不足以及外部验证缺乏的限制。因此，临床转化将依赖于精确的干预表征、稳健的因果与力学基础、校准的轨迹级不确定性、安全约束的规划，以及针对有临床意义的终点开展前瞻性多中心验证。

</details>


### [23] [FORGE: Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25266)
*Ghazal Kaviani,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的、模型无关的帧选择方法FORGE，通过在预训练多模态嵌入空间中构建查询条件几何，同时优化相关性和多样性，显著提升长视频理解中关键帧选择与问答性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着视频序列变长，相关内容的密度急剧下降，模型暴露于更多不相关内容会明显降低准确性。现有方法无法在推理时高效选择与查询最相关的帧子集。

Method: FORGE（Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry）是一种无需训练、模型无关的方法。它在预训练多模态嵌入空间上诱导出查询条件的几何结构，将相关性和多样性统一为单一目标：覆盖独立查询相关方向的帧彼此远离，选择最大信息量的帧子集以在预算内捕获多样化的查询相关内容。

Result: 在Video-MME和LongVideoBench上，16/32/64帧预算下，FORGE统一关键帧选择得分提升11.0-15.3分，关键帧召回率最高翻倍（Video-MME上K=64时0.415 vs 0.204）。在8个开源MLLM（4B-32B参数）的所有评估设置中，问答准确率最高提升8.7分（vs均匀采样）和5.2分（vs最强基线）。

Conclusion: 将嵌入空间与查询的高维结构对齐是推理时视频理解的一个有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled long-form video understanding at a scale that was not previously possible. However, the density of relevant content decreases sharply as video sequence length increases, and exposing the model to more irrelevant content measurably reduces its accuracy. In this paper, we address the problem of maximizing query-relevant information in a frame subset selected at inference time, without training. FORGE (Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry) is a model-agnostic method that induces a query-conditioned geometry on a pretrained multimodal embedding space, unifying relevance and diversity into a single objective. In this space, frames that cover independent query-relevant directions are far apart, and selecting the subset of maximum information captures diverse query-relevant content within the budget. Experiments on Video-MME and LongVideoBench at budgets of 16, 32, and 64 frames show that FORGE improves the unified keyframe selection score by 11.0-15.3 points over the strongest training-free baseline and up to doubles keyframe recall (0.415 vs. 0.204 at K=64 on Video-MME). The gains extend to question answering, where accuracy improves in every evaluated setting across eight open-source MLLMs spanning 4B to 32B parameters, by up to 8.7 points over uniform sampling and 5.2 points over the strongest baseline. Our findings suggest that aligning the embedding space with the query's high-dimensional structure is a promising direction for inference-time video understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）使得长视频理解达到了前所未有的规模。然而，随着视频序列长度的增加，相关内容的密度急剧下降，且将模型暴露于更多不相关的内容会显著降低其准确性。本文解决的是在推理时、无需训练的情况下，从所选帧子集中最大化与查询相关信息的问题。FORGE（Frame Orthogonality in Relevance Geometry）是一种模型无关的方法，它在预训练的多模态嵌入空间上诱导出查询条件几何结构，将相关性和多样性统一为单一目标。在该空间中，覆盖独立查询相关方向的帧彼此远离，选择最大信息量的子集能够在预算内捕获多样化的查询相关内容。在Video-MME和LongVideoBench上，以16、32和64帧为预算的实验表明，FORGE在统一关键帧选择得分上比最强的免训练基线高出11.0-15.3分，并且关键帧召回率最高提升至两倍（Video-MME上K=64时为0.415对比0.204）。这些收益延伸至问答任务，在涵盖4B到32B参数的八个开源MLLM的每个评估设置中，准确率相较于均匀采样提升高达8.7个百分点，相较于最强基线提升高达5.2个百分点。我们的发现表明，将嵌入空间与查询的高维结构对齐是推理时视频理解的一个有前景的方向。

</details>


### [24] [ScaleResfusion: Residual Rectified Flow based on Residual Vector Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25275)
*Zhenning Shi,Chen Xu,Junhao Zhang,Kefei Zhang,Linjie Liu,Zhedong Zheng,Tao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleResfusion 提出了一种基于预训练文本到图像整流流模型的可扩展扩散框架，通过残差整流流（Residual Rectified Flow）机制实现高效的真实世界图像修复，在保持感知质量的同时大幅提升效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散方法在真实图像修复中存在两个关键挑战：从高斯噪声出发的方法速度慢且对退化输入保真度低；基于残差的方法通常需从头训练，难以利用现代预训练生成先验。

Method: 提出残差整流流（Residual Rectified Flow），在标准整流流中引入残差项R，从含噪低质量图像出发构建残差传输路径，实现精确加速点。通过学习残差向量场，保持与预训练整流流模型的输出分布和线性扩散过程一致，从而实现参数高效微调。进一步引入知识蒸馏流程降低采样成本。

Result: 在多个真实图像修复任务上，ScaleResfusion 以更高的效率实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种实用且可扩展的方式，将大型预训练扩散模型适配到真实世界图像修复任务中。

Original Abstract: Real-world Image Restoration (Real-IR) aims to recover high-quality (HQ) images from complex and unknown degradations. Although recent diffusion-based methods have substantially improved perceptual quality, their current designs leave two key challenges unresolved. Methods that start from Gaussian noise are slow and often less faithful to the degraded input. Residual-based methods usually train from scratch, which makes it hard to exploit modern pre-trained generative priors. In this paper, we present ScaleResfusion, a scalable diffusion framework for real-world image restoration built on pre-trained text-to-image rectified-flow models. The core of our method is Residual Rectified Flow, which introduces the residual term R into Standard Rectified Flow. Instead of starting from pure noise, it uses a residual transport path that starts from noisy low-quality (LQ) images and admits an exact acceleration point. By learning the residual vector field, Residual Rectified Flow keeps the output distribution and linear diffusion process consistent with the pre-trained rectified-flow models. This makes parameter-efficient fine-tuning possible at scale. We further introduce a knowledge-distillation pipeline to reduce sampling cost while maintaining restoration quality. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world restoration tasks show that ScaleResfusion achieves state-of-the-art performance with much higher efficiency. These results suggest a practical and scalable way to adapt large pre-trained diffusion models to real-world image restoration. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/YukinoshitaLove/ScaleResfusion.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界图像修复（Real-IR）旨在从复杂且未知的退化过程中恢复高质量（HQ）图像。尽管最近的扩散方法在感知质量上取得了显著提升，但其现有设计仍然存在两个关键挑战未解决。从高斯噪声出发的方法速度较慢，且对退化输入的保真度不足。基于残差的方法通常需从头训练，难以利用现代预训练生成先验。本文提出ScaleResfusion，一个基于预训练文本到图像整流流模型的可扩展扩散框架，用于真实世界图像修复。该方法的核心是残差整流流（Residual Rectified Flow），它在标准整流流中引入了残差项R。不同于从纯噪声出发，它采用从含噪低质量（LQ）图像开始的残差传输路径，并支持精确加速点。通过学习残差向量场，残差整流流保持了与预训练整流流模型一致的输出分布和线性扩散过程，从而实现了参数高效的大规模微调。我们进一步引入了知识蒸馏流程，在降低采样成本的同时保持修复质量。在多个真实世界修复任务上的大量实验表明，ScaleResfusion以更高的效率实现了最先进的性能。这些结果提出了一种实用且可扩展的方式，将大型预训练扩散模型适配到真实世界图像修复中。我们的代码和模型已开源在https://github.com/YukinoshitaLove/ScaleResfusion。

</details>


### [25] [FunnelAL: Retrieve-then-Rank Active Learning for Single-Class Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25276)
*Reihaneh Rostami,Brian Goodwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunnelAL 是一个面向单类发现的检索-重排序主动学习系统，借鉴工业推荐系统的多级漏斗架构，将样本选择分解为级联阶段（嵌入检索→自适应排序→标注反馈），在三个图像分类基准上实现了最优的最终F1、最佳标注效率，并对标注错误具有鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模监督学习面临两大挑战：在庞大数据集中高效找到相关样本，以及当嵌入空间无法清晰分离类别时，将真阳性与视觉上混淆的假阴性区分开。传统主动学习将样本选择视为单阶段过程，无法高效应对这两个挑战。

Method: FunnelAL 采用检索-排序的级联架构：(1) 基于嵌入的检索打分，将语料库缩小到可管理的候选集；(2) 精度触发的排序阶段，先利用学习的排序模型（RankNet）保持高批量精度，在收益递减后自动融合基于委员会的探索（QBC）；(3) 利用标注者的反馈来细化后续迭代的两个阶段。

Result: 在三个图像分类基准上，使用完美标注者时，FunnelAL 在最终F1、标注效率（AULC排名第一）和标注轮次上均最优。最新的单类发现方法（GAL、PF-MA）最多只能在最终质量上匹配，但标注成本更高。在真实标注错误率下，FunnelAL仍然保持第一或统计持平第一，而传统不确定性方法性能下降2-3倍。

Conclusion: 该工作为多级推荐系统与主动学习之间建立了具体的桥梁，证明了工业推荐系统中的漏斗架构可以有效迁移到数据标注场景，提升主动学习的效率和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: We present FunnelAL, a retrieve-then-rank active learning system for single-class discovery, which adapts the multi-stage funnel architecture of industrial recommender systems to data annotation. Large-scale supervised learning faces two challenges: efficiently finding relevant samples in a massive corpus, and distinguishing true positives from visually confusable negatives when embeddings do not cleanly separate classes. Conventional active learning offers a principled framework for reducing annotation cost, yet it treats sample selection as a single-stage process that addresses neither challenge efficiently. FunnelAL decomposes the problem into cascaded stages. Starting from a single positive and negative example, the system iterates through: (1) embedding-based retrieval scoring that narrows the corpus to a manageable candidate set; (2) a precision-triggered ranking stage that exploits a learned ranker (RankNet) while batch precision remains high, then automatically blends in committee-based exploration (QBC) once returns diminish; and (3) feedback from the annotator's labels that refines both stages in subsequent iterations. We evaluate on three diverse image classification benchmarks. With a perfect annotator, FunnelAL attains the best final F1 on all three benchmarks, the best annotation efficiency (first in AULC), and the fewest annotation rounds. The most recent single-class discovery methods (GAL, PF-MA) at best match its final quality, and only at consistently higher labeling cost. Under annotator labeling errors at realistic rates, FunnelAL remains first or statistically tied for first while classical uncertainty-based methods degrade two to three times faster. Our work provides a concrete bridge between multi-stage recommender systems and active learning.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 FunnelAL，一个用于单类发现的检索-排序主动学习系统，它将工业推荐系统的多级漏斗架构适配到数据标注任务中。大规模监督学习面临两个挑战：在庞大数据集中高效地找到相关样本，以及在嵌入空间无法清晰分离类别时，将真阳性与视觉上易混淆的假阴性区分开。传统主动学习提供了一个减少标注成本的原则性框架，但其将样本选择视为单阶段过程，无法高效应对上述任一挑战。FunnelAL 将问题分解为级联阶段。从一个正例和一个负例出发，系统迭代执行以下步骤：(1) 基于嵌入的检索打分，将语料库缩小到可管理的候选集；(2) 精度触发的排序阶段，在批量精度保持较高时利用学习的排序模型（RankNet），一旦收益递减则自动融合基于委员会的探索（QBC）；(3) 来自标注者的标签反馈，在后续迭代中优化两个阶段。我们在三个不同的图像分类基准上进行评估。使用完美标注者时，FunnelAL 在所有三个基准上取得了最优的最终F1、最佳标注效率（AULC第一）和最少的标注轮次。最新的单类发现方法（GAL、PF-MA）最多只能在最终质量上与之匹配，且标注成本始终更高。在现实标注错误率下，FunnelAL 仍然保持第一或统计上并列第一，而传统基于不确定性的方法性能下降2至3倍。我们的工作为多级推荐系统与主动学习之间提供了具体的桥梁。

</details>


### [26] [CLBench-V: Evaluating Multimodal Context Learning from Grounding to Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25294)
*Lai Wei,Chengqi Li,Jiapeng Li,Ruina Hu,Yue Wang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CLBench-V，一个评估多模态上下文学习能力的基准，包含3,443个实例和六个维度，实验表明当前最佳模型得分仅0.2847，该任务远未饱和。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有上下文学习评估主要聚焦于纯文本模态，而现实场景中上下文往往是多模态的（如图表、地图、网页等），缺乏专门评估多模态上下文学习的基准。

Method: 围绕上下文基础、新信息应用和新知识学习三个维度构建基准，融合了转换后的公开数据集和新构建的数据集，涵盖科学、金融、长文档理解、空间推理和网页视觉问答等领域，并采用自动化构建与过滤流程降低成本。

Result: 在3,443个实例和六个多模态模型上，最佳总分仅0.2847；InternVL3.5-30B-A3B在上下文基础和新知识学习上最优，Qwen3.5-Plus在新信息应用上最优；进一步分析了裁判可靠性、上下文长度、图像数量和典型失败案例。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文学习仍远未饱和，现有模型在此任务上表现有限，为后续研究提供了系统的评估框架和方向。

Original Abstract: Real-world tasks often require models to learn from task-specific context rather than relying only on pre-trained knowledge. While recent work has highlighted this capability as context learning, existing evaluations mainly focus on textual contexts. In many practical settings, however, the context to be learned from is multimodal: scientific findings are conveyed through figures and tables, financial indicators are scattered across converted reports, and spatial decisions depend on maps, scenes, or web pages. We introduce CLBench-V, a benchmark for multimodal context learning that addresses the difficulty of localizing where context use breaks down by organizing tasks around three dimensions: context grounding, new information application, and new knowledge learning. CLBench-V combines converted public benchmarks with newly constructed datasets spanning domains such as science, finance, long-document understanding, spatial reasoning, and web-based visual question answering. To reduce the cost of constructing domain-specific context-learning tasks, we further use automated construction and filtering procedures for our newly built datasets. Across 3,443 instances and six recent multimodal models, the best overall score is only 0.2847, indicating that multimodal context learning remains far from saturated. Moreover, InternVL3.5-30B-A3B performs best on context grounding and new knowledge learning, while Qwen3.5-Plus performs best on new information application. We further analyze judge reliability, context length, image count, and representative failure cases. Code is available at https://github.com/IamLihua/CLBench-V.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的任务通常要求模型从任务特定的上下文中学习，而不仅仅依赖预训练知识。尽管近期研究已将此能力称为上下文学习，但现有评估主要集中于文本上下文。然而，在许多实际场景中，需要学习的上下文是多模态的：科学发现通过图表传达，金融指标分散在转换后的报告中，空间决策依赖于地图、场景或网页。我们提出了CLBench-V，一个用于多模态上下文学习的基准，通过围绕三个维度（上下文基础、新信息应用和新知识学习）组织任务，解决了定位上下文使用失效困难的痛点。CLBench-V将转换后的公开基准与我们新构建的数据集相结合，涵盖科学、金融、长文档理解、空间推理和基于网页的视觉问答等领域。为降低构建领域特定上下文学习任务的成本，我们进一步对新构建的数据集采用了自动化构建与过滤流程。在3,443个实例和六个近期多模态模型上，最佳总体得分仅为0.2847，表明多模态上下文学习仍远未饱和。此外，InternVL3.5-30B-A3B在上下文基础和新知识学习上表现最佳，而Qwen3.5-Plus在新信息应用上表现最佳。我们进一步分析了裁判可靠性、上下文长度、图像数量和代表性失败案例。代码已开源：https://github.com/IamLihua/CLBench-V。

</details>


### [27] [MEDit-Bench: A Dataset for Evaluating Message-Driven Narrative Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25300)
*Katsuya Ogata,Zongshang Pang,Mayu Otani,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEDit-Bench，一个用于评估消息驱动视频编辑的数据集和基准。它包含长视频与多个编辑消息及对应的专业编辑结果，揭示了不同消息会导致显著不同的编辑结果。研究发现LLM作为评判存在严重位置偏差，消息的模糊性和上下文依赖性会负面影响模型性能，当前最强模型在严格标准下仍落后于人类编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频摘要基准将编辑意图简化为单一、与消息无关的显著性概念，忽略了视频编辑本质上是消息驱动的多样性——即使相同源素材，不同叙事意图会产生不同的镜头选择。因此需要一个新的基准来评估消息驱动的视频编辑。

Method: 构建MEDit-Bench数据集，包含长视频、多个编辑消息及对应的专业编辑结果；定义基于时间对齐度量的自动评估协议；为每条消息标注模糊性和上下文依赖性分数；采用多模态大语言模型和强化微调基线进行实验。

Result: 不同消息导致显著不同的编辑结果；LLM作为评判存在严重位置偏差，不可靠；消息的模糊性和上下文依赖性均与模型性能负相关；强模型在宽松阈值下接近人类时间对齐，但在严格标准下所有模型均落后于人类；人类感知研究证实专业编辑始终优于模型输出。

Conclusion: 消息驱动的视频编辑任务具有挑战性，当前最强的MLLM模型在严格标准下仍与人类专业编辑存在显著差距，且LLM评判的不稳定性需要新的评估方法。

Original Abstract: Video editing is fundamentally message-driven: even from the same source footage, the selected shots change depending on the narrative the editor wishes to convey. Benchmarks for a closely related task, video summarization, reduce editorial intent to a single, message-agnostic notion of saliency and thus do not account for this diversity. For evaluating message-driven video editing, we present \textbf{MEDit-Bench}, a dataset and benchmark, which pairs long-form videos with multiple editing messages and multiple professionally produced edits per message, demonstrating that different messages yield substantially different edits from the same source. We define an automatic evaluation protocol based on temporal alignment metrics, and find that an LLM-as-a-judge preference, a natural proxy for narrative quality, is unreliable for this task due to severe position bias. We additionally annotate each message with ambiguity and contextfulness scores, and show that both dimensions negatively correlate with model performance, establishing message difficulty as a meaningful stratification factor. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs and reinforcement fine-tuned baselines show that while strong models approach human temporal alignment at lenient thresholds, all models fall behind humans at stricter criteria. A human perceptual study further confirms a large quality gap, with professional human edits remaining consistently preferred over model outputs.
