中文摘要翻译: 视频编辑本质上是消息驱动的：即使是相同的源素材，根据编辑者想要传达的叙事不同，所选取的镜头也会发生变化。与视频编辑密切相关的视频摘要任务，其基准将编辑意图简化为一种单一的、与消息无关的显著性概念，因此未能考虑这种多样性。为了评估消息驱动的视频编辑，我们提出了\textbf{MEDit-Bench}，一个数据集和基准，它将长视频与多个编辑消息以及每条消息对应的多个专业制作的编辑结果配对，证明了不同的消息会从相同源素材中产生显著不同的编辑结果。我们定义了一个基于时间对齐度量的自动评估协议，并发现作为叙事质量自然代理的LLM-as-a-judge偏好评估，由于严重的位置偏差，在此任务中不可靠。我们还为每条消息标注了模糊性和上下文依赖性分数，并表明这两个维度均与模型性能负相关，从而将消息难度确立为一个有意义的分层因子。使用最先进的多模态大语言模型和强化微调基线进行的实验表明，虽然强模型在宽松阈值下接近人类的时间对齐水平，但在更严格的标准下，所有模型均落后于人类。一项人类感知研究进一步证实了巨大的质量差距，专业人工编辑的输出始终比模型输出更受偏好。

</details>


### [28] [Sense it with your eyes: Sensation Generation and Understanding for Advertisements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25314)
*Aysan Aghazadeh,Sina Malakouti,Adriana Kovashka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了感官广告的理解、评估与生成，提出了感官广告数据集、感官分类任务（SenseClass）、自动化评估指标（SenseScore）以及多智能体生成框架（SAGA），为感官驱动的视觉说服奠定基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管AI在创意和说服性内容生成与理解方面应用日益增多，但广告如何通过视觉线索唤起感官体验这一问题仍未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建感官广告数据集；2) 定义感官分类任务（SenseClass）用于基准测试LLM和MLLM；3) 提出自动化评估指标SenseScore；4) 提出感官广告生成任务（SenseGen）及多智能体框架SAGA，用于提升信息-图像对齐、感官唤起和说服力。

Result: SenseScore作为自动化评估指标，与人类判断达到了强一致性。

Conclusion: 本文为感官驱动的视觉说服建立了基础性研究框架。

Original Abstract: Sensory advertising evokes human senses through visual cues, enabling audiences to mentally simulate experiences and increasing persuasive impact. Despite the recent increase in using AI in generating and understanding creative and persuasive content, how advertisements visually evoke sensations remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce the first study of understanding, evaluating, and generating sensory ads. We introduce the Sensory Ad dataset, and define sensation classification tasks (SenseClass) to benchmark LLMs and MLLMs. We further propose SenseScore, an automated evaluation metric for sensation evocation achieving strong agreement with human judgments. Finally, we introduce the Sensory Ad Generation (SenseGen) task and propose SAGA, a multi-agent framework that improves message image alignment, sensory evocation, and persuasion. Our work establishes a foundation for sensory-aware visual persuasion.

中文摘要翻译: 感官广告通过视觉线索唤起人类感官，使受众能够在心理上模拟体验，从而增强说服效果。尽管近年来AI在生成和理解创意及说服性内容方面的应用日益增多，但广告如何通过视觉方式唤起感官体验仍未被充分探索。本文首次系统研究了感官广告的理解、评估与生成。我们引入了感官广告数据集，并定义了感官分类任务（SenseClass），用于对大语言模型（LLM）和多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行基准测试。我们进一步提出了SenseScore，一种自动化评估指标，用于衡量感官唤起程度，与人类判断具有强一致性。最后，我们提出了感官广告生成任务（SenseGen），并提出了SAGA多智能体框架，该框架提升了信息与图像的对齐、感官唤起及说服效果。本工作为感官驱动的视觉说服建立了基础。

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Background Bias: Saliency-Driven Prototype Alignment for Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25318)
*Yawen Zou,Wenqi Cai,Guang Li,Ling Xiao,Chunzhi Gu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于显著性驱动的数据集蒸馏框架，通过构建类别判别性潜在原型来提升扩散式蒸馏方法的结构一致性和泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散式蒸馏方法在视觉复杂域中难以保持结构一致性和泛化能力，原因是潜在原型与类别判别性区域对齐弱且受无关背景污染。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 使用集成Grad-CAM显著性构建强调高置信度区域的原型；(2) 应用困难原型精炼构造具有挑战性但类别一致的原型。扩散骨干网络冻结，仅训练用于显著性提取的轻量分类器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法相较于强基线取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提显著性驱动蒸馏框架能有效提升扩散式数据集蒸馏的质量和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Dataset distillation aims to synthesize compact datasets that can approximate the performance of full-data training while significantly reducing computational and storage costs. However, diffusion-based distillation methods often struggle to preserve structural coherence and generalization, especially in visually complex domains. This issue often stems from latent prototypes that are weakly aligned with class-discriminative regions and contaminated by irrelevant background, thereby degrading generation quality and generalization. To address this limitation, we propose a saliency-driven distillation framework that constructs class-discriminative latent prototypes to enhance representativeness and generalization. The framework proceeds in two stages: (1) ensemble Grad-CAM saliency is used to construct prototypes emphasizing high-confidence regions, and (2) hard prototype refinement is then applied to construct challenging yet class-consistent prototypes, thereby enhancing discriminability and diversity. Importantly, the diffusion backbones (e.g., LDM and DiT) remain frozen; only lightweight classifiers used for saliency extraction are trained. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Code will be released.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集蒸馏旨在合成紧凑的数据集，其能够在显著降低计算和存储成本的同时近似全数据训练的性能。然而，基于扩散的蒸馏方法在视觉复杂领域中常常难以保持结构一致性和泛化能力。该问题通常源于潜在原型与类别判别性区域对齐较弱，并受到无关背景的污染，从而降低了生成质量和泛化性能。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种显著性驱动的蒸馏框架，通过构建类别判别性潜在原型来提升代表性和泛化能力。该框架分为两个阶段：(1) 使用集成Grad-CAM显著性构建强调高置信度区域的原型；(2) 随后应用困难原型精炼来构造具有挑战性但类别一致的原型，从而增强判别性和多样性。重要的是，扩散骨干网络（如LDM和DiT）保持冻结；仅训练用于显著性提取的轻量分类器。在多个基准上的大量实验表明，该方法相较于强基线取得了一致的性能提升。代码将公开。

</details>


### [30] [Balanced Soft mixture-of-expert model for Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25324)
*Sai Venkatesh Chilukoti,Krishna Rauniyar,Min Shi,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种平衡软混合专家模型（含三个专家和负载平衡损失），用于多模态青光眼检测，解决了多模态学习中单模态表示不平衡和优化不足的问题，在AUC指标上超越了现有方法，并可推广到其他疾病检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态学习在青光眼检测中面临单模态表示不平衡和优化不足的挑战，这是由于联合学习目标导致的，现有方法未能很好解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一个平衡软混合专家模型（Balanced Soft Mixture-of-Experts），包含三个专家网络和一个负载平衡损失（load balancing loss），以平衡和优化不同模态的表示。

Result: 在AUC指标上，该方法超越了所有单模态基线、传统多模态模型以及当前最先进的平衡多模态模型。

Conclusion: 提出的平衡软混合专家模型有效解决了多模态学习中的单模态不平衡问题，提升了青光眼检测精度，并可推广至糖尿病视网膜病变等其他疾病检测任务。

Original Abstract: Glaucoma is a group of eye diseases that damage the optic nerve, often caused by elevated intraocular pressure. It is a leading cause of irreversible vision loss and is typically developed slowly and painlessly, making it difficult to notice until significant damage has occurred. Therefore, early detection is crucial to prevent or slow the progression of vision loss. In recent years, deep learning based uni-modal models have improved the accuracy and efficiency of glaucoma detection, empowering doctors with tools for earlier diagnosis, better monitoring, and timely treatment. Building on this, multi-modal models have emerged, leveraging the strengths of different imaging modalities to learn richer and more robust representations, further enhancing glaucoma detection accuracy. However, multi-modal learning faces challenges such as imbalanced and under-optimized uni-modal representations due to joint learning objectives. To address this, we propose a balanced soft mixture-experts model with three experts and load balancing loss. The performance is measured by AUC, our proposed method surpasses the performance of all uni-modal baselines, conventional multi-modal models, and current stateof- the-art balanced multi-modal models. The proposed model can be generalized to other disease detections such as diabetic retinopathy.

中文摘要翻译: 青光眼是一组损害视神经的眼部疾病，通常由眼内压升高引起。它是导致不可逆视力丧失的主要原因，通常发展缓慢且无痛，因此在出现显著损伤之前难以察觉。因此，早期检测对于预防或减缓视力下降至关重要。近年来，基于深度学习的单模态模型提高了青光眼检测的准确性和效率，为医生提供了更早诊断、更好监测和及时治疗的工具。在此基础上，多模态模型应运而生，利用不同成像模态的优势学习更丰富和鲁棒的表示，进一步提升了青光眼检测的准确性。然而，多模态学习面临着因联合学习目标导致的单模态表示不平衡和优化不足的挑战。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种包含三个专家和负载平衡损失的平衡软混合专家模型。在AUC指标上，我们的方法超越了所有单模态基线、传统多模态模型以及当前最先进的平衡多模态模型。该模型可推广至糖尿病视网膜病变等其他疾病检测任务。

</details>


### [31] [PanoLess: Environment Reconstruction from Partial Reflective Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25362)
*Ahitagni Das,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Vivek Boominathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanoLess，一种基于高斯溅射的框架，仅从反射表面单侧拍摄的图像重建环境光照图，利用表面对齐的2D高斯和延迟着色恢复法线和反射线索，融合为神经立方体贴图，并生成可见性图标注可靠区域，在部分视角输入下实现了高质量的物理一致环境重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有逆渲染和反射感知高斯溅射方法通常需要完整的360度覆盖才能重建环境，在部分视角输入条件下表现不佳。而现实场景中，反光物体和玻璃幕墙的反射可以自然扩展相机视野，因此提出从反射表面单侧观测中重建完整环境图的方法。

Method: PanoLess采用表面对齐的2D高斯溅射结合延迟着色，逐像素恢复精确法线和反射线索，将这些信息融合为神经立方体贴图（neural cubemap）作为环境表示，同时生成可见性图，显式标注环境中被部分反射观测所支持的可靠区域。

Result: 在自定义合成基准和公开数据集上，PanoLess实现了高保真且几何一致的环境重建，性能优于反射感知基线方法，并展示了在真实世界反射场景中的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PanoLess能够在仅从反射表面单侧观测的条件下，实现一致且物理可信的环境光照估计与重建，突破了传统方法对全视角覆盖的依赖。

Original Abstract: Reflections from shiny objects and glass facades naturally extend the field of view of a camera, capturing the surrounding environment without the need to pan the camera or acquire a full panorama. We propose PanoLess, a Gaussian-splat-based framework that reconstructs the surrounding environment as a distant illumination map from images captured on only one side of a reflective surface. PanoLess leverages surface-aligned 2D Gaussian splats with deferred shading to recover accurate per-pixel normals and reflection cues, which are fused into a neural cubemap representation of the environment. In addition, PanoLess produces a visibility map that explicitly denotes which regions of the environment are supported by the partial reflective observations. Unlike existing inverse-rendering and reflection-aware Gaussian-splatting approaches, which typically require full 360-degree coverage and struggle under incomplete views, PanoLess enables consistent, physically grounded illumination estimation from partial-view input. We show that PanoLess achieves high-fidelity and geometrically consistent environment reconstruction, outperforming reflection-aware baselines on a new custom synthetic benchmark and publicly available datasets, and demonstrating generalization to real-world reflective captures.

中文摘要翻译: 来自反光物体和玻璃幕墙的反射自然地扩展了相机的视野，能够捕捉周围环境，而无需平移相机或获取全景图像。我们提出PanoLess，一种基于高斯溅射的框架，仅从反射表面单侧拍摄的图像即可将周围环境重建为远距离光照图。PanoLess利用表面对齐的2D高斯溅射结合延迟着色，恢复精确的逐像素法线和反射线索，并将其融合为环境的神经立方体贴图表示。此外，PanoLess生成可见性图，显式标注环境中由部分反射观测所支持的可靠区域。与现有通常需要全360度覆盖且在不完整视角下表现不佳的逆渲染和反射感知高斯溅射方法不同，PanoLess能够从部分视角输入实现一致且物理可信的光照估计。我们展示，PanoLess在自定义合成基准和公开数据集上实现了高保真且几何一致的环境重建，性能优于反射感知基线方法，并展示了其在真实世界反射场景中的泛化能力。

</details>


### [32] [Leak-Free Cross-Validated Stacking with Per-Architecture Calibration for Sand-Boil Segmentation in Earthen Levees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25367)
*Padam Jung Thapa,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Abdullah Bin Naeem,Steve Sloan,Kendall Niles,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种沙涌分割框架Updated SandBoilNet，通过数据过滤协议和元学习器集成方法，解决了因标注稀缺导致模型精度虚高的两大漏洞，在测试集上IoU达到0.707。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 沙涌是堤坝内部侵蚀的早期预警信号，但标注样本稀缺。现有两种常见做法——在测试集上调整集成权重以及在源自测试集的合成图像上训练——会虚报模型精度，需要一种能消除这些漏洞的框架。

Method: (1) 每张合成图像携带指向真实父图像的指针，通过逐折过滤器排除父图像被保留在测试集内的合成样本；(2) 五折交叉训练五个编码器-解码器骨干网络，每个用单一温度标量校准；(3) 使用逐像素元学习器（仅用折外预测拟合）进行集成，避免数据泄露。

Result: Updated SandBoilNet在测试集上IoU达0.707（原版为0.608）；堆叠协议下校准集成结果为0.681，未超过最强单模型（0.694），原因在于成员间平均成对误差相关性高达0.894；经标签保真度过滤的合成池使冠军模型提升至0.718。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效堵住了精度虚高的两个漏洞，但集成方法因成员间高度相关性未能超越最佳单模型；引入的掩码条件合成路径以零标注成本生成带标签训练图像。

Original Abstract: Sand boils, points where water seeping beneath an earthen levee re-emerges at the surface, are early warnings of internal erosion, and deep segmentation networks are increasingly used to find them in inspection photographs. Annotated examples are scarce, and two common ways of working around that scarcity quietly inflate reported accuracy: tuning ensemble weights on the same images later used to score them, and training on synthetic images derived from the very photographs held out for testing. We present a sand-boil segmentation framework that closes both loopholes. Every synthetic image carries a pointer to its real parent, and a per-fold filter excludes any image whose parent is held out; five encoder-decoder backbones are trained under five-fold cross-validation, calibrated by one temperature scalar each, and combined by a per-pixel meta-learner fitted only on out-of-fold predictions. On the held-out test set the proposed Updated SandBoilNet reaches an intersection-over-union of 0.707 over three seeds, against 0.608 for the published original re-evaluated on the same split. Under the stacking protocol the calibrated stack reaches 0.681 against 0.694 for the strongest fold-averaged member, so it does not improve on the best single model; eight meta-learner families reproduce that outcome, which we trace to a mean pairwise error correlation of 0.894 among members. A synthetic pool filtered for label fidelity lifts the champion to 0.718 over three seeds against a 0.707 control. We also introduce a mask-conditioned synthesis route that makes the conditioning mask the label by construction, giving labelled training images at zero annotation cost.

中文摘要翻译: 沙涌（sand boils）是水从土堤下方渗漏后重新出现在地表的位置，是内部侵蚀的早期预警信号，深度分割网络越来越多地被用于在巡检照片中检测沙涌。标注样本十分稀缺，而围绕这种稀缺性的两种常见做法会悄然推高报告的精度：一是在后续用于评分的同一图像上调整集成权重，二是在源自测试集照片的合成图像上进行训练。我们提出了一种沙涌分割框架，旨在堵住这两个漏洞。每张合成图像都携带一个指向其真实父图像的指针，逐折过滤器会排除任何父图像被保留在测试集之外的图像；五个编码器-解码器骨干网络在五折交叉验证下进行训练，每个网络通过一个温度标量进行校准，并由一个仅基于折外预测进行拟合的逐像素元学习器进行集成组合。在保留的测试集上，所提出的Updated SandBoilNet在三个随机种子上达到0.707的交并比（IoU），而同一数据划分下重新评估的已发表原版为0.608。在堆叠协议下，校准后的集成结果达到0.681，而最强折平均单模型为0.694，因此并未超过最佳单模型；八个元学习器系列均再现了这一结果，我们将其归因于成员间平均成对误差相关性为0.894。经过标签保真度过滤的合成池将冠军模型提升至三个随机种子上的0.718，而对照组为0.707。我们还引入了一条掩码条件合成路径，使得条件掩码本身即为标签，从而以零标注成本生成带标签的训练图像。

</details>


### [33] [Hyperspectral Intrinsic Decomposition: Joint Recovery of Reflectance and Photometric Components for Non-Lambertian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25371)
*Hao Ye,Zhan Shi,Chenglong Huang,Tao Lv,Mingjie Ji,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二分光反射模型的双尺度非朗伯高光谱本征分解方法，通过将四个耦合分量重新表述为两个谱-空目标变量进行估计，并构建了首个非朗伯高光谱本征分解真实数据集CITE及物理保真本征集生成器PISG。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高光谱本征分解方法大多基于朗伯或近似朗伯假设，少数非朗伯方法依赖简化镜面假设，难以处理真实世界中多样化的镜面反射，且通常需要辅助输入或仅能恢复部分耦合分量，无法实现完整且盲分解。

Method: 重新审视二分光反射模型，将四个耦合的反射与光度分量的恢复重新表述为两个谱-空目标变量的估计；提出双尺度分解方案：全局尺度上利用光度不变描述子作为边缘先验实现高保真本征边界保持，局部尺度上通过镜面引导注意力聚焦镜面主导区域（包括裁剪失真区域）进行细化。

Result: 构建了首个公开的真实世界非朗伯高光谱本征分解数据集CITE及可控数据合成工具PISG，大量消融实验和跨场景实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的双尺度非朗伯高光谱本征分解方法能够有效处理真实世界中多样化的镜面反射效应，实现完整且盲的分解，数据集和合成工具为后续研究提供了重要基准。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral intrinsic decomposition (HID) aims to disentangle material-related spectral properties and photometric effects in hyperspectral images (HSIs), which is essential for understanding real-world imaging processes and benefits a variety of downstream applications. Most existing HID studies have been developed under Lambertian or near-Lambertian assumptions. The few prior non-Lambertian efforts rely on simplified specular assumptions insufficient to handle diverse real-world specularity, and typically require auxiliary inputs or recover only a subset of the coupled reflectance and photometric components, hindering complete and blind decomposition. In this paper, we revisit the dichromatic reflection model (DRM) and develop a unified inversion paradigm that reformulates the recovery of four coupled reflectance and photometric components as the estimation of two spectral--spatial target variables. Building on this reformulation, we propose a dual-scale decomposition scheme to handle non-Lambertian effects with distinct spatial characteristics. At the global scale, photometrically invariant descriptors serve as edge priors for high-fidelity intrinsic boundary preservation; at the local scale, specularity-guided attention directs refinement with emphasis on specularity-dominated regions, including those affected by clipping distortion. To facilitate future research, we establish CITE, the first public real-world HID dataset for non-Lambertian objects, and develop a Physically-faithful Intrinsic Set Generator (PISG) for controllable data synthesis. Extensive ablation studies and experiments on the CITE and additional HSIs demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its robustness across diverse scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱本征分解旨在解耦高光谱图像中与材料相关的光谱特性与光度效应，这对于理解真实成像过程至关重要，并有益于多种下游应用。现有大多数高光谱本征分解研究基于朗伯或近似朗伯假设。少数先前的非朗伯方法依赖于简化的镜面假设，不足以处理真实世界中多样化的镜面反射，且通常需要辅助输入或仅能恢复耦合的反射分量与光度分量中的一部分，阻碍了完整且盲分解的实现。本文重新审视二分光反射模型，并发展了一种统一的反演范式，将四个耦合的反射与光度分量的恢复重新表述为两个谱-空目标变量的估计。基于这一重新表述，我们提出了一种双尺度分解方案来处理具有不同空间特征的非朗伯效应。在全局尺度上，光度不变描述子作为边缘先验，实现高保真本征边界保持；在局部尺度上，镜面引导注意力引导细化过程，重点关注镜面主导区域，包括受裁剪失真影响的区域。为促进未来研究，我们构建了CITE——首个针对非朗伯物体的公开真实世界高光谱本征分解数据集，并开发了用于可控数据合成的物理保真本征集生成器。在CITE及其他高光谱图像上的大量消融实验表明，我们的方法在多样场景下具有有效性和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [34] [Gaussian Volumetric Representation for Efficient Shear-Warp Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25377)
*Mayuri Mathur,Ojaswa Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯表示的医学图像体绘制方法，通过蒙特卡洛体估计和课程学习策略，在稀疏监督下实现高效、高保真的体数据可视化，达到43.86 FPS渲染速度与11.31:1压缩比。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模体数据计算成本高昂，现有方法难以在保持解剖结构和辐射度细节的同时实现高效渲染。

Method: 基于高斯表示的体绘制方法，结合蒙特卡洛体估计在稀疏体素子集上训练，并引入课程学习策略，逐步加入结构化切片采样以捕捉空间相关区域。

Result: 在稀疏监督下达到43.86 FPS渲染速度，压缩比为11.31:1，支持MRI和冷冻切片等多模态医学数据集的高效可视化。

Conclusion: 提出的高斯体表示方法能够在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持解剖结构的保真度，支持切片式渲染方法，适用于多模态医学体数据的实时可视化。

Original Abstract: Medical image visualization requires volumetric rendering algorithms that preserve anatomical fidelity while maintaining high rendering speeds. To address the high computational cost of large volumetric datasets, we propose a Gaussian-based volumetric representation for efficient visualization of dense medical volumes without compromising structural and radiometric details. We optimize the proposed representation using Monte Carlo volumetric estimation, which enables training on a highly sparse subset of voxels while maintaining consistency with the dense volumetric objective. In addition, we introduce a curriculum learning strategy that progressively incorporates structured slice-based sampling during training. Sparse voxel samples provide an early global coverage of the volume, while slice samples capture spatially correlated regions that aid geometric structure and texture continuity. This combination enables the Gaussian representation to learn anatomical details of various structures and corresponding textures from sparse supervision while significantly reducing the computational cost associated with dense voxel processing. The learned representation supports slice-based rendering methods such as shear-warp volume rendering, enabling efficient visualization of multimodal medical datasets including MRI and Cryosection volumes while preserving anatomical structures. Using sparse supervision, our method achieves up to 43.86 FPS rendering with a compression ratio of 11.31:1.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像可视化需要能够在保持解剖学保真度的同时实现高渲染速度的体绘制算法。为了解决大体数据集的高计算成本问题，我们提出了一种基于高斯的体表示方法，用于高效可视化密集的医学体数据，同时不损失结构和辐射度细节。我们利用蒙特卡洛体估计优化所提出的表示方法，使得能够在高度稀疏的体素子集上进行训练，同时保持与密集体目标的一致性。此外，我们引入了一种课程学习策略，在训练过程中逐步加入结构化的基于切片的采样。稀疏体素样本提供了体数据的早期全局覆盖，而切片样本则捕获空间相关区域，有助于几何结构和纹理连续性。这种组合使得高斯表示能够在稀疏监督下学习各种结构的解剖细节及相应纹理，同时显著降低密集体素处理带来的计算成本。学习到的表示支持基于切片的渲染方法（如剪切-扭曲体绘制），从而能够在保持解剖结构的同时高效可视化包括MRI和冷冻切片在内的多模态医学数据集。在稀疏监督下，我们的方法实现了高达43.86 FPS的渲染速度，压缩比为11.31:1。

</details>


### [35] [HOME: Robust Hough-space Matching Method for Structured and Textureless Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25389)
*Masaki Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HOME，一种无需训练的轻量级特征匹配框架，将图像变换到霍夫空间，把全局线性结构映射为稳定局部极值点，将复杂线匹配转化为高效一维点匹配，在结构化环境中速度快于现有线方法且鲁棒性优于点方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于点的特征（如ORB）在结构化环境中（强线性结构或无纹理表面）经常失败；而基于线的SLAM系统虽能缓解该问题，但传统线提取和描述算法计算开销大，无法满足边缘机器人的实时需求。

Method: 将图像变换到霍夫空间，将全局线性结构映射为稳定的局部极值点作为关键点，将复杂线匹配重新表述为高效的一维点匹配；提出一维径向描述子，无需显式方向估计即可数学保证旋转和平移不变性。

Result: 在点方法失效的挑战性场景中实现鲁棒的图像配准，运行速度远快于现有基于线的方法。

Conclusion: HOME作为一种轻量级训练无关的特征匹配框架，在结构化环境中兼具高精度和高效率，扩展至完整三维位姿估计是未来有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Visual front-ends for robotic localization typically rely on point-based features such as Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF (ORB), which frequently fail in structured environments dominated by strong linear structures or textureless surfaces. While line-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) systems mitigate this by utilizing line segments, conventional line extraction and description algorithms are computationally prohibitive for real-time edge robotics. To address this fundamental bottleneck, we propose HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema), an ultra-lightweight, training-free feature matching framework. HOME transforms images into Hough space, mapping global linear structures to stable local extrema, which serve as keypoints, thereby reformulating complex line matching into highly efficient one-dimensional point matching. The proposed 1D radial descriptor mathematically guarantees rotational and translational invariance without the overhead of explicit orientation estimation. As a proof of concept to validate the matching accuracy and efficiency of HOME, this paper focuses on homography estimation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that HOME achieves robust registration in challenging scenarios where point-based methods fail, operating at a much faster speed than existing line-based methods. Extending this robust matching engine to full 3D pose estimation remains a highly promising future direction.

中文摘要翻译: 机器人定位的视觉前端通常依赖基于点的特征，如ORB（Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF），这些特征在由强线性结构或无纹理表面主导的结构化环境中经常失效。基于线的同时定位与建图（SLAM）系统通过利用线段来缓解这一问题，但传统的线提取和描述算法在实时边缘机器人场景中计算开销过大。为解决这一根本性瓶颈，我们提出了HOME（Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema），一种超轻量级、无需训练的特征匹配框架。HOME将图像变换到霍夫空间，将全局线性结构映射为稳定的局部极值点作为关键点，从而将复杂的线匹配重新表述为高效的一维点匹配。所提出的一维径向描述子无需显式方向估计即可从数学上保证旋转和平移不变性。作为验证HOME匹配精度和效率的概念验证，本文聚焦于单应性估计。大量评估表明，HOME在点方法失效的挑战性场景中实现了鲁棒的图像配准，且运行速度远快于现有基于线的方法。将该鲁棒匹配引擎扩展至完整的二维位姿估计是一个非常有前景的未来方向。

</details>


### [36] [Noise-Free One-Step LoRA for Task-Driven Image Restoration with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25390)
*Jaeha Kim,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练扩散先验的确定性无噪声单步前向传播方法，用于任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR），并引入任务保持的GAN训练策略，在分类、分割、检测等下游任务上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 退化图像不仅降低视觉质量，还会损害下游高层视觉任务。现有基于扩散先验的TDIR方法因采样过程依赖随机噪声项而具有内在随机性，损害了任务一致性。本文旨在解决这一随机性问题。

Method: 1）采用确定性无噪声的单步前向传播替代传统多步扩散采样，利用预训练扩散先验；2）发现LoRA适配模块能带来持续增益，而ControlNet风格的条件注入则不行；3）提出任务保持的GAN训练策略，在提升感知质量的同时不牺牲任务性能。

Result: 在分类、分割、检测任务上的大量实验表明，该方法一致优于先前的TDIR方法，并在真实退化图像和OCR任务上验证了泛化能力。单步前向传播超越了传统的多步扩散TDIR基线。

Conclusion: 确定性无噪声的单步前向传播结合LoRA适配模块能有效提升任务驱动图像恢复的性能；任务保持的GAN训练可在保持任务性能的前提下改善感知质量。

Original Abstract: Degraded images not only reduce visual quality but also impair downstream high-level vision tasks. Task-driven image restoration (TDIR) addresses this issue by jointly optimizing restoration quality and task performance. Recent works show that pretrained diffusion priors benefit TDIR, yet diffusion-based restoration is inherently stochastic, as the sampling process depends on a random noise term, which can undermine task consistency. In this paper, we show that a deterministic, noise-free one-step forward pass with pretrained diffusion priors can substantially improve TDIR, but the benefit critically depends on the adaptation module: LoRA yields consistent gains, whereas ControlNet-style conditioning does not. This enables one-step forwarding that surpasses conventional multi-step diffusion TDIR baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a task-preserving GAN training strategy that improves perceptual quality without sacrificing task performance. Extensive experiments on classification, segmentation, and detection demonstrate consistent gains over prior TDIR methods, and we further validate generalization on real-world degraded images and OCR.

中文摘要翻译: 退化图像不仅降低视觉质量，还会损害下游高层视觉任务。任务驱动图像恢复（TDIR）通过联合优化恢复质量和任务性能来解决这一问题。近期研究表明，预训练扩散先验有利于TDIR，然而基于扩散的恢复本质上是随机的，因为采样过程依赖于随机噪声项，这可能损害任务一致性。本文表明，使用预训练扩散先验进行确定性、无噪声的单步前向传播可以显著改善TDIR，但这一收益关键取决于适配模块：LoRA带来持续增益，而ControlNet风格的条件注入则不然。这使得单步前向传播能够超越传统的多步扩散TDIR基线。此外，我们引入了一种任务保持的GAN训练策略，在提升感知质量的同时不牺牲任务性能。在分类、分割和检测任务上的大量实验表明，该方法一致优于先前的TDIR方法，我们还在真实退化图像和OCR上进一步验证了其泛化能力。

</details>


### [37] [RDVSv2: A Large-scale Benchmark for RGB-D Video Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25392)
*Tianyu Li,Jiahao He,Keren Fu,Qijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RDVSv2，一个大规模RGB-D视频显著性目标检测基准数据集，包含249个视频序列、29,077帧密集标注，并基于SAM2构建了高效的基线方法。实验表明该数据集更具挑战性，所提基线达到了当前最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-D视频显著性目标检测数据集规模有限、标注质量不高，且深度线索的几何一致性不足，难以支撑该领域的深入研究和模型泛化能力提升。

Method: 基于SAM2构建基线模型，采用参数高效微调（PEFT）策略，使SAM2编码器能够联合编码RGB、深度和光流三种模态线索，实现多模态融合的显著性检测。

Result: RDVSv2数据集对现有RGB-D VSOD方法具有明显更高的挑战性；所提出的基线方法在RDVSv2及现有基准上均取得了最优结果。

Conclusion: RDVSv2及其基线将为RGB-D VSOD及相关多模态视频理解任务提供有价值的基准资源，推动该领域的发展。

Original Abstract: We introduce RDVSv2, a large-scale benchmark for RGB-D video salient object detection (RGB-D VSOD) with dense frame-level annotations. Existing datasets in this emerging field are often limited in scale and annotation quality, while also relying on less geometry-consistent depth cues. To address these limitations, RDVSv2 is built from publicly accessible stereoscopic online videos and contains 249 video sequences with 29,077 annotated frames. It includes depth maps derived from stereoscopic videos, together with frame-wise salient object masks annotated with eye-tracking guidance. Compared with existing datasets, RDVSv2 is much larger in scale and covers more diverse and challenging scenarios. In addition, we establish a strong baseline for RGB-D VSOD based on Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2). Specifically, we employ a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategy to adapt the SAM2 encoder to jointly encode RGB, depth, and optical flow cues. Extensive experiments show that RDVSv2 is substantially more challenging for existing RGB-D VSOD methods. Meanwhile, the proposed baseline achieves state-of-the-art results on RDVSv2 and existing RGB-D VSOD benchmarks. We hope that RDVSv2 and the provided baseline will serve as useful resources for future research on RGB-D VSOD and related multi-modal video understanding tasks. Our dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/ltynick/RDVSv2.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了RDVSv2，一个大规模RGB-D视频显著性目标检测（RGB-D VSOD）基准数据集，具有密集的帧级标注。该新兴领域的现有数据集通常在规模和标注质量上有限，同时依赖于几何一致性较差的深度线索。为解决这些局限，RDVSv2基于公开可用的立体在线视频构建，包含249个视频序列、29,077帧标注帧。它包含从立体视频中提取的深度图，以及通过眼动追踪引导标注的逐帧显著性目标掩码。与现有数据集相比，RDVSv2规模更大，涵盖了更多样化和更具挑战性的场景。此外，我们基于Segment Anything Model 2（SAM2）建立了一个强基线用于RGB-D VSOD。具体而言，我们采用参数高效微调（PEFT）策略，使SAM2编码器能够联合编码RGB、深度和光流线索。大量实验表明，RDVSv2对现有RGB-D VSOD方法具有更高的挑战性。同时，所提出的基线在RDVSv2和现有RGB-D VSOD基准上均取得了最优结果。我们希望RDVSv2及提供的基线能为RGB-D VSOD及相关多模态视频理解任务的未来研究提供有价值的资源。我们的数据集和代码将在https://github.com/ltynick/RDVSv2上公开。

</details>


### [38] [Towards Reliable Stain Transfer: An Iterative Data-Model Co-Optimization Framework Based on Multimodal Expert-Guided Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25393)
*Siyuan Xu,Yan Wang,Haofei Song,Lili Gao,Jiansheng Wang,Qing Zhang,Dan Huang,Boxiang Yun,Hongkai Xiong,Qingli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMCoStain提出了一种数据-模型协同优化的染色迁移框架，通过多模态专家引导的精细选择策略（MEGFS）和首个大规模IPE指令遵循数据集ImmunoInstruction，在不受像素级监督的情况下实现了从H&E到IHC图像的高精度、可解释的染色迁移，在多个组织和生物标志物上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的染色迁移方法在异构生物标志物和像素未对齐监督下缺乏统一且可解释的建模能力，而IHC染色虽然提供关键的分子信息但成本高昂且需要专业知识，因此需要一种高效且准确的替代方案。

Method: 提出DMCoStain框架，核心包含两部分：(1) 多模态专家引导的精细选择策略（MEGFS），基于首创的IHC阳性表达（IPE）视觉-语言模型（VLM）模拟病理学家推理过程，以临床有意义的方式优化训练数据；(2) 构建ImmunoInstruction——首个大规模IPE指令遵循数据集（含15万VQA样本），支持MEGFS策略。整个框架通过迭代协同优化训练数据和模型能力，提升染色准确性和可解释性。

Result: 在多个组织和生物标志物上的大量实验表明，DMCoStain在染色迁移精度上达到了最先进的水平（SOTA），MEGFS还可作为未来模型开发的专用评估工具。

Conclusion: DMCoStain范式在病理染色迁移任务中具有强实用价值，其数据-模型协同优化思路为异构生物标志物的统一建模提供了新方案，同时MEGFS策略在模型评估方面也展现出额外价值。

Original Abstract: Histopathological examination primarily relies on hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) and immunohistochemistry (IHC) staining. Although IHC provides critical molecular information, it is costly and requires specialized expertise. Stain transfer provides an efficient alternative by computationally generating IHC from H&E images, but remains challenged by unified and interpretable modeling for heterogeneous biomarkers under pixel-unaligned supervision. We propose DMCoStain, a novel Data-Model Co-optimization framework for Stain transfer. It iteratively co-refines training data and model capability, improving staining accuracy and interpretability in both pathological and structural consistency. To refine training data in a clinically meaningful manner, it incorporates the Multimodal Expert-Guided Finer Selection (MEGFS) strategy, built upon a pioneering IHC-positive-expression (IPE) vision-language model (VLM) that emulates pathologist reasoning. To support MEGFS, we construct ImmunoInstruction, the first large-scale IPE instruction-following dataset with 150K VQA samples. Extensive experiments on multiple tissues and biomarkers demonstrate that DMCoStain achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy. This paradigm offers strong practical value, and MEGFS also functions as a specialized evaluation tool for future model development. Dataset, code, and more details are in https://github.com/SikangSHU/DMCoStain.

中文摘要翻译: 组织病理学检查主要依赖于苏木精-伊红（H&E）染色和免疫组织化学（IHC）染色。尽管IHC能够提供关键的分子信息，但其成本高昂且需要专业知识。染色迁移通过从H&E图像计算生成IHC图像提供了一种高效替代方案，但在异构生物标志物和像素未对齐监督下的统一且可解释的建模仍然面临挑战。本文提出DMCoStain——一种新颖的数据-模型协同优化的染色迁移框架。该框架通过迭代式协同精炼训练数据和模型能力，在病理一致性和结构一致性两方面提升了染色准确性与可解释性。为了以临床有意义的方式精炼训练数据，DMCoStain引入了多模态专家引导的精细选择策略（MEGFS），该策略建立在首创的IHC阳性表达（IPE）视觉-语言模型（VLM）之上，能够模拟病理学家的推理过程。为支持MEGFS，我们构建了ImmunoInstruction——首个包含15万VQA样本的大规模IPE指令遵循数据集。在多个组织和生物标志物上的大量实验表明，DMCoStain达到了最先进的准确率。该范式具有强大的实用价值，同时MEGFS也可作为未来模型开发的专用评估工具。数据集、代码及更多细节详见https://github.com/SikangSHU/DMCoStain。

</details>


### [39] [ANFI: Rethinking Neighbor Feature Interaction in Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25407)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Jiaze Li,Qinhong Yang,Tao Gong,Qi Chu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应邻居特征交互（ANFI）方法，在存在噪声邻居的情况下，通过同时建模亲和关系和差异关系并采用样本级自适应加权，实现行人重识别中的鲁棒邻居交互。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于邻居的行人重识别方法仅依赖亲和关系，在存在噪声邻居时性能严重下降，亟需一种能在噪声邻居环境中进行有效交互的方法。

Method: 提出ANFI方法，核心是同时建模亲和关系与差异关系，并采用样本级自适应加权；从新的邻居相似度中推导差异关系；提出噪声关系监督（NRS）训练策略，逐步向模型注入对噪声关系的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准、跨模态和跨域等多种设置下的大量实验表明，ANFI在各种邻居分布下均优于现有基于邻居的方法和重排序方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模噪声邻居的负面影响并采用自适应加权策略，ANFI有效解决了现有方法在噪声邻居环境下性能退化的问题，实现了更鲁棒的行人重识别。

Original Abstract: In person re-identification, neighbor-based methods have achieved significant success by interacting with neighbor samples to obtain more robust representations. However, existing methods rely only on affinity relations, causing their success to depend heavily on the reliability of selected neighbors. We find that affinity-only interaction often fails in challenging scenarios due to the inevitable presence of noisy neighbors. To enable effective interactions under noisy neighborhoods, we revisit neighbor-based methods under distinct reliability conditions and propose a novel Adaptive Neighbor Feature Interaction (ANFI) method. The core idea of ANFI is to account for negative effects from noisy neighbors, allowing samples to remain distinguishable from false positive neighbors. Unlike existing methods, ANFI models not only affinity relations but also discrepancy relations, and employs sample-wise adaptive weighting for these two types of relations. Given that capturing negative effects from noisy neighbors differs significantly from traditional relation learning, we derive discrepancy relations from a new neighborhood similarity, which provides more information than pairwise similarity. In addition, we propose Noisy Relation Supervision (NRS) to train ANFI, gradually injecting robustness to noisy relations into the model. Extensive experiments conducted under standard, cross-modal, and cross-domain settings, including comparisons with neighbor-based methods and re-ranking methods, demonstrate the superiority of our method across various neighbor distributions.

中文摘要翻译: 在行人重识别中，基于邻居的方法通过与邻居样本交互以获得更鲁棒的特征表示，取得了显著成功。然而，现有方法仅依赖亲和关系，其成功高度依赖于所选邻居的可靠性。我们发现，由于噪声邻居的不可避免存在，纯亲和关系交互在挑战性场景中经常失效。为了在噪声邻居环境下实现有效交互，我们重新审视了不同可靠性条件下基于邻居的方法，并提出了一种新颖的自适应邻居特征交互（ANFI）方法。ANFI的核心思想是考虑噪声邻居的负面影响，使样本能够与假阳性邻居保持可区分性。与现有方法不同，ANFI不仅建模亲和关系，还建模差异关系，并对这两种关系采用样本级自适应加权。鉴于捕获噪声邻居的负面影响与传统关系学习有显著差异，我们从一种新的邻居相似度中推导出差异关系，该相似度比 pairwise 相似度提供更多信息。此外，我们提出噪声关系监督（NRS）来训练ANFI，逐步向模型注入对噪声关系的鲁棒性。在标准、跨模态和跨域设置下进行的大量实验，包括与基于邻居的方法和重排序方法的比较，证明了我们的方法在各种邻居分布下的优越性。

</details>


### [40] [Bi-Level Collaborative Learning for Few-Shot Scribble-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25432)
*Xiang-Xiang Su,Yufan Ye,Yihang Zheng,Min Gan,Guang-Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双层协作学习框架，用于解决少量涂鸦标注下的医学图像分割问题，通过上层可学习超像素模型提供结构先验，下层分割模型反馈语义信息，双向交互显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 涂鸦标注虽然比像素级标注成本低，但在真实临床场景中涂鸦标注样本仍然有限，导致稀疏监督和样本稀缺的双重挑战，使模型缺乏完整区域恢复和精确边界描绘所需的结构证据。

Method: 提出双层协作学习框架：上层引入可学习超像素模型，提供区域结构先验并执行基于超像素的区域级伪标签传播及空间先验引导的过滤策略生成密集伪标签；下层分割模型在当前超像素指导下学习解剖语义，并反馈给上层以驱动其学习更符合分割任务的区域结构表示，上下层双向交互协同学习。

Result: 在ACDC和Prostate数据集上的少样本涂鸦监督设置下，该方法显著优于现有的最先进涂鸦监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过上下层之间的双向交互与协作学习，该框架有效缓解了稀疏监督和标注样本稀缺问题，在少样本涂鸦监督的医学图像分割任务中取得了优异性能。

Original Abstract: Scribble annotations offer an efficient alternative to costly pixel-wise labeling for medical image segmentation, yet in real clinical scenarios, scribble-annotated samples are often still limited, imposing the dual challenges of sparse supervision and annotated sample scarcity. These compounded constraints severely deprive models of the structural evidence needed for complete region recovery and precise boundary delineation. To break this bottleneck, we propose a bi-level collaborative learning framework for few-shot scribble-supervised medical image segmentation. Specifically, an upper-level learnable superpixel model is introduced to provide region-structural priors for lower-level segmentation, while superpixel-based region-wise pseudo-label propagation and a spatial-prior-guided filtering strategy are performed to generate reliable dense pseudo-labels for segmentation learning. Meanwhile, the anatomical semantics learned by the lower-level segmentation model under the guidance of the current superpixels are fed back to the upper level, further driving it to learn region-structural representations better aligned with the segmentation task. Through bidirectional interaction and collaborative learning between the upper and lower levels, the proposed framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art scribble-supervised methods on the ACDC and Prostate datasets under the few-shot scribble-supervised setting.

中文摘要翻译: 涂鸦标注为医学图像分割提供了一种高效的替代方案，避免了昂贵的像素级标注，但在真实临床场景中，涂鸦标注样本通常仍然有限，带来了稀疏监督和标注样本稀缺的双重挑战。这些复合约束严重剥夺了模型进行完整区域恢复和精确边界描绘所需的结构证据。为突破这一瓶颈，我们提出了一种面向少样本涂鸦监督医学图像分割的双层协作学习框架。具体而言，我们在上层引入了一个可学习的超像素模型，为下层分割提供区域结构先验，同时执行基于超像素的区域级伪标签传播和空间先验引导的过滤策略，为分割学习生成可靠的密集伪标签。与此同时，下层分割模型在当前超像素指导下学习到的解剖语义被反馈回上层，进一步驱动上层学习与分割任务更对齐的区域结构表示。通过上下层之间的双向交互与协作学习，所提出的框架在ACDC和Prostate数据集上的少样本涂鸦监督设置下，显著优于现有的最先进涂鸦监督方法。

</details>


### [41] [Reading Legends on Ancient Coins: An Object Detection Approach for Character Recognition on a Novel Roman Republican Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25455)
*Hafeez Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文采用YOLOv7等深度学习目标检测方法，对古罗马共和时期钱币上的文字铭文进行图像字符识别，在包含5,654张图像、38,808个标注的自建数据集上取得了90.4%的mAP50最佳性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 古钱币上的铭文对于确定其时代和发行者至关重要，但这些铭文存在放置不统一、铸造工艺原始、磨损严重等高度变化，加之光照、方向和尺度等成像条件不一致，传统识别方法效果不佳，因此需要基于深度学习的自动字符识别方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含5,654张罗马共和时期钱币图像、21个字符类别、共38,808个标注的大规模数据集；采用YOLOv3、v4、v5、v7、v8等多种YOLO系列目标检测模型进行字符识别。

Result: YOLOv7-Large取得了最佳性能，mAP50达到90.4%；YOLOv7-Extended和YOLOv7-xl分别达到90.2%和90.1%。

Conclusion: 基于YOLO的深度学习目标检测方法能够有效识别古罗马钱币上的铭文字符，YOLOv7系列模型表现最佳，验证了该方法在古钱币字符识别任务中的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: When it comes to the proper classification of ancient coins with respect to their time and issuer, the textual inscriptions on these coins, also known as legends, are of paramount importance. These legends consist of alphabets or characters still used in English. This paper addresses image based character recognition on ancient Roman Republican coins via a deep learning based object detection strategy. However, legends on these coins pose high variation due to non-uniform placement, primitive inscription techniques, and wear and tear. Additional challenges include inconsistent imaging conditions such as illumination, orientation, and scale. To accommodate these, we gathered a novel large-scale dataset of 5,654 Roman Republican coin images, manually annotated with 21 character labels, totaling 38,808 annotations. For recognition, we use You Only Look Once (YOLO) variants: YOLOv3, v4, v5, v7, and v8. YOLOv7-Large achieves the best mAP50 of 90.4%, followed by YOLOv7-Extended and YOLOv7-xl with 90.2% and 90.1%, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 对于根据时间和发行者对古钱币进行正确分类而言，钱币上的文字铭文（即legend）至关重要。这些铭文由英语中仍在使用的字母或字符构成。本文通过基于深度学习的目标检测策略，解决古罗马共和时期钱币上的图像字符识别问题。然而，由于铭文放置不统一、铸造技术原始以及磨损等因素，这些钱币上的铭文呈现出高度的变化性。此外，光照、方向和尺度等不一致的成像条件也带来了额外挑战。为应对这些问题，我们收集了一个包含5,654张罗马共和时期钱币图像的新颖大规模数据集，人工标注了21个字符类别，共计38,808个标注。在识别方面，我们使用了YOLO系列变体：YOLOv3、v4、v5、v7和v8。其中，YOLOv7-Large取得了最佳mAP50，达90.4%；YOLOv7-Extended和YOLOv7-xl紧随其后，分别达到90.2%和90.1%。

</details>


### [42] [DensFiLM: Density-Conditioned Video Saliency for Crowd Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25465)
*Anis Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DensFiLM通过轻量级特征调制层实现密度条件化的视频显著性预测，仅增加约10万参数即在人群视频显著性任务上超越ACLNet 14%以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频显著性模型在人群场景中采用单一的注视策略，忽略了注意力随人群密度发生的系统性变化——稀疏场景倾向于追踪个体，而密集场景转向集体运动和场景级地标。

Method: 在Video Swin Transformer的瓶颈处插入轻量级特征线性调制（FiLM）层，通过学习密度嵌入生成通道级的缩放和平移参数，使解码器能够根据密度状态重建显著性图。模块仅增加约10万参数。

Result: 在CrowdFix数据集上，DensFiLM达到NSS 1.434和CC 0.517，相比ACLNet分别提升14.7%和14.9%；密度预测条件化性能与真实标签相当；中心先验消融实验中密度条件化带来NSS增益0.462。

Conclusion: 轻量级瓶颈条件化为人群视频显著性预测提供了比增加模型容量更有效的归纳偏置。

Original Abstract: Video saliency models typically apply a single fixation strategy across crowd scenes, despite systematic changes in attention with crowd density. Sparse scenes encourage tracking individuals, whereas dense scenes shift attention toward collective motion and scene-level landmarks. We introduce DensFiLM, a density-conditioned video saliency model that inserts a lightweight Feature-wise Linear Modulation layer at the bottleneck of a Video Swin Transformer. A learned density embedding produces channel-wise scale and shift parameters, allowing the decoder to reconstruct saliency from features selected for each density regime. The module adds only ~100K parameters and can use either CrowdFix density labels or the model's own density prediction. On CrowdFix, DensFiLM achieves mean NSS 1.434 and CC 0.517 over four seeds, improving over ACLNet by 14.7% and 14.9%, respectively, while predicted-density conditioning matches oracle-label performance. Ablations show that explicit RAFT optical flow and larger temporal and social-force extensions provide no further improvement in this setting. In a centre-prior-subtraction diagnostic, density conditioning yields an NSS gain of 0.462 over the unconditioned backbone, compared with 0.124 under standard evaluation. These results show that lightweight bottleneck conditioning provides a more effective inductive bias than increasing model capacity for crowd-video saliency. Our code is available at https://github.com/aniskhan25/crowdfix-saliency.

中文摘要翻译: 视频显著性模型通常在人群场景中采用单一的注视策略，尽管注意力会随人群密度发生系统性变化。稀疏场景倾向于追踪个体，而密集场景将注意力转向集体运动和场景级地标。我们提出了DensFiLM，一种密度条件化的视频显著性模型，它在Video Swin Transformer的瓶颈处插入了一个轻量级的特征线性调制层。通过学习得到的密度嵌入产生通道级的缩放和平移参数，使解码器能够从为每种密度状态选择的特征中重建显著性图。该模块仅增加约10万参数，既可以使用CrowdFix密度标签，也可以使用模型自身的密度预测。在CrowdFix数据集上，DensFiLM在四个随机种子下达到平均NSS 1.434和CC 0.517，相比ACLNet分别提升了14.7%和14.9%，而预测密度条件化的性能与真实标签相当。消融实验表明，显式的RAFT光流以及更大的时间和社会力扩展在此设置下没有带来进一步改进。在中心先验减去诊断实验中，密度条件化相对于无条件基线在标准评估下NSS增益为0.124，而在该诊断中达到0.462。这些结果表明，对于人群视频显著性任务，轻量级瓶颈条件化比增加模型容量能提供更有效的归纳偏置。我们的代码已开源：https://github.com/aniskhan25/crowdfix-saliency。

</details>


### [43] [Seen, Said, or Forgotten? A Causal Audit of Visual KV Memory Across Dialog Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25467)
*Hong Chen,Kang Chen,Yuxuan Fan,Bo Wang,Yubo Gao,Yuanlin Chu,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果视觉记忆审计（CVMA）框架，以评估多模态助手中视觉信息何时可以安全遗忘，发现当前注意力机制无法可靠预测未来有用性，安全遗忘主要依赖于低的未来视觉依赖或已陈述事实的文本化，而非低的当前注意力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力驱动的视觉键值驱逐策略假设当前不相关的证据未来仍然不重要，但未来问题是未知的，可能导致未来有用的视觉信息被错误丢弃。因此需要明确何时才能真正安全地遗忘视觉事实。

Method: 提出因果视觉记忆审计（CVMA），一种配对单预填充框架，通过控制变量法分别移除视觉区域、整张图像或先前助手文本，测试这些移除对后续回答的影响，从而量化各视觉要素的因果重要性。

Result: 在VisDial和ConvBench上，当前注意力对未来有用区域的排序甚至差于随机；边际效用控制实验表明存在大量选择空间未被利用。另外发现，助手文本的KV缓存可以替代已陈述事实的图像KV，但对未陈述事实的替代并不可靠。

Conclusion: 在测试的模型堆栈中，安全遗忘依赖于低的未来视觉依赖或事实的文本化表达，而非低的当前注意力分数。当前注意力不能作为未来有用性的可靠指标。

Original Abstract: Stateful multimodal assistants encode an image once but may answer questions about it many turns later. Attention-guided visual-KV eviction assumes that evidence irrelevant now will remain dispensable, although future questions are unknown. We ask when a visual fact is actually safe to forget and introduce the Causal Visual Memory Audit (CVMA), a paired single-prefill framework that tests what later answers lose when a visual region, the whole image, or prior assistant text becomes unavailable. On VisDial and ConvBench, current attention can rank future-useful regions worse than random even though a diagnostic marginal-utility control shows substantial selection headroom. Aggregate scores hide this failure when later turns do not need vision; controlled and stock-generated histories reveal a second escape route, in which assistant-text KV replaces image KV for facts already stated but not reliably for unstated facts. In the tested stacks, safe forgetting is supported by low future visual dependence or fact-specific verbalization---not by low current attention.

中文摘要翻译: 有状态的多模态助手会将图像编码一次，但可能在多个轮次之后才回答关于该图像的问题。注意力驱动的视觉键值驱逐策略假设当前不相关的证据在未来仍然不重要，尽管未来的问题未知。我们询问视觉事实何时才是真正安全的遗忘对象，并引入了因果视觉记忆审计（CVMA），这是一种配对的单预填充框架，通过测试当视觉区域、整张图像或先前助手文本变得不可用时，后续回答会损失什么。在VisDial和ConvBench上，尽管诊断性边际效用控制显示存在显著的选择空间，但当前的注意力机制对未来有用区域的排序甚至差于随机。当后续轮次不需要视觉时，聚合分数隐藏了这一失败；受控和历史生成的数据集揭示了第二条逃逸路径：对于已陈述的事实，助手文本的KV缓存可以替代图像KV，但对于未陈述的事实，这种替代并不可靠。在测试的模型堆栈中，安全遗忘依赖于低的未来视觉依赖或特定事实的文本化——而非低的当前注意力。

</details>


### [44] [Safety-Aware Cascaded Inference for Crop Damage Assessment with Controlled Error Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25468)
*José Thiéry Messigbédé Hagbe,Gani Kawsar Gounou,Songbian Karim Zimé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CascadeCropNet 两阶段级联架构，通过阈值校准控制召回率，在农业图像理赔中优先减少漏检（漏损成本远高于误报），在肯尼亚小农玉米田数据集上达到 Rec-Damaged=0.974，将漏检减少54%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于图像的农业保险中，漏检（受损农户未获赔付）的代价远高于误报（额外专家审核），但标准多分类器优化全局准确率，无法在推理时控制这一非对称成本结构。

Method: 两阶段级联架构：轻量级 Sentinel 模型进行二值健康状态分诊，超过校准损伤概率阈值 τ 的样本升级至专家 Expert 模型进行细粒度诊断；通过阈值选择实现部署时对安全-效率权衡的显式控制。

Result: 在 Eyes on the Ground 数据集（23,804 张肯尼亚小农玉米田图像）上，在 τ=0.5 时达到 Rec-Damaged=0.974，漏检最多减少 54%；评估校准后表征差距降至 F1-macro +0.008；输入退化时系统优先升级而非错误分类。

Conclusion: 级联架构可通过校准路由在可靠性优先于聚合准确率的场景中操作化安全导向的决策约束，但这些特性依赖于阈值校准和部署条件，在任意分布偏移下不构成保证。

Original Abstract: In picture-based agricultural insurance for smallholder farmers, missed damage detections carry substantially higher cost than false alarms: a farmer who sustained real losses receives no payout, while unnecessary expert review is operationally costly but reversible. Standard multi-class classifiers optimize global accuracy but provide no mechanism to operationalize or control this asymmetric cost structure at inference time. We propose CascadeCropNet, a two-stage cascade architecture calibrated to satisfy a target recall constraint (Rec-Damaged >= 0.95) through threshold selection. A lightweight Sentinel model performs binary health triage; samples exceeding a calibrated damage probability threshold tau are escalated to a specialist Expert model for fine-grained diagnosis. This design provides explicit, deployment-time control over the safety-efficiency trade-off without retraining. Evaluated on the Eyes on the Ground dataset (23,804 images from Kenyan smallholder maize farms), the cascade achieves Rec-Damaged = 0.974 at tau = 0.5, reducing missed damage cases by up to 54% relative to a flat baseline. Under evaluation alignment, the representational gap reduces to +0.008 F1-macro, confirming the contribution is architectural rather than representational. Under input degradation, the system prioritizes escalation over confident misclassification, reflecting error containment through architectural isolation rather than intrinsic model robustness. These results demonstrate that cascade architectures can operationalize safety-oriented decision constraints through calibrated routing in settings where reliability matters more than aggregate accuracy. These properties depend on threshold calibration and deployment conditions and do not constitute guarantees under arbitrary distribution shift.

中文摘要翻译: 在以图像为基础的小农农业保险中，漏检成本远高于误报：遭受实际损失的农户得不到赔付，而不必要的专家审核虽然带来运营成本但可逆转。标准的多类分类器优化全局准确率，但无法在推理时操作化或控制这种非对称成本结构。我们提出 CascadeCropNet，一种两阶段级联架构，通过阈值选择校准以满足目标召回率约束（受损召回率 ≥ 0.95）。轻量级 Sentinel 模型执行二值健康状态分诊；超过校准损伤概率阈值 τ 的样本被升级至专家 Expert 模型进行细粒度诊断。该设计无需重新训练即可在部署时显式控制安全-效率权衡。在 Eyes on the Ground 数据集（23,804 张肯尼亚小农玉米田图像）上的评估表明，在 τ=0.5 时级联达到受损召回率 = 0.974，相较平坦基线最多减少 54% 的漏检。经过评估校准后，表征差距降至 F1 宏平均 +0.008，证实贡献来自架构而非表征。在输入退化情况下，系统优先升级而非自信地错误分类，反映出通过架构隔离而非内在模型鲁棒性实现错误遏制。这些结果表明，在可靠性比聚合准确率更重要的场景中，级联架构可以通过校准路由来操作化安全导向的决策约束。这些性质依赖于阈值校准和部署条件，在任意分布偏移下不构成保证。

</details>


### [45] [Agentic AI in medicine: architectures, applications, evaluation, and challenges for clinical translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25489)
*Zheng Tong,Yang Liu,Wanshu Fan,Jing Qin,Zhongbin Han,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Xiaofeng Liu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对1,649篇文献的系统筛选与557项研究的纳入分析，系统综述了医学领域中基于大语言模型和多模态基础模型的智能体AI的现状，发现其仍以公共基准和模拟环境为主，在过程可靠性、可追溯性、安全性、外部有效性等方面评估不足，临床转化需更清晰的定义、可复现的评估和前瞻性验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学AI正从孤立预测向多步骤临床任务发展，但智能体AI在医学中的范围尚未明确，现有评估方式与临床需求不匹配，因此需要系统梳理该领域的研究现状与评估实践。

Method: 采用范围综述（scoping review）结合系统性证据映射方法，对五个电子来源进行检索，筛选1,649条可导出记录，最终纳入557项符合预先设定标准的研究，涵盖目标导向任务执行、工具使用、外部资源交互、基于反馈的改进或多智能体协作。

Result: 纳入研究包括使用外部工具的单智能体、基于检索和外部知识的工作流、多模态智能体以及多智能体系统，应用于医学问答、图像解读、电子健康记录分析、药物安全与临床试验预测。证据基础仍以公共基准、模拟环境、回顾性数据集和小规模专家评估为主，过程可靠性、证据可追溯性、不确定性、安全性、工作流影响和外部有效性的评估较为不足。

Conclusion: 临床转化依赖于更清晰的定义、可复现的评估、可审计的监督、可互操作的系统设计以及在真实临床工作流中的前瞻性验证。

Original Abstract: Large language models and multimodal foundation models are enabling medical artificial intelligence (AI) systems to move beyond isolated prediction and undertake multistep clinical tasks that require planning, tool use, memory, iterative correction, and coordination among specialized agents. However, the scope of agentic AI in medicine remains unsettled, and current evaluation practices are not yet aligned with the requirements of clinical use. We conducted a scoping review with systematic evidence mapping across five electronic sources, screened 1,649 exportable records, and provisionally included 557 unique studies that met predefined criteria for goal-directed task execution, tool use, interaction with external resources, feedback-based refinement, or multi-agent collaboration. The included studies describe single agents that use external tools, workflows supported by retrieval and external knowledge, multimodal agents, and multi-agent systems applied to medical question answering, image interpretation, electronic health record analysis, drug safety, and clinical trial prediction. The evidence base remains dominated by public benchmarks, simulated settings, retrospective datasets, and small-scale expert evaluation. Process reliability, evidence traceability, uncertainty, safety, workflow impact, and external validity are evaluated less consistently. Clinical translation will depend on clearer definitions, reproducible evaluation, auditable oversight, interoperable system design, and prospective validation in real-world clinical workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型和多模态基础模型正使医学人工智能系统超越孤立的预测任务，执行需要规划、工具使用、记忆、迭代修正以及专门智能体之间协调的多步骤临床任务。然而，智能体AI在医学中的范围仍不明确，当前的评估实践尚未与临床使用需求对齐。我们在五个电子来源中进行了范围综述与系统性证据映射，筛选了1,649条可导出记录，并初步纳入了557项符合预先设定标准（目标导向任务执行、工具使用、与外部资源交互、基于反馈的改进或多智能体协作）的独特研究。纳入的研究描述了使用外部工具的单智能体、由检索和外部知识支持的工作流、多模态智能体以及应用于医学问答、图像解读、电子健康记录分析、药物安全和临床试验预测的多智能体系统。证据基础仍以公共基准、模拟环境、回顾性数据集和小规模专家评估为主。过程可靠性、证据可追溯性、不确定性、安全性、工作流影响和外部有效性的评估较为不足。临床转化将依赖于更清晰的定义、可复现的评估、可审计的监督、可互操作的系统设计以及在真实临床工作流中的前瞻性验证。

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Counts: A Distributional Robustness Margin For Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25497)
*Clément Grisi,Jeroen van der Laak,Geert Litjens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现病理基础模型的鲁棒性指数(RI)存在深层缺陷（忽略距离信息、池化设计掩盖样本异质性），并提出新的样本级鲁棒性度量CRoMa（交叉混杂鲁棒性边界），将鲁棒性建模为队列范围的边界分布而非单一分数，从而更有效地评估和选择模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有病理基础模型的鲁棒性评估方法——鲁棒性指数(RI)存在根本性缺陷：其基于计数的公式丢弃了距离信息，且采用池化的固定邻域设计掩盖了样本层面的异质性，导致仅能评估模型依赖的样本子集，无法准确反映模型对非生物变异（组织制备、染色、扫描差异）的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa)，一种样本级鲁棒性度量方法。CRoMa直接比较每个样本到跨混杂因子生物学匹配样本的距离与同一混杂因子下生物学干扰样本的距离，将鲁棒性重新定义为队列范围的边界分布而非单一池化分数，从而保留样本级异质性信息。

Result: 在3个基准上评估20个瓦片级编码器和第4个基准上评估4个全片级编码器的冻结表征。中位数CRoMa排名在不同数据集中基本一致，但底层分布揭示了显著的模型内异质性：每个瓦片编码器都存在混杂因子主导的下尾分布，其普遍性和严重性在不同模型间差异显著。更高的CRoMa与监督适应后更小的捷径学习导致的性能下降相关。

Conclusion: CRoMa将表征几何转化为分布式的鲁棒性读数，能够预测下游捷径学习的易感性，为鲁棒性评估和模型选择提供了原则性基础。不同模型的鲁棒性分布特征将模型选择框架化为典型鲁棒性与下尾鲁棒性之间的帕累托权衡。

Original Abstract: Pathology foundation models are approaching clinical deployment, yet remain vulnerable to systematic non-biological variation across centres. Differences in tissue preparation, staining and scanning are strongly encoded in their representations, enabling shortcut learning and weakening generalisation across cohorts and institutions. The Robustness Index (RI) quantifies whether local representation geometry is dominated by biology or by non-biological variation, but its count-based formulation discards distance information. We show that adding distance weights changes little because the deeper limitation lies in RI's pooled, fixed-neighbourhood design, which obscures sample-level heterogeneity and effectively evaluates only a model-dependent subset of samples. We introduce the Cross-confounder Robustness Margin (CRoMa), a sample-resolved measure that directly compares distances to cross-confounder biological matches and same-confounder biological distractors. CRoMa recasts robustness as a cohort-wide margin distribution rather than a single pooled score. We evaluated frozen representations from 20 tile-level encoders across three benchmarks and 4 slide-level encoders on a fourth. Rankings by median CRoMa were broadly consistent across datasets, while the underlying distributions revealed substantial within-model heterogeneity. Every tile encoder retained a confounder-dominated lower tail, whose prevalence and severity varied markedly across models. These distinct robustness profiles frame model selection as a Pareto trade-off between typical and lower-tail robustness. Higher CRoMa was also associated with smaller shortcut-induced performance drops after supervised adaptation. By turning representation geometry into a distributional robustness readout that anticipates downstream shortcut susceptibility, CRoMa provides a principled basis for robustness assessment and model selection.

中文摘要翻译: 病理基础模型正接近临床部署，但依然容易受到各中心间系统性非生物变异的影响。组织制备、染色和扫描的差异在其表征中被强烈编码，导致捷径学习并削弱跨队列和跨机构的泛化能力。鲁棒性指数（RI）量化了局部表征几何是由生物学主导还是由非生物变异主导，但其基于计数的公式丢弃了距离信息。我们证明添加距离权重几乎没有改变这一状况，因为更深的限制在于RI池化的、固定邻域的设计，这种设计掩盖了样本层面的异质性，实际上仅评估了模型依赖的样本子集。我们引入了交叉混杂鲁棒性边界（CRoMa），这是一种样本级度量方法，直接比较到跨混杂因子生物学匹配样本的距离与同一混杂因子下生物学干扰样本的距离。CRoMa将鲁棒性重新定义为队列范围的边界分布，而非单一池化分数。我们在三个基准上评估了20个瓦片级编码器的冻结表征，并在第四个基准上评估了4个全片级编码器。中位数CRoMa的排名在不同数据集中基本一致，而其底层分布揭示了显著的模型内异质性。每个瓦片编码器都保留了一个混杂因子主导的下尾，其普遍性和严重性在不同模型间差异显著。这些独特的鲁棒性特征将模型选择框架化为典型鲁棒性与下尾鲁棒性之间的帕累托权衡。更高的CRoMa也与监督适应后更小的捷径诱导性能下降相关。通过将表征几何转化为能够预测下游捷径易感性的分布式鲁棒性读数，CRoMa为鲁棒性评估和模型选择提供了原则性基础。

</details>


### [47] [Group Equivariant Diffusion for Anomaly Detection in Computational Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25503)
*Swarnadip Chatterjee,Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种D4-等变扩散框架，用于全切片图像的细胞学异常检测，通过引入旋转和反射对称性，实现了更稳定和准确的异常细胞检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 细胞学图像中恶性细胞稀少、异质性强且标注数据稀缺，现有无监督异常检测方法（如GAN和扩散模型）针对器官级成像设计，忽略了细胞级信号中旋转和翻转变换不应改变诊断类别的生物学不变性，导致变换依赖的重建和不稳定的异常评分。

Method: 提出D4-等变扩散框架，通过D4-等变U-Net在架构上强制执行旋转和反射对称性，并在推理阶段通过等变噪声耦合和帧平均实现等变性，从而生成变换一致的伪健康重建。

Result: 在两个公开的细胞学数据集（骨髓和外周血涂片）上，D4-等变扩散模型相比非等变生成基线、深度一类分类和多实例学习方法，实现了更高的AUC和Top K异常细胞召回率，同时显著降低了旋转和翻转下的评分方差。

Conclusion: D4-等变扩散框架通过对齐细胞学图像的生物学不变性，显著提升了异常检测的稳定性和准确性。

Original Abstract: Computational cytology on whole-slide images is challenging because malignant cells are rare, heterogeneous, and annotated slides are scarce. Anomaly detection frameworks can be trained on normal slide-negative patches and then applied at test time to flag abnormal patches in held-out slides. Most unsupervised anomaly detection approaches including generative ones (GAN-based and diffusion-based), are tuned to organ-level imaging and require large curated datasets. In cytology the signal is cell-centric: rotating or flipping a single-cell patch does not change its diagnostic class, yet standard diffusion models treat transformed views as distinct inputs, leading to transformation-dependent reconstructions and unstable anomaly scores. We propose a D4-equivariant diffusion framework that enforces rotation and reflection symmetry both architecturally, via a D4-equivariant U-Net, and at inference, via equivariant noise coupling and (optionally) frame averaging. This alignment with biological invariance yields transformation-consistent pseudo-healthy reconstructions and more stable anomaly ranking under symmetry. On two publicly available cytology datasets of bone marrow and peripheral blood smears, our D4-equivariant diffusion models achieve higher AUC and retrieve more abnormal cells in the top K predictions than non-equivariant generative baselines, a deep one-class, and a multiple instance learning based method, while substantially reducing score variance across rotations and flips. Code is available at https://swchmida.github.io/D4diffCyto/.

中文摘要翻译: 在全切片图像上进行计算细胞学具有挑战性，因为恶性细胞稀少、异质性强，且标注切片稀缺。异常检测框架可以在正常切片的负样本块上训练，然后在测试时应用于检测预留切片中的异常块。大多数无监督异常检测方法（包括基于GAN和基于扩散的生成式方法）都是针对器官级成像调优的，需要大规模精选数据集。在细胞学中，信号是以细胞为中心的：旋转或翻转单个细胞块不会改变其诊断类别，然而标准扩散模型将变换后的视角视为不同的输入，导致依赖于变换的重建和不稳定的异常评分。我们提出了一种D4-等变扩散框架，该框架在架构上通过D4-等变U-Net强制执行旋转和反射对称性，并在推理阶段通过等变噪声耦合和（可选的）帧平均来实现等变性。这种与生物学不变性的对齐产生了变换一致的伪健康重建，以及在对称性下更稳定的异常排序。在两个公开的细胞学数据集（骨髓和外周血涂片）上，我们的D4-等变扩散模型相比非等变生成基线、深度一类分类方法和多实例学习方法，实现了更高的AUC并在Top K预测中检索到更多的异常细胞，同时显著降低了旋转和翻转下的评分方差。代码可在https://swchmida.github.io/D4diffCyto/获取。

</details>


### [48] [I2VShield: An Efficient Proactive Defense Framework against DiT-based Image-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25522)
*Yimao Guo,Zuomin Qu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出I2VShield，一种针对DiT架构图像转视频模型的黑盒防御方法，通过文本自适应对抗扰动生成和非目标多模态注意力破坏攻击，以较低计算成本有效破坏生成视频的时空一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对I2V模型的主动防御方法主要依赖基于梯度的对抗攻击，要求防御者拥有大显存GPU，计算开销大且难以广泛部署。

Method: (1) 文本自适应扰动生成框架，融合对抗学习以降低计算开销并保持视觉不可感知性；(2) 非目标多模态注意力破坏攻击，利用DiT模型内在脆弱性最大化内部注意力特征与干净状态的偏离。

Result: 在多种数据集和主流DiT-based I2V模型上实现了高竞争力的保护性能，尤其能有效破坏时空连贯性，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 提出了一种低成本、高效的主动防御方法I2VShield，能有效对抗DiT架构的I2V模型滥用。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of video generation models has led to the increasing misuse of image-to-video (I2V) models. Although substantial progress has been made in detecting AI-generated videos, proactive defenses against I2V models remain underexplored. In particular, current proactive defenses against I2V models predominantly rely on gradient-based adversarial attacks, which require defenders to possess GPUs with substantial memory resources (VRAM) to generate adversarial examples. To address this issue, we propose I2VShield, a privacy protection method based on generative adversarial attacks tailored to Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based I2V models. The proposed method primarily consists of two components: (1) a text-adaptive perturbation generation framework integrating adversarial learning to mitigate computational overhead while maintaining visual imperceptibility; and (2) an untargeted Multimodal Attention Disruption (MAD) attack that exploits the inherent vulnerabilities of DiT-based I2V models, maximizing the deviation of the internal attention features from their clean states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves highly competitive protection performance across various datasets and mainstream DiT-based I2V models, particularly in disrupting spatiotemporal coherence, while substantially reducing computational costs.

中文摘要翻译: 视频生成模型的快速发展导致图像转视频模型的滥用日益严重。尽管在检测AI生成视频方面取得了显著进展，但针对I2V模型的主动防御仍未被充分探索。特别是，当前针对I2V模型的主动防御主要依赖基于梯度的对抗攻击，这要求防御者拥有大显存GPU来生成对抗样本。为解决该问题，我们提出I2VShield——一种针对基于扩散变换器架构的I2V模型的生成式对抗攻击隐私保护方法。该方法主要由两部分组成：(1) 融合对抗学习的文本自适应扰动生成框架，在保持视觉不可感知性的同时降低计算开销；(2) 非目标多模态注意力破坏攻击，利用基于DiT的I2V模型的内在脆弱性，最大化内部注意力特征与干净状态之间的偏差。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种数据集和主流基于DiT的I2V模型上实现了极具竞争力的保护性能，尤其在破坏时空一致性方面表现突出，同时大幅降低了计算成本。

</details>


### [49] [ReLATE: Reliability-Guided Evidence Fusion for Robust UAV--Satellite cross-view Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25524)
*Haochen Jiang,Jialei Pan,Yuzhe Sun,Zhe Dong,Lecheng Ren,Yanfeng Gu,Tianzhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UAVSat-Deg——一个面向无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位的大规模鲁棒性基准，覆盖27种退化类型和超过1170万张退化测试图像；同时提出了ReLATE方法，通过自适应令牌证据调节实现可靠性感知的特征融合，在退化场景下达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位方法在干净图像上表现优异，但在实际飞行中常受恶劣天气、光照变化、平台运动、传感器噪声和压缩等退化影响，现有方法在这些退化下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出ReLATE（Reliable Evidence Learning with Adaptive Token Evidence Regulation），该方法对视觉令牌估计结构平滑的可靠性场，聚合可信的局部证据，并自适应地将可靠证据融入查询导出的表示中；再将经过调节的查询表示与CLS令牌和GeM池化分支结合，形成最终的跨视角描述子。

Result: 在UAVSat-Deg基准上，ReLATE在退化测试集和检索方向上均取得了最佳的平均退化性能，同时在干净图像上保持了具有竞争力的精度。基准测试显示现有方法存在显著的鲁棒性差距，尤其是在严重和复合退化下。

Conclusion: 本文提出的UAVSat-Deg基准系统和ReLATE方法有效填补了无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位在退化场景下的研究空白，为实际部署提供了重要参考。

Original Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-satellite cross-view geo-localization matches UAV images against satellite imagery and has achieved impressive accuracy on clean (non-degraded) image benchmarks. In real-world flights, however, UAV observations are frequently affected by adverse weather, illumination changes, platform motion, sensor noise, and compression, while the robustness of existing methods under such degradations remains largely unexamined. In this paper, we present UAVSat-Deg, a large-scale robustness benchmark for degraded UAV-satellite geo-localization, comprising University-1652-Deg and SUES-200-Deg. UAVSat-Deg covers 27 corruption types, including 19 core and 8 compound corruptions, at three severity levels, supports bidirectional drone-to-satellite and satellite-to-drone retrieval as well as multi-height UAV acquisition, and contains more than 11.7 million pre-generated corrupted test images. Benchmarking representative methods under this protocol reveals substantial robustness gaps, particularly under severe and compound corruptions. To address this problem, we propose ReLATE, a Reliable Evidence Learning framework with Adaptive Token Evidence Regulation, which realizes reliability-adaptive feature fusion during descriptor construction. ReLATE estimates a structure-smoothed reliability field over visual tokens, aggregates trustworthy local evidence, and adaptively integrates it into query-derived representations; the regulated query representations are then combined with the CLS-token and GeM-pooled branches to form the final cross-view descriptor. Across both test sets and retrieval directions, ReLATE achieves the best average corrupted-test performance among the compared methods while maintaining competitive accuracy on clean images. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/JHC626/ReLATE.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机-卫星跨视角地理定位将无人机图像与卫星图像进行匹配，在干净（无退化）图像基准上取得了令人瞩目的精度。然而，在实际飞行中，无人机观测常受恶劣天气、光照变化、平台运动、传感器噪声和压缩的影响，而现有方法在这些退化条件下的鲁棒性仍未得到充分检验。本文提出了UAVSat-Deg——一个面向退化无人机-卫星地理定位的大规模鲁棒性基准，包含University-1652-Deg和SUES-200-Deg两个子集。UAVSat-Deg涵盖27种退化类型（包括19种核心退化和8种复合退化），三个严重等级，支持双向无人机到卫星和卫星到无人机检索以及多高度无人机采集，包含超过1170万张预生成的退化测试图像。在该协议下对代表性方法的基准测试揭示了显著的鲁棒性差距，特别是在严重和复合退化下。为解决这一问题，我们提出了ReLATE——一种具有自适应令牌证据调节的可靠证据学习框架，在描述子构建过程中实现可靠性自适应的特征融合。ReLATE对视觉令牌估计结构平滑的可靠性场，聚合可信的局部证据，并自适应地将其融入查询导出的表示中；经过调节的查询表示再与CLS令牌和GeM池化分支结合，形成最终的跨视角描述子。在两个测试集和两种检索方向上，ReLATE在退化测试上的平均性能均优于对比方法，同时在干净图像上保持了具有竞争力的精度。代码和数据集将在https://github.com/JHC626/ReLATE公开。

</details>


### [50] [Argus-Unified: Towards A Compact and Economical Unified Model for Image Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25527)
*Weiming Zhuang,Jiabo Huang,Jingtao Li,Zhizhong Li,Chen Chen,Sina Sajadmanesh,Lingjuan Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Argus-Unified 提出了一种低计算成本、低数据需求的统一多模态模型，通过利用预训练视觉语言模型和混合视觉标记，在理解和生成任务上取得了SOTA或竞争性表现，成本仅为同类模型的约1/10。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的统一视觉理解与生成模型面临计算和数据需求巨大、视觉特征冲突的挑战，亟需一种低成本、高效率的统一多模态建模方案。

Method: 提出Argus-Unified模型，利用预训练VLM提供多模态先验；引入混合视觉标记，保留连续标记用于理解，从冻结的统一视觉编码器中学习离散标记用于生成；两阶段训练：第一阶段学习量化器和图像解码器，第二阶段训练LLM进行统一多模态建模。

Result: 仅使用15.6M数据和约$2,000成本，在GQA、POPE、VQAv2上达到SOTA多模态理解，生成质量与Janus/Janus-Pro等专用模型持平，成本降低约10倍，数据减少约5倍。

Conclusion: 统一多模态模型可以在经济成本下实现强大的理解和生成性能，Argus-Unified可作为降低统一模型开发门槛的实用基线。

Original Abstract: Unifying visual understanding and generation in one model holds immense promise, but remains challenging and expensive due to heavy compute and data demands and conflicts between the visual features needed for these two capabilities. To address these challenges, we present Argus-Unified, a compact, effective and unified multimodal model built with low demand on computation and data. Instead of aligning modalities from scratch, Argus-Unified effectively leverages pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) that provide strong multimodal priors. Specifically, we introduce hybrid visual tokens that preserve continuous tokens for understanding while learning discrete tokens for generation from a frozen unified vision encoder. Our training pipeline includes two stages: the first stage learns a quantizer and image decoder on top of the frozen vision encoder, the second stage trains the LLM initialized from a pretrained VLM for the unified multimodal modeling. Using by far the least amount of data (15.6M) and the lowest cost (~$2,000), we demonstrate that unified multimodal models can be trained economically while achieving strong performance in both understanding and generation. Notably, our model attains state-of-the-art multimodal understanding on GQA, POPE, and VQAv2, and competitive generation quality compared to models with dedicated vision encoders (e.g., Janus, Janus-Pro), all at ~10x lower cost and with ~5x less data. We envision Argus-Unified as a useful baseline that lowers the development barrier for unified models.

中文摘要翻译: 将视觉理解与生成统一到单一模型中具有巨大潜力，但由于计算和数据需求庞大，以及这两种能力所需的视觉特征之间存在冲突，这一目标仍然具有挑战性且成本高昂。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了Argus-Unified，一个紧凑、高效且统一的多模态模型，其对计算和数据的需求较低。Argus-Unified并非从头开始对齐模态，而是有效利用了提供强大多模态先验的预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）。具体而言，我们引入了混合视觉标记，在保留用于理解的连续标记的同时，从冻结的统一视觉编码器中学习用于生成的离散标记。我们的训练流程包括两个阶段：第一阶段在冻结的视觉编码器之上学习量化器和图像解码器，第二阶段训练从预训练VLM初始化的LLM以进行统一的多模态建模。我们以迄今为止最少的数据量（15.6M）和最低的成本（约$2,000），证明了统一多模态模型可以在经济条件下进行训练，同时在理解和生成方面均取得强劲性能。值得注意的是，我们的模型在GQA、POPE和VQAv2上实现了最先进的多模态理解性能，生成质量与配备专用视觉编码器的模型（如Janus、Janus-Pro）相当，而成本降低约10倍，数据量减少约5倍。我们期望Argus-Unified能够作为一个有用的基线，降低统一模型的开发门槛。

</details>


### [51] [Visual prompt engineering for video models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25537)
*Robert Geirhos,Yuxuan Li,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Neha Kalibhat,Zi Wang,Mani Malek,Oyvind Tafjord,Kevin Swersky,Been Kim,Priyank Jaini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉提示工程（VIPE），通过自动修改任务图像（如将抽象草图转为逼真图像）来提升视频基础模型的视觉推理性能，实验证明该方法比文本提示工程和测试时缩放更有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在基础模型时代，提示工程对语言模型至关重要。随着视频模型成为视觉任务的基础模型，作者希望探究视觉提示工程是否能像文本提示工程提升语言模型一样，通过自动修改任务图像来提升视频模型的视觉推理性能。

Method: 视觉提示工程（VIPE）：对于给定的视觉推理任务图像（如抽象草图），使用图像编辑模型自动将其转换为更具信息量的版本（如逼真图像），从而增强视频模型对任务的理解和推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，视觉提示工程（VIPE）在多个视觉推理任务上一致提升了视频模型的性能；对于视频模型而言，视觉提示工程甚至比传统的文本提示工程和测试时缩放更有效。

Conclusion: 正如文本提示工程能系统性地提升语言模型性能，视觉提示工程作为一种简单且计算高效的方法，可以有效提升视频模型在视觉推理任务上的表现。

Original Abstract: In the age of foundation models, a model is only as good as its prompt. For this reason, prompt engineering has become an essential technique for improving language model performance. Since video models are currently becoming foundation models for visual tasks (e.g., visual reasoning), we here ask whether they similarly benefit from visual prompt engineering: automatically modifying the task image to improve model performance. For example, for a visual physics reasoning task ("Where does the ball land, after passing a set of obstacles?"), an abstract sketch-like scene can be turned into a photorealistic version with a simple call to an image editing model. We find that visual prompt engineering, or VIPE for short, improves video reasoning performance across tasks. In fact, for video models, visual prompt engineering can be even more effective than classic text-based prompt engineering or test-time scaling. Ultimately, just as text-based prompt engineering systematically improves language model performance, visual prompt engineering can serve as a simple, compute-efficient approach to elicit better visual reasoning performance from video models. Example videos on our project page at https://visual-prompt-engineering.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 在基础模型时代，一个模型的好坏取决于其提示词。因此，提示工程已成为提升语言模型性能的关键技术。由于视频模型正逐渐成为视觉任务（如视觉推理）的基础模型，本文探究它们是否同样能从视觉提示工程中受益——即自动修改任务图像以提升模型性能。例如，在视觉物理推理任务（"球经过一组障碍物后落在哪里？"）中，通过简单调用图像编辑模型，可以将抽象的草图场景转换为逼真版本。我们发现，视觉提示工程（简称VIPE）能够一致地提升视频推理任务的性能。事实上，对于视频模型而言，视觉提示工程甚至比传统的基于文本的提示工程或测试时缩放更为有效。最终，正如基于文本的提示工程系统性地提升了语言模型性能，视觉提示工程可以作为一种简单、计算高效的方法，从视频模型中引出更好的视觉推理能力。示例视频见项目页面：https://visual-prompt-engineering.github.io/。

</details>


### [52] [Less is More: Modality-Decoupling for General AIGC Audio-Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25543)
*Jielun Peng,Yabin Wang,Yaqi Li,Jincheng Liu,Xiaopeng Hong,Athanasios V. Vasilakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAV-Det提出了一种解耦的音频-视觉AIGC检测系统，采用决策级融合而非特征级融合，分别独立建模各模态的伪造证据，在IJCAI-ECAI 2026 DDL 2.0 Workshop的通用AIGC音视频检测挑战赛中排名第一。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AIGC检测方法假设音视频内容存在对应关系，通过寻找跨模态不一致来识别伪造。但实验发现该假设在通用场景下并不总是成立，因此需要更鲁棒的方法。

Method: 提出DAV-Det解耦音视频AIGC检测系统，采用决策级融合替代特征级融合。视觉检测器利用全局、图像块和片段多粒度表示捕获空间伪造线索；音频检测器通过门控时域-频域双分支架构建模时间与频谱不规则性。

Result: 在IJCAI-ECAI 2026 DDL 2.0 Workshop通用AIGC音视频检测挑战赛中排名第一，最终得分为0.8460。

Conclusion: 对于通用音视频AIGC检测，决策级融合相比特征级融合是更鲁棒的选择，解耦建模各模态的伪造证据能有效提升检测性能。

Original Abstract: Generative AI has rapidly expanded audio-visual forgery beyond human-centric deepfakes into general scenes. Existing AIGC detection methods assume audio-visual content correspondence, identifying forgeries by spotting cross-modal inconsistencies. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not consistently hold in general scenarios. We argue that, for general audio-visual AIGC detection, decision-level fusion is a more robust alternative to feature-level fusion. Therefore, we propose DAV-Det, a decoupled audio-visual AIGC detection system that independently models forensic evidence from each modality. The visual detector leverages multi-granularity representations at global, patch, and segment levels to capture spatial forgery cues, while the audio detector exploits both temporal and spectral irregularities via a gated temporal-spectral dual-branch architecture to model acoustic artifacts. Our method ranks 1st in the General AIGC Audio-Video Detection Challenge of the IJCAI-ECAI 2026 DDL 2.0 Workshop, with a final score of 0.8460. Code is available at https://github.com/tuffy-studio/DAV-Det.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能已迅速将音视频伪造从以人为中心的深度伪造扩展到通用场景。现有的AIGC检测方法假设音视频内容存在对应关系，通过发现跨模态不一致来识别伪造。然而，我们通过实验发现，这一假设在通用场景中并不总是成立。我们认为，对于通用音视频AIGC检测，决策级融合是比特征级融合更鲁棒的选择。因此，我们提出了DAV-Det，一种解耦的音视频AIGC检测系统，该系统独立地建模来自每个模态的取证证据。视觉检测器利用全局、图像块和片段级别的多粒度表示来捕获空间伪造线索，而音频检测器通过门控时域-频域双分支架构利用时间和频谱不规则性来建模音频伪影。我们的方法在IJCAI-ECAI 2026 DDL 2.0 Workshop的通用AIGC音视频检测挑战赛中排名第一，最终得分为0.8460。代码已开源：https://github.com/tuffy-studio/DAV-Det。

</details>


### [53] [Few-Shot Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation via Textual Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25563)
*Junhyuk Heo,Junghwan Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现开放词汇分割模型在遥感图像上表现不佳的主要原因在于文本查询（类别名称）无法有效嵌入视觉-语言空间，而非分割模型本身。通过文本反演（textual inversion）在冻结模型上从少量示例中学习更好的文本嵌入，可将受影响类别的mIoU从3.9提升至39.4，并在8个遥感数据集上优于基于视觉提示的少样本方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开放词汇分割模型在遥感图像上对某些类别的分割效果远差于其他图像域，作者发现其主要原因不是模型能力不足，而是文本查询（类别名称）在视觉-语言嵌入空间中缺乏有效的地址映射，导致模型无法正确理解目标类别。

Method: 通过文本反演（textual inversion）技术在冻结的开放词汇分割模型上，利用少量示例学习更优的文本嵌入表示，推理阶段仅使用文本输入，不引入视觉提示，从而在不改变模型结构的前提下改善遥感图像的类别名称嵌入质量。

Result: 在代表性基准上，受影响类别的平均交并比（mIoU）从3.9提升至39.4；在8个遥感数据集上，该方法优于依赖视觉提示的少样本方法。

Conclusion: 遥感图像开放词汇分割的性能瓶颈主要源于文本查询在视觉-语言空间中的弱嵌入，通过文本反演学习更好的类别名称嵌入可以显著缓解这一问题，且纯文本推理方式具有更好的泛化性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation labels arbitrary categories from a text query without per-class training, yet on remote sensing imagery it underperforms on categories it handles reliably elsewhere. We find that much of this gap traces to the text query rather than to the segmentation model. Because these models are not specialized for overhead imagery, the class name that serves as the query is often a weak address into the vision-language embedding space. We show that a better name repairs part of the gap, while the remaining failures call for an address that the tested natural-language rephrasings do not provide. We recover that address from a few examples through textual inversion on a frozen model, keeping inference text only. On a representative benchmark this raises the mean intersection over union on the affected categories from 3.9 to 39.4, and across eight remote sensing datasets it improves over few-shot methods that instead inject visual prompts at inference.

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇分割能够根据文本查询对任意类别进行分割，无需针对每个类别进行单独训练。然而，在遥感图像上，该技术在原本处理可靠的类别上表现不佳。我们发现，这一差距主要源于文本查询而非分割模型本身。由于这些模型并未针对俯视图像进行专门优化，作为查询的类别名称在视觉-语言嵌入空间中往往是一个弱地址。我们证明，更优的命名可以部分修复这一差距，而剩余失败则需要测试中自然语言改写所无法提供的地址。我们通过文本反演，在冻结模型上从少量示例中恢复该地址，推理时仅使用文本。在代表性基准上，受影响类别的平均交并比从3.9提升至39.4；在八个遥感数据集上，该方法优于在推理时注入视觉提示的少样本方法。

</details>


### [54] [ReDesign: Recovering Editable Design Structures from Images via Agentic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25565)
*Jooyeol Yun,Jintae Park,Hyesu Lim,Junha Hyung,Hyungjin Chung,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDesign是一个能够从光栅图像重建可编辑图层层次结构的智能体框架，通过优雅验证机制防止错误累积，并在Figma编辑重放基准测试中表现出最高的可编辑性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从光栅图像恢复可编辑的设计文件是现代设计工作流中常见且昂贵的瓶颈，但由于可编辑性取决于多模态属性（如排版、矢量几何、颜色、分组和图层顺序）的恢复，因此极具挑战性。

Method: 提出ReDesign智能体框架，通过选择和组合跨模态的专用工具逐步构建可编辑的图层层次结构；引入优雅验证机制，在每次扩展时提供接受、剪枝或重试的局部反馈，防止错误累积并避免大规模重运行。

Result: 在Figma编辑重放基准测试（包含909个原始Figma文件和14,796个受控编辑指令）上，ReDesign在视觉保真度、布局编辑、颜色编辑和文本编辑方面均优于图层分解基线和串行工具使用流水线，实现了最高的可编辑性。

Conclusion: ReDesign通过智能体框架和优雅验证机制，有效解决了从光栅图像恢复可编辑设计文件的难题，在标准重建指标和可编辑性评估上均取得了优异表现。

Original Abstract: Recovering an editable design file from a raster image is a common and costly bottleneck in modern design workflows, yet remains challenging since editability depends on recovering multi-modal attributes, such as typography, vector geometry, colors, grouping, and layer ordering. We present ReDesign, an agentic framework that grows an editable layer hierarchy by selecting and composing specialized tools across modalities. To keep this long decision process reliable despite imperfect tool outputs, we introduce graceful verification at each expansion, which provides local accept, prune, or retry feedback that prevents error accumulation and avoids large scale reruns. To evaluate editability at scale, we introduce the Figma Edit Replay Benchmark, consisting of 909 raw Figma files and 14,796 controlled edit instructions that replay edits on reconstructed outputs. Across this benchmark and standard reconstruction metrics, ReDesign achieves strong visual fidelity while delivering the highest editability across layout, color, and text edits, outperforming layered decomposition baselines and serial tool use pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 从光栅图像恢复可编辑的设计文件是现代设计工作流中一个常见且昂贵的瓶颈，然而由于可编辑性取决于多模态属性（如排版、矢量几何、颜色、分组和图层顺序）的恢复，这一任务仍充满挑战。我们提出ReDesign，一个智能体框架，通过选择并组合跨模态的专用工具来逐步构建可编辑的图层层次结构。为了在工具输出不完美的情况下仍能保持这一长决策过程的可靠性，我们在每次扩展时引入优雅验证机制，该机制提供局部接受、剪枝或重试反馈，从而防止错误累积并避免大规模重运行。为了在大规模下评估可编辑性，我们引入了Figma编辑重放基准测试，包含909个原始Figma文件和14,796个受控编辑指令，用于在重建输出上重放编辑操作。在该基准测试和标准重建指标上，ReDesign在实现强视觉保真度的同时，在布局编辑、颜色编辑和文本编辑方面均达到了最高的可编辑性，优于图层分解基线和串行工具使用流水线。

</details>


### [55] [The LAIA Dataset: Labelled Attention for Intelligent Automobiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25570)
*A. Contreras,D. Porres,R. Abad,P. Cano,G. Villalonga,A. M. López,A. Hernández-Sabaté*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了LAIA，一个包含人类注意力数据的合成数据集，用于增强端到端自动驾驶的可解释性，并通过对比人类注意力与模型注意力来洞察模型行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶范式虽然前景广阔，但其可解释性和可说明性不足，限制了实际应用。现有研究缺乏包含人类注意力标注的数据集来帮助理解模型行为。

Method: 使用CARLA模拟器在闭环环境中收集44名参与者超过15小时的驾驶数据，包含RGB图像（6种天气条件）、语义/实例分割、深度图、光流、CAN总线信号及同步眼动追踪数据，构建LAIA数据集。

Result: 利用LAIA数据集比较人类注意力与端到端驾驶模型中涌现的感知注意力，揭示模型行为与人类注意力之间的异同。

Conclusion: LAIA数据集可支持注意力感知的端到端AI驾驶、驾驶员行为预测、异常注意力模式检测以及模型可解释性提升等多种应用，为理解端到端驾驶模型提供了重要工具。

Original Abstract: The development of autonomous vehicles (AVs) usually relies heavily on data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) models that require large volumes of sensor data with ground-truth annotations. While modular architectures are widely used, end-to-end driving paradigms offer a promising alternative by directly mapping sensor inputs to control actions. However, their adoption is limited by challenges in interpretability and explainability. To address this, we present LAIA (Labelled Attention for Intelligent Automobiles), a novel synthetic dataset designed to enrich end-to-end driving research with human attention data. Collected using the CARLA simulator in closed-loop environments, LAIA comprises over 15 hours of driving from 44 participants across carefully crafted scenarios designed to evoke natural responses. Each sequence includes RGB images under six weather conditions, semantic and instance segmentation, depth, optical flow, CAN bus signals, and synchronized eye-tracking data. LAIA enables applications including training attention-aware end-to-end AI drivers, predicting driver behavior, developing methods to detect anomalous driver-attention patterns, and improving model explainability. In this work, we use LAIA to compare human attention with the perceptual attention emerging in our end-to-end driving models, thereby providing insight into their behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶汽车的发展通常严重依赖数据驱动的人工智能模型，这些模型需要大量带有真实标注的传感器数据。虽然模块化架构被广泛使用，但端到端驾驶范式通过将传感器输入直接映射到控制动作，提供了一种有前途的替代方案。然而，其应用受到可解释性和可说明性挑战的限制。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了LAIA（用于智能汽车的标注注意力数据集），这是一个新颖的合成数据集，旨在用人类注意力数据丰富端到端驾驶研究。该数据集使用CARLA模拟器在闭环环境中收集，包含44名参与者在精心设计的场景中超过15小时的驾驶数据，旨在引发自然反应。每个序列包含六种天气条件下的RGB图像、语义和实例分割、深度图、光流、CAN总线信号以及同步眼动追踪数据。LAIA支持多种应用，包括训练注意力感知的端到端AI驾驶员、预测驾驶员行为、开发检测异常驾驶员注意力模式的方法，以及改进模型可解释性。在本工作中，我们使用LAIA来比较人类注意力与端到端驾驶模型中涌现的感知注意力，从而洞察其行为。

</details>


### [56] [Forensic Reproducibility Audit of a Radiology Vision-Language Model Benchmark: From Intended Protocol to Released Artifact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25589)
*Mateusz Kozłowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对一项保存的胸部X光视觉语言模型（VLM）试点研究进行了追溯性法医可重复性审计，发现多项人工制品间存在不一致，包括DICOM渲染错误、提示绑定混淆、标签提取截断等问题，最终撤回了原始的性能、排名、提示效应和临床声明，并提出了机器可验证的控制规范。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学影像AI基准通常假设各个组件（数据集、DICOM渲染、提示、API、标注、代码、手稿等）之间的一致性，但这种假设很少被实际检验。本文旨在通过严格的追溯审计来检验这种假设是否成立。

Method: 对一项已保存的胸部X光视觉语言模型（VLM）试点研究进行回顾性法医可重复性审计（forensic reproducibility audit），不重新调用模型或重新标注数据，而是追溯追踪提示绑定、DICOM元数据、输出完整性、标签提取、匹配分析和发布传播等环节。

Result: 300次计划模型提示调用中，297次返回非空报告；60次Claude调用被分配了A/B标签但使用了相同的C提示；30项研究实际对应28名患者；4张MONOCHROME1图像未经所需极性反转即被渲染；数据集分割成员身份未保留；未经验证的提取器将5份报告截断至4000字符；重建后Cochran's Q从154.73变为182.29；45次McNemar比较中27次原始p<0.05，20次经Holm校正后仍低于0.05。

Conclusion: 撤回了原始的性能、排名、提示效应和临床声明，并提出了针对队列、DICOM渲染、提示和模型身份、调用状态、标注来源、关键分析和衍生人工制品的机器可验证控制规范。

Original Abstract: Medical-imaging AI benchmarks combine datasets, DICOM rendering, prompts, provider APIs, automated labels, statistical code, manuscripts, and repository releases. Agreement across these artifacts is usually assumed rather than tested. We performed a retrospective forensic reproducibility audit of a preserved chest-radiograph vision-language model (VLM) pilot; no model was called again and no image or report was newly annotated. We traced prompt bindings, DICOM metadata, output completeness, label extraction, matched analyses, and release propagation. Of 300 planned model-prompt calls, 297 yielded nonempty reports. Sixty Claude calls labeled A/B were executed with the same C prompt. The 30 studies represented 28 patients. Four MONOCHROME1 images were rendered without required polarity inversion, dataset split membership was not retained, and the unvalidated extractor truncated five reports to 4000 characters. Reconstructing one common cohort of 369 complete case-finding blocks changed Cochran's Q from 154.73 to 182.29. Of 45 McNemar comparisons, 27 had unadjusted p < 0.05 and 20 remained below 0.05 after Holm adjustment. These values describe only the archived automated-label matrix; they do not recover the intended prompt comparison or establish clinical performance. We withdraw the original performance, ranking, prompt-effect, and clinical claims and specify machine-verifiable controls for cohort, DICOM rendering, prompt and model identity, call status, annotation provenance, keyed analysis, and derived artifacts.

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像AI基准将数据集、DICOM渲染、提示、提供者API、自动标签、统计代码、手稿和仓库发布相结合。这些人工制品之间的一致性通常是被假设而非被检验的。我们对一项已保存的胸部X光视觉语言模型（VLM）试点进行了回顾性法医可重复性审计；未重新调用模型，也未重新标注任何图像或报告。我们追溯了提示绑定、DICOM元数据、输出完整性、标签提取、匹配分析和发布传播。在300次计划的模型-提示调用中，297次生成了非空报告。标记为A/B的60次Claude调用实际执行了相同的C提示。30项研究实际对应28名患者。4张MONOCHROME1图像未经所需的极性反转即被渲染，数据集分割成员身份未保留，且未经验证的提取器将5份报告截断至4000字符。重建一个包含369个完整病例查找块的共同队列后，Cochran's Q从154.73变为182.29。在45次McNemar比较中，27次未调整的p<0.05，20次经Holm调整后仍低于0.05。这些值仅描述已存档的自动标签矩阵；它们既不能恢复预期的提示比较，也不能确立临床性能。我们撤回了原始的性能、排名、提示效应和临床声明，并指定了针对队列、DICOM渲染、提示和模型身份、调用状态、标注来源、关键分析和衍生人工制品的机器可验证控制规范。

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Facial Consistency: Personalized Person Image Generation with Holistic Identity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25622)
*Yuxuan Xiao,Shanshan Zhang,Jian Yang,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态平衡缩放（DBS）微调策略，通过自适应时序门控和区域感知优化，解决个性化人物图像生成中面部保真度与外观一致性之间的权衡问题，并引入Pexels-100基准数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常只强调局部面部细节或整体外观线索单一层面的身份信息，导致面部保真度与整体外观一致性之间存在固有权衡，缺乏统一兼顾两者的有效方案。

Method: 首先提出一个简单的双分支基线，将全局外观控制和局部面部控制统一在同一生成框架中；进而提出动态平衡缩放（DBS）微调策略，包含两个组件：自适应时序门控（沿去噪轨迹动态调节分支贡献）和区域感知优化（改善面部、外观和全局监督的协调性）。

Result: 实验表明，DBS相比现有开源基线实现了面部保真度与外观一致性之间更好的权衡，并提供了一个可控的整体身份建模基础框架。同时引入了Pexels-100基准数据集用于评估整体身份一致性。

Conclusion: DBS通过缓解面部分支的过度主导并鼓励更有效的外观感知引导，有效改进了面部与外观身份的协调性，为个性化人物图像生成中的整体身份建模提供了更优的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Personalized person image generation requires preserving subject identity across both local facial details and broader appearance cues. Existing methods typically emphasize only one level of identity information, leading to an inherent trade-off between facial fidelity and overall appearance consistency. To address this, we first propose a simple dual-branch baseline that unifies global appearance control and local facial control within a shared generation framework. This simple combination of different branches yields promising results, but suffers from instability in practice due to uncoordinated branch contributions. To this end, we propose Dynamic Balancing Scaling (DBS), a fine-tuning strategy for improving face and appearance identity coordination. DBS consists of two components: adaptive temporal gating, which dynamically modulates branch contributions along the denoising trajectory, and region-aware optimization, which improves the coordination of facial, appearance, and global supervision. Together, these designs alleviate persistent face-branch over-dominance and encourage more effective appearance-aware guidance. We also introduce Pexels-100, a benchmark for evaluating holistic identity consistency in personalized person generation. Experiments show that DBS achieves a better trade-off between facial fidelity and appearance consistency than existing open-source baselines, while providing a controllable basic framework for holistic identity modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 个性化人物图像生成需要在局部面部细节和更广泛的外观线索上同时保持主体身份。现有方法通常只强调单一层面的身份信息，导致面部保真度与整体外观一致性之间存在固有权衡。为解决这一问题，我们首先提出一个简单的双分支基线，将全局外观控制和局部面部控制统一在共享生成框架中。这种不同分支的简单组合取得了有希望的结果，但在实践中由于分支贡献不协调而存在不稳定性。为此，我们提出动态平衡缩放（DBS），一种用于改善面部与外观身份协调性的微调策略。DBS包含两个组件：自适应时序门控，沿去噪轨迹动态调节分支贡献；以及区域感知优化，改善面部、外观和全局监督的协调性。这些设计共同缓解了持久的面部分支过度主导问题，并鼓励更有效的外观感知引导。我们还引入了Pexels-100，一个用于评估个性化人物生成中整体身份一致性的基准。实验表明，DBS在面部保真度和外观一致性之间实现了比现有开源基线更好的权衡，同时为整体身份建模提供了一个可控的基础框架。

</details>
