  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型缺乏交互式、实时且长时程的世界探索能力，用户无法像在3D世界中一样自由移动相机视角、发现未观测区域并回访已观测区域。论文旨在构建一个可玩的、相机可控的通用视频世界模型，弥合视频生成与交互式世界探索之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 提出密集坐标场（dense coordinate field）的相机条件控制方法，其渲染结果提供空间对齐的运动和方向线索，使模型将相机运动直接解释为视觉证据；2) 设计基于稀疏注意力的高效记忆机制，在推理时仅选择性地关注少量相关上下文token，支持快速精准的记忆检索；3) 开发多种技术修正自强迫式（self-forcing）蒸馏流程，提升学生模型对控制信号的遵循能力，同时保持教师模型的多样化生成模式和长期记忆。

Result: Wonder能够以16 FPS实时合成多样化、分钟级时长的视频，在长程生成过程中保持连贯的几何结构、外观和动态。除图像到视频生成外，还自然支持视频条件生成，允许对现有动态场景进行实时重拍。

Conclusion: 通过系统级协同设计相机控制、记忆机制和训练策略，Wonder实现了通用视频世界模型的实时、相机可控世界探索能力，在长时程视频生成中保持了一致的场景几何、外观和动态，为交互式视频生成和世界探索提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: We present Wonder, a general-purpose video world model for real-time, camera-controllable world exploration. Given an image or a conditional video, Wonder constructs a playable world where users can navigate interactively by moving the camera, discovering unseen regions, and revisiting previously observed areas in real time and over a long-term horizon. Achieving this capability requires a system-level co-design of control method, memory mechanism, and training strategy. We introduce a novel camera conditioning with a dense coordinate field whose renderings provide spatially aligned motion and orientation cues, allowing the model to interpret camera motion directly as visual evidence. To support fast and precise memory retrieval over a growing generation context, we propose an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism, enabling the model to selectively attend to a small set of relevant context tokens at inference time, regardless of actual context length. We further develop several techniques to rectify the self-forcing-style distillation pipeline, improving the student model's ability to respect control signals, as well as maintaining diverse generation modes and long-term memory from the teacher. Together, these components enable Wonder to synthesize diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS while preserving coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. Beyond image-to-video generation, Wonder naturally supports video-conditioned generation, allowing existing dynamic scenes to be re-shot in real time.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出Wonder，一个通用的视频世界模型，用于实时、相机可控的世界探索。给定一张图像或一段条件视频，Wonder构建一个可玩的世界，用户可以通过移动相机进行交互式导航，实时且长时程地发现未观测区域并回访之前观察过的区域。实现这一能力需要对控制方法、记忆机制和训练策略进行系统级的协同设计。我们引入一种新颖的相机条件控制方法——密集坐标场，其渲染结果提供空间对齐的运动和方向线索，使得模型能够直接将相机运动解释为视觉证据。为了在不断增长的生成上下文中支持快速且精准的记忆检索，我们提出了一种基于高效稀疏注意力的记忆机制，使模型在推理时能够选择性地关注少量相关上下文token，而无需考虑实际上下文长度。我们还开发了多种技术来修正自强迫式蒸馏流程，提升学生模型遵循控制信号的能力，同时保持教师模型中多样化的生成模式和长期记忆。这些组件共同使得Wonder能够以16 FPS的速度合成多样化、分钟级时长的视频，并在长程生成中保持连贯的几何结构、外观和动态。除了图像到视频的生成，Wonder还自然支持视频条件生成，允许对现有动态场景进行实时重拍。

</details>


### [88] [VetClaw: An Edge-Cloud Multimodal Agentic System for Veterinary Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26042)
*Syed Mhamudul Hasan,Anas AlSobeh,Hussein Zangoti,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VetClaw 是一个边缘-云端多模态智能体系统，用于早期兽医疾病筛查。它通过摄像头采集图像并结合症状描述，利用云端视觉-语言模型进行零样本疾病分类，并设计了分离的智能体交互与工作流编排架构以提升系统的安全性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统静态图像分类模型在兽医疾病筛查中存在局限性，无法灵活利用多种输入模态、缺乏工作流管理和安全机制，难以处理不确定病例。

Method: 提出了 VetClaw 系统，采用边缘设备（摄像头模块）感知数据，云端视觉-语言模型进行零样本分类。系统将智能体交互与工作流编排分离：OpenClaw 负责调度、工具调用、用户交互和通知，LangGraph 管理状态化筛查工作流，包括输入验证、图像传输、模型调用、安全检查、条件路由、故障处理和结构化日志记录。

Result: 仅使用图像的视觉-语言模型预测性能有限，而症状引导和多模态输入能够显著提升零样本分类性能。

Conclusion: VetClaw 将静态预测模型转化为一个可协调、具备安全意识的系统，能够使用工具、管理工作流、处理故障并对不确定病例进行升级处理。

Original Abstract: We present VetClaw, an edge-cloud multimodal agentic system for early veterinary disease screening. VetClaw uses a camera module as an edge sensing device and sends captured images, together with optional symptom descriptions, to a server-hosted vision-language model for zero-shot disease classification. The system separates agent interaction from workflow orchestration: OpenClaw provides scheduling, tool access, user interaction, and notification services on the edge device, while LangGraph manages the stateful screening workflow, including input validation, image transmission, model invocation, safety checks, conditional routing, failure handling, and structured logging. This design moves beyond static image classification by enabling the system to collect visual evidence, invoke external models, apply deterministic safety rules, and generate diagnostic-support alerts. Results show that image-only VLM prediction remains limited, whereas symptom-guided and multimodal inputs improve zero-shot classification performance. Thus, VetClaw transforms a static prediction model into a coordinated, safety-aware system that can use tools, manage workflows, handle failures, and escalate uncertain cases.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 VetClaw，一个用于早期兽医疾病筛查的边缘-云端多模态智能体系统。VetClaw 使用摄像头模块作为边缘感知设备，将采集到的图像连同可选的症状描述发送至服务器托管的视觉-语言模型，进行零样本疾病分类。该系统将智能体交互与工作流编排相分离：OpenClaw 在边缘设备上提供调度、工具调用、用户交互和通知服务，而 LangGraph 管理状态化的筛查工作流，包括输入验证、图像传输、模型调用、安全检查、条件路由、故障处理和结构化日志记录。这种设计超越了静态图像分类的限制，使系统能够收集视觉证据、调用外部模型、应用确定性安全规则并生成诊断辅助警报。结果表明，仅依赖图像的视觉-语言模型预测性能有限，而症状引导和多模态输入则能提升零样本分类性能。因此，VetClaw 将静态预测模型转化为一个可协调、具备安全意识的系统，该系统能够使用工具、管理工作流、处理故障并对不确定病例进行升级处理。

</details>

### [89] [Selective Impairment of Motor Recovery from Typing Errors in Parkinson's Disease: A Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24796)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文利用日常被动采集的键盘击键动态数据，区分帕金森病（PD）患者在错误监控（发现错误）与错误后运动恢复（恢复正常节奏）这两个可分离的认知运动控制阶段。研究发现错误后恢复时间与疾病严重程度显著相关，而错误前击键不稳定性则无关，验证了二者在行为层面的分离性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 帕金森病影响运动和认知控制的多个可分离阶段，现有研究多为实验室环境下的电生理证据，缺乏基于日常被动采集数据的行为层面分离证据。本研究旨在通过日常打字击键动态，区分PD患者的错误监控与错误后运动恢复这两个阶段。

Method: 利用neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD数据集中的退格事件作为自然发生的纠错线索，提取错误前击键不稳定性和错误后恢复时间两个指标。采用连续加速失效时间（AFT）生存模型对错误后恢复时间进行建模，并与离散化时间事件方法对比；同时进行相关性分析、联合模型分析、控制打字速度的回归分析及独立临床运动测试验证。

Result: 错误前击键不稳定性与疾病严重程度无关（r=0.164, p=0.413）；错误后恢复时间与疾病严重程度显著相关（两个独立子队列p<10^{-9}，置换检验p<0.0033，bootstrap 95% CI不含零）。两个指标互不相关（r=-0.065），联合模型中仅错误后恢复保持显著。离散化时间事件方法破坏信号，连续AFT模型恢复信号。效果在控制打字速度后仍然存在，并在交替手指敲击测试中复制（p=0.011）。

Conclusion: 日常打字击键动态可以非侵入地区分PD患者中由不同神经环路介导的错误监控与错误后运动恢复两个过程，验证了先前电生理学证据的行为层面分离性，为基于日常行为数据的PD运动-认知功能评估提供了新方法。

Original Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) affects multiple, dissociable stages of motor and cognitive control. We ask whether passively-collected keystroke dynamics can distinguish two of these stages: noticing a self-generated error (error monitoring) versus recovering normal motor rhythm afterward (motor restart). Using backspace events as naturally-occurring error-correction episodes in the public neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD dataset (57 subjects with sufficient backspace data, 27 with PD and UPDRS-III scores), we find no evidence that pre-error keystroke instability differs by disease severity ($r=0.164$, $p=0.413$), but strong evidence that post-error recovery time does, modeled as a continuous accelerated failure time (AFT) survival outcome ($p<10^{-9}$ in each of two independent sub-cohorts; permutation $p<0.0033$; bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero). The two measures are uncorrelated with each other ($r=-0.065$), and in a joint model only post-error recovery remains significant, confirming a genuine dissociation rather than two redundant signals. The effect survives controlling for raw typing speed and replicates against an independent clinical motor test (alternating finger-tapping, $p=0.011$). An initial attempt to model recovery as a discretized time-to-event outcome (analogous to Kaplan-Meier survival curves) destroyed the signal regardless of threshold choice; switching to a continuous AFT formulation, which also fits the data's right-skewed distribution far better than ordinary regression, recovered it. We relate this behavioral dissociation to existing electrophysiological evidence that error detection and post-error motor adjustment in PD are mediated by distinct neural circuits, the former largely spared, the latter linked to subthalamic nucleus activity, and argue that this dissociation is detectable through everyday typing alone.

中文摘要翻译: 帕金森病（PD）会影响运动和认知控制的多个可分离阶段。我们探究被动采集的击键动态能否区分其中两个阶段：发现自身产生的错误（错误监控）与之后恢复正常运动节奏（运动重启）。利用公共neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD数据集中退格事件作为自然发生的纠错线索（57名具有足够退格数据的受试者，其中27名PD患者伴有UPDRS-III评分），我们发现错误前击键不稳定性与疾病严重程度无关（r=0.164, p=0.413），但错误后恢复时间与疾病严重程度存在强烈证据，该指标被建模为连续加速失效时间（AFT）生存结局（两个独立子队列中p<10^{-9}；置换检验p<0.0033；bootstrap 95%置信区间不含零）。两个指标互不相关（r=-0.065），在联合模型中仅错误后恢复保持显著，证实了真实的分离性而非两个冗余信号。该效应在控制原始打字速度后仍然存在，并在独立临床运动测试（交替手指敲击，p=0.011）中得到复制。初始尝试将恢复建模为离散化时间-事件结局（类似于Kaplan-Meier生存曲线）会破坏信号，无论阈值如何选择；转而采用连续AFT公式，其能比普通回归更好地拟合数据的右偏分布，从而恢复信号。我们将这种行为分离与现有电生理学证据联系起来：PD中错误检测和错误后运动调整由不同神经环路介导，前者大致保留，后者与丘脑底核活动相关，并论证这种分离仅通过日常打字即可检测到。

</details>


### [90] [Reading Without a Reader: Large Language Models Collapse Reading and Writing into a Single Entangled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24797)
*Diego Saldaña Ulloa*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过定义纠缠指数E∈[0,1]（CKA、Procrustes残差、互k-NN），比较LLM输入侧"阅读编码"WE与输出侧"写作编码"WU的耦合程度，发现模型权重中两者耦合但低于完全绑定（E=0.23-0.35），行为上理解与生成正向耦合，与大脑的双分离模式相反，从而将LLM定位为可能心智空间中的独特存在。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人类大脑中阅读和写作是双重分离的两个系统（腹侧解码通路与额顶叶编码通路），而LLM仅通过一条自回归路径同时驱动两者。本文旨在量化这种单一机制的纠缠程度，探究LLM的"阅读"与"写作"在表征和行为层面是否存在类似大脑的分离。

Method: 定义纠缠指数E∈[0,1]，使用CKA、Procrustes残差和互k-NN三种度量，以独立初始化作为下界、完全权重绑定作为上界进行校准。在GPT-2、OPT、Pythia（14M-1.4B）、T5和BERT/RoBERTa共9个模型上进行探测，分别在权重层面和行为层面比较输入嵌入WE（阅读编码）和输出嵌入WU（写作编码）的耦合程度。

Result: 权重层面：未绑定模型中WE与WU耦合但低于完全绑定（E=0.23-0.35，远高于下界），呈非单调的"先耦合后分化"轨迹，WU每个频率十分位的漂移距离约是WE的3.2倍。行为层面：所有12个非退化模型中理解与生产正向耦合（符号检验p<0.001），与大脑的双分离相反，且编码器-解码器模型在表征上分离两条通路（高达0.96）但行为上仍保持耦合。几何→行为的桥梁为空（ρ=0.00）。

Conclusion: 由于单条前向路径使得某种程度的耦合是先验预期的，本文的贡献在于量化这种耦合及其跨层一致性。通过类比而非同源性分析，将LLM定位为可能心智空间中的一个独特节点。

Original Abstract: In the literate human brain, reading and writing are two doubly-dissociable systems: a ventral decoding route (impaired in pure alexia) and a fronto-parietal encoding route (impaired in pure agraphia), sharing a partial orthographic core. A decoder-only large language model (LLM) instead drives both from a single autoregressive path optimized on text, a recent cultural invention rather than an evolved instinct. We ask how entangled that one mechanism is, comparing an input-side "reading code" $W_E$ with an output-side "writing code" $W_U$ via an entanglement index $E \in [0,1]$ (CKA, Procrustes residual, mutual $k$-NN) calibrated against an independent-init floor and a tied ceiling. Across nine probes on GPT-2, OPT, Pythia (14M--1.4B), T5, and BERT/RoBERTa (six consolidating established results, three introducing the read/write analysis), two complementary levels agree in direction. In the weights, untied models hold one coupled but sub-ceiling code ($E=0.23$--$0.35$, far above floor) on a non-monotonic couple-then-differentiate trajectory, with $W_U$ drifting $\sim 3.2\times$ farther than $W_E$ in every frequency decile. In behaviour, comprehension and production are positively coupled in all 12 non-degenerate models (sign test $p<0.001$), the opposite of the brain's double dissociation. This coupling is general, not decoder-only: encoder--decoders separate the two pathways representationally (up to 0.96) yet stay behaviourally coupled. We report our nulls plainly (the geometry $\rightarrow$ behaviour bridge is null, $ρ=0.00$). Because a single forward path makes some coupling expected a priori, our contribution is its quantification and cross-level concordance; by analogy, not homology, this situates LLMs as a distinct point in the space of possible minds.

中文摘要翻译: 在有读写能力的人脑中，阅读和写作是两个可双重分离的系统：一个腹侧解码通路（在纯失读症中受损）和一个额顶叶编码通路（在纯失写症中受损），两者共享一个部分正字法核心。相比之下，仅使用解码器的大型语言模型（LLM）则通过一条在文本上优化的单一自回归路径来驱动这两个过程，这是一种近代文化发明而非进化本能。我们探究这一单一机制的纠缠程度，通过纠缠指数E∈[0,1]（CKA、Procrustes残差、互k-NN）比较输入侧"阅读编码"WE与输出侧"写作编码"WU，并以独立初始化作为下界、完全权重绑定作为上界进行校准。通过对GPT-2、OPT、Pythia（14M-1.4B）、T5和BERT/RoBERTa九个模型的探测（其中六个巩固已有结论，三个引入读/写分析），两个互补层面在方向上达成一致。在权重层面，未绑定模型持有一个耦合但低于完全绑定的编码（E=0.23-0.35，远高于下界），呈现非单调的"先耦合后分化"轨迹，且在每个频率十分位中WU的漂移距离约为WE的3.2倍。在行为层面，所有12个非退化模型中理解与生产呈正向耦合（符号检验p<0.001），与大脑的双分离相反。这种耦合是普遍的，而非仅解码器模型特有：编码器-解码器模型在表征上分离两条通路（高达0.96），但行为上仍保持耦合。我们如实报告零结果（几何→行为的桥梁为空，ρ=0.00）。由于单条前向路径使得某种程度的耦合是先验预期的，我们的贡献在于量化这种耦合及其跨层一致性；通过类比而非同源性分析，本文将LLM定位为可能心智空间中的一个独特节点。

</details>


### [91] [Forgetting Is Not a Fix: Path Dependence in Sequential Engram Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24805)
*Ferdinand M. Schessl*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文对AI Engram论文中提出的“交换记忆流形假说”（Compositional Memory States Hypothesis）进行了实证检验，发现该假说在顺序编辑场景下不成立：零样本组合与顺序重校准编辑存在61-71%的幅度偏差，剪枝顺序不可互换，未参与编辑的概念也会受累积影响，且已擦除的知识在后续无关编辑中部分恢复。该发现推翻了附录F中关于交换流形的假设，但对原论文的单次编辑结论未构成挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原论文AI Engram提出了记忆痕迹的线性可组合性假说，但将其在顺序编辑下的时间动力学问题留作未来工作。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统检验该假说在顺序加载编辑时是否仍然成立。

Method: 在原论文提供的参考实现上，采用其报告的最佳编辑强度（TOFU alpha=0.6），在三种不同模型（涵盖两家供应商、两种架构家族）上运行预先注册的预测性实验，检验顺序编辑下的表现。

Result: 四项发现跨所有模型一致复现：（1）零样本组合与顺序重校准编辑存在61-71%的幅度偏差；（2）剪枝顺序不可互换，且效应随概念重叠度放大——在某一模型中，两个巴黎地标的剪枝顺序决定了第三个无关概念能否存活；（3）幸存概念的层输入协方差（方法自身的充分统计量）随每次额外编辑单调漂移；（4）已擦除知识在后续无关编辑中部分恢复。

Conclusion: 附录F的交换流形假说在顺序编辑场景下被证伪；原论文的单次编辑结论不受影响。对于用作合规性擦除的场景：今天的擦除认证不能保证工件在下一次编辑后仍然合规。

Original Abstract: AI Engram (Kwon et al., 2026) formalizes the four engram criteria of neuroscience as a constrained inverse problem in weight space and solves it closed-form: concept-specific memory traces become linear objects that can be extracted once and combined arithmetically. Appendix F states the Compositional Memory States Hypothesis: edited models live on "a commutative manifold where the integration of A and B reaches a consistent equilibrium regardless of the learning sequence." The evidence base is single and paired edits -- in materials terms, single-cycle tests, in which fatigue accumulation is structurally invisible. Whether the hypothesis holds under sequential load is exactly the "temporal dynamics" question the paper defers to future work. We run that test on the authors' own reference implementation, at their reported best edit strength (TOFU alpha=0.6, a choice favoring the linearity hypothesis), with pre-registered predictions, across three model charges (two vendors, two architecture families). Four findings replicate across all three: (1) zero-shot composition and sequential re-calibrated editing diverge by 61-71% of the edit magnitude; (2) cut order is not interchangeable, and the effect scales with concept overlap -- in one charge the order of cutting two Paris landmarks decides whether an uninvolved third concept survives; (3) the survivors' layer-input covariances -- the method's own sufficient statistics, read as strain gauges -- drift monotonically with every further cut, in every surviving concept, in every charge; (4) erased knowledge partially returns under subsequent unrelated cuts. Appendix F's commutative-manifold hypothesis is thereby falsified for sequential editing; the single-edit results of the original paper are untouched. For unlearning-as-compliance: erasure certified today does not certify the artifact after its next edit.

中文摘要翻译: AI Engram（Kwon 等，2026）将神经科学的四条印记标准形式化为权重空间中的一个带约束逆问题，并给出了闭式解：概念特定的记忆痕迹成为线性对象，可被一次性提取并通过算术组合。附录F提出了交换记忆状态假说：编辑后的模型生活在“一个交换流形上，在该流形上A和B的整合达到一致均衡，不受学习顺序影响。”其证据基础是单次和成对编辑——从材料学角度看属于单循环测试，疲劳累积在结构上不可见。该假说在顺序负载下是否成立，正是论文推迟到未来工作的“时间动力学”问题。我们在作者自己的参考实现上，使用其报告的最佳编辑强度（TOFU alpha=0.6，这一选择有利于线性假说），以预先注册的预测，在三种模型载体（两家供应商、两种架构家族）上进行了该检验。四项发现在所有三种模型上一致复现：（1）零样本组合与顺序重校准编辑之间差异达到编辑幅度的61-71%；（2）剪枝顺序不可互换，且该效应随概念重叠度放大——在某一模型载体中，两个巴黎地标的剪枝顺序决定了第三个未涉及概念能否存活；（3）幸存者的层输入协方差——该方法自身的充分统计量，用作应变片读取——在每次进一步剪枝时单调漂移，涉及每一个幸存概念、每一个模型载体；（4）已擦除知识在后续无关编辑下部分恢复。附录F的交换流形假说因而在顺序编辑场景下被证伪；原论文的单次编辑结果未受影响。对于用作合规性的遗忘：今天的擦除认证并不能保证该制品在下一次编辑后仍然合规。

</details>


### [92] [Decoding Error-Related Potentials under Multisensory Feedback with Varying Congruency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24806)
*Yixin Liu,Kang Yin,Hye-Bin Shin,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多分支EEGNet和辅助监督的学习策略，用于在多模态（视觉、听觉、触觉）异质反馈条件下鲁棒解码错误相关电位（ErrP），实验证明该方法在异质多模态条件下分类性能优于基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在实际人机交互中，错误感知通常发生在异质多感官反馈环境下，感官模态和反馈一致性的变化给ErrP解码带来挑战，尤其是不一致反馈导致解码困难和分类性能下降。现有方法缺乏针对异质多模态条件下鲁棒ErrP解码的有效策略。

Method: 采用基于多分支EEGNet的架构并引入辅助监督，在无显式模态特定假设的前提下，提升模型在异质条件下的鲁棒性。实验采用迷宫观察任务，包含单模态、双模态和三模态反馈配置，涵盖视觉、听觉和触觉通道。

Result: 该方法在跨被试实验中，于异质多感官条件下实现了一致的分类性能，相比基线EEGNet模型准确率显著提升，尤其在多模态反馈条件下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 合理的架构设计和训练策略能够提升异质多模态条件下错误相关电位解码的稳定性。

Original Abstract: Error-related potentials (ErrPs) are widely studied neural signatures associated with error processing in human-machine interaction. In realistic settings, error perception often occurs under heterogeneous multisensory feedback, where variability induced by sensory modality and feedback congruency poses challenges for reliable ErrP decoding. In particular, incongruent feedback is associated with increased decoding difficulty and reduced classification performance. To address this challenge, we investigate learning strategies for robust ErrP decoding under multimodal visual, auditory, and tactile feedback with controlled sensory congruency. We adopt a multi-branch EEGNet-based architecture with auxiliary supervision to improve robustness across heterogeneous conditions, without relying on explicit modality-specific assumptions. Experiments were conducted using a maze-observation task with unimodal, bimodal, and trimodal feedback configurations. Across subjects, the proposed approach achieved consistent classification performance across heterogeneous sensory conditions and showed improved accuracy compared to baseline EEGNet models, particularly under multimodal feedback. These results suggest that appropriate architectural design and training strategies can improve the stability of ErrP decoding under heterogeneous multisensory conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 错误相关电位（ErrP）是与人在人机交互中错误处理相关的广泛研究的神经信号。在现实场景中，错误感知通常发生在异质多感官反馈条件下，感官模态和反馈一致性带来的变异性对可靠的ErrP解码构成了挑战。尤其是，不一致反馈与解码困难增大和分类性能下降相关。为应对这一挑战，本文研究了在受控感官一致性的多模态视觉、听觉和触觉反馈下进行鲁棒ErrP解码的学习策略。我们采用基于多分支EEGNet的架构并辅以辅助监督，以提高在异质条件下的鲁棒性，同时不依赖于显式的模态特定假设。实验采用迷宫观察任务，包含单模态、双模态和三模态反馈配置。跨被试实验结果表明，所提方法在异质感官条件下实现了稳定的分类性能，并且相比基线EEGNet模型准确率有所提升，尤其是在多模态反馈条件下。这些结果表明，适当的架构设计和训练策略能够改善异质多感官条件下ErrP解码的稳定性。

</details>


### [93] [A Path Integral Model of Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24807)
*Haruki Emori,Kazunori Kondo,Atsushi Iriki,Andrei Khrennikov*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文建立了认知成本优化的数学物理基础，将目标导向认知过程建模为投影哈密顿量下的虚时演化，证明其等价于双括号流和希尔伯特-施密特成本的黎曼梯度流。通过Wick旋转将该非酉下降过程重构为等价酉演化，并揭示了无意识到有意识处理的连续谱对应于认知系统与神经环境探针之间酉相互作用强度的变化，分别恢复出GKSL退相干模型和意识洞察的投射性固定化端点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 旨在为意识路径积分模型建立严格的数学物理基础，统一解释无意识处理与有意识洞察（“啊哈”体验）的连续谱机制。

Method: 将目标导向认知过程建模为投影哈密顿量下的虚时演化，证明其与双括号流和希尔伯特-施密特成本梯度流的等价性；通过Wick旋转将非酉演化重写为酉路径积分；通过改变认知系统与神经环境探针的相互作用强度，统一描述弱耦合极限下的GKSL退相干模型和强耦合极限下的投射性意识固定化。

Result: 建立了三个主要结果：(1)虚时演化等价于双括号流，是希尔伯特-施密特成本的黎曼梯度流；(2)Wick旋转将非酉下降表达为等价酉演化，具有精确的离散路径积分表示；(3)无意识到有意识处理的连续谱由认知-环境相互作用强度决定，弱耦合极限恢复GKSL退相干模型，强耦合极限得到洞察式“啊哈”端点。

Conclusion: Wick旋转是一种重新描述的技术，而非物理机制的变化；无意识和有意识处理共享相同的虚时演化和路径积分结构，仅测量-相互作用强度不同。

Original Abstract: We develop the mathematical and physical formulation of cognitive cost optimization that underlies the path-integral model of consciousness. The goal-directed cognitive process is modeled as imaginary-time evolution (ITE) under a projector Hamiltonian that rewards configurations consistent with a target concept. We establish three results. First, this ITE coincides with a double-bracket flow and is therefore the Riemannian gradient flow of a Hilbert--Schmidt cost whose unique minimum is the solution. Second, a Wick rotation re-expresses this non-unitary descent as an equivalent unitary evolution on the same Hilbert space, which admits an exact discrete path-integral representation in which the oracle and the initial-state diffusion projector play the roles of potential and kinetic energy. Third, we identify the continuum from unconscious to conscious processing with the strength of the unitary interaction between the cognitive system and a neural-environment probe, recovering the Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) decoherence model of Asano \textit{et al.} in the Markovian weak-coupling limit, and the projective, reportable fixation of an optimized state in the strong-coupling limit, an insight-like ``Aha'' endpoint. Both regimes share the same ITE and path-integral structure, and only the measurement-interaction strength varies. The Wick rotation is therefore a technique of re-description, not a physical regime change.

中文摘要翻译: 我们建立了意识路径积分模型背后的认知成本优化的数学与物理形式体系。目标导向的认知过程被建模为投影哈密顿量下的虚时演化，该哈密顿量奖励与目标概念一致的构型。我们确立了三个结果。第一，该虚时演化与双括号流重合，因此是希尔伯特-施密特成本的黎曼梯度流，其唯一最小值即为所求之解。第二，Wick旋转将这一非酉下降过程重新表达为同一希尔伯特空间上的等价酉演化，该演化具有精确的离散路径积分表示，其中预言机与初始态扩散投影子分别扮演势能与动能的角色。第三，我们将无意识处理到有意识处理的连续谱识别为认知系统与神经环境探针之间酉相互作用强度的变化，在马尔可夫弱耦合极限下恢复了Asano等人的Gorini–Kossakowski–Sudarshan–Lindblad退相干模型，在强耦合极限下则得到优化态的投射性可报告固定化——一种类似洞察的“啊哈”端点。两个区域共享相同的虚时演化和路径积分结构，仅测量-相互作用强度有所变化。因此，Wick旋转是一种重新描述的技术，而非物理机制的变化。

</details>


### [94] [EEG Emotion Recognition From AI-Generated Biodigital Architecture Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24808)
*Hongye Yang,Eva Guttmann-Flury*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究利用脑电图（EEG）数据评估AI生成的生命数字建筑图像引发的情绪反应，发现伽马和德尔塔频带在情绪分类中准确率最高，其中伽马频带对“敬畏”情绪的分类准确率达77.07%，并指出绿色植物和非均匀纹理能引发积极情绪，而潮湿感则触发负面反应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索生命数字建筑设计中如何通过客观的神经生理指标（EEG）评估用户情绪反应，从而为建筑师设计更具吸引力和可持续性的建筑环境提供数据支持。

Method: 通过预实验从600张AI生成图像中筛选出60张能引发强烈情绪（敬畏、厌恶、满足）的图像，随后对52名志愿者进行EEG记录；基于现有数据集分析进行通道选择和样本量估计，利用伽马和德尔塔频带特征进行情绪分类。

Result: 伽马和德尔塔频带分类准确率最高，其中伽马频带对“敬畏”情绪的分类准确率达77.07%±13.8%；绿色植物和非均匀颗粒感与积极情绪相关，而潮湿感触发负面反应。

Conclusion: EEG能够客观评估建筑偏好，在生命数字建筑中融入自然元素和多样纹理有助于提升美学吸引力和接受度。

Original Abstract: Emotional responses to biodigital architecture were examined using electroencephalographic (EEG) data from AI-generated images. A pre-experiment involving 336 participants identified 60 images, selected from an initial pool of 600, that elicited strong emotional responses categorized as awe, disgust, or content. These images were used for EEG recordings of 52 volunteers, with channel selection and sample size estimation based on the analysis of an existing dataset. Gamma and delta bands yielded the highest classification accuracy, with the gamma band achieving an accuracy of 77.07 percent +/- 13.8 percent for the awe emotion. Key factors such as greenery and non-uniform granularity were linked to positive emotions, while dampness triggered negative reactions. These results emphasize the significance of incorporating natural elements and varied textures in biodigital architecture to enhance aesthetic appeal and acceptance. The study demonstrates EEG's capability to objectively assess architectural preferences, providing valuable insights for architects to design engaging and sustainable environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究利用脑电图数据评估AI生成图像所引发的对生命数字建筑的情绪反应。一项涉及336名参与者的预实验从初始600张图像中筛选出60张能够引发强烈情绪（分为敬畏、厌恶或满足）的图像。基于现有数据集分析进行通道选择和样本量估计后，这些图像被用于52名志愿者的EEG记录。伽马频带和德尔塔频带取得了最高的分类准确率，其中伽马频带对“敬畏”情绪的分类准确率达到77.07%±13.8%。绿色植物和非均匀颗粒感等关键因素与积极情绪相关，而潮湿感则触发负面反应。这些结果强调了在生命数字建筑中融入自然元素和多样纹理以增强美学吸引力和接受度的重要性。本研究证明了EEG在客观评估建筑偏好方面的能力，为建筑师设计富有吸引力和可持续的环境提供了有价值的见解。

</details>


### [95] [More Electrodes, Faster Minds? Rethinking Bandwidth in Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24820)
*Boxuan Jiang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高带宽脑机接口（BCI）在提升有意义的人机输入/输出（I/O）方面的非线性缩放关系，指出虽然更高容量的接口能带来实际增益，但由于受限于具身认知、学习和主体表达等因素，极端提升会遭遇瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 厘清脑机接口带宽与有意义的人类信息吞吐量之间的缩放关系，区分可解码神经状态、实际可用信息等不同层次，避免对BCI能力产生过度乐观的预期。

Method: 通过概念分析和理论推演，区分带宽、可解码神经状态、神经状态以及人能够使用、确认和表达的信息等不同层次，分析慢更新任务状态如何通过身体、神经控制、感觉反馈、环境和共享上下文展开为复杂行为，并探讨刺激输入侧如何引导可塑性和加速学习。

Result: 高容量接口能带来实际增益，但有意义的人类I/O的极端增加面临源于具身认知、学习和主体表达的约束，缩放关系呈现非线性特征。

Conclusion: 脑机接口的性能提升并非与带宽呈线性关系，有意义的人类通信受限于主体层面的选择、确认和授权过程，以及具身技能协调等根本性约束。

Original Abstract: High-bandwidth brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) can bypass damaged pathways, reduce motor costs, and improve communication and control. They also inspire visions of accelerated thought output, mind reading, and instant skill acquisition. This Perspective asks how gains in meaningful human I/O scale with interface capacity. We distinguish bandwidth, decodable neural states, neural states, and information a person can use, confirm, and express. Slowly updated task states can unfold into complex behavior through the body, neural control, sensory feedback, the environment, and shared context. Decodable neural activity can support prediction and control; subject-level communication depends on selection, confirmation, and authorization. On the input side, stimulation may guide plasticity and accelerate learning, while embodied skills arise through coordination of a brain, body, and environment. The scaling relationship is likely nonlinear: higher-capacity interfaces can yield real gains, while extreme increases in meaningful human I/O encounter constraints rooted in embodiment, learning, and subject expression.

中文摘要翻译: 高带宽脑机接口（BCI）可以绕过受损通路、减少运动成本，并改善通信与控制能力。它们也激发了对加速思维输出、读心和即时技能获取的愿景。本文从视角性角度探讨有意义的类人输入/输出如何随接口容量扩展。我们区分了带宽、可解码神经状态、神经状态以及人能够使用、确认和表达的信息。缓慢更新的任务状态可以通过身体、神经控制、感觉反馈、环境和共享上下文展开为复杂行为。可解码的神经活动可以支持预测和控制；主体层面的通信依赖于选择、确认和授权。在输入侧，刺激可能引导可塑性并加速学习，而具身技能则通过大脑、身体和环境的协调产生。缩放关系很可能是非线性的：较高容量的接口可以带来实际增益，而有意义的类人输入/输出的极端增加会面临源于具身认知、学习和主体表达的约束。

</details>


### [96] [Foundation Models for EEG Are Blind to Long-Range Temporal Correlations: A Spectral-Temporal Dissociation Behind Their Cross-Population Fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24834)
*Marzieh Zare*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种EEG基础模型（REVE、LaBraM、BENDR、CBraMod、BIOT）对α频段包络长程时间相关性（LRTC，以DFA指数量化）的表征能力，发现所有模型均未能保留LRTC，而频谱输入模型虽能恢复1/f非周期斜率但无法恢复DFA指数，表明当前EEG基础模型忽视了重要的时间动态结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通常通过重构或对比学习短片段来训练，然后将输出池化为固定嵌入。作者想检验这些预训练嵌入是否保留了脑电信号中关键的长时间尺度动态特征（即α频段包络的LRTC），以及这些特征是否影响跨群体迁移能力。

Method: 在两种跨分布（out-of-distribution）队列上，对五种EEG基础模型（原始波形模型：REVE、LaBraM、BENDR；频谱输入模型：CBraMod、BIOT）进行探测，比较其对DFA指数（量化LRTC）和静态1/f非周期斜率的恢复能力。采用顺序保持和残差化对照实验控制池化效应和非周期阴影；并在三个队列（加入西方参考数据集）上进行导联协调、零样本迁移任务，比较冻结嵌入与DFA指数的表现。

Result: 五种模型均未在时间顺序上表征LRTC。原始波形模型（REVE、LaBraM、BENDR）对DFA指数和1/f斜率恢复均差（R²≤0.12）；频谱输入模型（CBraMod、BIOT）虽能强恢复1/f斜率（R²=0.59-0.73），但无法恢复DFA指数。经典DFA特征可恢复指数（R²=0.32-0.38，信度上限0.64），且LRTC与非周期斜率正交（r=-0.06）。跨群体迁移中，冻结REVE嵌入不优于随机（W→K，0.45），DFA指数虽呈方向性迁移但未达族系显著性；所有模型嵌入被记录位点主导（可解码准确率0.98-1.00），而DFA指针对位点鲁棒（0.71）。

Conclusion: 当前EEG基础模型未能捕获脑电信号中重要的长程时间相关性，而主要编码了与记录位点相关的静态频谱特征（如1/f斜率），表明需要重新设计基础模型架构以显式保留时间顺序动态。

Original Abstract: Objective. Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models (FMs) are trained to reconstruct or contrastively align short patches, then pooled into a fixed embedding. We tested whether these embeddings retained the long-range temporal correlations (LRTC) quantified by the detrended-fluctuation-analysis (DFA) exponent of the alpha-band envelope, and whether it governs cross-population transfer.
  Approach. We probed five EEG FMs spanning raw-waveform and spectral-input architectures (REVE, LaBraM, BENDR, CBraMod, BIOT) on two out-of-distribution cohorts, comparing recovery of the DFA exponent against the static 1/f aperiodic slope. Order-preserving and residualization controls tested for pooling or aperiodic shadowing. A montage-harmonized, zero-shot transfer task compared the frozen embedding with the DFA exponent across three cohorts (adding a Western reference).
  Main results. None of the five FMs represented the LRTC in the temporal order. Raw-waveform models (REVE, LaBraM, BENDR) recovered neither the DFA exponent nor the 1/f slope (R^2 <= 0.12); for these three the probe is uninformative, so the dissociation is specific to the spectral-input models (CBraMod, BIOT), which recovered 1/f strongly (R^2 = 0.59-0.73) but not DFA across cohorts. A classical DFA feature recovered the exponent (R^2 = 0.32-0.38 against a 0.64 reliability ceiling), and LRTC was orthogonal to the aperiodic slope (r = -0.06). On cross-population transfer, the frozen REVE embedding did not beat chance (W to K, 0.45) and the dimensionless DFA exponent transferred directionally but not at family-wise significance; the other four did not uniformly replicate it. All five were dominated by a recording-site axis (decodable at 0.98-1.00 vs. 0.500 chance), whereas the DFA exponent they discard is site-robust (0.71).

中文摘要翻译: 目的。脑电图（EEG）基础模型（FM）通过重构或对比对齐短片段进行训练，然后将输出池化为固定嵌入。本研究检验了这些嵌入是否保留了由α频段包络的去趋势波动分析（DFA）指数量化的长程时间相关性（LRTC），以及该指数是否主导跨群体迁移。方法。我们在两个跨分布队列上探测了五种覆盖原始波形和频谱输入架构的EEG基础模型（REVE、LaBraM、BENDR、CBraMod、BIOT），比较其对DFA指数与静态1/f非周期斜率的恢复能力。采用顺序保持和残差化对照实验控制池化效应或非周期阴影。在三个队列（加入西方参考数据集）上进行导联协调的零样本迁移任务，比较冻结嵌入与DFA指数的表现。主要结果。五种模型均未在时间顺序中表征LRTC。原始波形模型（REVE、LaBraM、BENDR）既未恢复DFA指数也未恢复1/f斜率（R²≤0.12）；对于这三个模型，探测无信息量，因此这一缺失特异性针对频谱输入模型（CBraMod、BIOT），后者在不同队列间强恢复1/f斜率（R²=0.59-0.73）但未恢复DFA指数。经典DFA特征能恢复指数（R²=0.32-0.38，信度上限0.64），且LRTC与非周期斜率正交（r=-0.06）。在跨群体迁移中，冻结的REVE嵌入未优于随机水平（W→K，0.45），无维度的DFA指数呈方向性迁移但未达族系显著性水平；其余四个模型未一致复现该结果。所有五个模型均被记录位点轴主导（可解码准确率0.98-1.00 vs. 随机水平0.500），而它们丢弃的DFA指针对位点鲁棒（0.71）。

</details>


### [97] [A Neural Network model of Cultural Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24886)
*Kingsley J. A. Cox,Paul R. Adams*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于简单神经网络的透明文化演化模型，证明群体通过称为“Light”的选择性交流机制，能够以棘轮式方式逐步学习更好的环境描述，而大多数不交流的个体无法做到。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管社会学习和个体学习都被认为是以类似人工神经网络的突触强度活动依赖性调整方式实现的，但将神经网络融入文化演化模型的尝试相对较少，这导致关于文化特质如何被创造、转化、传播和选择的争议与不确定性。

Method: 基于简单神经网络构建文化演化模型：生成由隐藏"原因"线性组合而成的输入向量，智能体通过非线性Hebbian学习方式从中学习，发现能"解混"数据以揭示隐藏原因的突触权重；智能体之间通过称为"Light"的选择性交流机制共享对隐藏原因的当前估计。

Result: 在大多数不交流的个体无法学会解混权重的条件下，通过"Light"机制交互的群体能够学会解混权重；并且群体能够以棘轮式方式逐步学习更好的解决方案。

Conclusion: 尽管模型高度简化，但这种简单性使人们能够深入理解此前模糊不清的文化演化机制，并为构建更复杂但依然相对透明的分析提供了基础。

Original Abstract: It has been proposed (Richerson and Boyd, 2008) that human intelligence is underpinned by a ratchet-like process called Cultural Evolution in which ideas, originated by individuals, can selectively spread by social learning and replace older, less fruitful ones. Useful ideas can thus accumulate beyond the lifetime of individuals. Although both social and individual learning are thought to be achieved by the selective activity-dependent adjustment of synaptic strengths in an artificial neural net-like manner, there have been relatively few attempts to incorporate neural networks into Cultural Evolution models. This has led to controversy and uncertainty about how cultural traits are created, transformed, transmitted and selected. We have constructed a transparent model of Cultural Evolution based on simple neural networks, and here we show that a population of communicating agents can learn progressively better descriptions of its environment. Specifically we generate input vectors by linearly combining hidden "causes", which agents can learn from in a nonlinear, Hebbian, manner, thus discovering synaptic weights that "unmix" the data to reveal the hidden causes. We previously showed that if agents can communicate their current estimates of hidden causes to other agents, in a selective manner we call "Light", the interacting population can learn unmixing weights under conditions where most noninteracting individuals cannot. Here we show that with "Light" a population can learn progressively better solutions, in a ratchet-like manner. Although our model is highly simplified, this simplicity allows insight into cultural evolution mechanisms that have hitherto been obscure, and provides a stepping stone to more complex, but still relatively transparent, analyses.

中文摘要翻译: 已有研究提出（Richerson and Boyd, 2008），人类智能的基础是一种被称为文化演化的棘轮式过程，其中个体创造的思想通过社会学习选择性传播，并替代旧的、低效的思想。有用的思想因此能够在个体生命周期之外不断积累。尽管社会学习和个体学习都被认为是通过类似人工神经网络的方式，对突触强度进行选择性活动依赖性调整来实现的，但将神经网络融入文化演化模型的尝试相对较少。这导致了关于文化特质如何被创造、转化、传播和选择的争议与不确定性。我们构建了一个基于简单神经网络的透明文化演化模型，并在此证明，一组相互通信的智能体能够逐步学习对其环境的更好描述。具体而言，我们通过线性组合隐藏的"原因"生成输入向量，智能体以非线性Hebbian方式从中学习，从而发现能够"解混"数据以揭示隐藏原因的突触权重。我们此前曾证明，如果智能体能够通过一种称为"Light"的选择性方式，将其对隐藏原因的当前估计传递给其他智能体，那么在大多数不互动的个体无法学会解混权重的条件下，互动群体能够学会解混权重。本文证明，通过"Light"机制，群体能够以棘轮式方式逐步学习更好的解决方案。尽管我们的模型高度简化，但这种简单性使人们能够深入理解此前模糊不清的文化演化机制，并为构建更复杂但依然相对透明的分析提供了铺垫。

</details>


### [98] [When Branch-Local Shunting Helps: A Gain-Load-Alignment Principle for Dendritic E/I Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24990)
*Houman Safaai,Maceo Richards,Naeem Khoshnevis,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出DendriNet框架，系统研究生物神经元中兴奋/抑制分枝树突的分流整合机制对群体编码读取的影响。结果表明分流整合在局部线性化条件下等价于加性E/I整合，其优势取决于增益-负载-对齐原则，而非深度或分流本身固有优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物神经元在分枝树突上通过分流机制（抑制对兴奋进行除性衰减）整合兴奋和抑制活动，但分流整合相对于加性整合能否改善群体编码读取尚不明确，本文旨在系统探究这一核心问题。

Method: 提出DendriNet可训练框架，可变化整合规则、形态结构、突触分配、除数局部性和树突非线性。通过理论分析和实验验证，研究分流读取的局部线性化、增益-负载-对齐原则、深层分流网络性能等。

Result: (1)分流读取的局部线性化等价于正加性E/I锥内的决策方向；(2)标量分流阈值存在精确仿射加性实现；(3)分流优势取决于增益-负载-对齐原则；(4)深层分流优于切线控制和拟合线性控制，但灵活非线性预测器在足够标签下超越它；(5)支持混洗和传感器损坏反转了线性比较结果；(6)小鼠V1实验中分流与加性解码器差距取决于读取宽度和私有噪声。

Conclusion: 分流整合在群体编码读取中的优势并非固有，而是取决于具体任务条件（增益-负载对齐、读取宽度、噪声水平等），形态结构可决定可靠干扰估计与任务相关信号的相遇位置，但深度和分流本身并无内在优势。

Original Abstract: Biological neurons combine excitatory and inhibitory (E/I) activity on branched dendrites through shunting, in which inhibition divisively attenuates excitation. Whether this improves population readout over additive E/I integration of the same nonnegative inputs remains unclear. We introduce DendriNet, a trainable framework that varies integration rule, morphology, synaptic allocation, divisor locality, and dendritic nonlinearities. For population codes with multiplicative gain, a local linearization of any realizable shunting readout yields a decision direction within the positive additive E/I cone; matching the additive optimum requires a positive self-consistent shunting realization. Every scalar shunting threshold also has an exact affine additive realization. Beyond this local limit, performance follows a gain-load-alignment principle: branch-local shunting helps when a reliable divisor suppresses signal-aligned gain more than it attenuates signal or adds denominator variability. Passive additive trees flatten to linear readouts, whereas shunting trees compose local divisors. In a designed hierarchy, deep shunting outperforms tangent and fitted-linear controls, but flexible nonlinear predictors overtake it with enough labels. Support shuffling reverses the linear comparisons, sensor corruption reverses the fitted-linear comparison, and resource-matched activated training shows no consistent depth benefit. The same support and reliability interaction appears in frozen-feature normalization. Across three mouse V1 sessions, the shunting-over-additive decoder gap is largest for narrow readouts, reverses under strong private noise at the widest readout, and varies across running states. Morphology can determine where reliable nuisance estimates meet task-relevant signals, but neither depth nor shunting is intrinsically advantageous.

中文摘要翻译: 生物神经元在分枝树突上通过分流机制（抑制对兴奋进行除性衰减）整合兴奋和抑制活动。对于相同非负输入的加性E/I整合，这种机制能否改善群体编码读取尚不清楚。我们引入DendriNet，一个可训练框架，可变化整合规则、形态结构、突触分配、除数局部性和树突非线性。对于具有乘法增益的群体编码，任何可实现的分流读取的局部线性化都会在正加性E/I锥内产生一个决策方向；达到加性最优需要正自洽的分流实现。每个标量分流阈值也存在精确的仿射加性实现。在此局部极限之外，性能遵循增益-负载-对齐原则：当可靠的除数抑制信号对齐增益的程度超过其衰减信号或增加分母变异时，分支局部分流有所助益。被动加性树突树退化到线性读取，而分流树突树构成局部除数。在设计好的层次结构中，深层分流优于切线控制和拟合线性控制，但在足够标签下灵活的非线性预测器会超越它。支持混洗反转了线性比较结果，传感器损坏反转了拟合线性比较结果，资源匹配的激活训练未显示一致的深度优势。相同的支持和可靠性交互作用出现在冻结特征归一化中。在三个小鼠V1会话中，分流相对于加性解码器的差距在窄读取中最大，在宽读取下强私有噪声时反转，并随奔跑状态变化。形态结构可以决定可靠的干扰估计在何处与任务相关信号相遇，但深度和分流本身并无内在优势。

</details>


### [99] [Phantom Evidence: How and Why Generative AI Manufactures False Positives in Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25991)
*Yukiyasu Kamitani,Ken Shirakawa*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 生成式AI让“以说服力代替证据”这一古老科学谬误以前所未有的规模和速度重现。问题不在于证据变弱，而在于观察者高估了AI输出覆盖的可能性空间。论文提出“幻象证据”概念，用概率形式化描述这一差距，并指出：更高分辨率与流畅度不增加证据；单次结果能承载的证据有上限；在说服力变得廉价的当下，科学可信度应依赖程序性验证而非输出本身的说服力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 培根四百年前警告过“以少数事实仓促概括”的谬误，而生成式AI让“说服力替代证据”这一长期存在于科学实践中的问题以前所未有的规模和速度重现，亟需理论化分析并给出解决方案。

Method: 提出“幻象证据”概念，将其形式化为一个概率量——观察者所想象的AI输出可能性空间（广阔）与其实际可达范围（狭窄）之间的差距，该量同时吸收了研究过程中的试错和数据泄漏效应。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 更高分辨率与流畅度不增加证据；(2) 单次结果的证据容量存在上限，无法通过打磨输出或让生成系统自我评估来超越；(3) 已发表发现中为真的比例会退回至任何观测之前的状态。

Conclusion: 科学可信度的处方在于培根式的“缺席表”——用概率语言重述：真正拓宽系统可达范围，并测量目标缺失时说服性输出是否依然出现。在说服力变得廉价的世界里，科学的可信度应依赖证明结果不可能由偶然产生的程序，而非更令人信服的输出本身。

Original Abstract: Four centuries ago Francis Bacon warned against the anticipations of nature, hasty generalization that wins assent on a few facts, and set against it the table of absence: checking that a property fails to appear where it should not. The demand was that looking convincing should not, on its own, count as evidence. Science has professed that demand ever since, while in practice letting persuasiveness do the work of evidence. It could be let to do so because making something persuasive was itself hard. Generative AI removes that difficulty, and an old error returns on a scale and at a speed it never had before. We locate the problem not in evidence growing weaker but in how surprise is counted. An observer marvels at a convincing output as a single point hit among a vast range of possibilities, yet what a system can actually reach is a small part of that range. The gap between the breadth imagined and the narrowness actually reached is what we call phantom evidence, and we formalize it as one quantity that also absorbs the trial and error and the data leakage a research process adds. Three things follow. Higher resolution and greater fluency add no evidence. The evidence a single result can carry has a ceiling that neither polishing the output nor letting a generative system grade itself can exceed. And the fraction of published findings that are true falls back to what it was before anything was observed. The prescription lies in the same place: genuinely widen what a system can reach, and measure whether convincing outputs still appear when the target is absent -- Bacon's table of absence, restated in the language of probability. In a world where the persuasive has become cheap, the credibility of science rests not on more convincing outputs but on procedures that show they could not have arisen by chance.

中文摘要翻译: 四百年前，弗朗西斯·培根警告过对自然的预断——仅凭少数事实就获得认同的草率概括，并提出了与之对立的“缺席表”：检查某一属性在其不应出现之处是否确实缺失。其要求是：看起来令人信服本身不应算作证据。自那时起，科学界一直宣称遵循这一原则，却在实践中让说服力充当证据的角色。说服力之所以能被这样使用，是因为让某物具有说服力本身是困难的。生成式AI消除了这一困难，导致一个古老的谬误以史无前例的规模和速度卷土重来。我们将问题定位于证据并未变弱，而在于惊讶如何被计算。观察者赞叹一个令人信服的输出，视之为广阔可能性空间中被精确命中的一点，然而系统实际能够到达的只是该空间的一小部分。想象中的广度与实际到达的狭窄性之间的差距，我们称之为“幻象证据”，并将其形式化为一个量，该量同时吸纳了研究过程中试错和数据泄漏所产生的效应。由此得出三点推论。第一，更高的分辨率和更强的流畅度不增加任何证据。第二，单个结果所能承载的证据存在一个上限，无论是打磨输出还是让生成系统自我评分都无法超越。第三，已发表发现中为真的比例回落至任何观测之前的状态。解决之道仍在于同一个方向：真正拓宽系统所能到达的范围，并测量当目标缺席时令人信服的输出是否依然出现——这正是培根的“缺席表”，以概率语言重述。在一个说服力已变得廉价的世界里，科学的可信度所依凭的，不是更令人信服的输出，而是那些能够证明结果不可能由偶然产生的程序。

</details>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Towards Real-World RUL Prediction for Aircraft Compressors Under Variable Conditions with Physics-Guided Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24809)
*Yang Zhang,Shashvat Prakash,Jiong Tang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理引导处理与自适应时序编码的框架，用于预测商用飞机离心式空调压缩机的剩余使用寿命，解决了跨域预测中性能严重下降的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际商用环境中，传感器信号叠加了真实退化与持续变化的工况，且无可靠退化通道先验；模型在同机队不同飞机与安装位置间泛化能力差，跨域问题在实际中严重但未被充分认识。

Method: (1) 群体级筛选选择喘振裕度作为退化指标；(2) 工况过滤与滑动窗口聚合恢复健康趋势；(3) 两个物理驱动特征（当前健康水平与累积退化速率）实现跨单元比较；(4) 正弦位置编码提供生命周期上下文；(5) 结构化跨域评估发现编码周期不匹配是性能损失主因；(6) 自适应重校准方案仅利用早期观测估计目标单元生命周期尺度。

Result: 所提方法在跨域距离递增的迁移场景下均取得了显著且一致的跨域精度提升；高斯过程回归模型提供了校准的不确定性估计，支持风险导向的维护调度。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了实际商用运营中离心式空调压缩机的跨域剩余寿命预测问题，自适应重校准方案无需未来信息或标注目标数据即可显著提升泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Remaining useful life prediction for aircraft centrifugal air compressors in real commercial operations poses challenges that controlled benchmark datasets do not expose. In-flight sensor signals superimpose genuine degradation on continuously varying operating conditions, and no channel can be assumed a priori to carry a reliable degradation signature. Moreover, models trained on one subset of a fleet generalize poorly to unseen aircraft and installation positions, a cross-domain problem of underappreciated practical severity. This paper presents a framework combining physics-guided processing with adaptive temporal encoding. A population-level selection procedure identifies surge margin as the most consistent degradation indicator across the fleet. Operating regime filtering and windowed aggregation recover the health trend from operational noise, and two physically motivated features encoding current health level and cumulative degradation rate enable cross-unit comparison without absolute signal values. Sinusoidal positional encoding provides lifecycle context without data leakage, and a structured cross-domain evaluation identifies encoding period mismatch as the primary mechanism of performance loss under fleet transfer. An adaptive recalibration scheme estimates each target unit's lifecycle scale from early observations alone, requiring no future information or labeled target data. The approach yields substantial, consistent gains in cross-domain accuracy across transfer scenarios of increasing domain distance, and a Gaussian Process Regression model further provides calibrated uncertainty estimates supporting risk-informed maintenance scheduling.

中文摘要翻译: 在真实商业运营中预测飞机离心式空调压缩机的剩余使用寿命，面临着受控基准数据集无法暴露的挑战。飞行中的传感器信号将真实的退化叠加在持续变化的运行工况之上，且没有任何通道可以预先假定携带可靠的退化特征。此外，在一个机队子集上训练的模型难以泛化到未见过的飞机和安装位置，这是一个实际严重性未被充分认识的跨域问题。本文提出了一种结合物理引导处理与自适应时序编码的框架。群体级筛选过程识别出喘振裕度是整个机队中最一致的退化指标。运行工况过滤和滑动窗口聚合从运行噪声中恢复健康趋势，两个基于物理动机的特征——编码当前健康水平和累积退化速率——实现了无需绝对信号值的跨单元比较。正弦位置编码在无数据泄漏的前提下提供生命周期上下文，结构化跨域评估识别出编码周期不匹配是机队迁移下性能损失的主要机制。自适应重校准方案仅利用早期观测估计每个目标单元的生命周期尺度，无需未来信息或标注的目标数据。该方法在跨域距离递增的迁移场景下均取得了显著且一致的跨域精度提升，高斯过程回归模型进一步提供了校准的不确定性估计，支持风险导向的维护调度。

</details>


### [101] [Dynamic Multi-Criteria Bottleneck Severity Index (DMBSI) for Semiconductor Wafer Manufacturing: A Genetically Optimised Framework for Reentrant Production Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24819)
*Mohammad Sharifur Rahman,Karl McCreadie,Saugat Bhattacharyya,M M Manjurul Islam,Cormac McAteer,Bryan John Baker,Nuala Parker,Girijesh Prasad*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出动态多准则瓶颈严重性指数(DMBSI)，一种数据驱动的半导体晶圆制造瓶颈识别方法，通过分析周期时间诊断信号和返工影响，在希捷200mm产线22批次数据上实现r=0.80的相关性，优于专家启发式(0.74)、约束理论(0.60)和价值流映射(-0.30)，并可识别瓶颈迁移模式及反事实优化效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 半导体晶圆制造具有重入工艺流、可变瓶颈和高度可变工艺条件等独特特征，现有瓶颈识别方法（如TOC、VSM）难以准确捕捉动态变化的瓶颈，亟需一种数据驱动的、可解释的统一度量方法来实时识别最严重的瓶颈。

Method: 提出动态多准则瓶颈严重性指数(DMBSI)，一种数据驱动方法，通过分析周期时间对工艺参数变化的多个诊断信号以及返工对周期时间的影响，生成可解释的统一瓶颈严重性度量。采用遗传算法(GA)优化，并在希捷科技200mm晶圆厂的22批次MES日志数据上进行5折交叉验证。

Result: GA优化的DMBSI在5折交叉验证中与实测周期时间贡献的Pearson相关系数r=0.80，比专家启发式基线(r=0.74)提升8.1%，显著优于TOC(r=0.60)和VSM(r=-0.30)。时间窗口分析揭示了瓶颈从早期电介质沉积步骤向后期的过度/不足检测步骤迁移的模式。反事实分析表明，排名第一的瓶颈步骤等待时间减少50%可使平均周期时间降低7.2%，前五大瓶颈步骤合计可降低约19%。

Conclusion: DMBSI能够有效识别半导体晶圆制造中的动态瓶颈，具备时序瓶颈迁移模式识别能力，并能通过反事实分析量化瓶颈缓解的潜在收益，为晶圆厂的产能优化和调度决策提供了实用工具。

Original Abstract: Wafer fabrication exhibits unique characteristics, including reentrant process flows, variable bottlenecks, and highly variable process conditions. In order to identify the most severe bottleneck at each moment in time for semiconductor wafer fabrication, this research presents the Dynamic Multi-Criteria Bottleneck Severity Index (DMBSI), a new, data-driven methodology for analysing multiple diagnostic signals of cycle time to changes in process parameters, and the impact of reworks on cycle time in order to generate an interpretable, unified measure of bottleneck severity. The experimental validation of DMBSI was conducted using Manufacturing Execution System (MES) logs collected from 22 wafer production lots at a commercial 200 mm wafer fabrication line operated by Seagate Technology. Using 5-fold cross-validation, the GA-optimised DMBSI achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.80 with observed cycle-time contributions, representing an 8.1% improvement over the expert heuristic baseline (r = 0.74) and substantially outperforming the Theory of Constraints (TOC; r = 0.60) and Value Stream Mapping (VSM; r = -0.30). Furthermore, the unique time-windowed component of DMBSI enabled the identification of temporal bottleneck migration patterns, which shifted from the dominant constraints associated with dielectric deposition steps in the early production windows to those associated with over- and under-inspection in the later production windows. The integrated what-if counterfactual analysis demonstrated that a 50% reduction in waiting time at the top-ranked bottleneck step would reduce the mean cycle time by 7.2%, with the top five bottleneck steps offering a combined potential reduction of approximately 19%.

中文摘要翻译: 晶圆制造具有独特的特性，包括重入工艺流、可变瓶颈以及高度可变的工艺条件。为了在半导体晶圆制造中识别每个时刻最严重的瓶颈，本研究提出了动态多准则瓶颈严重性指数（DMBSI），这是一种新的、数据驱动的方法论，用于分析周期时间对工艺参数变化的多个诊断信号以及返工对周期时间的影响，从而生成一个可解释的、统一的瓶颈严重性度量。DMBSI的实验验证使用了希捷科技运营的一条商用200毫米晶圆生产线上22个晶圆生产批次的制造执行系统（MES）日志数据。通过5折交叉验证，遗传算法优化的DMBSI与实测周期时间贡献的Pearson相关系数达到r=0.80，相比专家启发式基线（r=0.74）提升了8.1%，并显著优于约束理论（TOC；r=0.60）和价值流映射（VSM；r=-0.30）。此外，DMBSI独特的时间窗口组件能够识别时间上的瓶颈迁移模式，该模式从早期生产窗口中与电介质沉积步骤相关的主导约束，转移到后期生产窗口中与过度检测和不足检测相关的约束。集成的反事实假设分析表明，排名第一的瓶颈步骤的等待时间减少50%可使平均周期时间降低7.2%，而前五大瓶颈步骤合计可实现约19%的潜在降低。

</details>


### [102] [Efficient Automatic Modulation Classification for Next-Generation Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24831)
*To Truong An,Antonios Argyriou,Annisa Anggun Puspitasari,Simon L. Cotton,Byung Moo Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为阈值去噪循环神经网络（TDRNN）的深度学习模型，用于6G网络中的自动调制分类（AMC），该模型结合自适应阈值去噪算法与循环神经网络，在保证高精度的同时实现快速推理，实验表明其在准确率、速度和计算复杂度上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着第六代（6G）网络的快速发展，需要高精度、高计算效率且低推理时间的自动调制分类（AMC）算法，现有方法难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出阈值去噪循环神经网络（TDRNN），包含两个联合优化的子系统：（1）自适应阈值去噪（TD）模块，用于自适应降低接收信号的噪声水平；（2）循环神经网络（RNN）模块，用于对去噪后的信号进行调制分类。两个子系统通过联合优化达到最优架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，TDRNN在多种调制方案和信噪比（SNR）条件下，在准确率、速度和计算复杂度方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TDRNN是6G无线通信系统中自动调制分类的理想解决方案。

Original Abstract: With the imminent development of sixth-generation (6G) networks, there will be a demand for high-accuracy, computationally-efficient, and low-inference time automatic modulation classification (AMC) algorithms. To address this need, we propose a new deep-learning based model for AMC that is called the threshold denoise recurrent neural network (TDRNN). The TDRNN combines an adaptive threshold denoising (TD) algorithm and a recurrent neural network (RNN) that together achieve high accuracy and fast inference. The TD module adaptively reduces the noise level of the received signal, while the RNN module performs the modulation classification on the denoised result. The two subsystems are jointly optimized to reach the optimal architecture. The proposed TDRNN is evaluated for various modulation schemes and signal-to-noise ratios (SNR). The experimental results demonstrate that the TDRNN outperforms existing methods in terms of accuracy, speed, and computational complexity making it an ideal solution for 6G wireless communication systems.

中文摘要翻译: 随着第六代（6G）网络的即将发展，将需要高精度、高计算效率且低推理时间的自动调制分类（AMC）算法。为满足这一需求，我们提出了一种新的基于深度学习的自动调制分类模型，称为阈值去噪循环神经网络（TDRNN）。TDRNN结合了自适应阈值去噪（TD）算法和循环神经网络（RNN），共同实现了高精度和快速推理。TD模块自适应地降低接收信号的噪声水平，而RNN模块对去噪后的结果进行调制分类。两个子系统经过联合优化以达到最优架构。所提出的TDRNN在多种调制方案和信噪比（SNR）下进行了评估。实验结果表明，TDRNN在准确率、速度和计算复杂度方面均优于现有方法，使其成为6G无线通信系统的理想解决方案。

</details>


### [103] [Sensing-Aware Backscatter Communications: A Survey on Envelope Stability, Waveform Design, and Selection Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24899)
*Rahul Gulia,Ashish Sheikh,Feyisayo Favour Popoola,Serisha Vadlamudi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了感知感知反向散射通信，聚焦于包络稳定性、空间分集和时域信道鲁棒性三个紧密耦合的维度，提出了“照明器困境”概念来描述多载波波形与无源标签动态范围需求之间的固有冲突，并提供了多目标波形设计、远近效应缓解和信道状态信息老化等问题的统一处理框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着反向散射系统从简单识别标签向高保真集成感知与通信(ISAC)设备演进，在波形设计、硬件非线性和信道动态性的交叉领域出现了根本性挑战，亟需一个系统性综述来统一理解这些挑战及其解决方案。

Method: 采用综合性调查方法，对感知感知反向散射通信进行系统性文献综述，提出统一的多目标波形设计策略、远近效应缓解技术分类学，以及对反向散射网络中信道状态信息老化的深入分析。

Result: 提出了以标签为中心的度量指标，包括反向散射波峰因子(BCF)、包络稳定性因子(ESF)和感知保真度指数(SFI)；对PAPR降低技术、空间分集方法和非相干检测方案进行了比较评估。

Conclusion: 该综述为无线通信、物联网、射频感知及集成感知与通信领域的研究人员、工程师和研究生提供了反向散射通信在感知增强方向上的系统性参考框架。

Original Abstract: The vision of ubiquitous battery-free connectivity has positioned backscatter communication as a foundational technology for next-generation Internet of Things (IoT) and industrial sensing applications. However, as backscatter systems evolve from simple identification tags toward high-fidelity integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) devices, fundamental challenges emerge at the intersection of waveform design, hardware nonlinearity, and channel dynamics. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of sensing-aware backscatter communications, with a focus on three tightly coupled dimensions: envelope stability, spatial diversity, and temporal channel robustness. We introduce the concept of the ``Illuminator's Dilemma'' to describe the inherent conflict between high-peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) multicarrier waveforms optimized for active communication and the stringent dynamic range requirements of passive tags. The survey provides a unified treatment of multi-objective waveform design strategies, a comprehensive taxonomy of near-far mitigation techniques, and an in-depth analysis of channel state information (CSI) aging in backscatter networks. Key contributions include tag-centric metrics such as the Backscatter Crest Factor (BCF), Envelope Stability Factor (ESF), and Sensing Fidelity Index (SFI), along with a comparative evaluation of PAPR reduction techniques, spatial diversity methods, and non-coherent detection schemes. This survey is intended for researchers, engineers, and graduate students working in wireless communications, IoT, RF sensing, and integrated sensing and communications.

中文摘要翻译: 无处不在的无电池连接愿景已将反向散射通信定位为下一代物联网（IoT）和工业传感应用的基础技术。然而，随着反向散射系统从简单的识别标签向高保真集成感知与通信（ISAC）设备演进，在波形设计、硬件非线性和信道动态性的交叉领域出现了根本性挑战。本文对感知感知反向散射通信进行了全面综述，重点关注三个紧密耦合的维度：包络稳定性、空间分集和时域信道鲁棒性。我们引入了“照明器困境”这一概念，用以描述为主动通信优化的高峰均功率比（PAPR）多载波波形与无源标签严格的动态范围需求之间的固有冲突。本综述提供了多目标波形设计策略的统一处理、远近效应缓解技术的全面分类法，以及对反向散射网络中信道状态信息（CSI）老化的深入分析。关键贡献包括以标签为中心的度量指标，如反向散射波峰因子（BCF）、包络稳定性因子（ESF）和感知保真度指数（SFI），以及对PAPR降低技术、空间分集方法和非相干检测方案的比较评估。本综述面向无线通信、物联网、射频感知以及集成感知与通信领域的研究人员、工程师和研究生。

</details>


### [104] [Conceptual and Signal Processing Principles of Spread-Spectrum Color Doppler Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24979)
*Kian Esmailian,James C. Lacefield*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于扩频彩色多普勒成像的简化数学模型，分析了不同梳状滤波实现方式对多普勒频率估计精度的影响，并与单平面波成像进行了对比，为后续实际应用奠定了基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩频彩色多普勒方法在超快血流成像中具有高尼奎斯特速度、高帧率和长序列的优势，但其数学机理尚未充分阐明，特别是在不同梳状滤波器实现方式下的频率估计精度、空间定位能力以及对背景组织运动的敏感性方面缺乏系统分析。

Method: 建立了一个用于扩频彩色多普勒慢时间信号的简化数学模型，通过分析不同离散时间梳状滤波器实现方式对多普勒频率估计精度的影响，并与单平面波成像方法进行对比，评估该方法在空间定位和背景组织运动敏感性方面的表现。

Result: 揭示了扩频方法中平面波角度随机化对聚焦和离焦回波的不同调制机制，分析了不同梳状滤波器对多普勒频率估计精度的影响，证明了扩频方法在空间定位方面优于单平面波成像，并表征了该方法对背景组织运动的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该研究为扩频彩色多普勒方法奠定了理论基础，有助于进一步改善该方法在非平稳杂波和生理相关血流模式等实际应用场景中的实用性。

Original Abstract: The spread-spectrum color Doppler method provides an alternative to single-plane-wave color Doppler for ultrafast blood-flow imaging with a high Nyquist velocity, high frame rate, and long ensembles. Spread-spectrum images are acquired by transmitting plane waves at a sequence of azimuth angles that is repeated multiple times in a different random order during each repetition. Randomization of the plane-wave angle modulates in-focus and off-focus echoes in distinct manners that enable stationary clutter to be suppressed without compounding by application of a notching comb filter. In this paper, a simplified mathematical model for the slow-time signal acquired with a sequence of plane waves is used to explain the mechanics of the spread-spectrum method, investigate the effect of different discrete-time comb filter realizations on the accuracy of the resulting Doppler frequency estimates, compare the effectiveness of spread-spectrum to single-plane-wave imaging for spatial localization of a Doppler signal source, and characterize the method's sensitivity to background tissue motion. The results establish a foundation to further improve the utility of the spread-spectrum method for practical applications involving non-stationary clutter and physiologically relevant flow patterns.

中文摘要翻译: 扩频彩色多普勒方法为超快血流成像提供了一种替代单平面波彩色多普勒的方案，具有高尼奎斯特速度、高帧率和长序列的优势。扩频图像通过发射一系列方位角平面波获取，该序列在每次重复中以不同的随机顺序多次重复。平面波角度的随机化以不同方式调制聚焦和离焦回波，从而可以通过应用陷波梳状滤波器在不进行复合叠加的情况下抑制静止杂波。本文利用一种简化的慢时间信号数学模型（基于平面波序列采集的信号），解释了扩频方法的机理，研究了不同离散时间梳状滤波器实现方式对多普勒频率估计精度的影响，比较了扩频方法与单平面波成像在空间定位多普勒信号源方面的有效性，并表征了该方法对背景组织运动的敏感性。研究结果为在涉及非平稳杂波和生理相关血流模式的实际应用中进一步提高扩频方法的实用性奠定了基础。

</details>


### [105] [Joint Access and Fronthaul Resource Allocation for Cell-Free Massive MIMO with Wireless Fronthaul](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24993)
*Ozan Alp Topal,Özlem Tuğfe Demir,Emil Björnson,Cicek Cavdar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无线前传约束下的无小区大规模MIMO网络，提出了一种联合接入与前传资源分配算法，揭示了前传功率分配的修正注水结构，证明了前传严重受限会导致空间性能差异，并指出拆分选项8在无线前传下不可行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无线前传是实现灵活可扩展无小区大规模MIMO系统的关键，但其有限容量对维持高且均匀的用户性能构成重大挑战，因此需要分析并优化该场景下的系统性能。

Method: 提出了一种联合接入和前传资源分配算法，在满足前传负载约束的条件下最大化最小用户频谱效率，并基于该算法分析了功率分配的最优结构。

Result: 1) 无线前传上的功率分配遵循修正注水结构，水位由接入和前传信道增益共同决定；2) 严重的前传限制不仅降低用户速率，还会根据云位置引入空间性能差异；3) 拆分选项8在无线前传约束下不可行。

Conclusion: 动态前传比特分配对于降低前传负载和实现高效系统运行至关重要，拆分选项8在无线前传下不实用。

Original Abstract: Wireless fronthaul is a key enabler of flexible and scalable cell-free massive MIMO systems, but its limited capacity poses significant challenges for maintaining high and uniform user performance. In this work, we analyze the performance of a cell-free massive MIMO network with wireless fronthaul under realistic low physical layer functional splits. We propose a joint access and fronthaul resource allocation algorithm that maximizes the minimum user equipment (UE) spectral efficiency while satisfying fronthaul load constraints. Our analysis reveals that power allocation over the wireless fronthaul follows a modified water-filling structure, where the water level is jointly determined by the access and fronthaul channel gains. Furthermore, we show that severe fronthaul limitations not only reduce UE rates but also introduce spatial performance disparities depending on the cloud location. Finally, we demonstrate that split option 8 is impractical under wireless fronthaul constraints, underscoring the importance of dynamic fronthaul bit allocation to reduce fronthaul load and enable efficient system operation.

中文摘要翻译: 无线前传是实现灵活可扩展的无小区大规模MIMO系统的关键使能技术，但其有限容量对维持高且均匀的用户性能构成了重大挑战。本文分析了在现实低物理层功能拆分下具有无线前传的无小区大规模MIMO网络的性能。我们提出了一种联合接入和前传资源分配算法，该算法在满足前传负载约束的同时最大化最小用户设备频谱效率。我们的分析表明，无线前传上的功率分配遵循修正的注水结构，其中水位由接入和前传信道增益共同决定。此外，我们证明了严重的前传限制不仅会降低用户速率，还会根据云位置引入空间性能差异。最后，我们展示了拆分选项8在无线前传约束下不实用，这凸显了动态前传比特分配对于降低前传负载和实现高效系统运行的重要性。

</details>


### [106] [Characterizing and Mitigating the Effects of Device Temperature on RF Fingerprinting Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25070)
*Haytham Albousayri,Bechir Hamdaoui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种温度感知的射频指纹识别（RFFP）框架，通过将设备温度信息显式融入学习过程，有效缓解温度变化对设备认证性能的影响，在真实BLE数据集上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有射频指纹识别方法忽略了温度对设备硬件签名的影响，而温度变化（内部和环境影响）会显著改变设备信号特征，导致分类性能下降，亟需一种对温度鲁棒的RFFP方法。

Method: 提出一种温度感知的RFFP框架，将设备温度信息显式地融入深度学习模型的训练过程中，使模型能够学习温度与设备指纹之间的关联，从而提升对温度变化的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在多个设备和环境条件下采集的真实BLE数据集上，温度感知建模方法在分类准确率上持续优于其他温度缓解基线方法，尤其在未见过的温度和环境下表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 温度感知的RFFP框架能有效解决温度敏感性这一关键缺陷，显著提高设备认证的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为实际部署提供了重要方向。

Original Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprinting (RFFP) has emerged as a promising approach for device authentication by exploiting hardware-specific impairments embedded in transmitted signals. Yet existing methods largely overlook a major drawback: RFFP sensitivity to temperature--a critical factor influenced by both internal and environmental conditions--which can significantly alter device signatures and degrade classification performance. In this paper, we propose a novel temperature-aware RFFP framework that explicitly incorporates device temperature information into the learning process to improve robustness and generalization. We evaluate the proposed method on a real-world Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) dataset collected across multiple devices and environmental conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that temperature-aware modeling consistently outperforms other temperature mitigation baselines, achieving significant improvements in classification accuracy, particularly under unseen temperature and environmental conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 射频指纹识别（RFFP）利用嵌入在发射信号中与硬件相关的缺陷，已成为设备认证的一种有前景的方法。然而，现有方法在很大程度上忽略了一个主要缺陷：RFFP对温度的敏感性——这是一个受内部和环境影响的关键因素——会显著改变设备签名并降低分类性能。本文提出了一种新颖的温度感知RFFP框架，该框架将设备温度信息显式地融入学习过程，以提高鲁棒性和泛化能力。我们在多个设备和环境条件下采集的真实蓝牙低功耗（BLE）数据集上评估了所提方法。实验结果表明，温度感知建模在分类准确率上持续优于其他温度缓解基线方法，尤其在未见过的温度和环境条件下取得了显著提升。

</details>


### [107] [Covert Semantic Transmission in ISAC: Dual-Functional Waveform Design and Rectified Flow-Assisted Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25354)
*Yunfan Bai,Yuwen Qian,Cheng Zeng,Zhen Mei,Zhaohui Yang,Wei Zhu,Shuning Zhang,Feng Shu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSMIC，一种面向隐蔽性的语义通感一体化（ISAC）框架，通过语义旋转编码将感知结果嵌入双功能波形，并设计可靠性引导的整流流（RFlow）精炼器实现高保真语义重建，在严格隐蔽约束下实现优于扩散基线的语义重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开放无线信道使得双功能波形易被检测，传统ISAC难以同时保证隐蔽性、感知保真度和语义准确性。

Method: 1）语义旋转编码：将语义潜变量映射为高斯参考序列的成对旋转和缩放，并通过可微预算投影满足闭式隐蔽约束；2）可靠性引导的整流流（RFlow）精炼器：从粗观测中重建高保真语义表示。

Result: 在严格隐蔽约束下，CoSMIC的语义重建质量比扩散基线方案提升18%，推理延迟显著降低；同时雷达匹配滤波器主瓣响应不变，输出信干噪比有界。

Conclusion: CoSMIC验证了隐蔽性导向语义ISAC在实用场景中的可行性，兼顾隐蔽性、感知与语义性能。

Original Abstract: Semantic integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is envisioned as a promising paradigm for efficient and intelligent connectivity in future wireless networks. However, the open wireless channel exposes the dual-functional waveform to detection, which challenges the joint guarantee of covertness, sensing fidelity, and semantic accuracy. To address the challenge, we propose CoSMIC, a novel covertness-oriented semantic ISAC framework, where the sensing output is embedded into a dual-functional ISAC waveform through semantic modulation. Specifically, a semantic rotation coding scheme is established to map semantic latents onto the pairwise rotation and scaling of Gaussian reference sequences, which satisfies a derived closed-form covertness constraint by a differentiable budget projection. Moreover, the radar performance is analyzed to confirm an invariant matched-filter mainlobe response and a bounded output signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) under the semantic embedding. Subsequently, a reliability-guided rectified flow (RFlow) refiner is designed to effectively reconstruct high-fidelity semantic representations from coarse observations. Simulation results demonstrate that CoSMIC improves the semantic reconstruction quality by 18% over diffusion-based baseline schemes with substantially reduced inference latency under strict covertness constraints, which validates the applicability to practical ISAC scenarios. The source code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/LanceAnlan/CoSMIC-covertness-oriented-semantic-ISAC-framework.

中文摘要翻译: 语义通感一体化（ISAC）被视为未来无线网络中实现高效智能连接的一种有前景的范式。然而，开放的无线信道使得双功能波形面临被检测的风险，这对同时保障隐蔽性、感知保真度和语义准确性提出了挑战。为应对这一挑战，本文提出CoSMIC，一种新颖的面向隐蔽性的语义ISAC框架，通过语义调制将感知输出嵌入到双功能ISAC波形中。具体而言，建立了一种语义旋转编码方案，将语义潜变量映射为高斯参考序列的成对旋转和缩放，并通过可微预算投影满足推导出的闭式隐蔽性约束。此外，雷达性能分析表明，在语义嵌入下匹配滤波器主瓣响应保持不变，输出信干噪比（SINR）有界。随后，设计了一种可靠性引导的整流流（RFlow）精炼器，从粗观测中有效重建高保真语义表示。仿真结果表明，在严格隐蔽约束条件下，CoSMIC的语义重建质量相比扩散基线方案提升18%，且推理延迟大幅降低，验证了其在实用ISAC场景中的适用性。源代码和视频演示可在 https://github.com/LanceAnlan/CoSMIC-covertness-oriented-semantic-ISAC-framework 获取。

</details>


### [108] [Joint Channel Estimation and Data Detection for Multi-LEO-Satellite Cell-Free OTFS Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25562)
*Gangle Sun,Tianhao Liu,Jun Tian,Xin Cheng,Jian Wu,Jinfang Jiang,Wenjin Wang,Shi Jin,Guangjie Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对多低地球轨道卫星无蜂窝正交时频空间上行链路，提出了一种低复杂度的分层联合信道估计与数据检测（JCEDD）接收机，通过先本地JCEDD后中心聚合协作精化的方式，解决了链路依赖残余延迟和多普勒频移带来的挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多LEO卫星形成的无蜂窝网络虽能实现泛在连接，但链路依赖的残余延迟和多普勒频移严重制约了协作接收性能，现有方法难以有效处理这一问题。

Method: 将JCEDD问题建模为结构化双线性推理问题，设计分层接收机：各卫星先执行本地JCEDD，再汇聚到中心卫星进行协作精化；通过限制信道估计到粗信息辅助的局部波束-延迟-多普勒区域，并以无矩阵方式评估前向和伴随操作来降低计算复杂度。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提JCEDD接收机在信道估计精度和数据检测可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层JCEDD接收机能够在多LEO卫星无蜂窝OTFS上行链路中实现高效的信道估计与数据检测，且计算复杂度较低。

Original Abstract: Cell-free networks formed by multiple low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites offer a promising architecture for ubiquitous connectivity, but their cooperative reception is challenged by link-dependent residual delays and Doppler shifts. This paper investigates joint channel estimation and data detection (JCEDD) for multi-LEO-satellite cell-free orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) uplinks. The JCEDD problem is formulated as a structured bilinear inference problem involving link-specific sparse beam--delay--Doppler channels and a multiuser data vector. We develop a low-complexity hierarchical JCEDD receiver in which all satellites first perform local JCEDD, and their observations and local estimates are then aggregated at a central satellite for cooperative refinement. Computational complexity is reduced by restricting channel estimation to coarse-information-aided local beam--delay--Doppler regions and evaluating the required forward and adjoint operations in a matrix-free manner. Simulation results validate the channel-estimation accuracy and data-detection reliability of the proposed JCEDD receiver.

中文摘要翻译: 由多颗低地球轨道卫星构成的无蜂窝网络为泛在连接提供了一种有前景的架构，但其协作接收受到链路依赖的残余延迟和多普勒频移的挑战。本文研究了多LEO卫星无蜂窝正交时频空间上行链路的联合信道估计与数据检测问题。该JCEDD问题被建模为一个结构化双线性推理问题，其中涉及链路特定的稀疏波束-延迟-多普勒信道和多用户数据向量。我们开发了一种低复杂度的分层JCEDD接收机：所有卫星首先执行本地JCEDD，然后将它们的观测和本地估计汇聚到一颗中心卫星进行协作精化。通过将信道估计限制在粗信息辅助的本地波束-延迟-多普勒区域，并以无矩阵方式评估所需的前向和伴随操作，降低了计算复杂度。仿真结果验证了所提JCEDD接收机在信道估计精度和数据检测可靠性方面的有效性。

</details>


### [109] [CORF-GS: Real-Time Wireless Radiance Field Reconstruction via Coupled Optical-RF Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25569)
*Jinya Zhang,Jiajia Guo,Chao-Kai Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: CORF-GS是一个基于3D高斯泼溅的实时无线辐射场重建框架，通过共享几何与模态特定外观的统一高斯表示，结合光学引导采样和光-RF联合优化，实现了无线信道建模的实时重构，重构速度相比现有方法提升6.4倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的无线辐射场重建方法依赖预先收集的观测数据和离线优化，无法提供实时信道知识。为解决这一差距，需要一种能够处理序列光与射频关键帧的实时重建框架。

Method: 1) 构建光与RF的统一高斯表示，共享几何结构但保留模态特定的外观属性；2) 新关键帧到达时，采用光学引导的高斯采样对欠表示区域进行稠密化；3) 执行光-RF联合优化，共同优化共享高斯参数，避免RF被动适应冻结的光学几何。

Result: CORF-GS在RF频谱合成质量上达到最先进水平，且重建时间相比现有WRF方法减少6.4倍。

Conclusion: CORF-GS通过光学与射频的协同表示和联合优化，实现了实时高效的无线辐射场重建，解决了波长失配导致的光学引导局限性问题。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-based wireless radiance field (WRF) reconstruction provide an efficient solution for wireless channel modeling. However, existing WRF reconstruction methods rely on pre-collected observations and offline optimization, and thus struggle to provide real-time channel knowledge. To bridge this gap, we propose CORF-GS, a real-time WRF reconstruction framework that processes sequential optical and radio frequency (RF) keyframes. Specifically, CORF-GS constructs a unified Gaussian representation for optical and RF with shared geometry and modality-specific appearance, allowing high-resolution optical images to provide structural priors for WRF reconstruction. When a new keyframe arrives, CORF-GS first employs optical-guided Gaussian sampling to densify the WRF in under-represented regions. Since light and radio waves may respond differently to the same object surfaces due to wavelength mismatch, relying solely on optical guidance may neglect RF-informative areas. Therefore, CORF-GS performs coupled optical-RF optimization to jointly refine the shared Gaussians. Compared with the existing two-stage training pipelines, this prevents WRF from passively adapting to a frozen optical geometry and encourages the shared Gaussians to adapt to both optical structures and RF power distributions. Simulations show that CORF-GS achieves state-of-the-art RF spectrum synthesis quality and reduces the reconstruction time by $6.4\times$ compared with existing WRF methods.

中文摘要翻译: 基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的无线辐射场（WRF）重建的最新进展为无线信道建模提供了一种高效解决方案。然而，现有WRF重建方法依赖预先收集的观测数据和离线优化，因此难以提供实时的信道知识。为填补这一空白，我们提出CORF-GS，一种处理序列光与射频（RF）关键帧的实时WRF重建框架。具体而言，CORF-GS构建了光与RF的统一高斯表示，共享几何结构并保留模态特定的外观属性，从而允许高分辨率光学图像为WRF重建提供结构先验。当新关键帧到达时，CORF-GS首先采用光学引导的高斯采样，在欠表示区域稠密化WRF。由于光和无线电波可能因波长失配而对相同的物体表面产生不同响应，仅依赖光学引导可能会忽略RF信息丰富的区域。因此，CORF-GS执行光-RF联合优化，共同优化共享高斯参数。与现有的两阶段训练流程相比，这防止了WRF被动适应冻结的光学几何，并鼓励共享高斯同时适应光学结构和RF功率分布。仿真结果表明，CORF-GS在RF频谱合成质量上达到了最先进的水平，且重建时间相比现有WRF方法减少了6.4倍。

</details>


### [110] [Zeroth-Order Blind Interference Suppression for Multi-RIS-Aided Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25632)
*Binyao Ma,Peilan Wang,Bin Wang,Jun Fang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对多RIS辅助SISO系统中存在未知强干扰源的情况，提出一种基于分组的零阶自适应矩算法，通过相位参数化降维实现SINR最大化测量驱动优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多RIS辅助的无线通信系统中，干扰信道通常未知，导致SINR最大化问题成为目标函数解析形式未知的黑箱优化问题，且面临高维度和离散变量约束的挑战。

Method: 提出基于分组的相位参数化方法降低搜索维度，并在此基础上开发了分组零阶自适应矩（ZO-AdaMM）算法来解决高维黑箱离散优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的分组策略显著加快了收敛速度，在有限的测量预算下实现了优越的干扰抑制性能，尤其是在小预算场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 分组相位参数化结合零阶优化方法能有效应对多RIS系统中未知干扰下的SINR最大化问题，在测量受限条件下实现快速收敛和强干扰抑制。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we study measurement-driven signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) maximization for a multi-reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-aided single-input single-output (SISO) system with unknown strong interference sources. Specifically, the objective is to optimize the reflection coefficients such that the SINR is maximized at the receiver. As the interference channels are unknown, such an optimization problem is a black-box optimization problem with an objective function whose closed-form analytical expression is unknown. To address the high-dimensional black-box optimization problem with discrete variable constraints, we introduce a group-based phase parameterization that significantly reduces the search dimension. Building on this model, we develop a group-based zeroth-order adaptive moment (ZO-AdaMM) algorithm. Simulation results show that the proposed grouping strategy markedly accelerates the convergence speed and achieves a superior interference suppression performance under limited measurement budgets, especially in the small-budget regime.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在多可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的单输入单输出（SISO）系统中，存在未知强干扰源时基于测量驱动的信号与干扰加噪声比（SINR）最大化问题。具体而言，目标是通过优化反射系数来最大化接收端的SINR。由于干扰信道未知，该优化问题是一个目标函数闭式解析表达式未知的黑箱优化问题。为了解决具有离散变量约束的高维黑箱优化问题，我们引入了一种基于分组的相位参数化方法，显著降低了搜索维度。在此模型基础上，我们开发了一种基于分组的零阶自适应矩（ZO-AdaMM）算法。仿真结果表明，所提出的分组策略显著加速了收敛速度，并在有限的测量预算下实现了优越的干扰抑制性能，尤其是在小预算场景下表现突出。

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Dimensional Entropy for Vibration Measurement Data Quality Assessment and Erroneous Signal Identification in Wind Turbines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25761)
*Xiao-Ming Yuan,Zishun Wang,Donghui Zhao,Deshui Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于多维熵（MDE）的前端数据质量评估机制，用于识别风力发电机传动系统振动测量中的无效数据。在包含57,643个样本、涵盖12个风电场和14个机组的真实异构数据集上，该方法以超过99%的准确率识别错误振动测量值，可作为下游信号处理与状态监测的前端数据质量门控。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 风力发电机传动系统的振动测量数据常受传感器故障、停机状态等非诊断因素影响，导致无效数据污染，进而损害后续状态监测和数据驱动分析的可靠性。现有方法缺乏一个高效、鲁棒的前端数据质量评估机制。

Method: 提出多维熵（MDE）指标，从时域幅值、频谱幅值和频带能量等多个维度刻画信号分布特征，捕捉有效与错误振动测量之间的统计差异。将MDE与RMS整合为特征表示，采用轻量级机器学习模型作为评估工具进行分类识别。

Result: 在覆盖12个风电场、14个机组、多种传动部件、不同传感器品牌和采样配置的真实大规模异构数据集（57,643个振动样本）上，该方法识别错误振动测量的准确率超过99%。

Conclusion: 所提方法可作为前端数据质量门控部署于下游信号处理、特征提取和状态监测流程之前，确保后续分析使用可靠的振动测量数据。实验结果证明了MDE在异构传感器配置下的鲁棒性，展示了其在工业级振动测量质量评估中的应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Ensuring measurement data quality is essential for reliable condition monitoring of industrial wind turbine drivetrains, where vibration measurements can be affected by sensor malfunctions, turbine shutdown conditions, and other non-diagnostic states. Such invalid measurements may compromise the reliability of subsequent monitoring and data-driven analysis procedures. This study proposes a Multi-Dimensional Entropy (MDE) metric as a front-end data quality assessment and control mechanism for vibration measurement validity evaluation. By characterizing signal distributions from multiple perspectives, including time-domain amplitude, spectral amplitude, and frequency-band energy, MDE captures statistical differences between valid and erroneous vibration measurements. By integrating MDE and RMS as feature representations, lightweight machine learning models are employed as evaluation tools to assess the effectiveness of the proposed representation. Experiments on a large-scale, heterogeneous real-world dataset comprising 57,643 vibration samples collected from 12 wind farms and 14 turbine units, covering multiple drivetrain components, diverse sensor brands, and varying sampling configurations over long-term operation, demonstrate that the proposed method achieves over 99 percent accuracy in identifying erroneous vibration measurements. The proposed approach can be deployed as a front-end data quality gate before downstream signal processing, feature extraction, and condition monitoring procedures, ensuring that subsequent analyses are performed using reliable vibration measurements. The results demonstrate the robustness of MDE under heterogeneous sensor configurations and highlight its potential for industrial-scale vibration measurement quality assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 确保测量数据质量对于工业风力发电机传动系统的可靠状态监测至关重要，其中振动测量可能受到传感器故障、机组停机状态及其他非诊断性工况的影响。此类无效测量可能损害后续监测和数据分析过程的可靠性。本研究提出一种多维熵（Multi-Dimensional Entropy, MDE）指标，作为振动测量有效性评估的前端数据质量评估与控制机制。通过从时域幅值、频谱幅值和频带能量等多个角度刻画信号分布特征，MDE能够捕捉有效与错误振动测量之间的统计差异。通过将MDE和RMS作为特征表示进行整合，采用轻量级机器学习模型作为评估工具，检验所提表示方法的有效性。在涵盖12个风电场、14个机组、包含多种传动部件、不同传感器品牌及长期运行中多种采样配置的57,643个振动样本的大规模真实异构数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在识别错误振动测量方面准确率超过99%。所提方法可作为前端数据质量门控，部署于下游信号处理、特征提取和状态监测流程之前，确保后续分析使用可靠的振动测量数据。实验结果证明了MDE在异构传感器配置下的鲁棒性，并突显了其在工业级振动测量质量评估中的应用潜力。

</details>


### [112] [Beam Selection for Delay-Doppler Visibility in Multi-Target MIMO-OFDM Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25810)
*Amirhossein Azarbahram,Onel L. A. López*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对多目标MIMO-OFDM感知中的漏泄感知波束选择问题，提出了一种最大化最差目标可见性的优化框架，通过混合整数逐次凸逼近方法求解，并在角度或延迟-多普勒域接近的目标场景中显著优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多目标MIMO-OFDM感知中，不同目标之间的泄漏会干扰各自的延迟-多普勒（DD）检测，导致弱目标被强目标泄漏所掩盖。现有方法未充分考虑成对泄漏对目标可见性的影响，因此需要一种泄漏感知的波束选择与功率分配方案来保证每个目标的可靠检测。

Method: 提出可见性度量指标，将期望聚焦功率与由有限网格OFDM模糊核、接收角度耦合和发射码本增益引起的成对泄漏分离；建立最大最小化波束选择与功率分配优化问题；采用基于二分法的混合整数逐次凸逼近（MI-SCA）框架求解，同时提出时间共享公式作为全局可解基准。

Result: 在目标角度或DD域接近时，所提出的成对泄漏感知设计在最差目标可见性上优于时间共享、全局泄漏抑制、照明波束和随机波束选择等基线方法。

Conclusion: 泄漏感知的波束选择与功率分配能有效提升多目标MIMO-OFDM感知中弱目标的检测性能，特别是在目标空间邻近时优势更为显著。

Original Abstract: This paper studies leakage-aware beam selection for multi-target multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO)-orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) sensing. We focus on ensuring that each hypothesized target remains detectable at its own delay-Doppler (DD) bin despite leakage from other targets. For this, we derive a visibility metric that separates the desired focused power from pairwise leakage caused by the finite-grid OFDM ambiguity kernel, receive angular coupling, and transmit codebook gains. Then, we formulate a max-min beam-selection and power-allocation problem in which the sensing signal over the fixed OFDM time-frequency grid is transmitted by a limited number of selected beams. The resulting problem is solved via a bisection-based mixed-integer successive convex approximation framework. A time-sharing formulation is also presented as a globally solvable benchmark. Numerical results show that the proposed pairwise leakage-aware design achieves higher worst-target visibility than the time-sharing, global leakage-suppression, illumination-based, and random beam-selection baselines, especially when targets are close in angle or DD.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了面向多目标多输入多输出-正交频分复用（MIMO-OFDM）感知的泄漏感知波束选择问题。我们关注的是，尽管存在来自其他目标的泄漏，如何确保每个假设目标在其自身的延迟-多普勒（DD）单元中仍保持可检测性。为此，我们推导了一种可见性度量，将期望的聚焦功率与由有限网格OFDM模糊核、接收角度耦合以及发射码本增益引起的成对泄漏分离开来。然后，我们建立了一个最大-最小波束选择与功率分配问题，其中通过有限数量的选定波束在固定的OFDM时频网格上传输感知信号。该问题通过基于二分法的混合整数逐次凸逼近框架求解。此外，还提出了一种时间共享公式作为全局可解的基准。数值结果表明，所提出的成对泄漏感知设计在最差目标可见性上优于时间共享、全局泄漏抑制、照明波束以及随机波束选择等基线方法，尤其是当目标在角度或DD域接近时。

</details>


### [113] [Secure Energy-Efficient Uplink Transmission in Movable-Element RIS-aided Systems with Movable Antennas and Artificial Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25924)
*Ayda Nodel Hokmabadi,Mohamed Elhattab,Chadi Assi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合梯度元学习（H-GML）框架，用于在全双工基站配备可移动天线（MAs）和可移动元件可重构智能表面（ME-RIS）的系统中，联合优化多个变量以最大化安全能量效率（SEE），应对存在多个协作被动窃听者的上行安全传输问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 下一代无线网络需要同时保证能量可持续性和信息安全，安全能量效率（SEE）成为关键性能指标。现有工作在可移动天线和可移动元件RIS联合优化方面存在空白，且优化问题高度非凸、变量耦合复杂，需要新的求解方法。

Method: 提出混合梯度元学习（H-GML）框架：BS接收后编码器和人工噪声方向通过广义瑞利商闭式解更新；其余耦合变量（用户发射功率、RIS相移、天线和RIS元件位置等）由神经元优化器学习梯度更新方向直接优化SEE目标，无需离线标注训练数据。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提H-GML设计优于交替优化（AO）基准方法，并显著优于固定几何布局、随机RIS、无人工噪声和无窃听者知识等基线方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的H-GML框架能有效解决高度非凸的SEE最大化问题，在多窃听者场景下实现了比传统方法更高的安全能量效率。

Original Abstract: Secure energy efficiency (SEE) has emerged as a key performance metric for next-generation wireless networks, where energy sustainability and information security must be jointly guaranteed. This paper investigates secure uplink transmission in a full-duplex (FD) base station (BS) system equipped with movable antennas (MAs) and assisted by a movable-element reconfigurable intelligent surface (ME-RIS) in the presence of multiple cooperative passive eavesdroppers. The objective is to maximize SEE by jointly optimizing the users' transmit powers, BS receive postcoders, artificial noise (AN) transmit power and beamforming, RIS phase shifts, and the two-dimensional positions of both the BS antennas and RIS elements. The resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex due to the fractional SEE objective, coupled secrecy-rate expressions, residual self-interference (SI), unit-modulus RIS phase-shift constraints, movable-position constraints, inter-element spacing requirements, and the nonlinear dependence of the channels on the movable antenna and RIS-element positions. To address these challenges, we propose a hybrid gradient-based meta-learning (H-GML) framework. In the proposed method, the BS receive postcoders and AN direction are updated using closed-form solutions derived from generalized Rayleigh quotient formulations, while the remaining coupled variables are updated by neural meta-optimizers that learn gradient-based update directions directly from the SEE optimization objective without requiring offline labeled training data. Simulation results show that the proposed H-GML design achieves better performance than the AO benchmark and significantly outperforms fixed-geometry, random RIS, no-AN, and no-Eve-knowledge baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 安全能量效率（SEE）已成为下一代无线网络的关键性能指标，其中能量可持续性和信息安全必须同时得到保障。本文研究了在存在多个协作被动窃听者的情况下，配备可移动天线（MAs）并由可移动元件可重构智能表面（ME-RIS）辅助的全双工（FD）基站（BS）系统中的安全上行传输问题。目标是通过联合优化用户的发射功率、BS接收后编码器、人工噪声（AN）发射功率与波束成形、RIS相移以及BS天线和RIS元件的二维位置来最大化SEE。由于分数形式的SEE目标、耦合的保密速率表达式、残余自干扰（SI）、单位模RIS相移约束、可移动位置约束、元件间间距要求以及信道对可移动天线和RIS元件位置的非线性依赖，所得优化问题高度非凸。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于混合梯度元学习（H-GML）框架。在所提方法中，BS接收后编码器和AN方向通过广义瑞利商公式推导出的闭式解更新，而其余耦合变量由神经元优化器更新，这些优化器直接从SEE优化目标中学习基于梯度的更新方向，无需离线标注训练数据。仿真结果表明，所提H-GML设计比交替优化（AO）基准方法取得更好的性能，并显著优于固定几何布局、随机RIS、无AN和无窃听者知识等基线方案。

</details>


### [114] [A Synchronized Multi-IMU Wearable System for Tracking of Joint-Angles in Sports Motion Analysis With Reference-Based Validation and Dynamic Task Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26027)
*Thevindu Samarasekera,Praveen Rathnayaka,Sachintha Adhikari,Tharinda Navarathne,Mahela Pandukabhaya,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Chanaka Senanayake,Vijitha Herath,Asela Ratnayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、模块化、时间同步的IMU可穿戴平台及端到端关节角度估计管线，用于高动态运动分析。该系统通过RTC微秒时间戳、间接卡尔曼滤波、高通漂移抑制等方法，在长时间静置测试中漂移极低（1.5×10⁻⁶度/分钟），噪声分辨率0.6442°，并在抓举等动态任务中有效区分专业与业余运动员。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实验室外可靠的关节角度测量对运动与康复中的姿势评估和技术分析至关重要，但可穿戴IMU系统仍受漂移、多传感器时间一致性、验证和成本等因素制约。

Method: 构建了高采样率、本地鲁棒记录、RTC锁存微秒时间戳的同步IMU系统；采用间接卡尔曼滤波进行姿态估计并提取相对旋转关节角度；结合高通滤波抑制漂移和范围归一化；以YOLOv11标记的无标记视觉系统为参考进行验证。

Result: 标准坐姿屈膝实验低归一化误差复现14周期运动；2小时12分钟刚体肘部保持测试漂移几乎为零（r_drift=1.5×10⁻⁶度/分钟），噪声分辨率0.6442°；高动态抓举任务中保留阶段特征和快速瞬态，可区分专业与业余水平。

Conclusion: 该结果支持所提出的系统作为一种实用、时间一致且成本效益高的可穿戴方案，适用于高动态场景下的多关节技术分析。

Original Abstract: Reliable joint-angle measurement outside laboratory environments is important for posture assessment and technique analysis in sports and rehabilitation. Yet, wearable IMU systems, despite being the most practical solution, remain constrained by drift, multisensor timing consistency, validation, and cost. This paper presents a synchronized, cost-efficient, modular IMU wearable platform and an end-to-end pipeline for joint-angle estimation tailored to high-dynamic sports motion analysis. The system combines high-rate sensing with robust local logging, a Real Time Clock-disciplined microsecond timestamping scheme for inter-node synchronization, an indirect Kalman filter-based orientation estimator followed by relative-rotation joint-angle extraction, high-pass drift mitigation, and range normalization. Reference-based validation used a standardized seated knee flexion-extension protocol, comparing IMU-derived knee trajectories against a markerless vision reference computed from YOLOv11. The proposed method reproduced the expected 14-cycle motion and achieved low normalized error. Instrumentation performance was further evaluated using a long-duration (2h 12min) rigid-body elbow hold, yielding near-zero drift ($r_{\mathrm{drift}}=1.5\times10^{-6}$ deg/min) and a practical noise-limited resolution of $0.6442^\circ$. The system was also demonstrated on a high-dynamic clean & jerk task with two participants (professional and amateur) performing five consecutive lifts. Measured elbow and knee trajectories preserved stage-dependent signatures and rapid transients for technique interpretation, while enabling expertise-level discrimination through differences in transient timing and trajectory regularity. Overall, the results support the proposed system as a practical, temporally consistent, and cost-effective wearable approach for multi-joint technique analysis in high-dynamic settings.

中文摘要翻译: 在运动与康复领域，实验室外可靠的关节角度测量对姿势评估和技术分析至关重要。然而，可穿戴IMU系统尽管最为实用，仍受限于漂移、多传感器时间一致性、验证和成本等因素。本文提出了一种同步、低成本的模块化IMU可穿戴平台及一套面向高动态运动分析的全流程关节角度估计管线。该系统结合了高采样率传感与稳健本地日志存储、基于实时时钟（RTC）锁存的微秒级时间戳方案实现节点间同步、基于间接卡尔曼滤波的姿态估计器并提取相对旋转关节角度、高通滤波漂移抑制以及范围归一化。基于参考的验证采用标准化坐姿屈膝伸展协议，将IMU导出的膝关节轨迹与YOLOv11计算的无标记视觉参考进行对比。所提方法成功复现了预期的14次运动周期，并实现了较低的归一化误差。利用长时间（2小时12分钟）刚体肘部保持运动进一步评估仪器性能，结果显示漂移近乎为零（r_drift=1.5×10⁻⁶度/分钟），实际噪声限制分辨率为0.6442°。该系统还在两名参与者（专业运动员与业余爱好者）进行五次连续抓举的高动态任务中得到验证。测得的肘关节和膝关节轨迹保留了阶段依赖特征和快速瞬变信号，可用于技术解析，同时通过瞬态时序和轨迹规律性的差异实现了专业水平区分。总体而言，实验结果支持所提系统作为高动态场景下多关节技术分析的一种实用、时间一致且成本效益高的可穿戴方案。

</details>

### [115] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FinAbstain框架，面向金融预测中的不确定性校准多模态RAG，通过选择性预测（择时放弃）来平衡覆盖率与预测精度，利用点时间检索、多Agent证据聚合和混合不确定性评分实现校准式放弃决策。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在金融叙事生成中常因证据稀疏、过时或矛盾而表现出过度自信，这在金融预测中后果严重。现有方法缺乏对多模态金融证据的不确定性校准和选择性预测机制。

Method: 构建点时间检索器确保信息时效性，通过基本面、新闻、技术、风险、验证五个Agent分别评估概率，融合检索相关性、证据矛盾度、重复采样一致性和历史校准统计量，提出混合不确定性评分，结合温度缩放、等渗回归、保形预测等校准方法，在不确定性低于阈值时输出预测，否则放弃、请求证据、降低风险或转人工。

Result: 报告了明确标记的模拟结果（非实证声明），展示了校准式放弃能够以覆盖率为代价换取更低的预测错误率和回撤，涵盖1天/5天异常收益方向、20天波动区间、放弃决策等评估维度。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个时间安全的架构、一个复合不确定性公式以及一个可复现的评估蓝图，用于基于证据的选择性金融预测。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics.
