中文摘要翻译: 单细胞核糖核酸测序（scRNA-seq）是精准医疗工作流中的基础技术，为联合国可持续发展目标3（良好健康与福祉）作出贡献；其中无监督聚类是将原始表达矩阵转化为可解释细胞群的关键分析步骤。因此，从业者面临一个反复出现的工程决策：额外的深度表征阶段是否值得其计算和调参成本，还是经典的PCA流程已然足够？我们通过构建一个诊断基准来回答该问题，该基准在十个真实数据集（90-5,685个细胞，19,046-41,480个基因，4-11种细胞类型）上评估了九种聚类流程，并辅以七个数据集上的部分scVI V2专项比较。该协议集成了Optuna超参数搜索、重复运行鲁棒性评估、Friedman/Wilcoxon-Holm/TOST统计检验以及Sobol全局敏感性分析。对比自编码器取得了最高的平均调整兰德指数（0.7872），但Holm校正检验未确立其对最强基线的统计优势。按数据集分析揭示出三种可复现的模式：概率变分自编码器变体在最小数据集上表现最优，深度自编码器在具有多批次或多类型结构的中等规模数据上占优，而经典PCA流程在线性投影已能捕获主要变异时仍具竞争力。Sobol指数识别出学习率（ST=0.70）和潜在维度（ST=0.56）为主要方差贡献者，指明了有限调参预算应分配的方向。因此，本文的贡献在于提供一个数据集感知和算力感知的决策框架，以支持可持续医疗分析中的生物医学AI管线设计，而非声称某种方法具有普遍优越性。

</details>


### [145] [AMRD: Adaptive Multi-Teacher Relational Distillation for Lightweight Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25289)
*Yuqi Li,Yi-Cheng Lin,Xianglong Wang,Kuo Yang,Xiaoqin Feng,Yixuan Wang,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师关系蒸馏（AMRD），通过单类SVM动态分配教师权重并结合关系蒸馏损失，解决端侧语音情感识别中多教师知识蒸馏的可靠性与结构信息丢失问题，在IEMOCAP和CREMA-D上优于单教师蒸馏基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型自监督语音情感识别模型在边缘设备上部署成本过高；多教师知识蒸馏虽可压缩模型，但存在两个挑战：不同批次下教师模型的可靠性存在差异，以及仅使用logit层蒸馏会丢失样本间的关系结构信息。

Method: 提出AMRD方法：1）对每个教师模型的logit相似度矩阵应用单类SVM，为每批次分配权重以偏向更一致的教师；2）引入关系蒸馏损失，对齐教师和学生之间的相似度矩阵，捕捉logit匹配无法捕获的样本间结构信息。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和CREMA-D数据集上、四种不同学生架构下，AMRD在大多数设置中优于单教师蒸馏基线；消融实验证实两个组件（自适应权重与关系蒸馏）能产生互补增益。

Conclusion: AMRD通过自适应权重分配和关系结构保留，有效提升了多教师知识蒸馏在端侧语音情感识别中的性能，两个核心组件具有互补性。

Original Abstract: On-device speech emotion recognition (SER) is critical for real-time applications, yet large self-supervised models that excel at SER are too costly for edge devices. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation can compress them into a lightweight student, but two challenges remain: teacher reliability varies across batches, and logit-level distillation ignores inter-sample relational structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-teacher Relational Distillation (AMRD) to address both. A one-class SVM on each teacher's logit similarity matrix assigns per-batch weights favoring more coherent teachers. A relational distillation loss aligns teacher and student similarity matrices, capturing structure that logit matching misses. On IEMOCAP and CREMA-D datasets across four student architectures, AMRD outperforms single-teacher distillation baselines in most settings, and ablations confirm both components yield complementary gains.

中文摘要翻译: 端侧语音情感识别（SER）对实时应用至关重要，然而在此任务上表现出色的自监督大模型对于边缘设备来说成本过高。多教师知识蒸馏可以将大模型压缩为轻量级学生模型，但仍存在两个挑战：教师模型在不同批次上的可靠性存在差异，且logit层蒸馏忽略了样本间的关系结构。我们提出自适应多教师关系蒸馏（AMRD）来同时解决这两个问题。在每个教师模型的logit相似度矩阵上使用单类支持向量机，为每批次分配权重以偏向更一致的教师。关系蒸馏损失对齐教师和学生的相似度矩阵，捕捉logit匹配所遗漏的结构信息。在IEMOCAP和CREMA-D数据集上、四种不同学生架构下，AMRD在大多数设置中优于单教师蒸馏基线；消融实验证实两个组件能产生互补增益。

</details>


### [146] [Breaking the Periodicity Assumption: Robust Tensorial Multi-View Clustering via Graph-Spectral Low-Rank Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25295)
*Jintian Ji,Xingsu Li,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有张量多视图聚类（TMC）方法中基于FFT的低秩约束依赖样本排列顺序的严重缺陷，并提出基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架，消除了对特定样本排序的依赖，提升了聚类性能的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有t-SVD-based TMC方法沿样本模式使用FFT施加频域低秩约束，但这隐含了一个"周期性假设"——样本按类别排序时相邻索引语义相似，形成有利于低秩正则化的频谱结构。一旦样本随机排列，性能大幅下降。这种对样本排序的敏感性违背了聚类任务的置换不变性本质，说明部分性能提升来自样本排列的"特权"而非真正的高阶结构建模。

Method: 提出基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架：用数据驱动的图谱基替代沿样本模式的固定傅里叶基，从而捕捉数据内在的流形结构，不依赖特定样本排序。同时开发了基于锚点的变体以应对大规模数据集。

Result: 实验揭示了现有t-SVD-based TMC方法在随机排列样本时性能严重下降的现象；提出的GFT-based方法在各种基准数据集上取得了竞争性或优越的性能，且对样本排列具有置换不变性。

Conclusion: 基于FFT的TMC方法存在对样本排序的严重依赖，这是其性能报告中的系统性偏差；基于GFT的图谱低秩张量学习框架能够从根本上解决这一问题，实现置换不变的鲁棒多视图聚类。

Original Abstract: Tensorial multi-view clustering (TMC) has achieved strong performance due to its ability to capture high-order correlations across multiple views. Most existing t-SVD-based TMC frameworks apply the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) along the sample mode to impose frequency-domain low-rank constraints. However, we reveal that this widely adopted design critically relies on an implicit ``periodicity assumption'' induced by the sample arrangement. When samples are ordered by class, neighboring indices tend to be semantically similar, creating artificial local continuity along the sample mode and a favorable spectral structure for FFT-based low-rank regularization. Once this ordering is removed by random permutation, existing t-SVD-based TMC methods suffer severe performance degradation. This strong sensitivity to class ordering conflicts with the permutation-invariant nature of clustering and indicates that part of the reported performance may be attributed to a privileged sample arrangement rather than genuine high-order structure modeling. In this paper, we systematically investigate this phenomenon and its underlying algebraic and spectral mechanisms. To address this fundamental flaw, we further propose a graph-spectral low-rank tensor learning framework based on the Graph Fourier Transform (GFT), which replaces the fixed Fourier basis along the sample mode with a data-driven graph spectral basis, thereby capturing the intrinsic manifold structure without relying on a particular sample ordering. Moreover, we develop an anchor-based variant to address large-scale datasets efficiently. Extensive experiments on various benchmarks validate our findings and demonstrate the competitive or superior performance of the proposed methods compared with state-of-the-art TMC approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 张量多视图聚类（TMC）因其捕捉多视图间高阶关联的能力而取得了优异性能。现有大多数基于t-SVD的TMC框架沿样本模式应用快速傅里叶变换（FFT）以施加频域低秩约束。然而，我们发现这一广泛采用的设计关键性地依赖于样本排列所诱导的一个隐式"周期性假设"。当样本按类别排序时，相邻索引在语义上趋于相似，沿样本模式产生了人为的局部连续性，从而形成了有利于FFT低秩正则化的谱结构。一旦通过随机排列消除这种排序，现有的基于t-SVD的TMC方法就会遭受严重的性能退化。这种对类别排序的强烈敏感性与聚类的置换不变性本质相冲突，表明报告的部分性能可能归因于特权样本排列，而非真正的高阶结构建模。本文系统性地研究了这一现象及其背后的代数和谱机制。为解决这一根本缺陷，我们进一步提出了一种基于图傅里叶变换（GFT）的图谱低秩张量学习框架，该框架用数据驱动的图谱基替代沿样本模式的固定傅里叶基，从而在不依赖特定样本排序的情况下捕捉数据的内在流形结构。此外，我们开发了一种基于锚点的变体以高效处理大规模数据集。在多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了我们的发现，并证明了所提方法相较于最先进的TMC方法具有竞争性或优越的性能。

</details>


### [147] [Zhinv: Real-time hub-height wind field reconstruction using only local sparse observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25298)
*Zongwei Zhang,Chin Chun Ooi,Lianlei Lin,Sheng Gao,Tiantian He,Yew Soon Ong,Junkai Wang,Hangyi Yu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hanqing Zhao,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Zhinv，一个端到端重构框架，能从稀疏不规则观测数据直接重建高分辨率轮毂高度风场，实验显示误差比Kriging降低约66%，可跳过NWP实现实时风资源评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际运行中可获取的风信息多为稀疏、离散、不规则分布的局部观测数据，难以直接满足风电调控、风资源评估及低空环境感知等任务对连续区域风场的细粒度需求。

Method: 提出Zhinv，一个端到端的重构框架，直接将稀疏不规则的观测数据编织为高分辨率网格化的轮毂高度风场。

Result: 在中国东北、欧洲和东南亚的实验表明，Zhinv能够从稀疏观测中准确、鲁棒且高效地重建细粒度网格风场，相比Kriging方法误差降低约66%。

Conclusion: Zhinv使风电中心能够绕过NWP及复杂的同化过程，直接利用本地风电观测数据实现实时风资源评估，支持更高效的电力调控。

Original Abstract: The high proportion of wind power connected to the grid places higher demands on fine-grained knowledge of regional wind fields. Since the wind information directly obtainable in actual operations is mostly sparse, discrete, and irregularly distributed local observations, it is difficult to directly meet the needs of tasks such as wind power regulation, wind resource assessment, and low-altitude environmental perception of continuous regional wind fields. Therefore, we propose Zhinv, an end-to-end reconstruction framework that directly weaves sparse and irregular observations into a fine-grid wind field at hub-height. Experiments in Northeast China, Europe, and Southeast Asia demonstrate that Zhinv can accurately, robustly, and efficiently reconstruct fine-grid wind fields from sparse observations, reducing the error by about 66% compared with Kriging. With local wind-power observations as input, Zhinv enables wind power centers to bypass NWP and complex assimilation processes, supporting direct and real-time wind resource assessment from locally available data.

中文摘要翻译: 高比例风电并网对区域风场的细粒度知识提出了更高要求。由于实际运行中可直接获取的风信息多为稀疏、离散且不规则分布的局部观测数据，难以直接满足风电调控、风资源评估及低空环境感知等任务对连续区域风场的需求。为此，我们提出Zhinv，一种端到端的重构框架，能够直接将稀疏且不规则的观测数据编织为轮毂高度处的细粒度网格化风场。在中国东北、欧洲及东南亚的实验表明，Zhinv能够从稀疏观测中准确、鲁棒且高效地重建细粒度网格风场，相较于Kriging方法误差降低约66%。以本地风电观测数据作为输入，Zhinv使风电中心能够绕过数值天气预报（NWP）及复杂的同化过程，支持基于本地可用数据的直接实时风资源评估。

</details>


### [148] [Retraction-Free Optimization over the Stiefel Manifold for the LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25299)
*Yuan Zhang,Jiang Hu,Zhijian Lai,Lin Lin,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无回缩、无惩罚参数的Stiefel流形着陆算法，具有最优迭代复杂度，并将其应用于大语言模型的低秩适应微调（Manifold-LoRA），加速训练并提升下游性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Stiefel流形优化方法要么依赖回缩算子（需昂贵正交化），要么使用着陆方法（需精细步长选择和惩罚参数调优），难以高效处理大规模矩阵问题。

Method: 提出一种无回缩、无惩罚参数的直接着陆算法，利用二次罚函数的强凸性质和Stiefel流形的近端光滑性建立全局收敛保证；并将低秩适应微调问题重新表述为流形优化问题，设计Manifold-LoRA方法结合所提着陆技术和精心设计的步长策略加速训练。

Result: 在常数步长和递减步长下均达到已知最优迭代复杂度；在基准数据集上的数值实验证明了所提方法的效率和强下游性能。

Conclusion: 所提无回缩、无惩罚参数的着陆算法有效解决了Stiefel流形优化的计算瓶颈，Manifold-LoRA为大语言模型微调提供了一种几何加速的高效方案。

Original Abstract: Optimization over the Stiefel manifold plays a significant role in various machine learning tasks. Existing methods either use the retraction operators, requiring costly orthonormalization for large-scale matrices, or employ landing methods that rely on careful step size selection and penalty parameter tuning. To address these challenges, we propose a retraction-free and penalty parameter-free algorithm that directly lands on the manifold. By leveraging the strongly-convex-like property of the quadratic penalty function and the proximal smoothness of the Stiefel manifold, we establish global convergence guarantees with the best-known iteration complexities under both constant and diminishing step sizes. Then, we reformulate the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning problem for large language models as a manifold optimization problem, introducing Manifold-LoRA for geometry-accelerated adaptation. This approach employs the proposed landing technique and a carefully designed step size strategy to accelerate the training process. Numerical experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the efficiency and strong downstream performance of the proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 在Stiefel流形上进行优化在各类机器学习任务中扮演着重要角色。现有方法要么使用回缩算子，需要对大规模矩阵进行昂贵的正交化处理，要么采用依赖精细步长选择和惩罚参数调优的着陆方法。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种无回缩、无惩罚参数的算法，能够直接着陆在流形上。通过利用二次罚函数的强凸性质和Stiefel流形的近端光滑性，我们在常数步长和递减步长下均建立了具有已知最优迭代复杂度的全局收敛保证。随后，我们将大语言模型的低秩适应（LoRA）微调问题重新表述为流形优化问题，提出了用于几何加速适应的Manifold-LoRA方法。该方法采用所提出的着陆技术和精心设计的步长策略来加速训练过程。在基准数据集上的数值实验证明了所提方法的效率和强大的下游性能。

</details>


### [149] [Guiding Posterior Exploration with Optimizer-Derived Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25312)
*Moritz Schlager,Emanuel Sommer,Thomas Möllenhoff,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自适应优化器（如AdamW）在预热阶段计算的曲率估计来指导贝叶斯深度集成的采样，无需额外计算成本即可减少或消除采样烧入阶段，提升数值稳定性与不确定性量化效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络中基于采样的不确定性量化方法因后验分布高维、多峰而计算成本高昂，现有贝叶斯深度集成虽通过预热启动缓解了这一问题，但采样阶段仍需要较长的烧入过程。

Method: 提出一种基于优化器导出几何结构的预条件采样策略，利用AdamW等自适应优化器在预热阶段作为副产品计算的曲率估计来指导采样阶段，无需额外计算开销。

Result: 该方法能显著减少甚至消除采样烧入阶段，带来更好的数值稳定性，并在预测性能和不确定性量化上保持一致或有所提升，且无额外计算成本。多个数据集和网络架构上的结果一致。

Conclusion: 优化器预热阶段计算的曲率估计可有效用于指导贝叶斯深度集成的采样，在零额外成本下实现更高效、更稳定的不确定性量化。

Original Abstract: Sampling-based methods offer a principled approach to uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks. Their practical use, however, is often challenged by the computational cost of exploring high-dimensional and multimodal posterior distributions. To overcome these difficulties, Bayesian Deep Ensembles, i.e., warmstarting the sampling from several optimized solutions, have proven to be an effective strategy. In this paper, we demonstrate that curvature estimates computed during the warmstart as a byproduct in adaptive optimizers such as AdamW can inform the sampling phase at negligible additional cost. Specifically, our proposed preconditioned sampling strategy based on optimizer-derived geometries can substantially reduce or even eliminate the need for a lengthy sampling burn-in phase and leads to greater numerical stability. This approach consistently maintains or improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification without any additional computational costs. We confirm the consistency of our findings across various datasets and network architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 基于采样的方法为贝叶斯神经网络中的不确定性量化提供了一种 principled 的方式。然而，其实际应用常常因探索高维和多峰后验分布的计算成本而受到挑战。为克服这些困难，贝叶斯深度集成——即从多个优化解出发对采样进行预热启动——已被证明是一种有效策略。在本文中，我们证明了在自适应优化器（如AdamW）的预热过程中作为副产品计算的曲率估计，能够以可忽略的额外成本为采样阶段提供信息。具体而言，我们提出的基于优化器导出几何结构的预条件采样策略，能够显著减少甚至消除冗长的采样烧入阶段，并带来更高的数值稳定性。该方法在保持一致或提升预测性能与不确定性量化的同时，无需任何额外计算成本。我们在多种数据集和网络架构上验证了该发现的一致性。

</details>


### [150] [Explainable AI for Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction Using Simulated Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25348)
*Md Zahid Hasan Ontor,Md Al Amin,Anik Dev Nath,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于联邦学习与集成分类器的慢性肾病早期检测方法，结合可解释人工智能技术，在保护数据隐私的同时实现了99%的全局平均准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 慢性肾病（CKD）是重大公共卫生挑战，早期检测对预防严重并发症和改善患者预后至关重要，因此需要一种既高效又保护数据隐私的预测方法。

Method: 采用联邦学习（FL）框架结合VotingClassifier集成分类器，在客户端使用随机森林、AdaBoost和XGBoost三种模型进行训练与比较，并通过GridSearchCV优化模型性能；在全局服务器端选择最佳拟合模型；同时引入可解释人工智能（XAI）技术增强模型透明度和可信度。

Result: 全局模型的平均准确率达到99%，表明可解释联邦学习模型在支持早期CKD诊断方面具有显著潜力。

Conclusion: 可解释的联邦学习模型在慢性肾病早期诊断中具有巨大潜力，能够推动数据驱动的医疗解决方案发展。

Original Abstract: Chronic Kidney Disease (CKD), characterized by the gradual loss of kidney function, remains a significant public health challenge. Early detection is crucial for preventing severe complications and enhancing patient outcomes. In this study, Federated Learning (FL) with a VotingClassifier was used to predict CKD using a clinical dataset, where Random Forest, AdaBoost, and XGBoost were utilized to compare and identify the best-fitting model for the global server. Additionally, GridSearchCV was applied to optimize the models' performance on the client's side. To enhance model transparency and trustworthiness, explainable AI (XAI) techniques were incorporated to interpret the prediction mechanisms. The global model's average accuracy was 99%, highlighting the potential of interpretable FL models in supporting early CKD diagnosis and advancing data-driven healthcare solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 慢性肾病（CKD）以肾功能逐渐丧失为特征，仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战。早期检测对于预防严重并发症和改善患者预后至关重要。本研究采用联邦学习（FL）结合VotingClassifier集成分类器，利用临床数据集预测CKD，其中使用了随机森林、AdaBoost和XGBoost进行对比，以筛选出最适合全局服务器的模型。此外，在客户端应用GridSearchCV优化模型性能。为增强模型透明度和可信度，引入了可解释人工智能（XAI）技术来解释预测机制。全局模型的平均准确率达到99%，这表明可解释的联邦学习模型在支持早期CKD诊断和推进数据驱动的医疗解决方案方面具有巨大潜力。

</details>


### [151] [Raven: High-Recall Sequence Modeling with Sparse Memory Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25357)
*Arshia Afzal,Aviv Bick,Eric P. Xing,Volkan Cevher,Albert Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Raven是一种线性时间序列模型，通过固定大小的记忆槽和基于输入的路由机制，在状态模型（密集写入）和滑动窗口注意力（稀疏写入）之间取得平衡，有效缓解了写干扰和硬驱逐问题，实现了强大的长上下文召回能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有线性时间序列模型在长上下文召回中存在权衡：状态空间模型等状态模型对每个新token更新整个状态，导致写入密集、干扰严重；滑动窗口注意力虽稀疏写入但仅保留固定窗口内的显式token，一旦相关token被驱逐召回率骤降。两者均难以有效保持长距离上下文信息。

Method: Raven维护一组固定大小的记忆槽，在每个时间步中，通过学习到的、依赖于输入的路由机制，仅对选中的子集进行衰减和更新。这使得模型既能避免SWA中基于位置的覆写和硬驱逐，又能减少SSM中密集状态更新带来的干扰。

Result: 在召回密集的基准测试中，Raven达到或优于先前的线性时间基线模型；在SWA和SSM性能急剧下降的长上下文召回任务上表现出色；可外推到训练长度16倍的上下文长度，且在混合架构中也有类似增益。

Conclusion: Raven通过在固定记忆槽上执行输入依赖的选择性更新，有效融合了密集写入和稀疏写入的优点，在线性时间内实现了强大的长上下文召回能力，并展现出卓越的长度外推性能。

Original Abstract: Long-context recall in linear-time sequence models highlights a tradeoff in how they write to memory. State-based linear models, such as state-space models (SSMs) and linear Transformers, write densely, updating the entire state for each newly arrived token, which leads to interference and makes specific past tokens hard to recover. Sliding-window attention (SWA) exhibits the opposite behavior: it writes sparsely by storing explicit token representations, but only within a fixed window, so recall drops once the relevant token is evicted. Interpolating between these models, we introduce Raven, a linear-time sequence model that maintains a fixed set of memory slots and, at each step, decays and updates only a selected subset via learned, input-dependent routing. This lets Raven mitigate SWA's position-based overwriting and hard eviction while reducing interference from dense state updates in SSMs, thereby preserving long-range content much more effectively. Across recall-intensive benchmarks, Raven is competitive with or outperforms prior linear-time baselines, achieving strong long-context recall where both SWA and SSMs sharply degrade. It remains effective when extrapolating to context lengths as large as 16x its training length, with similar gains in hybrid architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 线性时间序列模型中的长上下文召回揭示了它们在写入记忆时的一种权衡。基于状态的线性模型，如状态空间模型（SSMs）和线性Transformer，采用密集写入方式，即每个新到达的token都会更新整个状态，这导致了干扰，使得特定的过去token难以恢复。滑动窗口注意力（SWA）则表现出相反的行为：它通过存储显式的token表示来实现稀疏写入，但仅限于一个固定的窗口，因此一旦相关token被驱逐，召回率就会下降。在这两类模型之间进行插值，我们提出了Raven——一种线性时间序列模型，它维护一组固定大小的记忆槽，并在每一步中，通过学习到的、依赖于输入的路由机制，仅对选中的子集进行衰减和更新。这使得Raven能够缓解SWA中基于位置的覆写和硬驱逐问题，同时减少SSM中密集状态更新带来的干扰，从而更有效地保留长距离内容。在召回密集的基准测试中，Raven与先前的线性时间基线模型表现相当或更优，在SWA和SSM性能急剧下降的长上下文召回任务上取得了强劲表现。当外推到训练长度16倍的上下文长度时，Raven仍然有效，并且在混合架构中也有类似的增益。

</details>


### [152] [Rethinking Likelihood distributions: Student's t Likelihood Boosts Bayesian Neural Network Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25376)
*Pei-Hsuan Hsia,Lars H. Heyen,Arvid Weyrauch,Markus Goetz,Achim Streit,Sebastian Krumscheid,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨贝叶斯神经网络中似然分布假设的选择问题，发现Student's t分布相比传统高斯分布能提升回归任务的预测性能，且不依赖于数据分布和MLP架构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明BNN中高斯先验假设可能不成立，但似然分布的选择对模型的影响尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在人工和真实数据集上，使用标准多层感知机（MLP）比较多种似然分布假设（如偏态分布、厚尾分布等）在回归任务中的表现。

Result: Student's t分布无论在何种数据分布和MLP架构（深度和宽度）下，其预测性能均优于高斯似然分布；在某些情况下还能缩短训练时间。

Conclusion: Student's t分布是BNN回归任务中优于高斯分布的一种易于实现且高效的似然分布选择。

Original Abstract: In Bayesian neural networks (BNNs), variational inference is a widely adopted framework for modeling uncertainty in a distributional way, with the evidence lower bound (ELBO) serving as the standard objective function. Several distributions contribute to the ELBO loss, such as the prior, approximated posterior, and likelihood distribution. Typically, these distributions are all approximated by a Gaussian distribution, since it is easy to compute, allows for reparameterized gradients, and provides a closed-form loss for training. However, several works have highlighted that this assumption may not generally hold, posing the risk of model misspecification. Alternative distributions have been proposed for the prior specifically, while the effect of distribution choice on the likelihood distribution remains unexplored. In this work, our aim is to close this gap by investigating whether alternative assumptions for the likelihood distribution can outperform the commonly used Gaussian. We compare several likelihood distribution assumptions, such as skewed or heavy-tailed, across regression tasks on both artificial and real-world datasets using standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Our findings demonstrate that Student's t yields better predictive performance than a Gaussian likelihood distribution, independent of the data distribution and MLP architecture (depth and width). In some cases, Student's t can also lead to shorter training times, while still being easy to implement.

中文摘要翻译: 在贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中，变分推断是一种广泛采用的用于以分布方式建模不确定性的框架，证据下界（ELBO）作为标准目标函数。多个分布共同构成ELBO损失，例如先验分布、近似后验分布和似然分布。通常，这些分布都采用高斯分布近似，因为其易于计算、支持重参数化梯度，并能提供闭式损失以进行训练。然而，多项研究指出这一假设通常不成立，存在模型设定错误的风险。已有工作针对先验分布提出了替代分布，但分布选择对似然分布的影响尚未被探索。在本工作中，我们的目标是填补这一空白，研究似然分布的替代假设能否优于常用的高斯分布。我们比较了多种似然分布假设（如偏态分布或厚尾分布），在人工和真实数据集上使用标准多层感知机（MLP）进行回归任务。实验结果表明，无论数据分布和MLP架构（深度和宽度）如何，Student's t分布都能比高斯似然分布取得更好的预测性能。在某些情况下，Student's t分布还能缩短训练时间，同时易于实现。

</details>


### [153] [Learned, Relied Upon, or Necessary? Separating Checkpoint Dependence from Task-Level Value in Sheaf GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25387)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在层图神经网络中学到的限制映射常被视为模型发现了有用边几何的证据，但这种结论并不成立。作者通过两种估计量（检查点依赖性和协议相对替换）分离了相关主张，并通过任务零定理、精确框架模型以及真实图数据集上的实验证明：学到的映射可以支配拟合计算，但并非构成不可或缺的边几何。因此，对学到传输的主张应同时结合检查点干预和匹配重训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究将层图神经网络中学习到的限制映射视为模型发现了有用边几何的证据，但这种结论从参数移动或事后消融中并不能合理得出，因为两者只能显示一个检查点的组织方式，却无法回答当模型其余部分适应后，学到的传输是否仍然有帮助。

Method: 提出两种估计量——检查点依赖性（干预固定预测器的映射）和协议相对替换（重训练匹配的模型族，移除映射容量、边变化或持久边分配）。通过任务零定理证明标签仅识别被传输的分类器方向，留下d²-d个不可见自由度；构建精确框架模型给出依赖性能变为不可替换任务值的边界。

Result: 仅标签训练实现了预测的分离；在NSD、DNSD和DSNN真实图数据集上的审计同时发现了可替换和不可替换的传输机制。五个DNSD基准均表现出固定检查点依赖性。重训练后，四个数据集上分配破坏或共享映射控制恢复了完整性能；Roman-Empire在十个官方划分上比持续重采样分配保持0.0675的优势，比参数匹配的共享映射保持0.0391的优势。

Conclusion: 学到的映射可以支配拟合计算，但未必构成不可或缺的边几何。对学到传输的主张应同时使用检查点干预和匹配重训练来验证。

Original Abstract: Learned restriction maps in sheaf graph neural networks are often treated as proof that the model has discovered useful edge geometry. That conclusion does not follow from parameter movement or from a post-hoc ablation: both can show how one checkpoint is organized while leaving open whether learned transport still helps after the rest of the model adapts. We separate these claims with two estimands. Checkpoint reliance intervenes on the maps of a fixed predictor; protocol-relative replacement retrains matched families that remove map capacity, edge variation, or persistent edge assignment. A task-null theorem shows why the claims can diverge: labels identify only the transported classifier directions, leaving $d^2-d$ invisible degrees of freedom in every full $d\times d$ map. An exact frame model then gives the boundary at which reliance becomes unreplaced task value. Label-only training realizes the predicted separation, while audits of public NSD, DNSD, and Directed Sheaf Neural Network (DSNN) implementations recover both replaceable and unreplaced transport regimes on real graphs. All five DNSD benchmarks exhibit fixed-checkpoint reliance. After retraining, assignment-breaking or shared-map controls recover Full performance on four; Roman-Empire retains a $.0675$ advantage over continually resampled assignment and a $.0391$ advantage over a parameter-matched shared map across ten official splits. Thus, a learned map can govern a fitted computation without constituting indispensable edge geometry. Claims of learned transport should pair checkpoint interventions with matched retraining.

中文摘要翻译: 层图神经网络中学到的限制映射常被视为模型发现了有用边几何的证据。然而，这一结论无法从参数移动或事后消融中合理得出：两者都只能显示一个检查点的组织方式，却无法回答当模型其余部分适应后，学到的传输是否仍然有帮助。我们通过两种估计量分离这些主张。检查点依赖性干预固定预测器的映射；协议相对替换重训练匹配的模型族，移除映射容量、边变化或持久边分配。任务零定理揭示了主张可能分叉的原因：标签仅识别被传输的分类器方向，在每个完整d×d映射中留下d²-d个不可见自由度。随后一个精确框架模型给出了依赖性能变为不可替换任务值的边界。仅标签训练实现了预测的分离，而对公开的NSD、DNSD和Directed Sheaf Neural Network（DSNN）实现的审计在真实图上同时恢复了可替换和不可替换的传输机制。所有五个DNSD基准均表现出固定检查点依赖性。重训练后，四个数据集上分配破坏或共享映射控制恢复了完整性能；Roman-Empire在十个官方划分上比持续重采样分配保持0.0675的优势，比参数匹配的共享映射保持0.0391的优势。因此，学到的映射可以支配拟合计算，但未必构成不可或缺的边几何。对学到传输的主张应同时结合检查点干预与匹配重训练。

</details>


### [154] [TWICE: Two-Clock, Two-Window Learning for Long-Horizon Conversion Prediction in Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25404)
*Kaiyuan Li,Kun Wang,Zhongbo Wang,Teng Sha,Ming Yan,Yanhua Cheng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWICE是一个将长时域后点击转化率(CVR)分解为目标窗口转化概率和分组经过延迟累积分布函数的框架，利用双时钟（点击时钟和转化时钟）协同监督，在工业级广告系统中取得了显著收益提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在线广告中存在"双时钟、双窗口"的学习问题：短基观测窗口在转化结果成熟前释放近期点击（点击时钟），而转化在更长的目标转化窗口内持续到达（转化时钟）。点击时钟提供及时但部分观测的状态监督，转化时钟揭示长尾延迟但受历史点击队列的流量和转化率影响存在混合偏差。现有方法难以同时处理这两种互补但异构的监督信号。

Method: TWICE将长时域CVR分解为两部分：目标窗口转化概率（由点击时钟通过当前状态似然在基观测窗口上训练）和分组经过延迟累积分布函数（由转化时钟上新到达的转化训练）。为处理队列混合问题，TWICE使用固定的点击时刻预测CVR质量作为队列暴露度，构建到达条件似然，从而在一次学习中产生所有请求时域的单调预测，无需历史查找或卷积操作。

Result: 在公开基准和工业广告数据集上验证了有效性。在快手广告系统的在线A/B测试中，TWICE使预期收入提升2.486%，实际收入提升1.858%，转化数提升2.061%，随后部署到全流量。

Conclusion: TWICE通过双时钟分解框架有效解决了长时域延迟反馈下的转化预测问题，且在实际工业系统中带来了显著的收入和转化提升，证明了方法的实用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Long-horizon conversion prediction under delayed feedback creates a two-clock, two-window learning problem in online advertising. A short base observation window releases recent clicks on the click clock before their outcomes mature, whereas conversions continue to arrive on the conversion clock throughout a longer target conversion window. The click clock provides timely but partially observed status supervision. The conversion clock reveals long-tail delays, but the delay composition within an arrival-time slice is weighted by historical click cohorts with different traffic volumes and target-window conversion rates.
  We present TWICE, a framework that factorizes long-horizon post-click conversion rate (CVR) into a target-window conversion probability and a grouped elapsed-delay cumulative distribution function (CDF). The two clocks provide complementary supervision. Click-clock records train the target-window CVR head through a current-status likelihood over the base observation window. Newly arrived conversions train the delay model on the conversion clock. To account for the cohort mixture, TWICE uses fixed click-time predicted CVR (pCVR) mass as cohort exposure in an arrival-conditioned likelihood. This accounts for differences in cohort traffic and conversion propensity. The resulting aggregate records are self-contained. A single learned CDF produces monotone predictions for all requested horizons up to the target conversion window. Serving requires neither historical lookup nor convolution. Experiments on a public benchmark and an industrial advertising dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of TWICE. In an online A/B test in Kwai's advertising system, TWICE increased expected revenue, revenue, and conversions by 2.486%, 1.858%, and 2.061%, respectively. It was subsequently deployed to full traffic.

中文摘要翻译: 延迟反馈下的长时域转化预测为在线广告创造了一个双时钟、双窗口的学习问题。短的基观测窗口在点击结果成熟前释放点击时钟上的近期点击，而转化则在转化时钟上持续到达直至更长的目标转化窗口。点击时钟提供及时但部分观测的状态监督。转化时钟揭示长尾延迟，但在到达时间切片内的延迟组成受不同流量规模和目标窗口转化率的历史点击队列加权。我们提出TWICE，一个将长时域后点击转化率(CVR)分解为目标窗口转化概率和分组经过延迟累积分布函数(CDF)的框架。两个时钟提供互补的监督。点击时钟记录通过基观测窗口上的当前状态似然训练目标窗口CVR头。新到达的转化在转化时钟上训练延迟模型。为处理队列混合问题，TWICE使用固定的点击时刻预测CVR质量作为到达条件似然中的队列暴露度，从而解释了队列流量和转化倾向的差异。由此产生的聚合记录是自包含的。单个学习到的CDF可为所有请求时域（直至目标转化窗口）产生单调预测。服务阶段既不需要历史查找也不需要卷积操作。在公开基准和工业广告数据集上的实验证明了TWICE的有效性。在快手广告系统的在线A/B测试中，TWICE使预期收入、收入和转化数分别提升了2.486%、1.858%和2.061%，随后部署到全流量。

</details>


### [155] [SPARC Segmentation to Prediction via Affine Regression and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25413)
*Shivani,Subhayan Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对B2B电子商务中交易倾向预测的挑战，提出了一种基于多样化反事实解释（DiCE）的合成数据生成方法和PyPARC分段仿射分类框架，替代传统SMOTE方法，在高度不平衡数据上实现了93.1%的精确率，显著优于SMOTE基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: B2B电子商务中组织实体的异构采购行为导致样本内类别特征异质性，违反了SMOTE的隐含假设；B2B买家呈现多模态采购周期，使得少数类样本间的线性插值在结构上无效，生成的合成数据不能代表真实购买行为。

Method: (1) 用DiCE（多样化反事实解释）生成合成数据替代传统的SMOTE增强，产生分布保真度更高的少数类样本；(2) 采用PyPARC分段仿射分类框架生成校准的倾向概率，实现客户可解释的风险层级分割。

Result: 在两年纵向数据（1:9不平衡比）上，所提架构在决策阈值为0.8时达到93.1%精确率，比SMOTE基线（83.9%）提升9.2个百分点；在阈值0.7时（66.04%）提升26.1个百分点，在所有操作点均表现出一致的优越性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现高精度营销活动，显著提升客户激活率和投资回报率，有效解决了B2B交易倾向预测中类别不平衡和特征异质性问题。

Original Abstract: Transaction propensity prediction in B2B e commerce presents unique challenges distinct from B2C contexts, primarily due to the heterogeneous procurement behaviors of organizational entities, which violate SMOTE's implicit assumption of within class feature homogeneity. Specifically, B2B buyers exhibit multi modal procurement cycles that render linear interpolation between minority class samples structurally invalid, producing synthetic data that does not represent real purchasing behavior. This paper introduces a production deployed propensity modeling framework designed to address these complexities through two primary contributions. First, we replace conventional SMOTE based augmentation with a synthetic data generation approach leveraging Diverse Counterfactual Explanations (DiCE). This method produces minority class samples with superior distributional fidelity compared to SMOTE, as validated through quantitative proximity analysis and UMAP cluster visualization. Second, we adapt the PyPARC piecewise affine classification framework to generate calibrated propensity probabilities, facilitating the interpretable segmentation of customers into actionable risk tiers. Evaluated on two years of longitudinal data from a large scale B2B e commerce platform with a 1 to 9 class imbalance ratio, the proposed architecture achieves 93.1% precision at a decision threshold of 0.8, a 9.2 percentage point improvement over SMOTE based baselines at the same threshold (83.9%), and a 26.1 point improvement over SMOTE at threshold 0.7 (66.04%), demonstrating consistent superiority across operating points. These results demonstrate the framework's efficacy in enabling high precision marketing campaigns with significant improvements in customer activation and return on investment.

中文摘要翻译: B2B电子商务中的交易倾向预测面临着与B2C场景截然不同的独特挑战，这主要是由于组织实体的异构采购行为违反了SMOTE算法关于类别内特征同质性的隐含假设。具体而言，B2B买家表现出多模态采购周期，这使得少数类样本之间的线性插值在结构上无效，生成的合成数据不能代表真实的采购行为。本文引入了一个已投入生产的倾向建模框架，旨在通过两项主要贡献来解决这些复杂性。首先，我们利用基于多样化反事实解释（DiCE）的合成数据生成方法取代了传统的SMOTE增强技术。与SMOTE相比，该方法生成的少数类样本具有更优的分布保真度，这一点已通过定量邻近性分析和UMAP聚类可视化得到验证。其次，我们调整了PyPARC分段仿射分类框架，以生成校准的倾向概率，从而促进客户可解释的风险层级分割。在来自大型B2B电子商务平台的两年纵向数据（类别不平衡比为1:9）上进行的评估表明，所提出的架构在决策阈值为0.8时达到了93.1%的精确率，比相同阈值下基于SMOTE的基线（83.9%）提升了9.2个百分点，在阈值为0.7时比SMOTE（66.04%）提升了26.1个百分点，在所有操作点上均表现出一致的优越性。这些结果表明，该框架能够有效支持高精度营销活动，在客户激活率和投资回报率方面实现显著提升。

</details>


### [156] [PIcsC: Partitioning-Induced Covariate Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25441)
*Behraj Khan,Behroz Mirza,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Fisher信息的正则化框架PIcsC，用于缓解数据划分引起的协变量偏移问题，适用于交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习场景，在40多个数据集上取得了一致性改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 训练数据划分带来的协变量偏移会导致交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习中的模型选择偏差和参数估计不准确，现有方法缺乏统一的校正机制。

Method: 利用Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)近似划分数据与参考分布之间的散度，将得到的统计量作为正则化项加入优化目标；结合FIM偏移与KL散度设计条件自适应机制，仅在检测到显著分布偏移时激活正则化。

Result: 在40多个数据集上取得一致性改进；碎片化批次和折次设置中性能退化分别降低超过20%和25%；在7个联邦学习基准上比FedAvg、FedProx和SCAFFOLD高3-5个百分点。

Conclusion: Fisher信息提供了一种有效且统一的机制，能够在集中式和分布式学习场景下缓解数据划分引起的协变量偏移。

Original Abstract: Covariate shift across training-data partitions biases model selection and parameter estimation in cross-validation, lifelong learning, and federated learning. We propose \textit{Partition-Induced Covariate-shift Correction} (\texttt{PIcsC}), a Fisher information-based regularization framework that mitigates distribution mismatch between data partitions and a reference distribution. \texttt{PIcsC} approximates partition divergence using the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) and incorporates the resulting statistic as a regularizer during optimization. The same formulation applies to both centrally partitioned datasets (batches or cross-validation folds) and inherently distributed data (federated clients or decentralized nodes), requiring only partition-local gradient statistics rather than raw data. We further introduce a conditional adaptation mechanism that combines FIM shift with KL divergence to detect significant distribution shifts and activates regularization only when necessary. Experiments on more than 40 datasets demonstrate consistent improvements under both natural and synthetic covariate shift. On fragmented batch and fold settings, \texttt{PIcsC} reduces fragmentation-induced performance degradation by more than 20\% and 25\%, respectively. On seven federated learning benchmarks, it consistently outperforms FedAvg, FedProx, and SCAFFOLD by 3 -5 percentage points without requiring client-specific personalization. These results demonstrate that Fisher information provides an effective and unified mechanism for mitigating partition-induced covariate shift across both centralized and distributed learning.

中文摘要翻译: 跨训练数据划分的协变量偏移会在交叉验证、终身学习和联邦学习中导致模型选择偏差和参数估计不准确。我们提出\textit{划分诱导协变量偏移校正}（\texttt{PIcsC}），一种基于Fisher信息的正则化框架，用于缓解数据划分与参考分布之间的分布不匹配。\texttt{PIcsC}利用Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）近似划分散度，并将所得统计量作为正则化项纳入优化过程。该公式同时适用于集中划分的数据集（批次或交叉验证折次）和天然分布的数据集（联邦客户端或去中心化节点），仅需划分局部的梯度统计量而非原始数据。我们进一步引入一种条件自适应机制，将FIM偏移与KL散度相结合以检测显著分布偏移，并仅在必要时激活正则化。在40多个数据集上的实验表明，在自然和合成协变量偏移下均取得一致性改进。在碎片化的批次和折次设置中，\texttt{PIcsC}分别将碎片化导致的性能退化降低超过20\%和25\%。在七个联邦学习基准上，它无需客户端特定个性化即持续优于FedAvg、FedProx和SCAFFOLD 3至5个百分点。这些结果表明，Fisher信息为缓解集中式和分布式学习中的划分诱导协变量偏移提供了一种有效且统一的机制。

</details>


### [157] [Bits and Memories: Measuring Verbatim Extraction Across LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25451)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量化对语言模型逐字记忆数据的影响，发现量化会选择性遗忘记忆数据但效果不足以作为隐私防御，且大模型在低位量化下仍能保留大部分记忆内容。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作主要用成员推断攻击来度量量化后的隐私风险，但作者认为用户真正担心的是模型逐字复现训练数据，而非成员推断，因此需要直接测量量化对逐字提取的影响。

Method: 使用Pythia模型及其已知记忆的公开序列，在五种精度（全精度到4比特）和三种模型大小下，跟踪逐字提取率，同时测量困惑度以评估通用能力，采用两种量化算法和两种评估语料库。

Result: 量化具有选择性遗忘特性：逐字记忆下降速度比能力下降更快；但这种选择性不足以使量化成为隐私防御手段；在最大模型中，4比特量化仍能复现大部分记忆序列，且记忆数据存活率随模型规模增长而增加。

Conclusion: 压缩不应被视为移除训练记忆数据的手段，从业者应关注提取攻击而非成员推断攻击。

Original Abstract: Language models are almost always quantized before they are deployed, and a growing line of work asks whether quantization also lowers their privacy risk. That work measures privacy almost entirely with membership inference. We think this is the wrong thing to measure for the risk that most people actually worry about, namely a model reproducing its training data word for word, and we measure that directly. Using the Pythia models and the public set of sequences each of them is known to have memorized, we track verbatim extraction across five precision levels, from full precision down to four bits, and across three model sizes, while measuring general capability (perplexity) at every point. We find two things. Quantization is a selective forgetter: verbatim memorization falls off faster than capability at every precision and every model size we tried, and this holds under two unrelated quantization algorithms and two evaluation corpora. But the selectivity is not enough to make quantization a privacy defense, which cuts against the optimistic reading of earlier membership-inference results. At the largest model we study, four-bit quantization still reproduces most of the memorized sequences while giving up only a few percent of capability, and the fraction of memorized data that survives quantization grows with model size. We conclude that compression should not be treated as a way to remove memorized training data, and that extraction, not membership inference, is the number practitioners should be watching. All code, sampled evaluation data, and per-configuration results are released.

中文摘要翻译: 语言模型在部署前几乎都会被量化，越来越多的研究工作开始探讨量化是否会降低其隐私风险。这些工作几乎完全使用成员推断来度量隐私。我们认为，对于大多数人真正担心的风险——即模型逐字复现其训练数据——成员推断是错误度量指标，因此我们直接测量逐字提取。利用Pythia模型及其已知记忆的公开序列集，我们跟踪了五种精度水平（从全精度到4比特）、三种模型规模下的逐字提取情况，并在每个设置下测量了通用能力（困惑度）。我们有两个发现。量化是一种选择性遗忘机制：在我们尝试的每种精度和每种模型规模下，逐字记忆的下降速度都快于能力下降，且这一结论在两种无关的量化算法和两种评估语料库上均成立。但这种选择性不足以使量化成为一种隐私防御手段，这与早期成员推断结果的乐观解读相悖。在我们研究的最大模型中，4比特量化在仅牺牲百分之几能力的情况下，仍然能复现大部分记忆序列，且记忆数据在量化后的存活率随模型规模增大而上升。我们的结论是：不应将压缩视为移除训练记忆数据的手段，从业者应关注提取攻击而非成员推断。所有代码、采样评估数据及每个配置的结果均已公开发布。

</details>


### [158] [Emergent Latent-State Computation under Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25459)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Lulu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列模型在部分可观测、含噪声的隐随机动力学场景下的内部表征机制，以多元随机波动率模型为基准，发现模型采用两阶段计算：隐层编码下一时刻潜状态信息，输出头将其映射为预测；在Transformer中，潜状态解码能力出现在特定架构阶段，且可简化为显式潜状态滤波器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前机械可解释性研究主要聚焦于语言模型和确定性玩具任务，对序列模型如何在含噪声、部分可观测的观测条件下内部表征隐式随机动力学的理解十分有限。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在受控的多元随机波动率设定下，模型仅观测收益率，而真实潜波动率状态对研究者已知。通过跨架构、损失函数和输出头的系统性分析，结合隐状态解码能力评估、输出头替换实验等方法，揭示模型内部的表征和计算机制。

Result: （1）两阶段计算：隐层编码下一时刻潜状态信息，输出头将其映射到平方收益率预测；（2）Transformer中潜状态解码能力出现在特定架构阶段，位置取决于波动率周期；（3）长周期模式下，计算简化为线性投影加ℓ²归一化的显式潜状态滤波器；（4）部分MSE训练下的性能退化源于读出对齐问题而非表征失败。

Conclusion: 随机波动率模型为在含噪声隐动力学和部分可观测条件下进行机械可解释性研究提供了一个有用的基准。

Original Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has largely focused on language models and deterministic toy tasks. Much less is known about how sequence models internally represent latent stochastic dynamics under noisy, partially observed observations. We study this question in a controlled multivariate stochastic volatility setting, where models observe only returns while the ground-truth latent volatility state is known to the researcher. This setting provides a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under partial observability: the latent state is hidden from the model but directly available for evaluation. Across architectures, losses, and output heads, we find evidence for a two-stage computation. Hidden representations encode substantial information about the next latent volatility state, and the output head maps this representation to squared return forecasts. Furthermore, in Transformers, latent-state decodability emerges at identifiable architectural stages whose location depends on the volatility period. In long-cycle regimes, this computation simplifies into an explicit latent-state filter consisting of a learned linear projection followed by $\ell^2$ normalization. Output-head replacement further shows that part of the degradation under noisy MSE training arises from readout misalignment rather than representation failure. These results suggest that stochastic volatility models provide a useful benchmark for mechanistic interpretability under noisy latent dynamics and partial observability.

中文摘要翻译: 机械可解释性研究主要集中于语言模型和确定性玩具任务。对于序列模型如何在含噪声、部分可观测的观测条件下内部表征隐式随机动力学，目前了解甚少。本文在一个受控的多元随机波动率设定下研究该问题，其中模型仅观测收益率，而真实潜波动率状态对研究者已知。该设定为部分可观测条件下的机械可解释性提供了一个有用的基准：潜状态对模型而言是隐藏的，但对研究者直接可用于评估。跨架构、损失函数和输出头的实验表明，模型存在一种两阶段计算。隐层表征编码了关于下一时刻潜波动率状态的显著信息，输出头将该表征映射到平方收益率预测。此外，在Transformer中，潜状态解码能力出现在可识别的架构阶段，其位置取决于波动率周期。在长周期模式下，该计算简化为一个显式的潜状态滤波器，由学习到的线性投影后接ℓ²归一化组成。输出头替换实验进一步表明，在含噪MSE训练下，部分性能退化源于读出对齐问题而非表征失败。这些结果表明，随机波动率模型为含噪声隐动力学和部分可观测条件下的机械可解释性研究提供了一个有用的基准。

</details>


### [159] [Data-Dependent Regret and Polyak Corrections for Constrained Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25480)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带约束的在线凸优化问题，改进了OGD结合Polyak可行步法的遗憾界分析，通过保留数据依赖的梯度累积项和Polyak校正项，获得了更紧的非负改进界，并提出了自适应步长方法AdaOGD-PFS，实验验证了38-43%的遗憾界提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有带约束在线凸优化方法虽然能达到O(√T)遗憾界，但标准最坏情况分析忽略了数据依赖信息，导致理论上界过于宽松。本文旨在通过保留被忽略的梯度累积量和Polyak校正项，推导更紧的数据依赖遗憾界。

Method: 1) 保留梯度累积量G_T = Σ_t ||∇f_t(x_t)||²替代固定上界G_f²T；2) 识别非负的Polyak校正项P_T，衡量可行投影导致的累积平方位移；3) 推导出非负改进项Δ_T = (η/2)(G_f²T - G_T) + P_T/(2η)；4) 提出自适应步长方法AdaOGD-PFS，实现O(√G_T)遗憾界并保持每轮可行性。

Result: 在球约束和半空间约束问题上，遗憾界改进了38%至43%，其中数据依赖梯度和Polyak校正项均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 通过保留数据依赖的梯度累积和Polyak校正项，可以显著收紧带约束在线凸优化的遗憾界，且自适应步长方法在实际问题中取得了实质性的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Constrained online convex optimization requires minimizing regret against adversarial convex costs while satisfying a convex constraint at every round, as needed in safety-critical applications. A computationally efficient method combines online gradient descent with a Polyak feasibility step, using one constraint evaluation and one subgradient per round. Although this method achieves O(sqrt(T)) regret with per-round feasibility, we derive a tighter, data-dependent analysis by retaining two quantities omitted by the standard worst-case argument. First, we replace the gradient envelope G_f^2 T with the observed accumulation G_T = sum_t ||grad f_t(x_t)||^2. Second, we identify a nonnegative Polyak correction P_T that measures the cumulative squared displacement caused by feasibility projections and enters the regret bound with a negative sign. The resulting improvement, Delta_T = (eta/2)(G_f^2 T - G_T) + P_T/(2 eta), is always nonnegative. We further propose AdaOGD-PFS, an adaptive-step-size method that achieves O(sqrt(G_T)) regret while preserving per-round feasibility. Experiments on ball- and halfspace-constrained problems improve the regret bound by 38 to 43 percent, with both data-dependent gradients and Polyak corrections contributing substantially.

中文摘要翻译: 约束在线凸优化要求在对抗性凸代价下最小化遗憾，同时满足每轮的凸约束（这在安全关键应用中必不可少）。一种计算高效的方法将在线梯度下降与Polyak可行步相结合，每轮仅需一次约束评估和一个次梯度。尽管该方法在每轮可行性下实现了O(√T)遗憾界，我们通过保留标准最坏情况论证中忽略的两个量，推导出更紧的数据依赖分析。首先，我们将梯度包络G_f²T替换为观测到的累积量G_T = Σ_t ||∇f_t(x_t)||²。其次，我们识别出一个非负的Polyak校正项P_T，它衡量由可行投影引起的累积平方位移，并以负号进入遗憾界。由此产生的改进项Δ_T = (η/2)(G_f²T - G_T) + P_T/(2η)始终非负。我们进一步提出自适应步长方法AdaOGD-PFS，在保持每轮可行性的同时实现了O(√G_T)遗憾界。在球约束和半空间约束问题上的实验将遗憾界改进了38%至43%，其中数据依赖梯度和Polyak校正项均做出了实质性贡献。

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Speedups for Stochastic Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25492)
*Bin Luo,Chengchang Liu,Jonathan Allcock,Shengyu Zhang,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾梯度噪声的随机优化问题，提出了量子均值估计器和量子随机梯度下降方法（QNSGD、QPSGD），在低维场景下显著降低了查询复杂度，并证明了与经典方法相比的量子优越性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典随机优化在处理重尾梯度噪声时存在查询复杂度下界瓶颈，量子计算有望通过量子均值估计突破这一限制，实现更高效的优化。

Method: 1) 提出了针对多元重尾随机变量的量子均值估计器，利用多级蒙特卡洛技术实现无偏估计；2) 基于该估计器，提出了量子归一化随机梯度下降（QNSGD）用于非凸优化，以及量子投影随机梯度下降（QPSGD）用于凸优化。

Result: 非凸优化中，QNSGD达到ε-驻点的查询复杂度为Õ(√d ε^{-(5p-4)/(2p-2)})；凸优化中，QPSGD达到ε-最优解的期望查询复杂度为Õ(√d ε^{-(3p-2)/(2p-2)} + ε^{-2})。在低维场景下，这些结果优于经典下界Ω(ε^{-(3p-2)/(p-1)})（非凸）和Ω(ε^{-p/(p-1)})（凸）。

Conclusion: 量子方法能在低维重尾随机优化中实现比经典方法更优的查询复杂度，且维度依赖在低维场景下是不可避免的。

Original Abstract: We study stochastic optimization with heavy-tailed gradient noise. We first propose a novel quantum mean estimator for multivariate heavy-tailed random variables that achieves lower query complexity than optimal classical estimators in the low-dimensional regime. We further develop an unbiased quantum mean estimator by applying a generalized multi-level Monte Carlo technique. We prove quantum lower bounds showing that, when the dimension $d$ of the random vector is small and can be viewed as a constant, our quantum estimators are optimal up to logarithmic factors. We further derive stronger dimension-dependent lower bounds for tail index $p>4/3$, showing that a nontrivial dependence on the dimension is unavoidable in the low-dimensional regime. Based on these estimators, we propose a quantum normalized stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QNSGD}$), which finds an $ε$-stationary point using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}}\big)$ queries to the quantum stochastic gradient oracle. For a convex objective function, we propose a quantum projected stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QPSGD}$), which computes a solution with $ε$-optimal solution using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+ε^{-2}\big)$ queries in expectation. These sharper bounds improve upon the classical lower bounds $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}}\big)$ for nonconvex problems and $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}\big)$ for convex problems in the low-dimensional regimes $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}$ and $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{2-p}{p-1}}$, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了具有重尾梯度噪声的随机优化问题。首先，针对多元重尾随机变量，我们提出了一种新颖的量子均值估计器，在低维场景下达到了比最优经典估计器更低的查询复杂度。通过应用广义多级蒙特卡洛技术，我们进一步开发了一种无偏的量子均值估计器。我们证明了量子下界：当随机向量的维度d较小且可视为常数时，我们的量子估计器在忽略对数因子意义下是最优的。对于尾部指数p>4/3，我们推导了更强的维度相关下界，表明在低维场景下对维度的非平凡依赖是不可避免的。基于这些估计器，我们提出了量子归一化随机梯度下降方法（QNSGD），该方法使用Õ(√d ε^{-(5p-4)/(2p-2)})次量子随机梯度查询即可找到ε-驻点。对于凸目标函数，我们提出了量子投影随机梯度下降方法（QPSGD），其期望查询复杂度为Õ(√d ε^{-(3p-2)/(2p-2)} + ε^{-2})。在低维场景d≲ε^{-p/(p-1)}和d≲ε^{-(2-p)/(p-1)}下，这些更优的复杂度界分别改进了非凸问题的经典下界Ω(ε^{-(3p-2)/(p-1)})和凸问题的经典下界Ω(ε^{-p/(p-1)})。

</details>


### [161] [Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection via Cumulative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25502)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将训练时后门防御问题形式化为反后门核心集选择问题，利用累积熵作为选择标准并引入样本遗忘机制，在几乎不影响自然精度的前提下有效防御多种后门攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有训练时后门防御方法通过从 poisoned 训练数据中隔离出良性子集来学习无后门模型，但缺乏统一的形式化框架，且存在自然精度下降、无法应对某些攻击等问题。

Method: 将防御策略形式化为核心集选择问题；使用累积熵作为选择标准，利用中毒样本预测不确定性低且数量少的特点，优先选择高信息量的良性样本；在每个epoch对已选样本进行遗忘以增强良性与中毒样本的可分离性。

Result: 与现有防御方法相比，该方法能一致地缓解后门攻击，且对自然性能的影响可忽略不计，克服了先前的防御方法在自然精度上妥协或无法应对某些攻击的缺陷。

Conclusion: 通过将训练时后门防御形式化为反后门核心集选择问题，结合累积熵和样本遗忘机制，能够构建出高效的良性核心集，从而训练出无后门模型，兼顾了防御效果与自然性能。

Original Abstract: Recent training-time defenses against neural backdoors isolate a benign subset from poisoned training data, to learn a backdoor-free model from it. In this paper, we formulate this defense strategy as a coreset selection problem, giving rise to so-called "Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection." Since poisonous samples have (a) lower prediction uncertainty and are (b) less frequent than benign samples, coreset selection naturally focuses more on samples associated with benign functionality than the backdoor functionality. We use the Cumulative Entropy as selection criterion to further facilitate this effect. The metric tracks the learning dynamics of training samples and allowing us to select benign samples with high informativeness for the coreset. Additionally, we unlearn the chosen samples in each epoch to facilitate the separability between benign and poisonous samples. Together, this yields an exceptionally effective training-time defense that constructs a benign coreset to train a backdoor-free model. Unlike prior defenses that compromise natural accuracy and fail against certain attacks, our method mitigates backdooring attacks consistently with a negligible impact on natural performance.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的训练时后门防御方法通过从被污染的训练数据中隔离出一个良性子集，并从中学习无后门模型。在本文中，我们将这一防御策略形式化为一个核心集选择问题，由此提出了所谓的"反后门核心集选择"。由于中毒样本(a)预测不确定性较低且(b)出现频率低于良性样本，核心集选择自然更关注与良性功能相关的样本而非后门功能。我们采用累积熵作为选择标准，以进一步增强这一效果。该度量追踪了训练样本的学习动态，使我们能够为核心集选择具有高信息量的良性样本。此外，我们在每个epoch中对所选样本进行遗忘处理，以增强良性与中毒样本之间的可分离性。通过这些方法，我们构建了一种极其有效的训练时防御机制，通过构建良性核心集来训练无后门模型。与那些会损害自然准确率且无法抵御某些攻击的先前防御方法不同，我们的方法能够一致地缓解后门攻击，同时对自然性能的影响可忽略不计。

</details>


### [162] [AMPBench-MT: A Homology-Controlled Benchmark for Antimicrobial Peptide Potency, Spectrum, and Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25518)
*Ziheng Zhou,Huiyu Luo,Xiaohu Zhu,Nan Wang,Xuebiao Qin,Chaoyan Zhang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMPBench-MT，一个保留数据来源的多任务基准，用于统一评估抗菌肽（AMP）的二元识别、物种条件效价、溶血毒性、选择性和广谱性。实验表明，高二元识别性能不能可靠预测实际化验端行为，应转向端点感知的证据审计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AMP基准仅覆盖二元识别或多标签标注等单一任务，缺乏一个在序列同源性控制协议下联合评估AMP识别、物种条件效价、安全性代理终点及跨终点行为的统一框架。

Method: 构建AMPBench-MT基准，标准化规范化的多肽记录，组织成三个任务：二元识别、物种条件pMIC回归、端点特异性的效价和安全性读数。采用序列同源性控制协议，保留数据来源信息。

Result: 在161个端点特异性模型评估中，高二元性能不能可靠指示化验端行为。冻结的蛋白质语言模型嵌入形成最优pMIC误差簇，图和经典回归器紧随其后。广谱标签显示PR导向指标在负样本稀缺时具有误导性，而低毒性、HC50溶血和选择性指标暴露更小但更面向化验的信号。

Conclusion: AMP评估应超越二元识别排行榜，转向端点感知的证据审计。

Original Abstract: Computational AMP discovery is often evaluated through AMP/non-AMP recognition, yet follow-up decisions depend on assay-derived evidence such as target-species potency, hemolysis, toxicity, and selectivity. Existing AMP and peptide benchmarks cover binary recognition, multilabel annotation, assay regression, or broader peptide-model comparison, but they do not jointly place AMP recognition, species-conditioned potency, spectrum, safety-facing proxy endpoints, and cross-endpoint behavior within one sequence-homology-controlled protocol. To address this problem, we introduce AMPBench-MT, a provenance-preserving benchmark that standardizes canonical peptide records and organizes them into binary recognition, species-conditioned pMIC regression, and endpoint-specific potency and safety-facing readouts. Across 161 endpoint-specific model evaluations, high binary performance does not reliably indicate assay-endpoint behavior. Frozen protein-language-model embeddings form the leading pMIC error cluster, while graph and classical regressors remain close. Spectrum labels further reveal that PR-oriented metrics can be misleading under scarce observed negatives, whereas low-toxicity, HC50 hemolysis, and selectivity expose smaller but more assay-facing signals. AMPBench-MT shows that AMP evaluation should move beyond recognition leaderboards toward endpoint-aware evidence auditing. Our proposed benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT.

中文摘要翻译: 计算抗菌肽（AMP）发现通常通过AMP/非AMP识别进行评估，但后续决策依赖于化验来源的证据，如靶物种效价、溶血性、毒性和选择性。现有的AMP和多肽基准涵盖了二元识别、多标签注释、化验回归或更广泛的多肽模型比较，但它们未能在单一序列同源性控制协议下联合考虑AMP识别、物种条件效价、谱系、安全性代理端点以及跨端点行为。为解决此问题，我们提出AMPBench-MT，一个保留数据来源的基准，它标准化了规范化的多肽记录，并将其组织为二元识别、物种条件pMIC回归以及端点特异性效价和安全性读数。在161次端点特异性模型评估中，高二元性能并不能可靠地指示化验端点行为。冻结的蛋白质语言模型嵌入构成了领先的pMIC误差簇，而图和经典回归器保持接近。广谱标签进一步揭示，在观测到的负样本稀缺的情况下，PR导向的指标可能具有误导性，而低毒性、HC50溶血和选择性则暴露了更小但更面向化验的信号。AMPBench-MT表明，AMP评估应超越识别排行榜，转向端点感知的证据审计。我们提出的基准可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT获取。

</details>


### [163] [Multi-Scale Structural Features for Continual, Comprehensible Visual Recognition in a Developmental Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25531)
*Zeki Doruk Erden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于发育学习框架的多尺度视觉特征表示方法，用于二维形状的类增量学习，无需回放缓冲区或预定义任务边界，在MNIST基准上显著提升准确率，并保持无破坏性适应和可解释的内部结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器学习在持续学习、重用先验知识和提供可解释内部结构方面存在困难。此前提出的基于发育的无梯度学习框架虽能实现固有持续学习保证，但其视觉特征表示表达能力有限，限制了识别精度。

Method: 引入一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示，跨多个尺度编码形状结构，捕获边缘和轮廓特征及其空间关系；将该表示与网络精炼学习过程集成；改进了学习动态和用于从学习模型预测的读出机制。

Result: 在类增量MNIST基准上，该方法相比先前表示显著提升了准确率，在可比存储条件下达到或超过基于回放和正则化的基线方法，且不存储任何历史数据。关键区别在于保留能力：基线方法会在训练新类时丢失刚学过的类知识而后重新学习，而本方法不会。

Conclusion: 该方法通过逐样本整合信息并可证明地保持对先前输入的响应，实现了真正的持续学习，同时保持模型的可解释性，其意义在于学习方式的根本性差异。

Original Abstract: Contemporary machine learning struggles to learn continually, reuse prior knowledge, and expose a comprehensible internal structure. A recently proposed developmental, gradient-free learning framework addresses these limitations by learning a discrete, topological model of its inputs through local variation and selection, yielding an inherent continual-learning guarantee: new observations refine existing structure without overwriting past knowledge, and without replay buffers or predefined task boundaries. Its extension to visual inputs demonstrated this principle on shape recognition, but relied on a feature representation of limited expressivity that capped recognition accuracy. We introduce a new visual feature representation that encodes shape structure across multiple scales, capturing edge and contour features together with their spatial relations, and integrate it with the network-refinement learning process; we further improve the learning dynamics and the read-out used to predict from the learned model. The study targets two-dimensional shape, with class-incremental MNIST as a controlled, interpretable benchmark in which continual-learning behavior can be measured directly. Our approach substantially increases accuracy over the prior representation, matching or exceeding replay- and regularisation-based baselines at comparable storage while storing no past data, and preserves the framework's defining behavior: earlier-learned classes are retained as new ones are introduced, with no destructive adaptation, and the learned representations remain human-interpretable. What separates the methods is retention: the baselines surrender most of a just-trained class within its own cycle and relearn it afterwards, which ours does not. The significance lies in the manner of learning. The system integrates information one sample at a time while provably preserving its responses to...

中文摘要翻译: 当代机器学习在持续学习、重用先验知识以及展示可理解的内部结构方面面临挑战。最近提出的一种基于发育的无梯度学习框架通过局部变异与选择来学习输入的离散拓扑模型，从而解决这些局限，该框架具有内在的持续学习保证：新的观测结果会精炼现有结构而不会覆盖过去的知识，且无需回放缓冲区或预定义任务边界。该框架在视觉输入上的扩展已在形状识别中验证了这一原理，但其依赖的特征表示表达能力有限，限制了识别精度。我们引入了一种新的视觉特征表示，该表示跨多个尺度编码形状结构，捕获边缘和轮廓特征及其空间关系，并将其与网络精炼学习过程集成；我们进一步改进了学习动态以及用于从学习模型中预测的读出机制。本研究以二维形状为对象，以类增量MNIST作为受控、可解释的基准，在该基准上可以直接测量持续学习行为。我们的方法相比于先前的表示显著提高了准确率，在可比存储条件下达到或超过了基于回放和正则化的基线方法，同时不存储任何历史数据，并保留了该框架的核心行为：随着新类别的引入，早期学习的类别得以保留，无破坏性适应，且学习的表示仍然具有人类可解释性。这些方法之间的区别在于保留能力：基线方法在其自身周期内就会丢失大部分刚训练过的类别并在之后重新学习，而我们的方法不会。其意义在于学习的方式：系统逐样本整合信息，同时可证明地保持其对先前输入的响应……

</details>


### [164] [Mind the Missing Split: Resolving Feature Heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25538)
*Mohammad Tajabadi,Dominik Heider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对水平Swarm Learning中站点间特征空间部分重叠导致的随机森林推理时特征缺失问题，提出了多种确定性和概率性的推理策略，无需限制训练特征集，实验表明所提方法优于交集基线方法和本地模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际应用中不同站点的数据集因测量协议和协变量不同，往往只有部分重叠的特征空间，而传统水平Swarm Learning假设所有站点共享相同特征集，导致随机森林在全局聚合后推理时可能遇到本地不存在的分裂特征，迫使站点丢弃自身特有特征。

Method: 提出了多种推理时策略来解决随机森林在部分重叠特征空间下的缺失分裂问题，包括确定性策略和概率性策略，不限制训练时只能使用特征交集。

Result: 在九个数据集上评估，所提方法在广泛场景下均优于仅使用特征交集的基线和本地训练的模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的推理时策略能够有效处理Swarm Learning中随机森林模型的特征异构性问题，使得各站点无需丢弃自身特有特征即可参与全局协作学习。

Original Abstract: Swarm Learning is a decentralized collaborative learning mechanism that allows multiple organizations to train a shared model without central coordination or direct data sharing. In typical horizontal Swarm Learning, datasets across sites are usually assumed to share the same feature set. However, in real-world applications, sites often have partially overlapping features because measurements, protocols, and available covariates differ across sites. This feature heterogeneity creates a practical issue for machine learning algorithms such as Random Forests. Specifically, when decision trees are pooled into a global Random Forest, inference at a given site can become ill-defined if a traversal encounters a split on a feature that is not available locally, often forcing organizations to discard site-specific variables upfront. In this paper, we address feature heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests under partially overlapping feature spaces. We propose several deterministic and probabilistic inference-time strategies that resolve such missing splits without restricting training to the intersection of features. We evaluate the methods on nine datasets and demonstrate that they outperform both the intersection baseline and locally trained models across a broad range of scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: Swarm Learning是一种去中心化的协作学习机制，允许多个组织在没有中央协调或直接数据共享的情况下训练共享模型。在典型的水平Swarm Learning中，通常假设各站点的数据集共享相同的特征集。然而，在实际应用中，由于不同站点的测量方法、协议和可用协变量存在差异，站点的特征往往只有部分重叠。这种特征异质性给随机森林等机器学习算法带来了实际问题。具体而言，当决策树被聚合为全局随机森林时，如果某次遍历遇到本地不可用的分裂特征，则该站点的推理可能变得无定义，这通常迫使组织在训练之初就丢弃站点特有的变量。本文研究了在部分重叠特征空间下基于随机森林的Swarm Learning中的特征异质性问题。我们提出了多种确定性和概率性的推理时策略，这些策略无需将训练限制在特征交集上即可解决缺失分裂问题。我们在九个数据集上对所提方法进行了评估，结果表明在广泛场景下，它们均优于仅使用特征交集的基线方法和本地训练的模型。

</details>


### [165] [OrthKD: Extracting Generalized Clinical Knowledge from Heterogeneous Teachers for Lightweight Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25545)
*Yi Xu,Cheng Chen,Mufan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OrthKD，一种面向糖尿病视网膜病变筛查的选择性信任知识蒸馏框架，通过差异化处理强CNN教师和弱ViT教师的监督信号，使轻量级学生模型在资源受限设备上保持高精度和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多教师知识蒸馏方法假定所有教师提供同等可信的监督，但在DR筛查场景中，强CNN教师和弱Transformer教师的互补性导致弱教师logits会误导学生模型，需要选择性信任蒸馏策略。

Method: OrthKD框架：从强CNN教师（EfficientNet-B3）进行完整logits蒸馏+特征蒸馏；从弱ViT教师（Swin-Base）仅进行特征蒸馏；并强制教师特定的学生投影之间正交，以鼓励互补而非冗余的证据。

Result: 在132,049张视网膜图像上，5.4M参数的MobileNetV3学生在EyePACS上达到0.885 QWK；零样本迁移至Messidor-2时性能从0.507提升至0.728 QWK；同时实现了强转诊AUC和良好校准。

Conclusion: 选择性地蒸馏异构教师模型能使糖尿病视网膜病变筛查模型在资源受限设备上实现实际部署，兼顾精度、安全性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Deploying diabetic retinopathy (DR) screening models in primary care requires edge-efficient systems that remain accurate, safe, and reliable under domain shift. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation (KD) is a natural compression strategy, but existing approaches largely assume that all teachers provide equally trustworthy supervision. In our setting, this assumption fails: a strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3, 0.876 QWK) and a weaker Transformer teacher (Swin-Base, 0.830 QWK) are complementary, yet the Transformer's logits can still mislead the student. We therefore propose OrthKD, a selective-trust distillation framework that transfers full supervision from the strong CNN, uses feature-only distillation from the weak ViT, and enforces orthogonality between teacher-specific student projections to encourage complementary rather than redundant evidence. This design preserves local lesion precision, injects global structural context, and improves robustness to distribution shift. On 132,049 retinal images, a 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student reaches 0.885 QWK on EyePACS and improves zero-shot Messidor-2 performance from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, while also achieving strong referral AUC and calibration. These results show that selectively distilling heterogeneous teachers can enable practical DR screening on resource-constrained devices.

中文摘要翻译: 在初级医疗中部署糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）筛查模型需要边缘高效的系统，使其在域偏移下仍保持准确、安全和可靠。多教师知识蒸馏（KD）是一种自然的压缩策略，但现有方法大多假设所有教师提供同等可信的监督。在我们的设定中，这一假设不成立：一个强CNN教师（EfficientNet-B3，0.876 QWK）和一个弱Transformer教师（Swin-Base，0.830 QWK）具有互补性，但Transformer的logits仍可能误导学生。因此，我们提出OrthKD，一种选择性信任蒸馏框架，从强CNN教师传递完整监督，从弱ViT教师仅进行特征蒸馏，并强制教师特定的学生投影之间正交，以鼓励互补而非冗余的证据。该设计保留了局部病灶精度，注入了全局结构上下文，并提升了对分布偏移的鲁棒性。在132,049张视网膜图像上，5.4M参数的MobileNetV3学生在EyePACS上达到0.885 QWK，并将零样本Messidor-2性能从0.507 QWK提升至0.728 QWK，同时实现了强转诊AUC和良好校准。这些结果表明，选择性地蒸馏异构教师模型能够实现资源受限设备上的实用DR筛查。

</details>


### [166] [Physics-Informed Broad Learning System: An Efficient Backpropagation-Free Framework for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25608)
*Pinki Khatun,M. Sajid,Abhinav Jha,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息宽度学习系统（PI-BLS），通过将物理定律嵌入线性输出层优化问题，用伪逆求解代替非线性梯度训练，实现高效求解偏微分方程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统物理信息神经网络（PINNs）依赖计算昂贵的梯度优化和深层架构，导致训练速度慢、计算成本高、可扩展性有限。

Method: 提出PI-BLS框架，将控制微分算子及初边值条件直接嵌入线性输出层优化问题，通过伪逆求解确定性最小二乘解，将整个学习过程简化为单步线性优化，无需迭代反向传播。

Result: 在代表性前向偏微分方程基准测试上，PI-BLS在减少训练时间和模型参数的同时，取得了与常规PINNs相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: PI-BLS为求解偏微分方程提供了一种高效的学习范式，消除了迭代反向传播同时保持物理约束，具有更快的训练速度和更低的计算成本。

Original Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding governing physical laws into deep neural networks. However, their reliance on computationally expensive gradient-based optimization and deep architectures often results in slow training, high computational cost, and limited scalability. In this work, we propose a novel physics-informed broad learning system (PI-BLS), the first physics-informed learning framework based on broad RdNNs. The proposed formulation embeds the governing differential operator and the associated initial and boundary constraints directly into a linear output-layer optimization problem, thereby replacing nonlinear gradient-based training with a deterministic least-squares solution obtained via the pseudoinverse. Consequently, the entire learning process is reduced to a single linear optimization stage while preserving the underlying physical constraints. As a result, PI-BLS offers an efficient learning paradigm for a physics-informed learning framework for solving PDEs that eliminates iterative backpropagation while preserving the underlying physical constraints. Experimental results on representative forward PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PI-BLS achieves competitive and often superior performance with reduced training time and model parameters compared with conventional PINNs.

中文摘要翻译: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）通过将控制物理定律嵌入深度神经网络，已成为求解偏微分方程（PDEs）的强大范式。然而，其依赖计算昂贵的基于梯度的优化和深层架构，往往导致训练缓慢、计算成本高且可扩展性有限。本文提出了一种新颖的物理信息宽度学习系统（PI-BLS），这是首个基于宽度RdNNs的物理信息学习框架。该公式将控制微分算子及相关的初始条件和边界条件直接嵌入线性输出层优化问题，从而将非线性梯度训练替换为通过伪逆求解的确定性最小二乘解。因此，整个学习过程简化为单步线性优化阶段，同时保持底层物理约束。实验结果表明，在代表性前向PDE基准测试上，PI-BLS在减少训练时间和模型参数的同时，取得了与常规PINNs相当甚至更优的性能。

</details>


### [167] [Contrastive Representation Learning of Longitudinal Disease Trajectories on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25609)
*Bastian Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比图神经网络的框架，将纵向临床数据建模为时序图，通过结构感知随机游走进行对比表示学习，从而实现对疾病进展轨迹的鲁棒聚类和潜在结构挖掘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从纵向临床数据中理解疾病轨迹具有挑战性，主要由于复杂的时序动态和异质性的患者群体。

Method: 将多变量疾病轨迹建模为时序图（节点为历次观测，边捕获时间连续性和轨迹间的结构相似性），利用结构感知随机游走引导对比学习，生成保留时序上下文和轨迹拓扑的嵌入表示。

Result: 生成的表示能够实现对具有相似疾病进展模式的患者的鲁棒聚类，并揭示纵向数据中的潜在结构。

Conclusion: 该对比表示学习框架能有效建模疾病轨迹的复杂时序动态，发现异质性患者群体中隐藏的进展模式。

Original Abstract: Understanding disease trajectories from longitudinal clinical data remains challenging due to complex temporal dynamics and heterogeneous patient cohorts. Here, we present a contrastive representation learning framework that models multivariate disease trajectories as temporal graphs and learns representations using contrastive graph neural networks. Nodes represent patient observations over time, while edges capture temporal continuity and structural similarity between trajectories. Structure-aware random walks guide contrastive learning to generate embeddings that preserve temporal context and trajectory topology. The resulting representations enable robust clustering of patients with similar disease progression patterns and reveal latent structure in longitudinal data.

中文摘要翻译: 从纵向临床数据中理解疾病轨迹仍然具有挑战性，原因在于复杂的时序动态和异质性的患者群体。本文提出了一种对比表示学习框架，将多变量疾病轨迹建模为时序图，并使用对比图神经网络进行学习。图中的节点表示患者在不同时间点的观测数据，边则捕获轨迹之间的时间连续性和结构相似性。结构感知的随机游走引导对比学习过程，生成保留时序上下文和轨迹拓扑结构的嵌入表示。所得表示能够对具有相似疾病进展模式的患者进行鲁棒聚类，并揭示纵向数据中的潜在结构。

</details>


### [168] [MemSFT: Mitigating Alignment Tax with an External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25614)
*Jiarui Wang,Xiang Shi,Jiaqi Cao,Rubin Wei,Xiquan Wang,Hao Sun,Jingzhi Wang,Zhiqi Yang,Qipeng Guo,Bowen Zhou,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MemSFT通过即插即用参数化记忆解耦领域专业化与骨干参数更新，在生物学、地球科学和法律等领域的评估中（Qwen3-8B至235B模型），显著提升领域性能而通用性能几乎无损，解决了全参数微调带来的灾难性遗忘问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将大语言模型适配到专业领域时，领域微调会导致灾难性遗忘，严重损害通用任务性能（即"对齐税"）。现有方法难以在增强领域能力的同时保持通用能力。

Method: 提出MemSFT框架：1) 训练一个可插拔的参数化记忆模块，模仿非参数化检索器在领域数据上的行为；2) 训练好的记忆模块可跨不同规模LLM复用；3) 解码时，学习到的路由动态融合记忆与骨干网络的输出分布，选择性调用领域知识。

Result: 在生物学、地球科学和法律三个领域，使用Qwen3-8B到Qwen3-235B-A22B等不同规模模型，MemSFT持续提升领域性能，通用性能退化极小；而全参数SFT在通用任务上遭受严重遗忘。

Conclusion: MemSFT在参数级别将通用模型能力与领域知识解耦，使LLM在不牺牲通用能力的前提下获得新的专业能力，为领域自适应提供了一条实用路径。

Original Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains often incurs an alignment tax, as fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks can cause catastrophic forgetting and substantially degrade performance on general tasks. We propose MemSFT, which mitigates the alignment tax by decoupling domain specialization from backbone parameter updates through a plug-and-play parametric memory. The memory is trained to imitate the behavior of a non-parametric retriever operating over domain data, thereby memorizing knowledge and patterns that would otherwise be accessed through retrieval. Once trained on a specific domain, the memory can be reused across LLMs of different sizes. During generation, a learned router dynamically fuses the output distributions of the memory and backbone at each decoding step, allowing domain expertise to be invoked selectively. Across biology, geoscience, and law, evaluations with models ranging from Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-235B-A22B show that MemSFT consistently improves domain performance with negligible degradation in general performance, whereas full SFT suffers severe forgetting on general tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate a practical path to decoupling general model capabilities from domain-specific knowledge at the parameter level, thereby equipping LLMs with new specialized capabilities without compromising their general capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 将大语言模型（LLM）适配到专业领域通常会带来"对齐税"，因为对领域特定任务的微调可能导致灾难性遗忘，并显著降低通用任务的表现。我们提出MemSFT，通过一个即插即用的参数化记忆模块将领域专业化与骨干参数更新解耦，从而缓解对齐税。该记忆模块经过训练以模仿一个在领域数据上运行的非参数化检索器的行为，从而记忆那些本应通过检索获取的知识和模式。一旦在特定领域完成训练，该记忆模块可跨不同规模的LLM复用。在生成过程中，一个学习到的路由在每个解码步骤动态融合记忆模块与骨干网络的输出分布，从而有选择性地调用领域知识。在生物学、地球科学和法律领域的评估中，使用从Qwen3-8B到Qwen3-235B-A22B的模型，结果表明MemSFT持续提升领域性能，而通用性能退化极小，相比之下全参数SFT在通用任务上遭受严重的遗忘。总体而言，我们的结果展示了一条在参数级别将通用模型能力与领域知识解耦的实用路径，从而使LLM在不牺牲通用能力的前提下获得新的专业能力。

</details>


### [169] [Using Data-Derived Priors to Guide CNN Architecture Design for NIR Chemometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了25个近红外光谱回归任务中数据集属性与CNN架构超参数之间的关系，发现光谱熵、内在秩、小波能量支撑分数等描述符可以指导卷积核尺寸和学习率的选择，提出了基于数据先验的启发式CNN设计策略，其性能接近贝叶斯超参数优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前近红外化学计量学中的CNN设计通常采用通用架构规则，忽略了光谱数据集在采样、平滑度、冗余度和样本量等方面的差异。作者希望验证是否可以利用这些数据集属性作为CNN设计的经验先验知识，从而更高效地指导模型配置。

Method: 在25个近红外回归任务上，计算数据集大小、光谱长度与间距、熵、内在秩、自相关和小波尺度结构等描述符。设计两种可解释的1D-CNN架构（最小单卷积模型和扩展浅层模型），通过五折交叉验证的贝叶斯超参数优化进行调优。从近优试验中提取关系并转化为热启动启发式规则，通过直接评估和留一数据集验证进行检验。此外，还进行了联合预处理与CNN超参数优化的对比实验。

Result: 最小CNN中，首选卷积核比例随光谱熵和内在秩增加而降低，随小波能量支撑比例增加而升高；学习率随训练集规模增大而降低。直接启发式和留一数据集启发式的测试RMSE比值中位数分别为0.953和1.017，与超参数优化性能相当。扩展CNN的结构关系类似但可迁移性较弱。联合预处理与CNN超参数优化在25个任务中有19个优于仅标准化光谱的超参数优化。

Conclusion: 光谱数据集描述符可以为CNN设计提供实用的先验知识，在目标特定微调之前引导浅层近红外模型进入合理的超参数区域，从而减少对昂贵超参数优化的依赖。

Original Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) for near-infrared (NIR) chemometrics are often designed using generic architectural rules, although spectral datasets differ in sampling, smoothness, redundancy, and sample size. We tested whether these properties can provide empirical priors for CNN design. Across 25 NIR regression tasks, we computed descriptors of dataset size, spectral length and spacing, entropy, intrinsic rank, autocorrelation, and wavelet-scale structure. Two interpretable 1D-CNN scaffolds (a minimal single-convolution model and an extended shallow model with optional branching, dilation, etc) were optimized using five-fold cross-validated Bayesian hyperparameter optimization (HPO). Relationships extracted from near-optimal trials were converted into warm-start heuristics and evaluated directly and through leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) validation. The clearest relationships involved convolutional receptive fields. In the minimal CNN, the preferred kernel fraction decreased with spectral entropy and intrinsic rank, increased with the wavelet energy-support fraction, and the learning rate tended to decrease with training-set size. Direct and LODO heuristics were competitive with HPO, with median test-RMSE ratios of 0.953 and 1.017, respectively. The extended CNN showed similar but less transferable structure across branch usage, dilation, dropout, filter counts, and receptive-field choices. Ten stochastic refits showed seed sensitivity comparable to that of HPO-selected configurations. In a separate experiment, joint preprocessing and CNN HPO outperformed standardized-spectra HPO in 19 of 25 tasks, although gains were dataset-dependent. These results show that spectral descriptors can provide practical CNN design priors, guiding shallow NIR models toward plausible hyperparameter regions before target-specific tuning

中文摘要翻译: 用于近红外化学计量学的卷积神经网络通常采用通用架构规则设计，尽管光谱数据集在采样、平滑度、冗余度和样本量上存在差异。我们测试了这些属性是否能为CNN设计提供经验先验。在25个近红外回归任务中，我们计算了数据集规模、光谱长度与间距、熵、内在秩、自相关和小波尺度结构等描述符。两种可解释的1D-CNN骨架（一个最小单卷积模型和一个扩展浅层模型，支持分支、膨胀等可选结构）通过五折交叉验证的贝叶斯超参数优化进行调优。从近优试验中提取的关系被转化为热启动启发式规则，并通过直接评估和留一数据集验证进行检验。最清晰的关系涉及卷积感受野。在最小CNN中，首选卷积核比例随光谱熵和内在秩增加而降低，随小波能量支撑比例增加而升高；学习率随训练集规模增大而降低。直接启发式和留一数据集启发式与超参数优化表现相当，测试RMSE比值中位数分别为0.953和1.017。扩展CNN在分支使用、膨胀、丢弃率、滤波器数量和感受野选择上表现出类似但可迁移性较差的结构。十次随机重播种对超参数优化选择配置的种子敏感性相当。在另一项实验中，联合预处理与CNN超参数优化在25个任务中有19个优于标准化光谱超参数优化，尽管增益依赖于具体数据集。这些结果表明，光谱描述符可以为CNN设计提供实用的先验知识，在目标特定调优之前引导浅层近红外模型进入合理的超参数区域。

</details>


### [170] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文反卷积（CD）方法，将需求感知重构为凸分解问题，通过核调制带状算子分离促销冲击与平滑基线，并利用层次化部分池化实现目录级部署。实验表明，CD在降低安全库存、持有成本和订单方差的同时，仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时才能降低总成本，其核心贡献在于预测可靠性而非中心趋势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 机器学习需求预测虽能优化统计精度，但会遗留过度运营波动性，导致安全库存膨胀和牛鞭效应放大。现有方法未能有效分离促销驱动的瞬态冲击与结构性需求基线，迫切需要一种既能提升运营稳定性又无需逐SKU训练的预测框架。

Method: 提出Contextual Deconvolution (CD)两阶段估计器：1）核调制带状算子将瞬态促销冲击从平滑结构基线中凸分解；2）层次化部分池化实现目录级规模部署，无需逐SKU训练。该算子由数据驱动而非预设，当促销响应为脉冲式时退化为恒等映射，仅在存在真实多日结转效应时发挥作用。

Result: 在30,490个M5 SKU和2,845个Favorita商品上进行严格样本外评估：CD在11个基线方法中取得了最低的横截面误差离散度，在四个M5数据划分中均排名第一；仅0.8%的SKU出现高度误预测（>200%），远低于基线的9.9-20.6%。但CD会低估事件峰值，仅在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%（95% CI [17%,25%]）时降低总成本。

Conclusion: CD的准确性贡献在于可靠性而非中心趋势；它作为运营稳定性和库存资本层发挥作用，而非预期成本最小化器。方差比率和基于标准差的安全库存应作为诊断指标而非优化目标。学习到的需求算子是非正态的，但CD的紧凑参数化核能以可解释方式匹配其运营性能。

Original Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习需求预测优化了统计精度，却留下了过度的运营波动性，这会导致安全库存膨胀和牛鞭效应放大。我们提出上下文反卷积（Contextual Deconvolution, CD），一种两阶段估计器，将需求感知重构为凸分解问题：核调制带状算子将瞬态促销驱动的冲击与平滑的结构基线分离开来，层次化部分池化使得无需逐SKU训练即可实现目录级规模部署。该算子由数据推导而非人为施加——当促销响应为脉冲式时（M5的大部分数据、Favorita的所有数据），它退化为恒等映射，仅当存在真实的多日结转效应时才发挥作用，因此增益源自结构分解本身。我们在30,490个M5 SKU和2,845个Favorita商品上进行了严格的样本外评估，在给予CD相同未来日历的日历感知基线条件下，通过完整的库存成本核算锚定贡献：CD降低了安全库存、持有成本和订单方差，但低估了事件峰值，仅当持有成本超过缺货成本约20%（95%置信区间[17%,25%]）时才降低总成本；否则，它作为运营稳定性和库存资本层发挥作用，而非预期成本最小化器。其准确性贡献在于可靠性而非中心趋势：在11个基线方法中，CD在所有四个M5数据划分中均取得了最低的跨SKU误差横截面离散度，且仅有0.8%的SKU出现超过200%的误预测，而每个基线方法的这一比例为9.9-20.6%。由于方差比率和基于标准差的安全库存会被任何足够平滑的预测最小化，我们将它们视为诊断指标而非优化目标。一项支持性分析表明，学习到的需求算子是非正态的，但CD的紧凑参数化核能够以可解释的方式匹配其运营性能。

</details>


### [171] [A Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Thermal Ranking of Low-Cost Wall Materials in Hot-Dry Climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25668)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan,Fahim Raees,Ubaida Fatima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段计算框架，结合Crank-Nicolson有限差分法和物理信息神经算子（PINO），对五种低成本本土墙体材料进行热性能排序。在热干气候条件下，黏土-秸秆土坯墙性价比最优；在次环境温度条件下，材料排序反转，导热性好的烧结黏土砖更优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在炎热干燥气候地区（夏季气温常超45°C），低收入农村住宅需要识别经济实惠的本土建筑材料以最小化墙体热渗透，从而改善室内热舒适性。现有研究缺乏系统的、计算高效的材料热性能评估框架。

Method: 两阶段计算框架：阶段一，使用经验证的Crank-Nicolson有限差分法（FDM）求解一维瞬态热传导方程（Robin边界条件），通过拉丁超立方采样生成1500个周期日解；阶段二，构建基于傅里叶神经算子（FNO）的物理信息神经算子（PINO）学习参数到解的算子，同时保证数据保真度和PDE一致性。

Result: PINO的相对L2场误差为5.14e-4，峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差0.201 K，完全保持FDM的材料排序；用150个FDM样本训练的PINO与用300个样本训练的纯数据FNO性能相当；ISO 13786时间滞后和衰减因子的再现精度分别达到0.99 h和0.010；黏土-秸秆土坯墙在标称热干夏季条件下性价比最优；气候扫描发现材料排序在次环境温度条件下发生反转。

Conclusion: 该框架支持热干地区灾后重建中基于证据的材料选择，揭示了热排除与热排斥两种不同工况下的材料选择策略。

Original Abstract: Identifying cost-effective indigenous building materials that minimise heat penetration through walls is critical for indoor thermal comfort in low-income rural housing in hot-dry climates, where summer temperatures routinely exceed 45 C. We present a two-stage computational framework for thermal ranking of five low-cost indigenous wall materials: mud brick, clay-straw adobe, lime-stabilised bamboo panel, fired clay brick, and lime-mud composite. First, a validated Crank-Nicolson finite difference method (FDM) solves the one-dimensional transient heat equation with Robin boundary conditions under diurnal solar and outdoor air-temperature forcing, generating 1500 periodic-day solutions across a nine-dimensional parameter space by Latin Hypercube sampling. Second, a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone learns the parameter-to-solution operator mu -> T(x,t), enforcing both data fidelity and PDE consistency. The trained PINO attains a relative L2 field error of 5.14e-4 and a 0.201 K mean absolute error on the peak inner surface temperature, preserving the FDM material ranking exactly; PINO trained on 150 FDM samples matches a data-only FNO trained on twice as many, so the physics loss is most valuable when data are scarce. The periodic-day formulation also yields the ISO 13786 time lag and decrement factor, reproduced to within 0.99 h and 0.010. At nominal hot-dry summer conditions, clay-straw adobe achieves the best cost-performance index among widely available materials. A climate sweep, confirmed by FDM spot checks, reveals a regime boundary: under sub-ambient outdoor conditions the ranking inverts to conductive fired clay brick, delineating heat-exclusion and heat-rejection regimes. The framework supports evidence-based material selection for post-flood reconstruction in hot-dry regions.

中文摘要翻译: 在炎热干燥气候地区，夏季气温常超过45°C，识别能够最小化墙体热渗透的经济实惠的本土建筑材料对于低收入农村住宅的室内热舒适性至关重要。我们提出了一个两阶段计算框架，用于对五种低成本本土墙体材料进行热排序：泥砖、黏土-秸秆土坯、石灰稳定竹板、烧结黏土砖和石灰-泥复合材料。首先，一个经过验证的Crank-Nicolson有限差分法在日夜太阳辐射和室外气温强迫下，结合Robin边界条件求解一维瞬态热方程，通过拉丁超立方采样在九维参数空间中生成1500个周期日解。其次，一个以傅里叶神经算子为骨干的物理信息神经算子学习参数到解的算子μ→T(x,t)，同时保证数据保真度和偏微分方程一致性。训练后的PINO达到了5.14e-4的相对L²场误差和0.201 K的峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差，完全保持了FDM的材料排序；用150个FDM样本训练的PINO与用双倍数据训练的纯数据FNO性能相当，因此当数据稀缺时物理损失最为有价值。周期日公式还得到了ISO 13786时间滞后和衰减因子，再现精度在0.99小时和0.010以内。在标称热干夏季条件下，黏土-秸秆土坯在广泛可用的材料中实现了最佳成本-性能指数。经FDM抽样验证的气候扫描揭示了一个工况边界：在次环境室外温度条件下，材料排序反转，导热性好的烧结黏土砖居前，划分了热排除和热排斥两种工况。该框架为热干地区灾后重建中基于证据的材料选择提供了支持。

</details>


### [172] [DynaBridge: Dynamic Summary-Guided Cross-Task Multimodal Fusion for DASS-Structured Mental Health Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25679)
*Shiyu Teng,Haichen Yu,Jiaqing Liu,Hao Sun,Yu Song,Shurong Chai,Ruibo Hou,Lanfen Lin,Yen-Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynaBridge框架，通过动态摘要引导的多模态融合与DASS-21心理测量结构对齐，在抑郁、焦虑、压力评估中取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通用多模态融合模型忽略了DASS-21问卷标签的心理测量结构（如固定条目到子量表的映射、有序症状条目的序数特性），导致模型未充分利用标签的结构化信息。

Method: DynaBridge是一种动态摘要引导的跨任务多模态框架：1）编码声学、视觉和文本线索；2）用冻结大语言模型生成DASS感知摘要作为语义证据；3）预测有序条目分布，从条目级软分数重建D/A/S风险证据；4）融合条目级证据与直接多模态风险预测；5）采用置信度感知的保守融合策略整合高置信度语义线索。

Result: 在AdoDAS官方验证集上，DynaBridge在D/A/S风险预测上达到0.5012平均F1，在DASS-21条目预测上达到0.3216平均QWK，优于官方基线和代表性多模态方法。

Conclusion: 桥接多模态线索、语义摘要与DASS-21心理测量结构能够显著提升心理健康评估性能，验证了结构化标签知识引导多模态融合的有效性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal behavioral analysis offers a scalable approach to assessing depression, anxiety, and stress, yet generic fusion models often ignore the psychometric structure of questionnaire labels. In DASS-21, risk labels are derived from ordered symptom items through fixed item-to-subscale mappings. We propose \textbf{DynaBridge}, a dynamic summary-guided cross-task multimodal framework for DASS-structured mental health assessment. DynaBridge encodes acoustic, visual, and textual cues across multiple sessions and augments them with frozen-LLM-generated DASS-aware summaries as participant-level semantic evidence. It predicts ordinal item distributions, reconstructs depression, anxiety, and stress risk evidence from item-level soft scores, and fuses this evidence with direct multimodal risk predictions. A confidence-aware refinement strategy further incorporates high-confidence semantic cues conservatively. On the official AdoDAS validation split, DynaBridge outperforms the official baseline and representative multimodal methods, achieving 0.5012 mean F1 for D/A/S risk prediction and 0.3216 mean QWK for DASS-21 item prediction. These results show the value of bridging multimodal cues, semantic summaries, and DASS-21 psychometric structure.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态行为分析为评估抑郁、焦虑和压力提供了一种可扩展的方法，然而通用的融合模型通常忽略问卷标签的心理测量结构。在DASS-21中，风险标签是通过固定的条目到子量表映射从有序症状条目推导得出的。本文提出DynaBridge——一种面向DASS结构化心理健康评估的动态摘要引导跨任务多模态框架。DynaBridge编码跨多个会话的声学、视觉和文本线索，并用冻结大语言模型生成的DASS感知摘要作为参与者级别的语义证据进行增强。该框架预测有序条目分布，从条目级软分数重建抑郁、焦虑和压力的风险证据，并将该证据与直接的多模态风险预测相融合。一种置信度感知的精炼策略进一步保守地整合高置信度语义线索。在官方AdoDAS验证集上，DynaBridge优于官方基线和代表性多模态方法，在D/A/S风险预测上达到0.5012的平均F1，在DASS-21条目预测上达到0.3216的平均QWK。这些结果表明桥接多模态线索、语义摘要和DASS-21心理测量结构具有重要价值。

</details>


### [173] [Rashomon Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25680)
*Moisés Santos,Peter van der Putten,Bernhard Pfahringer,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何Rashomon对齐（Geometric Rashomon Alignment, RA），一种从几何视角衡量模型间功能相似性的新方法，通过在全数据空间均匀采样评估决策边界对齐程度，补充了传统基于数据分布的相似性度量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有功能相似性度量仅依赖真实数据分布，只能评估数据分布覆盖区域内的模型差异，缺乏对整个输入空间（包括无数据覆盖区域）决策边界对齐程度的全局视角。

Method: 提出几何Rashomon对齐（RA），从几何角度衡量模型相似性：在全实例空间中均匀采样数据，基于采样点上模型输出的一致性计算相似性，从而获得与数据分布无关的决策边界对齐度量。

Result: 在90余个数据集上的实验表明，几何对齐与分布对齐提供了不同且互补的模型相似性视角；在模型对齐与预测准确性出现分歧的关键案例中，RA展现了独特价值。

Conclusion: RA可用于模型选择、集成构建以及增强机器学习模型与算法的可解释性，是对现有分布对齐度量（如预测一致性）的有力补充。

Original Abstract: We propose Rashomon Alignment (RA), a new measure to assess functional similarity between two models. Existing functional similarity measures are distributional, quantifying differences between outputs of models applied to real-world data. However, these measures can be regarded as ecologically valid only for regions in the input space represented by the available data. We introduce a geometrical perspective on functional model similarity, which estimates it across the entire data space, offering a comprehensive view of decision boundary alignment independent of any specific data distribution. We also propose geometric Rashomon Alignment as a measure of geometrical similarity, which is computed using data uniformly sampled from the instance space. We perform an experimental analysis on more than 90 datasets, examining critical cases where model alignment diverges from predictive accuracy. Our results show that geometrical and distributional alignment provide different and complementary perspectives on the similarity between models and algorithms. RA can be used for multiple purposes, including model selection, ensemble construction, and enhanced interpretability of machine learning models and algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出Rashomon对齐（RA），一种衡量两个模型之间功能相似性的新指标。现有的功能相似性度量是分布性的，通过量化模型在真实数据上输出的差异来评估。然而，这些度量仅在可用数据所覆盖的输入空间区域具有生态效度。我们引入了几何视角下的功能模型相似性，该度量在整个数据空间上进行估计，提供了独立于任何特定数据分布的决策边界对齐的全局视图。我们还提出了几何Rashomon对齐（geometric Rashomon Alignment）作为几何相似性的度量，该度量使用从实例空间中均匀采样的数据来计算。我们在90余个数据集上进行了实验分析，考察了模型对齐与预测准确性出现分歧的关键案例。结果表明，几何对齐与分布对齐为模型和算法之间的相似性提供了不同且互补的视角。RA可用于多种用途，包括模型选择、集成构建以及增强机器学习模型与算法的可解释性。

</details>


### [174] [From Deterministic to Generative Deep Learning for Urban Air Quality Reconstruction from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25687)
*Abhishek A. Sabnis,Mihai Mitrea,Lya Lugon,Karine Sartelet,Marc Bocquet,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Shupeng Zhu,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散生成框架的多污染物重建方法，利用深度学习从稀疏观测数据中重构NO2、O3、PM2.5和PM10的全场分布，在巴黎城区9-28个监测站数据上验证，模型在模拟数据上结构相似度高，并通过数据增强无需重新训练即可泛化到真实观测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 空气污染的全场重构对污染暴露评估和公共卫生决策至关重要，但由于污染物间复杂相互作用、难以预测的天气模式以及监测站覆盖有限，这一任务具有挑战性。

Method: 采用基于扩散的生成框架进行多污染物联合重建，训练数据来自全场模拟数据，引入数据增强方法实现无需重新训练即可迁移到真实观测数据，并与确定性深度学习模型进行基准对比。

Result: 在模拟验证数据上取得高结构相似度（SSIM）；在真实观测数据上通过功率谱分析显示出合理的空间模式；数据增强方法使模型能够在无需重新训练的情况下泛化到训练期之外的时段。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型在空气污染全场重建任务中具有可靠的现实部署潜力。

Original Abstract: Full-field reconstruction of air pollution is essential for evaluating pollution exposure and supporting public health decision-making. However, the complex interactions among pollutants, hard-to-predict weather patterns, and limited monitoring station coverage make this a complex task. We apply deep learning techniques to provide fast and accurate reconstructions from sparse observations of four key pollutants: NO2, O3, PM2.5 and PM10. Models are trained on full-field simulation data and evaluated on real-world observations collected from 9 to 28 monitoring stations in the city of Paris. We introduce a diffusion-based generative framework for multi-pollutant reconstruction and benchmark its performance against deterministic deep learning models. Despite noisy observations and strong spatial variability, the models achieve high structural similarity on simulated validation data and produce realistic spatial patterns on real-world observations, as indicated by power-spectrum analysis.
